<div class="wrap">
<header>
  <p class="eyebrow">Gas turbines, propellers, ram-air engines and rockets · concept to preliminary design</p>
  <h1>Aircraft engine design studio</h1>
  <p class="lead">Pick an architecture, set the flight condition and the thrust or power you need, and shape the cycle; the studio solves the physics, sizes the engine, and redraws it to match. Pin a baseline to see what each change buys you, run sweeps and carpet plots for trade studies, then fly the finished design across the envelope on its component maps.</p>
  <p class="disc" role="note"><b>For training only, not for design.</b> This studio is an educational tool for learning how engines work and how design trade-offs behave. Its models are simplified and only partly validated. Do not use its results to design, build, modify, operate or certify any hardware.</p>
</header>

    <optgroup label="Shaft engines and propellers"><option value="turboprop">Turboprop</option><option value="turboshaft">Turboshaft</option><option value="piston">Piston + prop</option><option value="electric">Electric + prop</option></optgroup>
    <optgroup label="Ram-air engines"><option value="ramjet">Ramjet</option><option value="scramjet">Scramjet</option><option value="pulsejet">Pulsejet</option></optgroup>

<section class="card" aria-labelledby="hOd" id="odSec">
  <h2 class="sec" id="hOd">Off-design performance</h2>
  <p class="sub" id="odSub">Your design is now fixed hardware: the nozzle areas are frozen and each fan, compressor and turbine follows a map scaled through its design point (NASA public maps, as used in pyCycle). Fly it somewhere else, or throttle it back, and the solver re-matches the spools, airflow and bypass ratio.</p>
  <div class="odgrid">

<section class="card" aria-labelledby="hTs" id="tsCard">
  <h2 class="sec" id="hTs">Temperature–entropy diagram</h2>
  <p class="sub" id="tsSub">The cycle traced through each station. Compression climbs steeply, the combustor adds heat at nearly constant pressure, the turbines step back down, and the nozzle converts what is left into jet speed. The area enclosed is the useful work.</p>
  <div class="chartbox"><canvas id="tsChart" aria-label="Temperature-entropy diagram"></canvas></div>
</section>

<section class="card" aria-labelledby="hTr">
  <h2 class="sec" id="hTr">Trade study</h2>
  <p class="sub">Sweep one input while everything else stays at the current design. Thrust stays fixed, so the engine resizes at every point. The dot marks the current design.</p>

<section class="card" aria-labelledby="hCp">
  <h2 class="sec" id="hCp">Carpet plot</h2>
  <p class="sub" id="cpSub">Each line holds one parameter constant while the other varies. Lower is better on fuel burn; further right means a smaller, lighter engine for the same thrust.</p>
  <div class="chartbox" style="height:380px"><canvas id="cpChart" aria-label="Carpet plot"></canvas></div>
</section>

<section class="card" aria-labelledby="hPd" id="pdSec" hidden>

</section>

<section class="card" aria-labelledby="hOp" id="opSec">
  <h2 class="sec" id="hOp">Optimizer</h2>
  <p class="sub">Pick an objective and the design variables to free up. It finds a local optimum near your current design; try a few starting points for confidence. Constraints are checked at the design point only, so for a cruise-designed engine also check takeoff in the off-design panel.</p>

<section class="card" aria-labelledby="hVa">
  <h2 class="sec" id="hVa">Validation</h2>
  <p class="sub">Four reference cycles were run in NASA's open-source pyCycle (OpenMDAO) with identical inputs; this studio's solver reruns them live below. To compare like with like, the studio uses pyCycle's fuel-energy convention here: its tabular Jet-A model sets the fuel's enthalpy to zero, worth about 3.4% more heat per kilogram. The design tool itself uses Jet-A's real heating value.</p>
  <div class="tbl"><table id="vaTable"></table></div>
  <h3 class="h3" style="margin-top:18px">Off-design</h3>
  <p class="sub">The same reference engines, designed in pyCycle and then flown at three other conditions with T4 held at the values shown. Both tools use the same NASA component maps scaled through the design point, so these numbers test the matching solver directly.</p>
  <div class="tbl"><table id="vaODTable"></table></div>
  <h3 class="h3" style="margin-top:18px">Piston engines against published ratings</h3>
  <p class="sub">The piston model's two empirical constants (the fuel-air cycle factor and the friction-pressure curve) were calibrated so that these engines land close to their published sea-level ratings, using typical volumetric efficiencies for carbureted and fuel-injected engines. Treat this as a calibration check, not independent validation.</p>
  <div class="tbl"><table id="vaPiston"></table></div>
  <h3 class="h3" style="margin-top:18px">Shock relations against NACA Report 1135</h3>
  <p class="sub">The inlet model's oblique- and normal-shock relations (calorically perfect air, γ = 1.4), checked against the standard tables in NACA Report 1135.</p>
  <div class="tbl"><table id="vaShock"></table></div>
  <h3 class="h3" style="margin-top:18px">Ramjet and scramjet against equilibrium chemistry</h3>
  <p class="sub">The studio burns fuel to complete products with no dissociation ("frozen" chemistry). For the same combustor states, Cantera with NASA Glenn data computes the frozen case, which the studio matches almost exactly, and the equilibrium case, where hot CO₂ and H₂O partly dissociate and recombine in the nozzle. The gap grows with temperature: negligible for a kerosene ramjet near 2,300 K, about 3–6% in specific impulse for a hydrogen scramjet above 3,400 K. Treat scramjet numbers as optimistic by about that much.</p>
  <div class="tbl"><table id="vaRam"></table></div>
  <h3 class="h3" style="margin-top:18px">Pulsejet against the Argus As 014</h3>
  <p class="sub">The pulsejet model is semi-empirical: three constants (chamber fill fraction, ram charging and pulse-to-jet efficiency) were fitted so the Argus geometry reproduces its published static thrust, thrust at 750 km/h and TSFC. That makes it a calibration, not independent validation; frequency and mass are the out-of-sample checks. Sources: Wikipedia (Argus As 014) and published V-1 data.</p>
  <div class="tbl"><table id="vaPulse"></table></div>
  <h3 class="h3" style="margin-top:18px">Rocket engines against published specific impulse</h3>
  <p class="sub">Chamber and nozzle performance come from shifting-equilibrium tables computed with Cantera (NASA Glenn thermodynamic data, condensed alumina included), then scaled by default efficiencies (c* 97.5%, thrust coefficient 98%; 95% and 97% for the small R-4D). Gas-generator engines also pay for the turbine exhaust they dump. Published values are from manufacturers and NASA via Wikipedia's comparison of orbital rocket engines; the representative inputs (chamber pressure, mixture ratio, area ratio) are public figures.</p>
  <div class="tbl"><table id="vaRocket"></table></div>
  <h3 class="h3" style="margin-top:18px">Engine mass against published dry masses</h3>
  <p class="sub">Mass is estimated from statistical correlations, not from component layout: Raymer's thrust-based equations for jets (using sea-level static thrust), power-law fits for shaft engines, thrust-to-weight by feed cycle for liquid rockets, and shell area for ram-air engines. Expect ±20–30% for any single engine, and more for outliers: SpaceX's Merlin and Raptor are far lighter than the historical average for their cycles, and modern high-bypass turbofans run heavier than Raymer's 1980s-era fit. Use it to compare concepts, not to sign off a weight budget.</p>
  <div class="tbl"><table id="vaMass"></table></div>
  <h3 class="h3" style="margin-top:18px">Liquid rocket preliminary design against published component data</h3>
  <p class="sub">The RS-25 and F-1 presets in Preliminary mode with predicted efficiencies, against published figures. Throat heat flux uses the Bartz correlation. Pump speeds are what the studio picks automatically from suction, specific-speed and bearing limits, so they test the sizing logic and not a fitted number. Pump power runs high or low mainly through the pressure schedule (injector, jacket and line losses), which is generic here.</p>
  <div class="tbl"><table id="vaPD"></table></div>
  <h3 class="h3" style="margin-top:18px">Liquid rocket mass buildup against published dry masses</h3>
  <p class="sub">Thrust chamber, injector and turbopump masses are built up from the sized geometry; valves, lines, gimbal, controls and any gas generator or preburner are a fraction of that, fitted to these same engines: one constant per feed cycle for flanged heritage engines and one reduction factor for integrated modern builds (Merlin, Raptor). The first seven rows show how well five constants fit seven engines; the two marked as outside the fit are the independent check, and they land about 20% off.</p>
  <div class="tbl"><table id="vaPDm"></table></div>
  <h3 class="h3" style="margin-top:18px">Gas turbine mass buildup against published dry masses</h3>
  <p class="sub">Blades, disks, casings, combustor, shafts and gearboxes are built up from the stage-by-stage design. Every item is now a physical part: there is no lump scaled on airflow. Three constants are fitted to these nine engines and to the published CFM56-7B module weights: a size factor on bladed parts (small parts carry proportionally more mass in roots, platforms, flanges and fittings, and accessories are spread across them), a fan frame and case term on fan frontal area, and a frame and bearing term on radius squared. So the table shows goodness of fit (about 9% rms); leaving each engine out in turn gives about 21%, which is the error to expect on a new design. For the CFM56 the modules come out at fan +11%, core −33% and low-pressure turbine +9% against the published weights. Stage counts come from the cycle sizing and are compared with the real engines as a check on loading assumptions.</p>
  <div class="tbl"><table id="vaGT"></table></div>
  <h3 class="h3" style="margin-top:18px">Known deficiencies: the calibration worklist</h3>
  <p class="sub">Every place where the studio is known to disagree with published data or to lean on an unvalidated assumption, with the measured gap where there is one. This list drives the next calibration pass.</p>
  <div class="tbl"><table id="vaKI"></table></div>
</section>

<section class="card" aria-labelledby="hMe">
  <h2 class="sec" id="hMe">Method, assumptions and accuracy</h2>
  <div class="method">
    <section><h3>Thermodynamics</h3><p>Air and lean Jet-A (C₁₂H₂₃) combustion products are ideal gases with temperature-dependent properties from NASA 7-coefficient polynomials (GRI-Mech 3.0 data). Heat release uses Jet-A's heat of formation (LHV 43.35 MJ/kg). Dissociation is ignored, which matters little below ~2,000 K but makes afterburner fuel flow a few percent optimistic.</p></section>
    <section><h3>Components</h3><ul><li>Compressor and turbine efficiencies can be polytropic (the default, so trades across pressure ratio stay fair) or isentropic.</li><li>Subsonic inlet recovery is an input; above Mach 1 the MIL-E-5008B shock-loss schedule is applied on top.</li><li>Turbine cooling is drawn from compressor exit: 60% re-enters before the HPT rotor (and does work), 40% after it. The automatic estimate assumes modern film cooling and thermal barrier coatings.</li><li>Mixed-flow engines solve the fan pressure ratio so that the bypass and core streams arrive at the mixer at equal total pressure.</li><li>Nozzles follow pyCycle's convention: convergent nozzles choke and add pressure thrust; con-di nozzles expand fully.</li></ul></section>
    <section><h3>Sizing and geometry</h3><p>Airflow is scaled to deliver the required thrust. Annulus areas come from mass flow and assumed axial Mach numbers. Stage counts come from stage-loading limits (Δh/U²) with blade speeds set by tip-speed limits: fan ψ 0.28 and ≤470 m/s by default, HPC ψ 0.25 at 450 m/s tip, HPT ψ 2.3, LPT ψ 2.7. A fan gearbox lets the LP turbine spin faster, which is why it needs fewer stages. Lengths and radii are representative, not detailed design.</p></section>
    <section><h3>Off-design matching</h3><p>Each turbomachine follows a NASA public map (the ones shipped with pyCycle), scaled so that it passes exactly through your design point. At any other condition a Newton solver finds the airflow, bypass ratio, spool speeds and map operating points that satisfy flow continuity through every fan, compressor, turbine and nozzle, plus power balance on each shaft. Nozzle throat areas are frozen at their design values, except that an afterburner's variable nozzle opens to keep the core matched. The max rating is the highest T4 that respects the limits you set on T4, spool speeds and T3. Surge margin is measured at constant corrected speed against the map's surge line.</p></section>
    <section><h3>Turboprops and turboshafts</h3><p>A single-spool gas generator (compressor, combustor, turbine) feeds a free power turbine that expands the gas down to the exhaust pressure ratio you set; whatever is left becomes residual jet thrust. Off-design, the power turbine is held at constant speed by the governor, as on a real turboprop or helicopter engine, and the max rating also respects a shaft-power (flat-rating) limit.</p></section>
    <section><h3>Propellers</h3><p>Blade-element momentum theory with Prandtl tip and hub loss and swirl, on a generic cambered section with a stall model, rotational stall delay inboard, and compressibility drag rise. The blade planform comes from diameter, blade count and activity factor, with twist set for the design advance ratio. A constant-speed propeller changes pitch to absorb the shaft power. Expect about ±5% on efficiency in cruise and ±10–15% on static thrust.</p></section>
    <section><h3>Piston and electric</h3><p>Piston: four-stroke air flow from volumetric efficiency and manifold density, a fuel-air cycle approximated from the Otto efficiency, rich-mixture combustion loss, and a friction mean effective pressure that rises with rpm, calibrated to published ratings. Turbocharging holds manifold pressure to a critical altitude with intercooling and back-pressure losses. Electric: battery, inverter and motor efficiencies, C-rate and endurance.</p></section>
    <section><h3>Ram-air engines</h3><p>Ramjet inlets use exact oblique- and normal-shock relations (γ = 1.4) with ramp angles optimized for maximum recovery at the design Mach number; off design the ramps stay fixed, the first shock moves off the cowl lip, and the captured flow falls (two-dimensional spillage). The combustor adds heat in a constant-area duct (Rayleigh flow, with a check for thermal choking) plus a flameholder loss. The scramjet follows Heiser and Pratt's one-dimensional analysis: inlet compression set by T3/T0 and a kinetic-energy efficiency, a constant-pressure supersonic combustor with wall friction and fuel-injection momentum, and an expansion efficiency; off design the inlet throat area is fixed. Hydrogen and Jet-A products use the same NASA polynomials as the gas turbines, without dissociation. The pulsejet is semi-empirical: acoustic frequency a/4L, a constant-volume pressure rise per cycle, ram charging and a valve-closure limit, with three constants fitted to the Argus As 014. The turbo-ramjet is a turbojet with a translating-spike inlet (two oblique shocks re-optimized at every Mach number) and a compressor bleed schedule that dumps air into the afterburner above about Mach 2, as in the J58.</p></section>
    <section><h3>Rockets</h3><p>Chamber and nozzle performance come from tables computed with Cantera and NASA Glenn thermodynamic data: equilibrium combustion at constant pressure, then shifting-equilibrium isentropic expansion to the throat (found as the point of maximum mass flux) and on to area ratios from 1.5 to 200. Aluminized solids include condensed Al₂O₃ in equilibrium with the gas, with no velocity or thermal lag. The studio interpolates c*, vacuum thrust coefficient and exit pressure in mixture ratio, log chamber pressure and log area ratio, then applies c* and thrust-coefficient efficiencies. Feed systems are sized from pump power (propellant flow × pressure rise ÷ density ÷ efficiency): gas generators dump their turbine exhaust at low velocity, closed cycles balance turbine power against pumps by raising the pump discharge pressure, and the expander's heat pickup scales with chamber pressure^0.8 and throat area^0.9, calibrated to the RL10 and Vinci. Solids use a BATES grain with Saint-Robert's burn-rate law, and aluminized propellants lose thrust coefficient to two-phase flow as the expansion ratio grows (one constant, fitted to the Star 48BV); hybrids, a single circular port with r = a·Gox^n. Flow separation uses the Kalt–Bendall criterion.</p></section>
    <section><h3>Liquid rocket preliminary design</h3><p>Switching Detail to Preliminary sizes the hardware behind the cycle. The chamber follows a characteristic length and contraction ratio; the nozzle is a Rao parabolic bell (angles from fits to Rao's charts) or a 15° cone. Gas-side heat transfer uses the Bartz correlation with a recovery temperature, a film-cooling effectiveness you set, and, for kerosene, a carbon-layer resistance from Huzel and Huang scaled with pressure. The coolant is marched counterflow along the jacket: Dittus–Boelter convection with a fin correction for milled channels, simple property fits for RP-1, hydrogen, methane and MMH, and friction pressure drop. Channels widen, deepen, then split as the radius grows; tubes follow the wall. Nozzle efficiency combines a divergence factor with a flat-plate skin-friction integral along the wall; finite-rate chemistry losses are not included. The injector is sized from an orifice equation. Pump speed is the fastest allowed by suction specific speed, a specific speed of about 2,700 (US units) per stage, bearing DN and a 120,000 rpm cap; stage count comes from impeller tip speed; efficiency is Anderson's correlation with a 10% knock-down for rocket pumps; turbine efficiency follows blade-to-spouting velocity ratio. In Predicted mode these feed back into the cycle (nozzle, pump and turbine efficiencies, jacket pressure drop, and the expander's turbine inlet temperature) and the two are iterated to agreement. Structure checks the hot wall in bending between lands (or tube hoop stress) and sizes the closeout for hoop load. Mass is built up from the liner, jacket, extension, injector and turbopumps (Humble's torque correlation), plus a fraction for valves, lines, gimbal and controls calibrated per feed cycle. Material properties are approximate handbook values.</p></section>
    <section><h3>Requirements, margins and sensitivity</h3><p>Flight-condition requirements are checked by running the fixed design at full rating at each condition (max-rating match for gas turbines, fixed geometry for ram-air engines, fixed throat and nozzle for rockets). Sizing scales the engine until the most demanding condition has zero margin. Sensitivity perturbs one slider at a time, so it shows local, first-order effects and not interactions.</p></section>
    <section><h3>System level</h3><p>Engine mass uses statistical correlations (Raymer's thrust-based equations for jets at sea-level static thrust, power-law fits for shaft engines, thrust-to-weight by feed cycle for liquid rockets, shell area for ram-air ducts). The mission card flies a Breguet cruise at the throttle setting that balances drag, adding 5% reserve fuel; for rockets it gives the stage's ideal Tsiolkovsky Δv. The optimizer is a bounded Nelder–Mead search that reruns the full cycle at every point. The comparison chart flies each preset at full rating along a constant dynamic-pressure climb.</p></section>
    <section><h3>Preliminary design for the other engine families</h3><p>Every family has a Preliminary level. Gas turbines get a mean-line design of each compressor and turbine stage (flow and loading coefficients, de Haller number, diffusion factor, Smith-chart efficiency, blade counts, AN² and rim speed), combustor residence time and loading, blade metal temperatures and a component mass buildup. Solid motors burn the chosen port shape back numerically to give the pressure and thrust trace, then size the case, insulation and nozzle; hybrids add multi-port grains, oxidizer-to-fuel shift and the tank. Piston engines get cylinder proportions, piston speed, knock and peak-pressure limits and a turbocharger balance; electric powertrains get a motor sized from torque and airgap shear stress and a pack sized by cells, with voltage sag, heating and discharge-rate checks; propellers get tip Mach number, root stress and mass. Ramjets get shock-detachment and terminal-shock margins, residence time and uncooled wall temperatures; scramjets get the wall heat load against the fuel's heat sink, leading-edge heating and the dynamic-pressure corridor; pulsejets get shell, valve and reed checks. Most of this is tagged unvalidated: the methods are standard textbook ones, but only the gas turbine and liquid rocket mass buildups and the shuttle booster have been compared with published engines so far. In Preliminary, gas turbines draw the designed annulus in the 3D view. The table of known deficiencies in the validation section lists every gap.</p></section>
    <section><h3>What to expect</h3><ul><li>Thrust and TSFC: within about 1% of pyCycle for the same inputs; 5–10% for real engines, depending mostly on how well the efficiencies, losses and cooling are known.</li><li>Stage counts and diameters: right ballpark for the presets (±1–2 stages).</li><li>Off-design: within about 1% of pyCycle when the same maps are used; real engines differ most where the generic maps differ from their actual hardware.</li><li>Ramjet and kerosene-fueled ram engines: within a few percent of equilibrium chemistry; hydrogen scramjets read 3–6% optimistic on specific impulse because the model doesn't dissociate the products.</li><li>Rocket specific impulse: within about ±4% of the published values for the engines in the validation table, using default efficiencies.</li><li>Engine mass: ±20–30% typical, worse for outliers (see the validation table).</li><li>Liquid rocket preliminary design: throat heat flux within the published range for the RS-25; pump speeds within about 15% and pump power within about 25% of published values; wall temperatures are sensitive to the heat-transfer correlations and should be read as ±15–20%. Treat every margin as a prompt to look closer, not a clearance.</li><li>Throttle and start response: first-order lag estimates from rotor inertia and chamber volume, unvalidated.</li><li>The 3D view: blades are lofted airfoil sections with representative twist, camber and thickness, not designed profiles. Detail is set automatically from measured frame time: the pixel ratio rises above the screen's own when there is headroom and falls, with shadows and blade detail, when frames slow down.</li><li>Not modeled: installation effects beyond ramjet spillage, variable stator scheduling, power offtake, noise, emissions, detailed transients, combustion instability, structural or thermal life, and stage-level trajectory losses for rockets.</li></ul><p>Use it for learning and conceptual trade studies, not for decisions anything flies on.</p></section>
  </div>

Pass 3: a search for the flattest grain was added (shuttle booster: peak over average 1.33 with 7 fin slots)","fix":"Add a grain optimizer that solves for the flattest trace; add erosive burning"},{"area":"Solid rockets, preliminary","item":"Burn-back is two-dimensional; end burning, segment gaps and tapered ports are not modelled","measure":"Not measured","fix":"Add axial burn-back for segmented and tapered grains"},{"area":"Solid rockets, preliminary","item":"Solid motor model against a space motor (Star 48B)","measure":"Pass 5: a near-spherical space-motor option and a two-phase flow loss were added. Star 48B: propellant +3%, vacuum specific impulse +1.7%, inert mass 169 kg against 126 kg (+34%). Star 37FM, not used in any fit: propellant +1%, specific impulse +0.4%, inert mass +9%, diameter 0.97 m against 0.93 m. The two-phase loss constant is fitted to the Star 48BV. The pressure trace for this option is prescribed, not computed, and the Concept level still sizes it as a BATES grain, which gives nonsense for this preset (it opens in Preliminary)","fix":"Model the slotted head-end grain in three dimensions; give Concept a matching simple space-motor sizing; find why the Star 48B's inert mass is over while the 37FM's is close"},{"area":"Hybrid rockets, preliminary","item":"Regression-rate law is a single published fit; no validation of oxidizer-to-fuel shift or mass","measure":"Pass 5: the LOX/HTPB constants (0.0304 mm/s, exponent 0.681) match the published space-time-averaged fit exactly. Oxidizer-to-fuel shift, multi-port slivers and masses are still unvalidated; no published hybrid motor mass breakdown was found","fix":"Find a hybrid with a published mass breakdown (AMROC H-500 or SpaceShipOne's motor)"},{"area":"Piston engines, preliminary","item":"Knock limit, peak pressure and valve Mach index are engineering estimates","measure":"Not measured","fix":"Calibrate against published manifold-pressure limits and cylinder data for Lycoming and Continental engines"},{"area":"Piston engines, preliminary","item":"Mass buildup has not been compared with published dry masses","measure":"O-360: 119 kg without propeller against 117 kg published (+2%, not fitted). TIO-540: 193 kg, published figure not yet sourced","fix":"Source TIO-540 and Continental masses; add a validation table"},{"area":"Electric, preliminary","item":"Motor mass rests on one calibration point; pack heating check has no cooling model","measure":"Pass 3: liquid cold-plate option added. Velis pack rises 34 K at full power against a 30 K limit (15% over); the cold-plate conductance is an estimate","fix":"Add more motors to the mass fit; source pack thermal data"},{"area":"Propellers, preliminary","item":"No noise, no blade-element stress or flutter check; root stress is a single-coefficient estimate","measure":"Not measured","fix":"Add blade-element loads and a noise estimate"},{"area":"Ramjets, preliminary","item":"Wall temperatures assume a radiating uncooled shell; no validated heat-transfer data","measure":"Talos wall 3% over the Haynes 230 limit, which a film-cooled liner would fix","fix":"Add film-cooled liner model and validate against published ramjet combustor data"},{"area":"Scramjets, preliminary","item":"Scramjet wall heating for the X-43A preset","measure":"Pass 3: a short-burn heat-sink mode was added and the preset uses it (10 s burn, 20 mm copper). The wall still reaches 1,540 K against a 1,000 K limit, and the leading edge is 9% over, yet the real vehicle survived. The reference-enthalpy heat flux (7.6 MW/m\u00b2) is unvalidated and probably high. Pass 4: the predicted 7.6 MW/m\u00b2 is in line with published scramjet combustor averages (above 5.8 MW/m\u00b2), so the flux is probably not the problem; the real wall thickness, material and water cooling are what is unknown","fix":"Validate the heat flux against published X-43A and X-51A data; add water-cooled leading edges"},{"area":"Scramjets, preliminary","item":"Mass is areal density times panel area with no validation","measure":"Not measured","fix":"Compare with published X-51A engine mass"},{"area":"Pulsejets, preliminary","item":"Shell thickness, wall temperature and reed frequency are estimates on top of a three-constant cycle fit","measure":"Pass 1: Argus mass 147 kg against 153\u2013170 kg published (was 100 kg); the minimum shell gauge is fitted to this one engine. Wall 5% over the stainless limit","fix":"Validate against the published Argus As 014 mass breakdown"},{"area":"All preliminary families except liquid rockets","item":"The 3D view still shows the Concept geometry rather than the designed parts","measure":"Pass 5: gas turbines draw the designed annulus, solids the designed grain or case, piston engines the designed bore and stroke, and electric motors the sized rotor. Ram-air preliminary design does not change the flow path, so there is nothing new to draw; the battery pack is still drawn from energy alone","fix":"Draw the pack from cell count and layout"},{"area":"Phase 13 tools","item":"Throttle response, trade-off front, report and STL export are first versions","measure":"Throttle response is a chain of first-order lags from estimated rotor inertia: CFM56 idle to 90% thrust in 6.8 s, RS-25 hardware-limited start 0.5 s. Not validated. The STL is the display model (open surfaces), not watertight solids. The trade-off front is random sampling, not a true multi-objective optimizer","fix":"Validate spool-up against published acceleration times; export closed solids or STEP; use an evolutionary multi-objective search"},{"area":"Liquid rocket, preliminary","item":"Chamber wall temperature against material limits","measure":"Pass 2: RS-25 predicted throat wall 863 K against about 813 K published normal (+6%); the NARloy-Z limit is now the 922 K used in the published life analysis, so the preset passes. RL10 (11% over) and F-1 coolant side (7% over) still fail. Pass 4: a NASA study of a hydrogen-cooled 347 stainless tube nozzle found predicted gas-side wall temperatures from 861 K to 1,250 K depending on the correlation used, so the RL10's 1,110 K against a 1,000 K limit is inside the method's own scatter","fix":"Source RL10 tube-wall and F-1 coolant-side temperatures; check the coolant-side correlation at low pressure"},{"area":"Liquid rocket, preliminary","item":"Flow-separation check uses mean exit pressure, not wall pressure","measure":"Pass 3: fixed. The check now uses lip wall pressure from the bell's exit angle; RS-25 passes at sea level","fix":"Validate the wall-pressure estimate against published nozzle data"}];

</script>
<script>
/* Jet engine design-point cycle solver.
   Variable-cp ideal gas (NASA 7-coefficient polynomials, GRI-Mech 3.0 data) for air and lean Jet-A (C12H23)
   combustion products, no dissociation. Station numbering follows SAE ARP755. SI units throughout. */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  const Ru = 8314.462618; // J/(kmol K)
  const SP = {
    N2: { mw: 28.014, lo: [3.298677, 0.0014082404, -3.963222e-06, 5.641515e-09, -2.444854e-12, -1020.8999, 3.950372], hi: [2.92664, 0.0014879768, -5.68476e-07, 1.0097038e-10, -6.753351e-15, -922.7977, 5.980528] },
    O2: { mw: 31.998, lo: [3.78245636, -0.00299673416, 9.84730201e-06, -9.68129509e-09, 3.24372837e-12, -1063.94356, 3.65767573], hi: [3.28253784, 0.00148308754, -7.57966669e-07, 2.09470555e-10, -2.16717794e-14, -1088.45772, 5.45323129] },
    AR: { mw: 39.95, lo: [2.5, 0, 0, 0, 0, -745.375, 4.366], hi: [2.5, 0, 0, 0, 0, -745.375, 4.366] },
    CO2: { mw: 44.009, lo: [2.35677352, 0.00898459677, -7.12356269e-06, 2.45919022e-09, -1.43699548e-13, -48371.9697, 9.90105222], hi: [3.85746029, 0.00441437026, -2.21481404e-06, 5.23490188e-10, -4.72084164e-14, -48759.166, 2.27163806] },
    H2O: { mw: 18.015, lo: [4.19864056, -0.0020364341, 6.52040211e-06, -5.48797062e-09, 1.77197817e-12, -30293.7267, -0.849032208], hi: [3.03399249, 0.00217691804, -1.64072518e-07, -9.7041987e-11, 1.68200992e-14, -30004.2971, 4.9667701] }
  };
  const AIRX = { N2: 0.78084, O2: 0.209476, AR: 0.00934, CO2: 0.000314 };
  const MWAIR = Object.keys(AIRX).reduce((s, k) => s + AIRX[k] * SP[k].mw, 0);
  const YAIR = {}; Object.keys(AIRX).forEach(k => (YAIR[k] = AIRX[k] * SP[k].mw / MWAIR));
  const MWF = 12 * 12.011 + 23 * 1.008; // C12H23
  const DEL = { CO2: 12 * SP.CO2.mw / MWF, H2O: 11.5 * SP.H2O.mw / MWF, O2: -17.75 * SP.O2.mw / MWF };
  const H_FUEL = -249657e3 / MWF; // J/kg, Jet-A(g) heat of formation at 298.15 K
  const FAR_STOICH = YAIR.O2 / -DEL.O2;
  function combo(w) { const lo = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0], hi = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]; let R = 0;
    Object.keys(w).forEach(k => { const r = Ru / SP[k].mw; R += w[k] * r; for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) { lo[i] += w[k] * r * SP[k].lo[i]; hi[i] += w[k] * r * SP[k].hi[i]; } });
    return { lo, hi, R }; }
  const A = combo(YAIR), D = combo(DEL);
  const cf = (c, T) => (T < 1000 ? c.lo : c.hi);
  const cpC = (c, T) => { const a = cf(c, T); return a[0] + T * (a[1] + T * (a[2] + T * (a[3] + T * a[4]))); };
  const hC = (c, T) => { const a = cf(c, T); return T * (a[0] + T * (a[1] / 2 + T * (a[2] / 3 + T * (a[3] / 4 + T * a[4] / 5)))) + a[5]; };
  const sC = (c, T) => { const a = cf(c, T); return a[0] * Math.log(T) + T * (a[1] + T * (a[2] / 2 + T * (a[3] / 3 + T * a[4] / 4))) + a[6]; };
  // per kg of mixture, far = kg fuel burned per kg air
  const G = {
    R: f => (A.R + f * D.R) / (1 + f),
    cp: (T, f) => (cpC(A, T) + f * cpC(D, T)) / (1 + f),
    h: (T, f) => (hC(A, T) + f * hC(D, T)) / (1 + f),
    phi: (T, f) => (sC(A, T) + f * sC(D, T)) / (1 + f),
    gamma(T, f) { const cp = G.cp(T, f); return cp / (cp - G.R(f)); },
    TfromH(h, f, T = 800) { for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) { const dT = (G.h(T, f) - h) / G.cp(T, f); T -= dT; if (T < 120) T = 120; if (Math.abs(dT) < 1e-7) break; } return T; },
    TfromPhi(p, f, T = 800) { for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) { const dT = (G.phi(T, f) - p) / (G.cp(T, f) / T); T -= dT; if (T < 120) T = 120; if (Math.abs(dT) < 1e-7) break; } return T; }
  };
  const LHV = H_FUEL - hC(D, 298.15);
  const GAS0 = G;

  function isa(alt, dT = 0) {
    let T, P;
    if (alt <= 11000) { T = 288.15 - 0.0065 * alt; P = 101325 * Math.pow(T / 288.15, 5.255877); }
    else if (alt <= 20000) { T = 216.65; P = 22632.06 * Math.exp(-(alt - 11000) / 6341.62); }
    else { T = 216.65 + 0.001 * (alt - 20000); P = 5474.889 * Math.pow(T / 216.65, -34.16319); }
    return { T: T + dT, P };
  }
  // flow station: W (kg/s), Tt, Pt, far (burned fuel/air), h (J/kg)
  const st = (W, Tt, Pt, far) => ({ W, Tt, Pt, far, h: G.h(Tt, far) });
  const airOf = s => s.W / (1 + s.far);
  function staticAt(s, M) { // static state at Mach M (subsonic or supersonic branch from guess)
    let T = s.Tt / (1 + 0.2 * M * M), V = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const a = Math.sqrt(G.gamma(T, s.far) * G.R(s.far) * T); V = M * a; const Tn = G.TfromH(s.h - V * V / 2, s.far, T); if (Math.abs(Tn - T) < 1e-6) { T = Tn; break; } T = Tn; }
    const R = G.R(s.far), P = s.Pt * Math.exp((G.phi(T, s.far) - G.phi(s.Tt, s.far)) / R), rho = P / (R * T);
    return { T, P, V, rho, area: s.W / (rho * V) };
  }
  function compress(s, PR, eff, poly) {
    const R = G.R(s.far); let T = s.Tt, h = s.h;
    const N = poly ? 24 : 1, r = Math.pow(PR, 1 / N);
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) { const Ts = G.TfromPhi(G.phi(T, s.far) + R * Math.log(r), s.far, T); const hs = G.h(Ts, s.far); h = h + (hs - h) / eff; T = G.TfromH(h, s.far, Ts); }
    const o = st(s.W, T, s.Pt * PR, s.far); return { out: o, dh: o.h - s.h };
  }
  function expandPR(s, PR, eff, poly) { // returns exit state for a given turbine expansion ratio
    const R = G.R(s.far); let T = s.Tt, h = s.h; const N = poly ? 24 : 1, r = Math.pow(PR, 1 / N);
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) { const Ts = G.TfromPhi(G.phi(T, s.far) - R * Math.log(r), s.far, T); const hs = G.h(Ts, s.far); h = h - eff * (h - hs); T = G.TfromH(h, s.far, Ts); }
    return st(s.W, T, s.Pt / PR, s.far);
  }
  function turbine(s, dh, eff, poly) { // find PR that extracts dh (J/kg)
    if (dh <= 0) return { out: Object.assign({}, s), PR: 1 };
    let lnPR = Math.max(0.05, dh / (eff * G.R(s.far) * s.Tt));
    for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
      const o = expandPR(s, Math.exp(lnPR), eff, poly), err = (s.h - o.h) - dh;
      if (Math.abs(err) < 1e-4 * Math.max(1, dh) * 1e-2) break;
      const d = eff * G.R(s.far) * o.Tt; lnPR -= err / d; if (lnPR < 1e-6) lnPR = 1e-6; if (lnPR > 6) lnPR = 6;
    }
    const PR = Math.exp(lnPR); return { out: expandPR(s, PR, eff, poly), PR };
  }
  function burn(s, Tout, dPqP, eta, hF) { // returns exit state and fuel (kg/s)
    const Wa = airOf(s), fi = s.far, Ti = s.Tt, HF = hF === undefined ? H_FUEL : hF;
    const fo = (hC(A, Tout) - hC(A, Ti) + fi * (HF - hC(D, Ti))) / (HF - hC(D, Tout));
    const o = st(Wa * (1 + fo), Tout, s.Pt * (1 - dPqP), fo);
    return { out: o, fuel: Wa * (fo - fi) / eta, far: fo };
  }
  function mix(a, b, Pt) { // adiabatic mixing of two streams
    const Wa = airOf(a) + airOf(b), Wf = a.W - airOf(a) + b.W - airOf(b), W = a.W + b.W, far = Wf / Wa;
    const h = (a.W * a.h + b.W * b.h) / W, T = G.TfromH(h, far, (a.Tt + b.Tt) / 2);
    return st(W, T, Pt, far);
  }
  const duct = (s, dPqP) => st(s.W, s.Tt, s.Pt * (1 - dPqP), s.far);
  function nozzle(s, Pamb, Cv, type, gas) {
    const G = gas || GAS0;
    const R = G.R(s.far), f = s.far, ph0 = G.phi(s.Tt, f);
    const out = { W: s.W, Tt: s.Tt, Pt: s.Pt, NPR: s.Pt / Pamb };
    if (s.Pt <= Pamb * 1.0001) { return Object.assign(out, { Fg: 0, V: 0, Vid: 0, A8: NaN, A9: NaN, choked: false, Ps: Pamb, Ts: s.Tt, bad: true }); }
    const Te = G.TfromPhi(ph0 - R * Math.log(s.Pt / Pamb), f, s.Tt * 0.8), Vid = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 2 * (s.h - G.h(Te, f))));
    let lo = s.Tt * 0.5, hi = s.Tt; // sonic throat
    for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const T = (lo + hi) / 2, v2 = 2 * (s.h - G.h(T, f)), a2 = G.gamma(T, f) * R * T; if (v2 > a2) lo = T; else hi = T; }
    const Tst = (lo + hi) / 2, Vst = Math.sqrt(G.gamma(Tst, f) * R * Tst), Pst = s.Pt * Math.exp((G.phi(Tst, f) - ph0) / R), Ast = s.W / (Pst / (R * Tst) * Vst);
    const Ae = s.W / (Pamb / (R * Te) * Vid);
    if (Pst > Pamb && type !== "cd") {
      return Object.assign(out, { Fg: Cv * s.W * Vst + (Pst - Pamb) * Ast, V: Cv * Vst, Vid, A8: Ast, A9: Ast, choked: true, Ps: Pst, Ts: Tst, Me: 1, Te: Tst });
    }
    const choked = Pst > Pamb;
    return Object.assign(out, { Fg: Cv * s.W * Vid, V: Cv * Vid, Vid, A8: choked ? Ast : Ae, A9: Ae, choked, Ps: Pamb, Ts: Te, Me: Vid / Math.sqrt(G.gamma(Te, f) * R * Te), Te });
  }
  const ramRecovery = (M, sub) => (M <= 1 ? sub : sub * (1 - 0.075 * Math.pow(M - 1, 1.35)));
  const _spk = {};
  function spikeRec(M, sub) { if (M <= 1.05) return sub; const k = Math.round(M * 50); if (!_spk[k]) { const m = k / 50, t = optimizeRamps(m, 2); _spk[k] = inletShocks(m, t, 1).shockRec; } return sub * _spk[k]; }
  const pinOf = (p, M) => (p.trj ? spikeRec(M, p.inletRec) : ramRecovery(M, p.inletRec));
  const trjB = (p, M) => (p.trj ? p.bypMax * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (M - p.bypOpen) / (p.bypFull - p.bypOpen))) : 0);
  const autoCooling = T4 => Math.min(0.28, Math.max(0, 0.00036 * (T4 - 1250)));

  /* ---------- core cycle, per kg/s of total inlet air ---------- */
  function coreCycle(p, FPR) {
    const amb = isa(p.alt, p.dISA || 0), f0 = 0;
    const T0 = amb.T, P0 = amb.P, a0 = Math.sqrt(G.gamma(T0, f0) * G.R(f0) * T0), V0 = p.mach * a0;
    const Tt0 = G.TfromH(G.h(T0, f0) + V0 * V0 / 2, f0, T0), Pt0 = P0 * Math.exp((G.phi(Tt0, f0) - G.phi(T0, f0)) / G.R(f0));
    const poly = p.effBasis !== "isentropic", S = {};
    const W0 = 1, isFan = p.config !== "turbojet", BPR = isFan ? p.BPR : 0;
    const pin = pinOf(p, p.mach);
    S["0"] = st(W0, Tt0, Pt0, 0); S["2"] = duct(S["0"], 1 - pin);
    const Wc = W0 / (1 + BPR), Wb = W0 - Wc;
    let wFan = 0, s21 = S["2"], s13 = null;
    if (isFan) { const c = compress(S["2"], FPR, p.effFan, poly); wFan = c.dh; s21 = st(Wc, c.out.Tt, c.out.Pt, 0); s13 = st(Wb, c.out.Tt, c.out.Pt, 0); S["21"] = s21; S["13"] = s13; }
    else s21 = st(Wc, S["2"].Tt, S["2"].Pt, 0);
    const LPCPR = isFan ? p.LPCPR : 1;
    const cL = compress(s21, LPCPR, p.effLPC, poly); S["25"] = cL.out;
    const HPCPR = p.OPR / ((isFan ? FPR : 1) * LPCPR);
    const cH = compress(S["25"], HPCPR, p.effHPC, poly); S["3"] = cH.out;
    const eps = p.coolAuto ? autoCooling(p.T4) : p.cool, bl = p.bleed || 0;
    const Wcool = eps * Wc, Wbleed = bl * Wc, Wtrj = trjB(p, p.mach) * Wc, s3b = st(Wc - Wcool - Wbleed - Wtrj, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0);
    const b = burn(s3b, p.T4, p.dPburner, p.effBurner, p.hFuel); S["4"] = b.out;
    const ngv = st(Wcool * 0.6, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0), rot = st(Wcool * 0.4, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0);
    S["41"] = Wcool > 0 ? mix(S["4"], ngv, S["4"].Pt) : S["4"];
    const PwHPC = Wc * cH.dh, PwHPT = PwHPC / p.effMech;
    const tH = turbine(S["41"], PwHPT / S["41"].W, p.effHPT, poly); S["44"] = tH.out;
    S["45"] = Wcool > 0 ? mix(S["44"], rot, S["44"].Pt) : S["44"];
    const PwFan = W0 * wFan, PwLPC = Wc * cL.dh, gear = p.geared ? p.effGear : 1;
    const PwLPT = (PwFan + PwLPC) / (p.effMech * gear);
    const tL = turbine(S["45"], PwLPT / S["45"].W, p.effLPT, poly); S["5"] = tL.out;
    return { amb, T0, P0, V0, a0, S, Wc, Wb, BPR, FPR: isFan ? FPR : 1, LPCPR, HPCPR, eps, fuel: b.fuel, far4: b.far, pin, Wbleed, Wcool, Wtrj,
      pw: { fan: PwFan, lpc: PwLPC, hpc: PwHPC, hpt: PwHPT, lpt: PwLPT }, PRt: { hpt: tH.PR, lpt: tL.PR } };
  }

  function solve(p) {
    if (isShaft(p)) return solveShaft(p);
    if (p.config === "ramjet" || p.config === "scramjet" || p.config === "pulsejet") return solveRam(p);
    const warn = [];
    let c, FPR = p.FPR;
    if (p.config === "mixed") { // FPR set so bypass and core total pressures match at the mixer
      const resid = F => { const r = coreCycle(p, F); return r.S["13"].Pt * (1 - p.dPbypass) - r.S["5"].Pt * (1 - p.dPcore) * p.mixPtRatio; };
      let lo = 1.02, hi = Math.min(8, p.OPR / (p.LPCPR * 1.3));
      const rl = resid(lo), rh = resid(hi);
      if (rl > 0) { FPR = lo; warn.push({ lvl: "bad", t: "No fan pressure ratio balances the mixer: the core exhaust pressure is too low. Raise T4 or OPR, or lower the bypass ratio." }); }
      else if (rh < 0) { FPR = hi; warn.push({ lvl: "bad", t: "Fan pressure ratio hit its limit before the mixer balanced. Lower the bypass ratio or raise OPR." }); }
      else { for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2; if (resid(m) > 0) hi = m; else lo = m; } FPR = (lo + hi) / 2; }
    }
    c = coreCycle(p, FPR);
    const S = c.S, P0 = c.P0, V0 = c.V0;
    let fuelAB = 0, nozCore, nozByp = null, ab = false, mixIn = null, A8dry = null;
    if (p.config === "separate") {
      S["16"] = duct(S["13"], p.dPbypass); S["8"] = duct(S["5"], p.dPcore);
      nozCore = nozzle(S["8"], P0, p.CvCore, p.nozzleCore); nozByp = nozzle(S["16"], P0, p.CvByp, p.nozzleByp);
    } else {
      let s6 = duct(S["5"], p.dPcore);
      if (p.config === "mixed") { S["16"] = duct(S["13"], p.dPbypass); mixIn = { c: s6, b: S["16"] }; s6 = mix(s6, S["16"], (s6.Pt * s6.W + S["16"].Pt * S["16"].W) / (s6.W + S["16"].W) * (1 - p.dPmixer)); }
      if (c.Wtrj > 0) { S["trj"] = st(c.Wtrj, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0); s6 = mix(s6, S["trj"], s6.Pt); }
      S["6"] = s6;
      if (p.ab) { ab = true; let T7 = p.T7; const fmax = FAR_STOICH * 0.95;
        let bb = burn(s6, T7, p.dPab, p.effAB, p.hFuel);
        if (bb.far > fmax) { let lo = s6.Tt, hi = T7; for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2; if (burn(s6, m, p.dPab, p.effAB, p.hFuel).far > fmax) hi = m; else lo = m; } T7 = lo; bb = burn(s6, T7, p.dPab, p.effAB, p.hFuel);
          warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Afterburner limited to " + Math.round(T7) + " K: the stream has run out of oxygen (95% of stoichiometric)." }); }
        S["7"] = bb.out; fuelAB = bb.fuel;
      } else S["7"] = duct(s6, p.dPabDry);
      S["8"] = S["7"]; nozCore = nozzle(S["7"], P0, p.CvCore, p.nozzleCore);
      if (ab) A8dry = nozzle(duct(S["6"], p.dPabDry), P0, p.CvCore, p.nozzleCore).A8;
    }
    const nozBad = nozCore.bad || (nozByp && nozByp.bad);
    const Fg = nozCore.Fg + (nozByp ? nozByp.Fg : 0), Fram = 1 * V0, Fs = Fg - Fram;
    const fuel = c.fuel + fuelAB;
    const ke = 0.5 * nozCore.W * Math.pow(nozCore.Fg / nozCore.W, 2) + (nozByp ? 0.5 * nozByp.W * Math.pow(nozByp.Fg / nozByp.W, 2) : 0) - 0.5 * (1 - c.Wbleed) * V0 * V0;
    const etaTh = ke / (fuel * LHV), etaP = V0 > 1 ? Fs * V0 / ke : NaN, etaO = V0 > 1 ? Fs * V0 / (fuel * LHV) : NaN;
    // sizing
    let W = p.sizeBy === "W" ? p.W : (Fs > 0 ? p.Fn / Fs : NaN);
    if (!(Fs > 0)) warn.push({ lvl: "bad", t: "Net thrust is zero or negative at this design point: the jets leave slower than the aircraft flies." });
    const r = { p, ok: Fs > 0 && !nozBad, W, Fs, Fn: Fs * W, Fg: Fg * W, Fram: Fram * W, fuel: fuel * W, fuelAB: fuelAB * W, TSFC: fuel / Fs, etaTh, etaP, etaO,
      V0, T0: c.T0, P0, a0: c.a0, BPR: c.BPR, FPR: c.FPR, LPCPR: c.LPCPR, HPCPR: c.HPCPR, OPR: p.OPR, eps: c.eps, far4: c.far4, pin: c.pin, ab,
      FARab: ab ? S["7"].far : null, Wc: c.Wc * W, Wb: c.Wb * W,
      pw: {}, PRt: c.PRt, S: {}, nozCore: scaleNoz(nozCore, W), nozByp: nozByp ? scaleNoz(nozByp, W) : null, warn, LHV, FAR_STOICH };
    Object.keys(c.pw).forEach(k => (r.pw[k] = c.pw[k] * W));
    Object.keys(S).forEach(k => { const s = S[k]; r.S[k] = { W: s.W * W, Tt: s.Tt, Pt: s.Pt, far: s.far, s: G.phi(s.Tt, s.far) - G.R(s.far) * Math.log(s.Pt / 101325) }; });
    r.Fcore = nozCore.Fg * W; r.Fbyp = nozByp ? nozByp.Fg * W : 0;
    if (r.ok) { r.geo = geometry(r, c); checks(r); try { r.ref = makeRef(r, c, { nozCore, nozByp, mixIn, A8dry }); } catch (e) { r.ref = null; } }
    return r;
  }
  function scaleNoz(n, W) { return Object.assign({}, n, { W: n.W * W, Fg: n.Fg * W, A8: n.A8 * W, A9: n.A9 * W }); }

  function checks(r) {
    const p = r.p, S = r.S, w = r.warn;
    if (r.HPCPR < 1.15) w.push({ lvl: "bad", t: "The high-pressure compressor has almost nothing to do (pressure ratio " + r.HPCPR.toFixed(2) + "). Raise OPR or lower the fan and booster pressure ratios." });
    if (S["3"].Tt > 980) w.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Compressor exit temperature T3 = " + Math.round(S["3"].Tt) + " K is above ~980 K, the usual limit for the last compressor disk." });
    if (S["4"] && p.T4 > 2000) w.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "T4 above 2,000 K is beyond today's production engines and needs advanced cooling or ceramic matrix composites." });
    if (r.eps > 0.22) w.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Turbine cooling takes " + Math.round(r.eps * 100) + "% of core air. That much cooling eats into the benefit of the higher T4." });
    if (S["4"] && r.far4 > r.FAR_STOICH * 0.6) w.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Combustor fuel-air ratio is high (" + r.far4.toFixed(3) + "); it is approaching rich operation." });
    if (r.nozCore.bad || (r.nozByp && r.nozByp.bad)) w.push({ lvl: "bad", t: "A nozzle has less pressure than the outside air, so that stream cannot exit." });
    const g = r.geo;
    if (g && g.fan && g.fan.Utip > 520) w.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Fan tip speed " + Math.round(g.fan.Utip) + " m/s is very high: expect shock noise and losses." });
    if (p.config === "separate" && r.nozByp && r.nozCore) { const vr = r.nozByp.V / r.nozCore.V; if (vr < 0.5) w.push({ lvl: "info", t: "Bypass-to-core jet velocity ratio is " + vr.toFixed(2) + ". Fuel burn is usually lowest around 0.65–0.8, so try a higher fan pressure ratio (or use Optimize FPR)." }); else if (vr > 0.95) w.push({ lvl: "info", t: "The bypass jet is faster than the core jet (ratio " + vr.toFixed(2) + "). Try a lower fan pressure ratio or a higher BPR." }); }
    if (g && g.lpt && g.lpt.stages > 7 && !g.shaft) w.push({ lvl: "info", t: "The low-pressure turbine needs " + g.lpt.stages + " stages because the slow fan limits its blade speed. A fan gearbox would cut that." });
  }

  /* ---------- geometry and turbomachinery sizing ---------- */
  function annulus(s, M) { return staticAt({ W: s.W, Tt: s.Tt, Pt: s.Pt, far: s.far, h: G.h(s.Tt, s.far) }, M).area; }
  function fromHubTip(A, ht) { const rt = Math.sqrt(A / (Math.PI * (1 - ht * ht))); return { rh: rt * ht, rt }; }
  function fromMean(A, rm) { const h = A / (2 * Math.PI * rm); return { rh: rm - h / 2, rt: rm + h / 2 }; }
  function geometry(r, c) {
    const p = r.p, S = r.S, cfg = p.config, gp = p.geo;
    const g = { cfg, parts: [], x: {} };
    const fan = cfg !== "turbojet";
    const A2 = annulus(S["2"], gp.M2), ht2 = fan ? (cfg === "mixed" ? 0.35 : gp.htFan) : 0.45;
    const f2 = fromHubTip(A2, ht2);
    g.D = 2 * f2.rt; g.rt2 = f2.rt; g.rh2 = f2.rh;
    // speeds
    const dhFan = fan ? (r.pw.fan / r.W) : 0;
    let Nlp = 0, Nfan = 0, nFan = 0, Utip = 0;
    if (fan) { nFan = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dhFan / (gp.psiFan * gp.UfanMax * gp.UfanMax))); Utip = Math.min(gp.UfanMax, Math.sqrt(dhFan / (gp.psiFan * nFan))); Nfan = Utip / f2.rt; Nlp = Nfan * (p.geared ? p.gearRatio : 1); g.fan = { stages: nFan, Utip, rpm: Nfan * 60 / (2 * Math.PI), rt: f2.rt, rh: f2.rh }; }
    // core annuli
    const s21 = S["21"] || S["2"], s25 = S["25"], s3 = S["3"], s41 = S["41"] || S["4"], s45 = S["45"] || S["44"], s5 = S["5"];
    let c21, c25;
    if (fan) { const A21 = annulus(s21, 0.45); c21 = { rh: f2.rh * 1.03, rt: Math.sqrt(Math.pow(f2.rh * 1.03, 2) + A21 / Math.PI) }; }
    const A25 = annulus(s25, 0.5); c25 = fan ? fromHubTip(A25, 0.5) : f2;
    const hasLPC = fan && r.LPCPR > 1.0001;
    const rm25 = (c25.rh + c25.rt) / 2, c3 = fromMean(annulus(s3, 0.3), rm25);
    const Uhpc = fan ? gp.UhpcTip : Math.min(gp.UhpcTip, 420), Nhp = Uhpc / c25.rt;
    const dhHPC = r.pw.hpc / r.Wc, nHPC = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dhHPC / (gp.psiHPC * Uhpc * Uhpc)));
    let nLPC = 0, cL = null;
    if (hasLPC) { const rmL = (c21.rh + c21.rt) / 2, aOut = fromMean(annulus(s25, 0.45), rmL); cL = { in: c21, out: aOut }; const UL = Nlp * c21.rt; nLPC = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((r.pw.lpc / r.Wc) / (gp.psiLPC * UL * UL))); }
    const rmT = (c3.rh + c3.rt) / 2 * 1.04;
    const t41 = fromMean(annulus(s41, 0.25), rmT), t44 = fromMean(annulus(S["44"], 0.42), rmT * 1.02);
    const UhptM = Nhp * rmT, nHPT = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((r.pw.hpt / S["41"].W) / (gp.psiHPT * UhptM * UhptM)));
    let nLPT = 0, t5 = null;
    if (r.pw.lpt > 0) { const A5 = annulus(s5, 0.45), t5f = fromHubTip(A5, 0.6), rmMin = (t5f.rh + t5f.rt) / 2, rmWant = fan && cfg === "separate" ? (p.geared ? gp.kLPTgeared : gp.kLPT) * f2.rt : rmMin; t5 = fromMean(A5, Math.max(rmMin, rmWant)); const rmL = 0.3 * (t44.rh + t44.rt) / 2 + 0.7 * (t5.rh + t5.rt) / 2, U = Nlp * rmL; nLPT = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((r.pw.lpt / s45.W) / (gp.psiLPT * U * U))); g.lpt = { stages: nLPT, Umean: U, rpm: Nlp * 60 / (2 * Math.PI) }; }
    g.hpc = { stages: nHPC, Utip: Uhpc, rpm: Nhp * 60 / (2 * Math.PI) }; g.hpt = { stages: nHPT, Umean: UhptM };
    g.lpc = hasLPC ? { stages: nLPC, rpm: Nlp * 60 / (2 * Math.PI) } : null;
    // ---- axial layout ----
    const parts = g.parts, L = (x0, x1) => x1 - x0;
    let x = 0;
    const hF = f2.rt - f2.rh;
    if (fan) {
      parts.push({ k: "cone", x0: -1.5 * f2.rh, x1: 0.02 * hF, r: f2.rh, role: "spinner", name: "Spinner" });
      const stL = 0.42 * hF / Math.max(1, nFan * 0.6);
      for (let i = 0; i < nFan; i++) { parts.push({ k: "row", comp: "fan", spool: "LP", x0: x, x1: x + stL * 0.55, rh0: f2.rh, rt0: f2.rt, rh1: f2.rh * 1.02, rt1: f2.rt, n: cfg === "mixed" ? 26 : 22, chord: stL * 0.55, name: nFan > 1 ? "Fan (" + nFan + " stages)" : "Fan" }); x += stL; }
      g.x.fanEnd = x;
    } else {
      parts.push({ k: "cone", x0: -1.6 * f2.rh, x1: 0, r: f2.rh, role: "spinner", name: "Nose cone" });
    }
    // core path
    const rowsBetween = (comp, spool, n, x0, a, b, name, rowL) => { for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const t0 = i / n, t1 = (i + 1) / n, rh0 = a.rh + (b.rh - a.rh) * t0, rt0 = a.rt + (b.rt - a.rt) * t0, rh1 = a.rh + (b.rh - a.rh) * t1, rt1 = a.rt + (b.rt - a.rt) * t1, hh = (rt0 - rh0 + rt1 - rh1) / 2, len = Math.max(0.025, rowL * hh); parts.push({ k: "row", comp, spool, x0, x1: x0 + len * 0.5, rh0, rt0, rh1, rt1, n: Math.max(16, Math.min(40, Math.round(2 * Math.PI * (rh0 + rt0) / 2 / (hh * 0.5)))), chord: len * 0.5, name }); x0 += len; } return x0; };
    let xs = x;
    if (fan) { xs = x + 0.18 * hF; g.x.split = xs; g.rSplit = c21.rt * 1.02; }
    let xc = xs;
    if (hasLPC) { xc = rowsBetween("lpc", "LP", nLPC, xs + 0.02, cL.in, cL.out, "Booster / LP compressor (" + nLPC + " stages)", 1.1); g.x.lpcEnd = xc; g.lpcIn = cL.in; g.lpcOut = cL.out; xc += 0.6 * Math.max(0, (cL.out.rh + cL.out.rt) / 2 - rm25) + 0.03; }
    else if (fan) { xc += 0.4 * Math.max(0, (c21.rh + c21.rt) / 2 - rm25) + 0.03; }
    g.x.hpcStart = xc;
    xc = rowsBetween("hpc", "HP", nHPC, xc, c25, c3, (fan ? "High-pressure compressor" : "Compressor") + " (" + nHPC + " stages)", 1.0);
    g.x.hpcEnd = xc;
    const h3 = c3.rt - c3.rh, Lb = Math.max(0.1, 5.5 * h3 + 0.04 * g.D), rmB = (c3.rh + c3.rt) / 2 * 1.02;
    g.burner = { x0: xc + 0.25 * Lb, x1: xc + Lb, ri: rmB - 1.4 * h3, ro: rmB + 1.4 * h3 };
    parts.push({ k: "burner", x0: g.burner.x0, x1: g.burner.x1, ri: g.burner.ri, ro: g.burner.ro, name: "Combustor" });
    xc += Lb + 0.02;
    xc = rowsBetween("hpt", "HP", nHPT, xc, t41, t44, (fan ? "High-pressure turbine" : "Turbine") + " (" + nHPT + (nHPT > 1 ? " stages)" : " stage)"), 1.4);
    g.x.hptEnd = xc;
    let tEnd = t44;
    if (nLPT) { xc += 0.05 * g.D * 0.3 + 0.02; xc = rowsBetween("lpt", "LP", nLPT, xc, t44, t5, "Low-pressure turbine (" + nLPT + (nLPT > 1 ? " stages)" : " stage)"), 1.2); tEnd = t5; }
    g.x.turbEnd = xc; g.tEnd = tEnd;
    parts.push({ k: "shaft", x0: fan ? -0.02 : 0, x1: xc, r: Math.max(0.02, f2.rh * 0.22), spool: "LP", name: fan ? "Low-pressure shaft" : "Shaft" });
    if (fan) parts.push({ k: "shaft", x0: g.x.hpcStart, x1: g.x.hptEnd, r: Math.max(0.03, f2.rh * 0.38), spool: "HP", name: "High-pressure shaft (concentric)" });
    if (p.geared && fan) parts.push({ k: "gear", x0: g.x.fanEnd + 0.02 * hF, x1: g.x.fanEnd + 0.16 * hF, r: f2.rh * 0.95, name: "Planetary fan gearbox (" + p.gearRatio.toFixed(2) + ":1)" });
    // core casing profile (tip radii along the core)
    const casing = [];
    parts.filter(q => q.k === "row" && q.comp !== "fan").forEach(q => { casing.push([q.x0, q.rt0 * 1.02]); casing.push([q.x1 + (q.x1 - q.x0), q.rt1 * 1.02]); });
    casing.push([g.burner.x0, g.burner.ro * 1.05]); casing.push([g.burner.x1, g.burner.ro * 1.05]);
    casing.sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]);
    g.casing = casing;
    // exhaust
    const nz = r.nozCore;
    if (cfg === "separate") {
      const rPlug0 = tEnd.rh, Ln = 1.6 * tEnd.rt, xThroat = xc + 0.55 * Ln, rPlugT = rPlug0 * 0.55, r8 = Math.sqrt(nz.A8 / Math.PI + rPlugT * rPlugT);
      g.coreNoz = { x0: xc, xT: xThroat, rPlug0, rPlugT, r8, rIn: tEnd.rt * 1.02, xPlugEnd: xc + Ln };
      parts.push({ k: "plug", x0: xc, x1: xc + Ln, r: rPlug0, name: "Core exhaust plug" });
      const ccR = c => Math.max(...casing.filter(q => q[0] <= c + 0.05).map(q => q[1]).concat([0.0])) ;
      const cowlPts = [[xs, g.rSplit], [hasLPC ? g.x.lpcEnd : xs + 0.1, (hasLPC ? cL.out.rt : c21.rt) * 1.12 + 0.02 * g.D], [g.burner.x1, g.burner.ro * 1.08 + 0.015 * g.D], [xc, tEnd.rt * 1.1 + 0.012 * g.D], [xThroat, r8 * 1.04]];
      g.cowl = cowlPts;
      const x18 = Math.min(g.burner.x0, Math.max(g.x.hpcStart, xs + 0.55 * g.D * 0.5));
      const rcc = interp(cowlPts, x18), r18 = Math.sqrt(r.nozByp.A8 / Math.PI + rcc * rcc);
      g.bypNoz = { x18, r18, rcc };
      const xl = -gp.inletLD * g.D;
      g.nacelle = { inner: [[xl, f2.rt * 0.96], [-0.12 * g.D, f2.rt * 1.0], [g.x.fanEnd + 0.25 * hF, f2.rt * 1.0], [x18, r18]], outer: [[xl, f2.rt * 1.03], [xl + 0.12 * g.D, f2.rt * 1.12], [0.15 * g.D, f2.rt * 1.15], [x18, r18 * 1.04]] };
      parts.push({ k: "vanes", x0: g.x.fanEnd + 0.12 * hF, x1: g.x.fanEnd + 0.22 * hF, ri: g.rSplit * 1.05, ro: f2.rt, n: 40, name: "Outlet guide vanes (straighten the bypass flow)" });
      g.len = xc + Ln - xl; g.xMin = xl; g.xMax = xc + Ln;
    } else {
      // turbojet / mixed: long casing, optional mixer and afterburner, convergent or con-di nozzle
      const s7 = S["7"], Mtp = r.ab ? 0.22 : 0.4, Atp = annulus(s7, Mtp), rtp = Math.max(tEnd.rt * 1.05, Math.sqrt(Atp / Math.PI));
      let x6 = xc + 0.05;
      if (cfg === "mixed") { parts.push({ k: "mixer", x0: xc, x1: xc + 0.35 * tEnd.rt, r: (tEnd.rt + f2.rt) / 2 * 0.95, name: "Mixer (blends bypass and core streams)" }); x6 = xc + 0.4 * tEnd.rt; }
      const Ltp = r.ab ? 2.6 * rtp : 0.8 * rtp, x7 = x6 + Ltp;
      if (r.ab) { parts.push({ k: "ab", x0: x6 + 0.25 * rtp, x1: x7, r: rtp, name: "Afterburner (reheat)" }); }
      parts.push({ k: "plugTJ", x0: xc, x1: xc + Math.max(0.6 * tEnd.rt, 0.9 * tEnd.rh * 2), r: tEnd.rh, name: "Tail cone" });
      const r8 = Math.sqrt(nz.A8 / Math.PI), r9 = Math.sqrt(nz.A9 / Math.PI), Lc = 0.8 * (rtp - r8) + 0.15 * rtp, Ld = Math.max(0.05, 1.4 * (r9 - r8));
      g.nozzle = { x7, rtp, xT: x7 + Lc, r8, xE: x7 + Lc + (p.nozzleCore === "cd" ? Ld : 0), r9: p.nozzleCore === "cd" ? r9 : r8 };
      const rOuter = cfg === "mixed" ? f2.rt * 1.04 : Math.max(f2.rt, ...casing.map(q => q[1])) * 1.06;
      g.outerR = rOuter; g.tailR = rtp; g.x6 = x6;
      const xl = -0.45 * g.D;
      g.shellPts = [[xl, f2.rt * 0.98], [0, f2.rt * 1.01], [cfg === "mixed" ? xc : g.x.hpcStart, rOuter], [x6, Math.max(rOuter * 0.98, rtp * 1.05)], [x7, rtp * 1.05], [g.nozzle.xT, r8 * 1.06], [g.nozzle.xE, g.nozzle.r9 * 1.04]];
      if (cfg === "mixed") { g.rSplit = c21 ? c21.rt * 1.02 : f2.rh; }
      g.len = g.nozzle.xE - xl; g.xMin = xl; g.xMax = g.nozzle.xE;
    }
    // representative internal velocities (m/s) for flow animation
    g.vel = { inlet: staticAt(Object.assign({}, S["2"], { h: G.h(S["2"].Tt, 0) }), gp.M2).V, comp: 150, burner: 40, turb: 260 };
    return g;
  }
  function interp(pts, x) { if (x <= pts[0][0]) return pts[0][1]; for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) if (x <= pts[i][0]) { const a = pts[i - 1], b = pts[i]; return a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * (x - a[0]) / (b[0] - a[0]); } return pts[pts.length - 1][1]; }


  /* ================= Off-design: component maps and matching ================= */
  const MAPS = (typeof module !== "undefined" && typeof require !== "undefined") ? require("./maps.json") : root.ENGINE_MAPS;
  function lin2(xs, ys, Z, x, y) {
    let i = 0; while (i < xs.length - 2 && x > xs[i + 1]) i++;
    let j = 0; while (j < ys.length - 2 && y > ys[j + 1]) j++;
    const tx = (x - xs[i]) / (xs[i + 1] - xs[i]), ty = (y - ys[j]) / (ys[j + 1] - ys[j]);
    const a = Z[i][j], b = Z[i][j + 1], c = Z[i + 1][j], d = Z[i + 1][j + 1];
    return a * (1 - tx) * (1 - ty) + b * (1 - tx) * ty + c * tx * (1 - ty) + d * tx * ty;
  }
  const cmap = (m, Nc, R) => ({ Wc: lin2(m.Nc, m.R, m.Wc, Nc, R), PR: lin2(m.Nc, m.R, m.PR, Nc, R), eff: lin2(m.Nc, m.R, m.eff, Nc, R) });
  const tmap = (m, Np, PR) => ({ Wp: lin2(m.Np, m.PRm, m.Wp, Np, PR), eff: lin2(m.Np, m.PRm, m.eff, Np, PR) });
  const TSTD = 288.15, PSTD = 101325;
  function compRef(name, sin, sout, W, PR) {
    const m = MAPS[name], R = G.R(sin.far);
    const Ts = G.TfromPhi(G.phi(sin.Tt, sin.far) + R * Math.log(PR), sin.far, sout.Tt), eff = (G.h(Ts, sin.far) - sin.h) / (sout.h - sin.h);
    const th = sin.Tt / TSTD, Wc = sin.W * W * Math.sqrt(th) / (sin.Pt / PSTD), Nc = 1 / Math.sqrt(th), d = cmap(m, m.Ncd, m.Rd);
    return { map: name, sNc: Nc / m.Ncd, sPR: (PR - 1) / (d.PR - 1), sWc: Wc / d.Wc, seff: eff / d.eff, Wc, PR, eff, Rd: m.Rd };
  }
  function turbRef(name, sin, sout, W, PR) {
    const m = MAPS[name], R = G.R(sin.far);
    const Ts = G.TfromPhi(G.phi(sin.Tt, sin.far) - R * Math.log(PR), sin.far, sout.Tt), eff = (sin.h - sout.h) / (sin.h - G.h(Ts, sin.far));
    const Np = 1 / Math.sqrt(sin.Tt), Wp = sin.W * W * Math.sqrt(sin.Tt) / sin.Pt, d = tmap(m, m.Npd, m.PRd);
    return { map: name, sNp: Np / m.Npd, sPR: (PR - 1) / (m.PRd - 1), sWp: Wp / d.Wp, seff: eff / d.eff, Wp, PR, eff };
  }
  const gam = (T, f) => G.gamma(T, f);
  const mfp = (M, g) => M * Math.sqrt(g) * Math.pow(1 + (g - 1) / 2 * M * M, -(g + 1) / (2 * (g - 1)));
  function mixerArea(s, M) { const g = gam(s.Tt, s.far), R = G.R(s.far); return { A: s.W * Math.sqrt(R * s.Tt) / (s.Pt * mfp(M, g)), Ps: s.Pt * Math.pow(1 + (g - 1) / 2 * M * M, -g / (g - 1)) }; }
  function psFromArea(s, A) { // subsonic static pressure for a stream through area A
    const g = gam(s.Tt, s.far), R = G.R(s.far), target = s.W * Math.sqrt(R * s.Tt) / (s.Pt * A);
    let lo = 1e-4, hi = 1, pen = 0; if (target >= mfp(1, g)) { lo = hi = 1; pen = target / mfp(1, g) - 1; }
    else for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2; if (mfp(m, g) < target) lo = m; else hi = m; }
    const M = (lo + hi) / 2; return s.Pt * Math.pow(1 + (g - 1) / 2 * M * M, -g / (g - 1)) * (1 - pen);
  }
  function makeRef(r, c, x) {
    const p = r.p, W = r.W, S = c.S, fan = p.config !== "turbojet", hasLPC = fan && c.LPCPR > 1.0001;
    const ref = { p: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p)), cfg: p.config, W, BPR: c.BPR, hasLPC, eps: c.eps, Fn: r.Fn, TSFC: r.TSFC, T3: r.S["3"].Tt, rpm: {} };
    if (fan) { ref.fan = compRef("fan", S["2"], S["13"], W, c.FPR); if (hasLPC) ref.lpc = compRef("lpc", S["21"], S["25"], W, c.LPCPR); }
    ref.hpc = compRef(fan ? "hpc" : "axi5", S["25"], S["3"], W, c.HPCPR);
    ref.hpt = turbRef(fan ? "hpt" : "lpt2269", S["41"], S["44"], W, c.PRt.hpt);
    if (fan) ref.lpt = turbRef("lpt", S["45"], S["5"], W, c.PRt.lpt);
    ref.PwHPC = c.pw.hpc * W; ref.PwLPT = c.pw.lpt * W;
    ref.A8c = (x.A8dry || x.nozCore.A8) * W; ref.A8b = x.nozByp ? x.nozByp.A8 * W : null;
    if (x.mixIn) { const a = mixerArea(x.mixIn.c, 0.4), g = gam(x.mixIn.b.Tt, 0), Mb = Math.sqrt(2 / (g - 1) * (Math.pow(x.mixIn.b.Pt / a.Ps, (g - 1) / g) - 1)); ref.Ac = a.A * W; ref.Ab = mixerArea(x.mixIn.b, Mb).A * W; }
    if (r.geo) { ref.rpm = { hp: r.geo.hpc.rpm, lp: r.geo.lpt ? r.geo.lpt.rpm : null, fan: r.geo.fan ? r.geo.fan.rpm : null }; }
    return ref;
  }
  function compOD(cr, s, n, R, Wdes) {
    const m = MAPS[cr.map], th = s.Tt / TSTD, NcMap = n / Math.sqrt(th) / cr.sNc, q = cmap(m, NcMap, R);
    const PR = cr.sPR * (q.PR - 1) + 1, eff = Math.min(0.99, Math.max(0.3, cr.seff * q.eff)), Wc = cr.sWc * q.Wc;
    const c = compress(s, Math.max(1.0005, PR), eff, false), Wca = s.W * Math.sqrt(th) / (s.Pt / PSTD);
    const st0 = cmap(m, NcMap, m.Rstall), PRs = cr.sPR * (st0.PR - 1) + 1, Wcs = cr.sWc * st0.Wc;
    return { out: c.out, dh: c.dh, PR, eff, Wc: Wca, res: (Wca - Wc) / cr.Wc, NcMap, R, SM: ((Wca / Wcs) / (PR / PRs) - 1) * 100, Nrel: NcMap / m.Ncd };
  }
  function turbOD(tr, s, n, PR) {
    const m = MAPS[tr.map], Np = n / Math.sqrt(s.Tt), NpMap = Np / tr.sNp, PRmap = (PR - 1) / tr.sPR + 1, q = tmap(m, NpMap, PRmap);
    const eff = Math.min(0.99, Math.max(0.3, tr.seff * q.eff)), Wp = tr.sWp * q.Wp, out = expandPR(s, PR, eff, false), Wpa = s.W * Math.sqrt(s.Tt) / s.Pt;
    return { out, PR, eff, res: (Wpa - Wp) / tr.Wp, NpMap, PRmap };
  }
  function odVars(ref) {
    if (ref.kind === "shaft") return ["lnW", "nH", "Rh", "lnPh", "lnPpt"];
    const v = ["lnW"]; if (ref.cfg !== "turbojet") v.push("BPR", "nL"); v.push("nH");
    if (ref.cfg !== "turbojet") v.push("Rf"); if (ref.hasLPC) v.push("Rl"); v.push("Rh", "lnPh"); if (ref.cfg !== "turbojet") v.push("lnPl");
    return v;
  }
  function odGuess(ref) {
    const g = { lnW: Math.log(ref.W), BPR: ref.BPR, nL: 1, nH: 1, Rf: ref.fan ? ref.fan.Rd : 2, Rl: ref.lpc ? ref.lpc.Rd : 2, Rh: ref.hpc.Rd, lnPh: Math.log(ref.hpt.PR), lnPl: ref.lpt ? Math.log(ref.lpt.PR) : 0, lnPpt: ref.pt ? Math.log(ref.pt.PR) : 0 };
    return g;
  }
  function odForward(ref, cond, x, full) {
    if (ref.kind === "shaft") return odForwardShaft(ref, cond, x, full);
    const p = ref.p, fan = ref.cfg !== "turbojet", res = [];
    const amb = isa(cond.alt, cond.dISA || 0), T0 = amb.T, P0 = amb.P, a0 = Math.sqrt(G.gamma(T0, 0) * G.R(0) * T0), V0 = cond.mach * a0;
    const Tt0 = G.TfromH(G.h(T0, 0) + V0 * V0 / 2, 0, T0), Pt0 = P0 * Math.exp((G.phi(Tt0, 0) - G.phi(T0, 0)) / G.R(0));
    const W = Math.exp(x.lnW), pin = pinOf(p, cond.mach), S = {};
    S["0"] = st(W, Tt0, Pt0, 0); S["2"] = st(W, Tt0, Pt0 * pin, 0);
    const BPR = fan ? x.BPR : 0, Wc = W / (1 + BPR);
    let F = null, s21 = st(Wc, S["2"].Tt, S["2"].Pt, 0);
    if (fan) { F = compOD(ref.fan, S["2"], x.nL, x.Rf); res.push(F.res); s21 = st(Wc, F.out.Tt, F.out.Pt, 0); S["13"] = st(W - Wc, F.out.Tt, F.out.Pt, 0); S["21"] = s21; }
    let Lc = null, s25 = s21;
    if (ref.hasLPC) { Lc = compOD(ref.lpc, s21, x.nL, x.Rl); res.push(Lc.res); s25 = Lc.out; }
    S["25"] = s25;
    const H = compOD(ref.hpc, s25, x.nH, x.Rh); res.push(H.res); S["3"] = H.out;
    const Wcool = ref.eps * Wc, Wbleed = (p.bleed || 0) * Wc, Wtrj = trjB(p, cond.mach) * Wc;
    const bu = burn(st(Wc - Wcool - Wbleed - Wtrj, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0), cond.T4, p.dPburner, p.effBurner, p.hFuel); S["4"] = bu.out;
    S["41"] = Wcool > 0 ? mix(S["4"], st(Wcool * 0.6, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0), S["4"].Pt) : S["4"];
    const HT = turbOD(ref.hpt, S["41"], x.nH, Math.exp(x.lnPh)); res.push(HT.res); S["44"] = HT.out;
    S["45"] = Wcool > 0 ? mix(S["44"], st(Wcool * 0.4, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0), S["44"].Pt) : S["44"];
    const pwHPC = Wc * H.dh, pwHPT = S["41"].W * (S["41"].h - S["44"].h);
    res.push((pwHPT * p.effMech - pwHPC) / ref.PwHPC);
    let LT = null; S["5"] = S["45"];
    let pwFan = 0, pwLPC = 0;
    if (fan) {
      LT = turbOD(ref.lpt, S["45"], x.nL, Math.exp(x.lnPl)); res.push(LT.res); S["5"] = LT.out;
      pwFan = W * F.dh; pwLPC = Lc ? Wc * Lc.dh : 0;
      const gear = p.geared ? p.effGear : 1;
      res.push((S["45"].W * (S["45"].h - S["5"].h) * p.effMech * gear - (pwFan + pwLPC)) / ref.PwLPT);
    }
    let nozC, nozB = null, fuelAB = 0, ab = false;
    if (ref.cfg === "separate") {
      S["16"] = duct(S["13"], p.dPbypass); S["8"] = duct(S["5"], p.dPcore);
      nozC = nozzle(S["8"], P0, p.CvCore, p.nozzleCore); nozB = nozzle(S["16"], P0, p.CvByp, p.nozzleByp);
      res.push(((nozC.bad ? 0 : nozC.A8) - ref.A8c) / ref.A8c); res.push(((nozB.bad ? 0 : nozB.A8) - ref.A8b) / ref.A8b);
    } else {
      let s6 = duct(S["5"], p.dPcore);
      if (ref.cfg === "mixed") {
        S["16"] = duct(S["13"], p.dPbypass);
        const pc = psFromArea(s6, ref.Ac), pb = psFromArea(S["16"], ref.Ab); res.push((pb - pc) / pc);
        s6 = mix(s6, S["16"], (s6.Pt * s6.W + S["16"].Pt * S["16"].W) / (s6.W + S["16"].W) * (1 - p.dPmixer));
      }
      if (Wtrj > 0) s6 = mix(s6, st(Wtrj, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0), s6.Pt);
      S["6"] = s6;
      const dry = duct(s6, p.dPabDry), nd = nozzle(dry, P0, p.CvCore, p.nozzleCore);
      res.push(((nd.bad ? 0 : nd.A8) - ref.A8c) / ref.A8c);
      nozC = nd; S["7"] = dry;
      if (full && cond.ab && p.ab) { let T7 = cond.T7 || p.T7; const fmax = FAR_STOICH * 0.95; let bb = burn(s6, T7, p.dPab, p.effAB, p.hFuel);
        if (bb.far > fmax) { let lo = s6.Tt, hi = T7; for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2; if (burn(s6, m, p.dPab, p.effAB, p.hFuel).far > fmax) hi = m; else lo = m; } bb = burn(s6, lo, p.dPab, p.effAB, p.hFuel); }
        S["7"] = bb.out; fuelAB = bb.fuel; ab = true; nozC = nozzle(bb.out, P0, p.CvCore, p.nozzleCore); }
    }
    if (!full) return { res };
    const Fg = nozC.Fg + (nozB ? nozB.Fg : 0), Fram = W * V0, Fn = Fg - Fram, fuel = bu.fuel + fuelAB;
    const ke = 0.5 * nozC.W * Math.pow(nozC.Fg / nozC.W, 2) + (nozB ? 0.5 * nozB.W * Math.pow(nozB.Fg / nozB.W, 2) : 0) - 0.5 * (W - Wbleed) * V0 * V0;
    const o = { res, W, BPR, Fn, Fg, Fram, fuel, fuelAB, TSFC: fuel / Fn, V0, ab, etaTh: ke / (fuel * LHV), etaP: V0 > 1 ? Fn * V0 / ke : NaN, etaO: V0 > 1 ? Fn * V0 / (fuel * LHV) : NaN,
      FPR: F ? F.PR : 1, LPCPR: Lc ? Lc.PR : 1, HPCPR: H.PR, OPR: S["3"].Pt / S["2"].Pt, nL: fan ? x.nL : null, nH: x.nH, T3: S["3"].Tt, T4: cond.T4, T45: S["45"].Tt, T5: S["5"].Tt,
      far4: bu.far, trjB: Wtrj / Wc, pin, nozCore: nozC, nozByp: nozB, comp: { fan: F, lpc: Lc, hpc: H }, turb: { hpt: HT, lpt: LT }, S: {}, amb, P0 };
    Object.keys(S).forEach(k => { const s = S[k]; o.S[k] = { W: s.W, Tt: s.Tt, Pt: s.Pt, far: s.far }; });
    return o;
  }
  function linsolve(A, b) { const n = b.length, M = A.map((r, i) => r.concat([b[i]]));
    for (let c = 0; c < n; c++) { let piv = c; for (let r = c + 1; r < n; r++) if (Math.abs(M[r][c]) > Math.abs(M[piv][c])) piv = r; [M[c], M[piv]] = [M[piv], M[c]]; const d = M[c][c]; if (Math.abs(d) < 1e-14) return null;
      for (let r = 0; r < n; r++) { if (r === c) continue; const f = M[r][c] / d; if (f) for (let k = c; k <= n; k++) M[r][k] -= f * M[c][k]; } }
    return M.map((r, i) => r[n] / r[i]); }
  const BOUNDS = { lnW: [-20, 20], BPR: [0.02, 40], nL: [0.15, 1.5], nH: [0.3, 1.4], Rf: [0.3, 4], Rl: [0.3, 4], Rh: [0.3, 4], lnPh: [0.003, 3], lnPl: [0.003, 3.5], lnPpt: [0.003, 3] };
  function newtonOD(ref, cond, x0, maxIt = 40) {
    const vars = odVars(ref), x = Object.assign({}, x0), vec = () => vars.map(k => x[k]);
    const F = xv => { const xx = Object.assign({}, x); vars.forEach((k, i) => (xx[k] = xv[i])); try { const r = odForward(ref, cond, xx, false).res; return r.every(isFinite) ? r : null; } catch (e) { return null; } };
    let xv = vec(), f = F(xv); if (!f) return null; let nrm = Math.max(...f.map(Math.abs));
    for (let it = 0; it < maxIt && nrm > 2e-8; it++) {
      const J = f.map(() => []);
      for (let j = 0; j < xv.length; j++) { const h = 1e-6 * Math.max(1, Math.abs(xv[j])), xp = xv.slice(); xp[j] += h; const fp = F(xp); if (!fp) return null; for (let i = 0; i < f.length; i++) J[i][j] = (fp[i] - f[i]) / h; }
      const dx = linsolve(J, f.map(v => -v)); if (!dx) return null;
      let lam = 1, ok = false;
      for (let ls = 0; ls < 12; ls++) { const xn = xv.map((v, j) => { const b = BOUNDS[vars[j]]; return Math.min(b[1], Math.max(b[0], v + lam * dx[j])); }); const fn = F(xn);
        if (fn) { const nn = Math.max(...fn.map(Math.abs)); if (nn < nrm * (1 - 1e-4 * lam) || nn < 1e-9) { xv = xn; f = fn; nrm = nn; ok = true; break; } } lam *= 0.5; }
      if (!ok) break;
    }
    if (nrm > 1e-6) return null;
    vars.forEach((k, i) => (x[k] = xv[i])); return x;
  }
  function solveOD(ref, cond, guess) {
    let x = guess ? newtonOD(ref, cond, guess) : null;
    if (!x) { // continuation from the design point
      const d = { alt: ref.p.alt, mach: ref.p.mach, dISA: ref.p.dISA || 0, T4: ref.p.T4 };
      for (const steps of [4, 10, 24]) { let xg = odGuess(ref), okAll = true;
        for (let k = 1; k <= steps; k++) { const t = k / steps, c = { alt: d.alt + (cond.alt - d.alt) * t, mach: d.mach + (cond.mach - d.mach) * t, dISA: d.dISA + ((cond.dISA || 0) - d.dISA) * t, T4: d.T4 + (cond.T4 - d.T4) * t };
          const xn = newtonOD(ref, c, xg); if (!xn) { okAll = false; break; } xg = xn; }
        if (okAll) { x = xg; break; } }
    }
    if (!x) return { ok: false };
    const o = odForward(ref, cond, x, true); o.ok = (ref.kind === "shaft" ? o.Pshaft > 0 : o.Fn > 0) && !o.nozCore.bad && !(o.nozByp && o.nozByp.bad); o.x = x; return o;
  }
  function limitRatio(o, lim) { const r = { T4: o.T4 / lim.T4max, NH: o.nH / lim.nHmax, T3: o.T3 / lim.T3max }; if (o.nL !== null && o.nL !== undefined) r.NL = o.nL / lim.nLmax; if (lim.PWmax && o.Pshaft) r.PW = o.Pshaft / lim.PWmax; return r; }
  function solveMax(ref, cond, lim, guess) { // highest T4 that respects every limit (or the edge of the solvable map)
    const run = (T, g) => { const o = solveOD(ref, Object.assign({}, cond, { T4: T }), g); if (o.ok) o.f = Math.max(...Object.values(limitRatio(o, lim))) - 1; return o; };
    const top = run(lim.T4max, guess); let best = null, edge = false;
    if (top.ok && top.f <= 0) best = top;
    else {
      let hiT = lim.T4max, hiF = top.ok ? top.f : null, lo = null, T = lim.T4max, g = top.ok ? top.x : guess, step = top.ok ? Math.max(20, top.f * 700) : lim.T4max * 0.06;
      for (let k = 0; k < 20 && !lo; k++) { T = T - step; if (T < 450) break; const t = run(T, g);
        if (t.ok && t.f <= 0) lo = t; else { hiT = T; hiF = t.ok ? t.f : null; if (t.ok) { g = t.x; step = Math.max(15, t.f * 700); } else step = T * 0.06; } }
      if (!lo) return { ok: false };
      edge = hiF === null;
      for (let it = 0; it < 30; it++) {
        if (lo.f > -2e-3) break;
        let m = (hiF !== null) ? lo.T4 + (hiT - lo.T4) * (-lo.f) / (hiF - lo.f) : (lo.T4 + hiT) / 2;
        m = Math.min(hiT - 0.1 * (hiT - lo.T4) * 0.5, Math.max(lo.T4 + 0.05 * (hiT - lo.T4), m));
        const t = run(m, lo.x);
        if (t.ok && t.f <= 0) lo = t; else { hiT = m; hiF = t.ok ? t.f : null; }
        if (hiT - lo.T4 < 0.05) break;
      }
      best = lo;
    }
    const rr = limitRatio(best, lim); best.limitRatios = rr;
    best.limiting = edge && Math.max(...Object.values(rr)) < 0.995 ? "map" : Object.keys(rr).reduce((m, k) => (rr[k] > rr[m] ? k : m), Object.keys(rr)[0]);
    return best;
  }
  function speedLines(cr) { // scaled map for plotting: lines of constant corrected speed
    const m = MAPS[cr.map], lines = m.Nc.map((Nc, i) => ({ rel: Nc / m.Ncd, pts: m.R.map((R, j) => ({ Wc: cr.sWc * m.Wc[i][j], PR: cr.sPR * (m.PR[i][j] - 1) + 1, eff: cr.seff * m.eff[i][j] })) }));
    const stall = m.Nc.map((Nc, i) => { const q = cmap(m, Nc, m.Rstall); return { Wc: cr.sWc * q.Wc, PR: cr.sPR * (q.PR - 1) + 1 }; });
    return { lines, stall, design: { Wc: cr.Wc, PR: cr.PR } };
  }


  /* ================= Shaft-power gas turbines: turboshaft and turboprop (free power turbine) ================= */
  const PropLib = () => (typeof module !== "undefined" && typeof require !== "undefined") ? require("./prop.js") : root.Prop;
  const isShaft = p => p.config === "turboshaft" || p.config === "turboprop";
  function coreShaft(p) {
    const amb = isa(p.alt, p.dISA || 0), T0 = amb.T, P0 = amb.P, a0 = Math.sqrt(G.gamma(T0, 0) * G.R(0) * T0), V0 = p.mach * a0;
    const Tt0 = G.TfromH(G.h(T0, 0) + V0 * V0 / 2, 0, T0), Pt0 = P0 * Math.exp((G.phi(Tt0, 0) - G.phi(T0, 0)) / G.R(0));
    const poly = p.effBasis !== "isentropic", S = {}, pin = ramRecovery(p.mach, p.inletRec);
    S["0"] = st(1, Tt0, Pt0, 0); S["2"] = duct(S["0"], 1 - pin); S["25"] = S["2"];
    const cH = compress(S["2"], p.OPR, p.effHPC, poly); S["3"] = cH.out;
    const eps = p.coolAuto ? autoCooling(p.T4) : p.cool, Wcool = eps, Wbleed = p.bleed || 0;
    const b = burn(st(1 - Wcool - Wbleed, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0), p.T4, p.dPburner, p.effBurner, p.hFuel); S["4"] = b.out;
    S["41"] = Wcool > 0 ? mix(S["4"], st(Wcool * 0.6, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0), S["4"].Pt) : S["4"];
    const PwHPC = cH.dh, tH = turbine(S["41"], PwHPC / p.effMech / S["41"].W, p.effHPT, poly); S["44"] = tH.out;
    S["45"] = Wcool > 0 ? mix(S["44"], st(Wcool * 0.4, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0), S["44"].Pt) : S["44"];
    const Pt5 = p.NPR * P0 / (1 - p.dPcore), PRpt = S["45"].Pt / Pt5;
    if (!(PRpt > 1.0005)) return { bad: true, amb, T0, P0, V0, a0, S, PRpt };
    S["5"] = expandPR(S["45"], PRpt, p.effLPT, poly);
    const PwPT = S["45"].W * (S["45"].h - S["5"].h), Pshaft = PwPT * p.effMech * (p.config === "turboprop" ? p.effGear : 1);
    return { amb, T0, P0, V0, a0, S, Wc: 1, Wb: 0, BPR: 0, FPR: 1, LPCPR: 1, HPCPR: p.OPR, eps, fuel: b.fuel, far4: b.far, pin, Wbleed, Wcool,
      pw: { fan: 0, lpc: 0, hpc: PwHPC, hpt: PwHPC / p.effMech, lpt: 0, pt: PwPT }, PRt: { hpt: tH.PR, lpt: 1, pt: PRpt }, Pshaft };
  }
  function propAt(p, P, V0, T0, P0, geom) { const L = PropLib(); if (!L) return null; const rho = P0 / (G.R(0) * T0), a = Math.sqrt(G.gamma(T0, 0) * G.R(0) * T0); const g = geom || L.fromParams(p, V0); const o = L.solvePower(g, V0, rho, a, p.propRPM, P); o.geom = g; o.rho = rho; return o; }
  function solveShaft(p) {
    const warn = [], c = coreShaft(p);
    if (c.bad) return { p, ok: false, warn: [{ lvl: "bad", t: "The power turbine has nothing left to extract: the exhaust pressure ratio target is higher than the gas generator can deliver. Lower the exhaust pressure ratio or raise OPR or T4." }], S: {}, kind: "shaft" };
    const S = c.S, P0 = c.P0, V0 = c.V0;
    S["8"] = duct(S["5"], p.dPcore); const noz = nozzle(S["8"], P0, p.CvCore, "conv");
    const W = p.sizeBy === "W" ? p.W : p.Pshaft / c.Pshaft, Psh = c.Pshaft * W, fuel = c.fuel * W, Fjet = (noz.Fg - V0) * W;
    let prop = null, Fn = Fjet;
    if (p.config === "turboprop") { prop = propAt(p, Psh, V0, c.T0, P0); if (prop) Fn = prop.T + Fjet; if (prop && prop.short) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "The propeller can't absorb all the shaft power at any blade pitch. Use a larger diameter, more blades or a higher activity factor." }); if (prop && prop.Mtip > 0.88) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Propeller helical tip Mach " + prop.Mtip.toFixed(2) + " is high: expect compressibility losses and noise. Lower the prop rpm or diameter." }); }
    const keJet = 0.5 * noz.W * W * Math.pow(noz.Fg / noz.W, 2) - 0.5 * (W - c.Wbleed * W) * V0 * V0;
    const r = { p, kind: "shaft", ok: true, W, Pshaft: Psh, PSFC: fuel / Psh, Psp: c.Pshaft, Fn, Fjet, Fs: Fn / W, fuel, fuelAB: 0, TSFC: Fn > 1 ? fuel / Fn : NaN,
      etaTh: (Psh / (p.config === "turboprop" ? p.effGear : 1) + keJet) / (fuel * LHV), etaP: prop && V0 > 1 ? prop.eta : NaN, etaO: V0 > 1 && Fn > 0 ? Fn * V0 / (fuel * LHV) : NaN,
      V0, T0: c.T0, P0, a0: c.a0, BPR: 0, FPR: 1, LPCPR: 1, HPCPR: p.OPR, OPR: p.OPR, eps: c.eps, far4: c.far4, pin: c.pin, ab: false, Wc: W, Wb: 0, pw: {}, PRt: c.PRt, S: {},
      nozCore: scaleNoz(noz, W), nozByp: null, warn, LHV, FAR_STOICH, prop, Fram: V0 * W, Fg: noz.Fg * W, Fcore: noz.Fg * W, Fbyp: 0 };
    Object.keys(c.pw).forEach(k => (r.pw[k] = c.pw[k] * W));
    Object.keys(S).forEach(k => { const s = S[k]; r.S[k] = { W: s.W * W, Tt: s.Tt, Pt: s.Pt, far: s.far, s: G.phi(s.Tt, s.far) - G.R(s.far) * Math.log(s.Pt / 101325) }; });
    r.geo = geometryShaft(r, c); checks(r);
    try { r.ref = makeRefShaft(r, c, noz); } catch (e) { r.ref = null; }
    return r;
  }
  function geometryShaft(r, c) {
    const p = r.p, S = r.S, gp = p.geo, g = { cfg: p.config, parts: [], x: {}, shaft: true };
    const c2 = fromHubTip(annulus(S["2"], 0.5), 0.45); g.D = 2 * c2.rt; g.rt2 = c2.rt; g.rh2 = c2.rh;
    const rm2 = (c2.rh + c2.rt) / 2, c3 = fromMean(annulus(S["3"], 0.3), rm2);
    const U = Math.min(gp.UhpcTip, 470), Nhp = U / c2.rt, nHPC = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((r.pw.hpc / r.W) / (gp.psiHPC * U * U)));
    const rmT = (c3.rh + c3.rt) / 2 * 1.05, t41 = fromMean(annulus(S["41"], 0.25), rmT), t44 = fromMean(annulus(S["44"], 0.42), rmT * 1.02);
    const Uh = Nhp * rmT, nHPT = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((r.pw.hpt / S["41"].W) / (gp.psiHPT * Uh * Uh)));
    const A5 = annulus(S["5"], 0.4), t5 = fromHubTip(A5, 0.55), rmP = (t5.rh + t5.rt) / 2, Upt = gp.Upt, Npt = Upt / rmP;
    const nPT = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((r.pw.pt / S["45"].W) / (1.7 * Upt * Upt))); /* 1.7 rather than the turbofan loading: real small power turbines run slower than Upt at mean radius (T700 and PW127 have two stages) */
    g.hpc = { stages: nHPC, Utip: U, rpm: Nhp * 60 / (2 * Math.PI) }; g.hpt = { stages: nHPT, Umean: Uh }; g.pt = { stages: nPT, rpm: Npt * 60 / (2 * Math.PI), Umean: Upt };
    g.lpt = { stages: nPT, rpm: g.pt.rpm }; g.lpc = null; g.fan = null;
    if (p.config === "turboprop") g.gearRatio = g.pt.rpm / p.propRPM;
    const parts = g.parts; let x = 0;
    const rowsBetween = (comp, spool, n, x0, a, b, name, rowL) => { for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const t0 = i / n, t1 = (i + 1) / n, rh0 = a.rh + (b.rh - a.rh) * t0, rt0 = a.rt + (b.rt - a.rt) * t0, rh1 = a.rh + (b.rh - a.rh) * t1, rt1 = a.rt + (b.rt - a.rt) * t1, hh = (rt0 - rh0 + rt1 - rh1) / 2, len = Math.max(0.018, rowL * hh); parts.push({ k: "row", comp, spool, x0, x1: x0 + len * 0.5, rh0, rt0, rh1, rt1, n: Math.max(14, Math.min(40, Math.round(2 * Math.PI * (rh0 + rt0) / 2 / (hh * 0.5)))), chord: len * 0.5, name }); x0 += len; } return x0; };
    g.x.hpcStart = 0; x = rowsBetween("hpc", "HP", nHPC, 0, c2, c3, "Compressor (" + nHPC + " stages)", 1.0); g.x.hpcEnd = x;
    const h3 = c3.rt - c3.rh, Lb = Math.max(0.13, 5.5 * h3 + 0.05 * g.D), rmB = Math.max((c3.rh + c3.rt) / 2 * 1.02, 0.075), hB = Math.max(1.4 * h3, 0.03); /* small engines keep a minimum liner height and length: residence time does not scale down */
