    g.burner = { x0: x + 0.25 * Lb, x1: x + Lb, ri: rmB - hB, ro: rmB + hB }; parts.push({ k: "burner", x0: g.burner.x0, x1: g.burner.x1, ri: g.burner.ri, ro: g.burner.ro, name: "Combustor" });
    x += Lb + 0.02; x = rowsBetween("hpt", "HP", nHPT, x, t41, t44, "Gas-generator turbine (" + nHPT + (nHPT > 1 ? " stages)" : " stage)"), 1.4); g.x.hptEnd = x;
    x += 0.03; x = rowsBetween("pt", "LP", nPT, x, t44, t5, "Free power turbine (" + nPT + (nPT > 1 ? " stages)" : " stage)"), 1.2); g.x.turbEnd = x; g.tEnd = t5;
    parts.push({ k: "shaft", x0: g.x.hpcStart, x1: g.x.hptEnd, r: Math.max(0.012, c2.rh * 0.35), spool: "HP", name: "Gas-generator shaft" });
    const casing = []; parts.filter(q => q.k === "row").forEach(q => { casing.push([q.x0, q.rt0 * 1.03]); casing.push([q.x1 + (q.x1 - q.x0), q.rt1 * 1.03]); });
    casing.push([g.burner.x0, g.burner.ro * 1.05]); casing.push([g.burner.x1, g.burner.ro * 1.05]); casing.sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]); g.casing = casing;
    const A8 = r.nozCore.A8, rE = Math.sqrt(A8 / Math.PI + Math.pow(t5.rh * 0.8, 2)), Lx = 1.4 * t5.rt + 0.05;
    parts.push({ k: "plugTJ", x0: x, x1: x + Lx * 0.8, r: t5.rh, name: "Exhaust center body" });
    g.nozzle = { x7: x, rtp: t5.rt * 1.05, xT: x + Lx * 0.5, r8: rE, xE: x + Lx, r9: rE }; g.x6 = x;
    const rOuter = Math.max(c2.rt * 1.08, ...casing.map(q => q[1])) * 1.05;
    g.outerR = rOuter; g.tailR = t5.rt;
    if (p.config === "turboprop") {
      const Lg = 0.55 * g.D + 0.06, xg0 = -0.12 * g.D - Lg, xp = xg0 - 0.08;
      parts.push({ k: "gearbox", x0: xg0, x1: xg0 + Lg, r: Math.max(c2.rt * 1.05, 0.12), name: "Reduction gearbox (" + g.gearRatio.toFixed(1) + ":1)" });
      parts.push({ k: "outshaft", x0: xg0 + Lg, x1: x, r: Math.max(0.01, c2.rh * 0.18), name: "Power turbine shaft (runs forward to the gearbox)" });
      const pg = r.prop ? r.prop.geom : null;
      parts.push({ k: "prop", x: xp, D: p.propD, B: p.propB, chord: pg ? pg.cMax * p.propD : 0.08 * p.propD, pitch: r.prop ? r.prop.theta75 : 0.6, name: "Propeller (" + p.propB + " blades, " + p.propD.toFixed(2) + " m)" });
      parts.push({ k: "cone", x0: xp - 0.35 * p.propD * 0.25, x1: xp + 0.02, r: Math.max(0.08, 0.11 * p.propD), role: "spinner", name: "Spinner" });
      g.prop = { x: xp, D: p.propD }; g.xMin = xp - 0.1 * p.propD;
      g.shellPts = [[xg0, Math.max(c2.rt * 1.05, 0.12) * 1.15], [-0.05 * g.D, rOuter], [g.burner.x1, rOuter], [x, Math.max(t5.rt * 1.1, rOuter * 0.95)], [g.nozzle.xE, rE * 1.05]];
    } else {
      const xo = -0.9 * g.D - 0.15;
      parts.push({ k: "outshaft", x0: xo, x1: x, r: Math.max(0.012, c2.rh * 0.22), name: "Output shaft (" + Math.round(g.pt.rpm).toLocaleString() + " rpm, to the rotor gearbox)" });
      parts.push({ k: "flange", x0: xo - 0.02, x1: xo + 0.01, r: Math.max(0.05, c2.rh * 0.9), name: "Output flange" });
      g.xMin = xo - 0.05;
      g.shellPts = [[-0.25 * g.D, c2.rt * 1.35], [0, c2.rt * 1.04], [g.burner.x1, rOuter], [x, Math.max(t5.rt * 1.1, rOuter * 0.95)], [g.nozzle.xE, rE * 1.05]];
    }
    g.xMax = g.nozzle.xE; g.len = g.xMax - g.xMin; g.vel = { inlet: staticAt(Object.assign({}, S["2"], { h: G.h(S["2"].Tt, 0) }), 0.5).V, comp: 150, burner: 40, turb: 260 };
    return g;
  }
  function makeRefShaft(r, c, noz) {
    const p = r.p, W = r.W, S = c.S;
    const ref = { kind: "shaft", p: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p)), cfg: p.config, W, BPR: 0, hasLPC: false, eps: c.eps, Fn: r.Fn, TSFC: r.TSFC, T3: r.S["3"].Tt, Pshaft: r.Pshaft, PSFC: r.PSFC };
    ref.hpc = compRef("axi5", S["2"], S["3"], W, c.HPCPR);
    ref.hpt = turbRef("lpt2269", S["41"], S["44"], W, c.PRt.hpt);
    ref.pt = turbRef("lpt2269", S["45"], S["5"], W, c.PRt.pt);
    ref.PwHPC = c.pw.hpc * W; ref.A8c = noz.A8 * W; ref.propGeom = r.prop ? r.prop.geom : null; ref.rpm = { hp: r.geo.hpc.rpm, pt: r.geo.pt.rpm };
    return ref;
  }
  function odForwardShaft(ref, cond, x, full) {
    const p = ref.p, res = [];
    const amb = isa(cond.alt, cond.dISA || 0), T0 = amb.T, P0 = amb.P, a0 = Math.sqrt(G.gamma(T0, 0) * G.R(0) * T0), V0 = cond.mach * a0;
    const Tt0 = G.TfromH(G.h(T0, 0) + V0 * V0 / 2, 0, T0), Pt0 = P0 * Math.exp((G.phi(Tt0, 0) - G.phi(T0, 0)) / G.R(0));
    const W = Math.exp(x.lnW), pin = ramRecovery(cond.mach, p.inletRec), S = {};
    S["0"] = st(W, Tt0, Pt0, 0); S["2"] = st(W, Tt0, Pt0 * pin, 0); S["25"] = S["2"];
    const H = compOD(ref.hpc, S["2"], x.nH, x.Rh); res.push(H.res); S["3"] = H.out;
    const Wcool = ref.eps * W, Wbleed = (p.bleed || 0) * W;
    const bu = burn(st(W - Wcool - Wbleed, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0), cond.T4, p.dPburner, p.effBurner, p.hFuel); S["4"] = bu.out;
    S["41"] = Wcool > 0 ? mix(S["4"], st(Wcool * 0.6, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0), S["4"].Pt) : S["4"];
    const HT = turbOD(ref.hpt, S["41"], x.nH, Math.exp(x.lnPh)); res.push(HT.res); S["44"] = HT.out;
    S["45"] = Wcool > 0 ? mix(S["44"], st(Wcool * 0.4, S["3"].Tt, S["3"].Pt, 0), S["44"].Pt) : S["44"];
    res.push((S["41"].W * (S["41"].h - S["44"].h) * p.effMech - W * H.dh) / ref.PwHPC);
    const PT = turbOD(ref.pt, S["45"], cond.nPT || 1, Math.exp(x.lnPpt)); res.push(PT.res); S["5"] = PT.out;
    S["8"] = duct(S["5"], p.dPcore); const nz = nozzle(S["8"], P0, p.CvCore, "conv");
    res.push(((nz.bad ? 0 : nz.A8) - ref.A8c) / ref.A8c);
    if (!full) return { res };
    const PwPT = S["45"].W * (S["45"].h - S["5"].h), Psh = PwPT * p.effMech * (ref.cfg === "turboprop" ? p.effGear : 1), Fjet = nz.Fg - W * V0;
    let prop = null, Fn = Fjet; if (ref.cfg === "turboprop") { prop = propAt(p, Psh, V0, T0, P0, ref.propGeom); if (prop) Fn += prop.T; }
    const o = { res, kind: "shaft", W, BPR: 0, Pshaft: Psh, PSFC: bu.fuel / Psh, Fn, Fjet, Fram: W * V0, fuel: bu.fuel, fuelAB: 0, TSFC: Fn > 1 ? bu.fuel / Fn : NaN, V0, ab: false, prop,
      etaTh: (PwPT * p.effMech + 0.5 * nz.W * Math.pow(nz.Fg / nz.W, 2) - 0.5 * W * V0 * V0) / (bu.fuel * LHV), etaP: prop && V0 > 1 ? prop.eta : NaN, etaO: V0 > 1 && Fn > 0 ? Fn * V0 / (bu.fuel * LHV) : NaN,
      FPR: 1, LPCPR: 1, HPCPR: H.PR, OPR: S["3"].Pt / S["2"].Pt, nL: null, nH: x.nH, T3: S["3"].Tt, T4: cond.T4, T45: S["45"].Tt, T5: S["5"].Tt,
      far4: bu.far, nozCore: nz, nozByp: null, comp: { fan: null, lpc: null, hpc: H }, turb: { hpt: HT, lpt: PT }, S: {}, amb, P0 };
    Object.keys(S).forEach(k => { const s = S[k]; o.S[k] = { W: s.W, Tt: s.Tt, Pt: s.Pt, far: s.far }; });
    return o;
  }

  function solveODPower(ref, cond, Ptarget, guess) { // throttle to a shaft-power target (secant on T4)
    let a = ref.p.T4 * 0.9, b = ref.p.T4, oa = solveOD(ref, Object.assign({}, cond, { T4: a }), guess), ob = solveOD(ref, Object.assign({}, cond, { T4: b }), oa.ok ? oa.x : guess);
    for (let i = 0; i < 25 && oa.ok && ob.ok; i++) { const fa = oa.Pshaft - Ptarget, fb = ob.Pshaft - Ptarget; if (Math.abs(fb) < 1e-6 * Ptarget) break; const c = b - fb * (b - a) / (fb - fa); a = b; oa = ob; b = c; ob = solveOD(ref, Object.assign({}, cond, { T4: b }), oa.x); }
    return ob;
  }

  /* ================= Ram-air engines: ramjet, scramjet, pulsejet, and the shock-system inlet ================= */
  function mkGas(Dc, HF) {
    const Dx = combo(Dc), g = {
      R: f => (A.R + f * Dx.R) / (1 + f), cp: (T, f) => (cpC(A, T) + f * cpC(Dx, T)) / (1 + f), h: (T, f) => (hC(A, T) + f * hC(Dx, T)) / (1 + f), phi: (T, f) => (sC(A, T) + f * sC(Dx, T)) / (1 + f),
      gamma(T, f) { const cp = g.cp(T, f); return cp / (cp - g.R(f)); },
      TfromH(h, f, T = 800) { for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const dT = (g.h(T, f) - h) / g.cp(T, f); T -= dT; if (T < 120) T = 120; if (Math.abs(dT) < 1e-7) break; } return T; },
      TfromPhi(p, f, T = 800) { for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const dT = (g.phi(T, f) - p) / (g.cp(T, f) / T); T -= dT; if (T < 120) T = 120; if (Math.abs(dT) < 1e-7) break; } return T; },
      hA: T => hC(A, T), hD: T => hC(Dx, T), HF, farSt: YAIR.O2 / -Dc.O2 };
    g.LHV = HF - hC(Dx, 298.15); return g;
  }
  const GJ = mkGas(DEL, H_FUEL), GH2 = mkGas({ H2O: 2 * 18.015 / 4.032 * 1, O2: -31.998 / 4.032 }, 0);
  const fuelGas = f => (f === "h2" ? GH2 : GJ);
  const stG = (g, W, Tt, Pt, far) => ({ W, Tt, Pt, far, h: g.h(Tt, far) });
  function burnG(g, s, Tout, dPqP, eta, HF) { // burn to Tout in gas g; returns fuel per actual fuel injected
    const Wa = s.W / (1 + s.far), fi = s.far, Ti = s.Tt, H = HF === undefined ? g.HF : HF;
    const fo = (g.hA(Tout) - g.hA(Ti) + fi * (H - g.hD(Ti))) / (H - g.hD(Tout));
    return { out: stG(g, Wa * (1 + fo), Tout, s.Pt * (1 - dPqP), fo), fuel: Wa * (fo - fi) / eta, far: fo };
  }
  function burnFar(g, s, farInj, eta, dPqP) { // burn a given injected fuel-air ratio; returns exit Tt
    const Wa = s.W / (1 + s.far), fb = s.far + farInj * eta, Ht = (g.hA(s.Tt) + s.far * g.hD(s.Tt)) + farInj * eta * g.HF;
    let T = s.Tt + 2000 * farInj / 0.03; for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const hm = g.hA(T) + fb * g.hD(T), dT = (hm - Ht) / ((1 + fb) * g.cp(T, fb)); T -= dT; if (T < 200) T = 200; if (Math.abs(dT) < 1e-6) break; }
    return { out: stG(g, Wa * (1 + fb), T, s.Pt * (1 - dPqP), fb), fuel: Wa * farInj, far: fb };
  }
  // ---- calorically perfect shock relations for the inlet (gamma 1.4 air) ----
  const GM = 1.4;
  function normalShock(M, g = GM) { if (M <= 1) return { M2: M, pr: 1, Tr: 1, ptr: 1 };
    const M2 = Math.sqrt((1 + (g - 1) / 2 * M * M) / (g * M * M - (g - 1) / 2)), pr = 1 + 2 * g / (g + 1) * (M * M - 1);
    const Tr = pr * (2 + (g - 1) * M * M) / ((g + 1) * M * M), ptr = Math.pow((g + 1) * M * M / ((g - 1) * M * M + 2), g / (g - 1)) * Math.pow((g + 1) / (2 * g * M * M - (g - 1)), 1 / (g - 1));
    return { M2, pr, Tr, ptr }; }
  const thetaOf = (M, b, g = GM) => Math.atan(2 / Math.tan(b) * (M * M * Math.sin(b) ** 2 - 1) / (M * M * (g + Math.cos(2 * b)) + 2));
  function betaMax(M, g = GM) { let lo = Math.asin(1 / M), hi = Math.PI / 2; for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) { const a = lo + (hi - lo) / 3, b = hi - (hi - lo) / 3; if (thetaOf(M, a, g) < thetaOf(M, b, g)) lo = a; else hi = b; } return (lo + hi) / 2; }
  function oblique(M, th, g = GM) { // weak solution; null if detached
    if (M <= 1) return null; const bm = betaMax(M, g); if (th > thetaOf(M, bm, g)) return null;
    let lo = Math.asin(1 / M) + 1e-9, hi = bm; for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2; if (thetaOf(M, m, g) < th) lo = m; else hi = m; }
    const b = (lo + hi) / 2, Mn = M * Math.sin(b), n = normalShock(Mn, g), M2 = n.M2 / Math.sin(b - th);
    return { beta: b, theta: th, M2, pr: n.pr, Tr: n.Tr, ptr: n.ptr, Mn };
  }
  function inletShocks(M, thetas, diff) { // sequential oblique shocks, then a terminal normal shock
    const steps = []; let m = M, rec = 1, detached = false;
    for (const th of thetas) { if (m <= 1) break; const o = oblique(m, th); if (!o) { detached = true; break; } steps.push({ kind: "oblique", M1: m, M2: o.M2, beta: o.beta, theta: th, ptr: o.ptr, pr: o.pr }); rec *= o.ptr; m = o.M2; }
    if (m > 1) { const n = normalShock(m); steps.push({ kind: "normal", M1: m, M2: n.M2, ptr: n.ptr, pr: n.pr }); rec *= n.ptr; m = n.M2; }
    return { rec: rec * diff, shockRec: rec, steps, Mexit: m, detached };
  }
  function optimizeRamps(M, n) { // ramp angles that maximize shock recovery
    if (n === 0 || M <= 1.05) return [];
    let th = Array(n).fill(Math.min(0.12, 0.6 / n));
    const f = t => { const r = inletShocks(M, t, 1); return r.detached || t.some(x => x <= 0) ? 0 : r.shockRec; };
    let step = 0.05;
    for (let it = 0; it < 200 && step > 1e-5; it++) { let improved = false;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (const s of [step, -step]) { const t = th.slice(); t[i] += s; if (f(t) > f(th) + 1e-12) { th = t; improved = true; } }
      if (!improved) step /= 2; }
    return th;
  }
  function captureRatio(M, th1, Md) { // 2D first-ramp spillage; full capture at design Mach (shock on lip)
    if (!th1 || M <= 1) return M <= 1 ? 1 : 1; const od = oblique(Md, th1), o = oblique(M, th1); if (!od || !o) return 0.85;
    const tb = Math.tan(od.beta), tt = Math.tan(th1), L = 1, H = L * tb, tbm = Math.tan(o.beta);
    const h = (H - L * tt) / (1 - tt / tbm); return Math.min(1, Math.max(0.3, h / H));
  }
  function rayleighPt(M3, tau, g = 1.3) { // total-pressure ratio for heat addition in a constant-area duct
    const F = M => 2 * (g + 1) * M * M * (1 + (g - 1) / 2 * M * M) / Math.pow(1 + g * M * M, 2), Pf = M => (g + 1) / (1 + g * M * M) * Math.pow((2 + (g - 1) * M * M) / (g + 1), g / (g - 1));
    let f4 = F(M3) * tau, choked = false; if (f4 >= 1) { f4 = 1; choked = true; }
    let lo = M3, hi = 1; for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2; if (F(m) < f4) lo = m; else hi = m; }
    const M4 = (lo + hi) / 2; return { ptr: Pf(M4) / Pf(M3), M4, choked, tauMax: 1 / F(M3) };
  }
  function freestream(alt, dISA, mach) { const a = isa(alt, dISA || 0), T0 = a.T, P0 = a.P, a0 = Math.sqrt(G.gamma(T0, 0) * G.R(0) * T0), V0 = mach * a0;
    const Tt0 = G.TfromH(G.h(T0, 0) + V0 * V0 / 2, 0, T0), Pt0 = P0 * Math.exp((G.phi(Tt0, 0) - G.phi(T0, 0)) / G.R(0)); return { T0, P0, a0, V0, Tt0, Pt0, rho0: P0 / (G.R(0) * T0) }; }
  function ramjetCore(p, cond, geom) { // per unit inlet airflow; geom fixes ramp angles (off-design)
    const fs = freestream(cond.alt, cond.dISA, cond.mach), g = fuelGas(p.fuel), warn = [];
    const thetas = geom ? geom.thetas : optimizeRamps(cond.mach, p.nShocks), inl = inletShocks(cond.mach, thetas, p.diffRec);
    const S = {}; S["0"] = stG(g, 1, fs.Tt0, fs.Pt0, 0); S["2"] = stG(g, 1, fs.Tt0, fs.Pt0 * inl.rec, 0);
    const farInj = cond.phi * g.farSt; let b = burnFar(g, S["2"], farInj, p.effBurner, 0), tau = b.out.Tt / S["2"].Tt;
    const ray = rayleighPt(p.M3, tau); let phiEff = cond.phi;
    if (ray.choked) { const T4 = S["2"].Tt * ray.tauMax * 0.995, bb = burnG(g, S["2"], T4, 0, p.effBurner); phiEff = bb.far / g.farSt / p.effBurner; b = burnFar(g, S["2"], phiEff * g.farSt, p.effBurner, 0); warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Thermal choking: the combustor can't take this much heat at its entry Mach number, so the fuel was cut back to equivalence ratio " + phiEff.toFixed(2) + ". Lower the combustor entry Mach number." }); }
    const r2 = rayleighPt(p.M3, b.out.Tt / S["2"].Tt);
    S["4"] = stG(g, b.out.W, b.out.Tt, S["2"].Pt * r2.ptr * (1 - p.dPfh), b.out.far);
    const noz = nozzle(S["4"], fs.P0, p.CvCore, p.nozzleCore, g);
    const Fs = noz.Fg - fs.V0, fuel = farInj * (phiEff / cond.phi);
    const ke = 0.5 * noz.W * Math.pow(noz.Fg / noz.W, 2) - 0.5 * fs.V0 * fs.V0;
    return { fs, g, inl, thetas, S, noz, Fs, fuel, phiEff, ray: r2, warn, T4: S["4"].Tt, etaTh: ke / (fuel * g.LHV), etaP: fs.V0 > 1 && Fs > 0 ? Fs * fs.V0 / ke : NaN, etaO: fs.V0 > 1 ? Fs * fs.V0 / (fuel * g.LHV) : NaN };
  }
  function scramCore(p, cond, geom) {
    const fs = freestream(cond.alt, cond.dISA, cond.mach), g = fuelGas(p.fuel), warn = [], Ga = GJ, R = Ga.R(0);
    const h0 = Ga.h(fs.T0, 0), ht = h0 + fs.V0 * fs.V0 / 2;
    const comp = T3 => { const h3 = Ga.h(T3, 0), V3 = Math.sqrt(Math.max(1, 2 * (ht - h3))), Tx = T3 - p.etaC * (T3 - fs.T0), P3 = fs.P0 * Math.exp((Ga.phi(T3, 0) - Ga.phi(Tx, 0)) / R); return { T3, V3, P3, rho3: P3 / (R * T3) }; };
    let c3;
    if (geom) { // fixed geometry: find T3 that passes the captured flow through the combustor area
      const cap = captureRatio(cond.mach, geom.th1, geom.Md), mdot = fs.rho0 * fs.V0 * geom.A0 * cap;
      let lo = fs.T0 * 1.05, hi = Math.min(ht / Ga.cp(fs.T0, 0) * 0.98, fs.Tt0 * 0.98);
      for (let i = 0; i < 70; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2, q = comp(m); if (q.rho3 * q.V3 * geom.A3 < mdot) lo = m; else hi = m; }
      c3 = comp((lo + hi) / 2); c3.cap = cap;
    } else c3 = comp(p.psi * fs.T0);
    const a3 = Math.sqrt(Ga.gamma(c3.T3, 0) * R * c3.T3), M3 = c3.V3 / a3;
    if (M3 < 1.05) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "The flow reaches the combustor at Mach " + M3.toFixed(2) + ": that's subsonic combustion (ramjet mode). Lower the compression (T3/T0) or fly faster." });
    const f = cond.phi * g.farSt, fb = f * p.effBurner, Vf = p.Vfx * c3.V3;
    const V4 = (c3.V3 + f * Vf - p.CfAw / 2 * c3.V3) / (1 + f);
    const Ht = g.hA(c3.T3) + fb * g.HF + c3.V3 * c3.V3 / 2 + f * Vf * Vf / 2 - (1 + f) * V4 * V4 / 2;
    let T4 = c3.T3 + 1500; for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const hm = g.hA(T4) + fb * g.hD(T4), dT = (hm - Ht) / ((1 + fb) * g.cp(T4, fb)); T4 -= dT; if (T4 < 200) T4 = 200; if (Math.abs(dT) < 1e-6) break; }
    const Rb = g.R(fb), M4 = V4 / Math.sqrt(g.gamma(T4, fb) * Rb * T4), P4 = c3.P3;
    const P10 = fs.P0 * p.P10ratio, T10s = g.TfromPhi(g.phi(T4, fb) - Rb * Math.log(P4 / P10), fb, T4 * 0.6), h4 = g.h(T4, fb), h10 = h4 - p.etaE * (h4 - g.h(T10s, fb));
    const V10 = Math.sqrt(V4 * V4 + 2 * (h4 - h10)), T10 = g.TfromH(h10, fb, T10s);
    const Fs = (1 + f) * V10 - fs.V0 + (1 + f) * Rb * T10 / V10 * (1 - fs.P0 / P10);
    const ke = 0.5 * (1 + f) * V10 * V10 - 0.5 * fs.V0 * fs.V0;
    const Pt3 = c3.P3 * Math.exp((Ga.phi(fs.Tt0, 0) - Ga.phi(c3.T3, 0)) / R), rec = Pt3 / fs.Pt0;
    if (T4 > 2900) warn.push({ lvl: "info", t: "Combustor static temperature " + Math.round(T4) + " K: real combustion products dissociate here, so the frozen-chemistry model is optimistic. The validation section shows by how much." });
    return { fs, g, c3, M3, f, fb, V4, T4, M4, P4, T10, V10, Fs, fuel: f, rec, warn, cap: c3.cap || 1, etaTh: ke / (f * g.LHV), etaP: Fs > 0 ? Fs * fs.V0 / ke : NaN, etaO: Fs * fs.V0 / (f * g.LHV) };
  }
  function pulseCore(p, cond) {
    const fs = freestream(cond.alt, cond.dISA, cond.mach), g = fuelGas("jeta"), R = G.R(0);
    const Tm = 950, am = Math.sqrt(1.32 * 287.05 * Tm), freq = am / (4 * p.tubeL), Lc = 0.28 * p.tubeL, Vc = Math.PI / 4 * p.chamberD * p.chamberD * Lc;
    const P2 = fs.Pt0 * 0.92, T2 = fs.Tt0, rho2 = P2 / (R * T2), kv0 = Math.max(0, 1 - Math.pow(cond.mach / p.pjMv, 4)), W = p.pjFill * (1 + p.pjRam * (fs.Pt0 / fs.P0 - 1) * (0.3 + 0.7 * kv0)) * rho2 * Vc * freq;
    const far = cond.phi * g.farSt * p.effBurner, u = (T, f) => g.h(T, f) - g.R(f) * T, U = G.h(T2, 0) - R * T2 + far * g.HF;
    let T3 = T2 + 1800 * cond.phi; for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const dT = ((1 + far) * u(T3, far) - U) / ((1 + far) * (g.cp(T3, far) - g.R(far))); T3 -= dT; if (Math.abs(dT) < 1e-6) break; }
    const prCV = T3 / T2 * g.R(far) / R, kv = Math.max(0, 1 - Math.pow(cond.mach / p.pjMv, 4)), PG = 1 + (prCV - 1) * kv;
    const P3 = P2 * PG, Te = g.TfromPhi(g.phi(T3, far) - g.R(far) * Math.log(Math.max(1.0001, P3 / fs.P0)), far, T3 * 0.7), Tj = T3;
    const Vj = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 2 * p.pjEff * (g.h(T3, far) - g.h(Te, far))));
    const Fn = W * (1 + far) * Vj - W * fs.V0, fuel = W * cond.phi * g.farSt;
    return { fs, W, freq, far, T3, prCV, PG, kv, Vj, Fn, fuel, Tj, Vc, Lc, etaTh: (0.5 * W * (1 + far) * Vj * Vj - 0.5 * W * fs.V0 * fs.V0) / (fuel * g.LHV) };
  }
  function solveRam(p) {
    const warn = [], g0 = 9.80665, cond = { alt: p.alt, dISA: p.dISA, mach: p.mach, phi: p.phi };
    let r;
    if (p.config === "ramjet") {
      if (p.mach < 1.2) return { p, kind: "ram", ok: false, warn: [{ lvl: "bad", t: "A ramjet design point needs supersonic flight (about Mach 1.5 or more): below that the inlet can't compress the air enough." }], S: {} };
      const c = ramjetCore(p, cond), W = p.Fn / c.Fs, A0 = W / (c.fs.rho0 * c.fs.V0);
      if (!(c.Fs > 0)) return { p, kind: "ram", ok: false, warn: [{ lvl: "bad", t: "No net thrust: the exhaust leaves slower than the aircraft flies. Add fuel (equivalence ratio) or improve inlet recovery." }].concat(c.warn), S: {} };
      r = { p, kind: "ram", ok: true, W, Fn: p.Fn, Fs: c.Fs, fuel: c.fuel * W, TSFC: c.fuel / c.Fs, Isp: c.Fs / (c.fuel * g0), V0: c.fs.V0, T0: c.fs.T0, P0: c.fs.P0, rec: c.inl.rec, inl: c.inl, thetas: c.thetas, T4: c.T4, phi: c.phiEff,
        etaTh: c.etaTh, etaP: c.etaP, etaO: c.etaO, nozCore: scaleNoz(c.noz, W), nozByp: null, warn: warn.concat(c.warn), LHV: c.g.LHV, A0, ray: c.ray, S: {}, FAR_STOICH: c.g.farSt };
      const ScaledS = c.S; Object.keys(ScaledS).forEach(k => { const s = ScaledS[k]; r.S[k] = { W: s.W * W, Tt: s.Tt, Pt: s.Pt, far: s.far, s: c.g.phi(s.Tt, s.far) - c.g.R(s.far) * Math.log(s.Pt / 101325) }; });
      r.geo = geoRamjet(r, c, p); r.ref = { kind: "ram", cfg: "ramjet", p: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p)), W, A0, thetas: c.thetas, Fn: p.Fn, Md: p.mach };
      if (c.inl.rec < 0.6) r.warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Inlet recovery is only " + (c.inl.rec * 100).toFixed(0) + "%. Add oblique shocks (ramps) to slow the air more gently." });
    } else if (p.config === "scramjet") {
      const c = scramCore(p, cond), W = p.Fn / c.Fs, A0 = W / (c.fs.rho0 * c.fs.V0), A3 = W / (c.c3.rho3 * c.c3.V3), rhoE = c.fs.P0 * p.P10ratio / (c.g.R(c.fb) * c.T10), A10 = W * (1 + c.f) / (rhoE * c.V10);
      if (!(c.Fs > 0)) return { p, kind: "ram", ok: false, warn: [{ lvl: "bad", t: "No net thrust at this design point. Raise the equivalence ratio, improve the component efficiencies, or check that the combustor isn't choking." }].concat(c.warn), S: {} };
      r = { p, kind: "ram", ok: true, W, Fn: p.Fn, Fs: c.Fs, fuel: c.f * W, TSFC: c.f / c.Fs, Isp: c.Fs / (c.f * g0), V0: c.fs.V0, T0: c.fs.T0, P0: c.fs.P0, rec: c.rec, T4: c.T4, M3: c.M3, M4: c.M4, V10: c.V10, T10: c.T10, c3: c.c3, phi: p.phi,
        etaTh: c.etaTh, etaP: c.etaP, etaO: c.etaO, nozCore: { V: c.V10, W: W * (1 + c.f), Fg: W * (c.Fs + c.fs.V0), A8: A10, NPR: c.P4 / c.fs.P0 }, nozByp: null, warn: warn.concat(c.warn), LHV: c.g.LHV, A0, A3, A10, S: {}, FAR_STOICH: c.g.farSt, sc: c };
      const sr = (T, Pp, f) => c.g.phi(T, f) - c.g.R(f) * Math.log(Pp / 101325);
      r.S = { "0": { W, Tt: c.fs.T0, Pt: c.fs.P0, far: 0, s: sr(c.fs.T0, c.fs.P0, 0), stat: 1 }, "3": { W, Tt: c.c3.T3, Pt: c.c3.P3, far: 0, s: sr(c.c3.T3, c.c3.P3, 0), stat: 1 }, "4": { W: W * (1 + c.f), Tt: c.T4, Pt: c.P4, far: c.fb, s: sr(c.T4, c.P4, c.fb), stat: 1 }, "10": { W: W * (1 + c.f), Tt: c.T10, Pt: c.fs.P0 * p.P10ratio, far: c.fb, s: sr(c.T10, c.fs.P0 * p.P10ratio, c.fb), stat: 1 } };
      r.geo = geoScram(r, p); r.ref = { kind: "ram", cfg: "scramjet", p: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p)), W, A0, A3, Md: p.mach, th1: r.geo.th1, Fn: p.Fn };
    } else {
      const c = pulseCore(p, cond);
      if (!(c.Fn > 0)) return { p, kind: "ram", ok: false, warn: [{ lvl: "bad", t: "The valves can no longer seal against the ram pressure at this speed, so the tube makes no net thrust. Pulsejets top out well below Mach 1." }], S: {} };
      r = { p, kind: "ram", ok: true, W: c.W, Fn: c.Fn, Fs: c.Fn / c.W, fuel: c.fuel, TSFC: c.fuel / c.Fn, Isp: c.Fn / (c.fuel * g0), V0: c.fs.V0, T0: c.fs.T0, P0: c.fs.P0, pj: c, T4: c.T3, phi: p.phi,
        etaTh: c.etaTh, etaP: c.fs.V0 > 1 ? c.Fn * c.fs.V0 / (0.5 * c.W * (1 + c.far) * c.Vj * c.Vj - 0.5 * c.W * c.fs.V0 * c.fs.V0) : NaN, etaO: c.fs.V0 > 1 ? c.Fn * c.fs.V0 / (c.fuel * GJ.LHV) : NaN,
        nozCore: { V: c.Vj, W: c.W * (1 + c.far), Fg: c.W * (1 + c.far) * c.Vj, A8: Math.PI / 4 * p.tailD * p.tailD, NPR: c.PG }, nozByp: null, warn, LHV: GJ.LHV, S: {}, FAR_STOICH: GJ.farSt };
      if (c.kv < 0.5) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Near the valves' speed limit: ram pressure is holding them partly open, so each pulse leaks forward." });
      r.geo = geoPulse(r, p); r.ref = { kind: "ram", cfg: "pulsejet", p: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p)), Fn: c.Fn };
    }
    return r;
  }
  function odRam(ref, cond) {
    const p = ref.p, g0 = 9.80665, c2 = Object.assign({ phi: p.phi }, cond);
    if (ref.cfg === "ramjet") {
      if (c2.mach < 0.3) return { ok: false };
      const c = ramjetCore(p, c2, { thetas: ref.thetas }), cap = captureRatio(c2.mach, ref.thetas[0], ref.Md), W = c.fs.rho0 * c.fs.V0 * ref.A0 * cap, Fn = c.Fs * W;
      return { ok: true, kind: "ram", Fn, W, fuel: c.fuel * W, TSFC: Fn > 0 ? c.fuel * W / Fn : NaN, Isp: Fn > 0 ? c.Fs / (c.fuel * g0) : NaN, rec: c.inl.rec, cap, T4: c.T4, V0: c.fs.V0, phi: c.phiEff, inl: c.inl, nozCore: { V: c.noz.V }, etaP: c.etaP, etaO: c.etaO, choked: c.ray.choked };
    }
    if (ref.cfg === "scramjet") {
      if (c2.mach < 3) return { ok: false };
      const c = scramCore(p, c2, { A0: ref.A0, A3: ref.A3, th1: ref.th1, Md: ref.Md }), W = c.fs.rho0 * c.fs.V0 * ref.A0 * c.cap, Fn = c.Fs * W;
      return { ok: c.M3 > 1, kind: "ram", Fn, W, fuel: c.f * W, TSFC: Fn > 0 ? c.f * W / Fn : NaN, Isp: Fn > 0 ? c.Fs / (c.f * g0) : NaN, rec: c.rec, cap: c.cap, T4: c.T4, M3: c.M3, psi: c.c3.T3 / c.fs.T0, V0: c.fs.V0, phi: c2.phi, nozCore: { V: c.V10 }, etaP: c.etaP, etaO: c.etaO };
    }
    const c = pulseCore(p, c2);
    return { ok: c.Fn > 0, kind: "ram", Fn: c.Fn, W: c.W, fuel: c.fuel, TSFC: c.Fn > 0 ? c.fuel / c.Fn : NaN, Isp: c.Fn > 0 ? c.Fn / (c.fuel * g0) : NaN, freq: c.freq, kv: c.kv, T4: c.T3, V0: c.fs.V0, phi: c2.phi, nozCore: { V: c.Vj }, etaO: c.fs.V0 > 1 && c.Fn > 0 ? c.Fn * c.fs.V0 / (c.fuel * GJ.LHV) : NaN };
  }
  function geoRamjet(r, c, p) {
    const rc = Math.sqrt(r.A0 / Math.PI), th = c.thetas, parts = [], pts = [[0, 0]];
    let Th = 0, C = [0, 0];
    const L = th.length ? [rc / Math.tan(c.inl.steps[0].beta), rc] : [0, rc];
    for (let i = 0; i < th.length; i++) { const st0 = c.inl.steps[i];
      if (i > 0) { const ang = st0.beta + Th, dx = Math.cos(Th), dy = Math.sin(Th); // find corner on current body line so the shock hits the lip
        const tA = Math.tan(ang), t = (L[1] - C[1] - tA * (L[0] - C[0])) / (dy - tA * dx); C = [C[0] + t * dx, C[1] + t * dy]; pts.push(C.slice()); }
      Th += th[i]; }
    const S2 = r.S["2"], gg = c.g, A3 = (() => { const s = stG(gg, S2.W, S2.Tt, S2.Pt, 0); return staticAt(s, p.M3).area; })();
    const xL = L[0], rBodyL = C[1] + Math.tan(Th) * (xL - C[0]), r3 = Math.sqrt(A3 / Math.PI + Math.pow(rBodyL * 0.5, 2));
    pts.push([xL, Math.min(rBodyL, rc * 0.92)]);
    const xc0 = xL + 1.2 * rc, xc1 = xc0 + 3.2 * r3, r8 = Math.sqrt(r.nozCore.A8 / Math.PI), r9 = Math.sqrt(r.nozCore.A9 / Math.PI), xT = xc1 + 0.8 * r3, xE = xT + Math.max(0.2 * r3, 1.6 * (r9 - r8));
    const body = pts.concat([[xL + 0.5 * rc, rBodyL * 0.75], [xc0, rBodyL * 0.45], [xc0 + 0.6 * r3, 0.02]]);
    const shocks = c.inl.steps.filter(s => s.kind === "oblique").map((s, i) => ({ from: i === 0 ? [0, 0] : pts[i], to: [xL, rc] }));
    return { kind: "ramjet", cfg: "ramjet", D: 2 * Math.max(r3, rc) * 1.08, rc, r3, xL, xc0, xc1, xT, xE, r8, r9, body, shocks, lip: [xL, rc],
      cowl: [[xL, rc], [xL + 0.6 * rc, Math.max(rc, r3) * 1.02], [xc1, r3 * 1.05], [xT, r8 * 1.04], [xE, r9 * 1.03]], xMin: -0.1, xMax: xE, len: xE + 0.1, vel: { inlet: 200 }, parts };
  }
  function geoScram(r, p) {
    const W2 = 1.6, h0 = Math.sqrt(r.A0 / W2), h3 = r.A3 / (W2 * h0) * 1.0, h10 = r.A10 / (W2 * h0 * 1.0), th1 = Math.atan((h0 - h3) / (4.2 * h0)) * 0.6;
    const Lf = 4.5 * h0, Lc = 2.6 * h0, La = 3.5 * h0;
    return { kind: "scramjet", cfg: "scramjet", D: h0, h0, h3, h10, W2, Lf, Lc, La, th1, xMin: -0.1, xMax: Lf + Lc + La, len: Lf + Lc + La, vel: { inlet: r.V0 }, parts: [] };
  }
  function geoPulse(r, p) {
    const Lc = 0.28 * p.tubeL; return { kind: "pulsejet", cfg: "pulsejet", D: p.chamberD, L: p.tubeL, Lc, Dc: p.chamberD, Dt: p.tailD, xMin: -0.3 * p.chamberD, xMax: p.tubeL, len: p.tubeL * 1.1, vel: { inlet: 60 }, parts: [] };
  }

  const DEFAULTS = {

    sa: 4.6e-3, sn: 0.35, coreRatio: 0.45, nSeg: 4, massFrac: 0.9, Gox0: 350,
    pd: false, pdMode: "pred", pdModeGT: "spec", Tmetal: 1350, packCool: "air", scCool: "regen", scBurn: 10, sinkT: 0.02, bladeMat: "al", shear: 60e3, busV: 400, wallMat: "h230", leR: 3e-3, grain: "bates", webFrac: 0.55, starN: 7, starIn: 0.5, throatEro: 1e-4, caseMat: "steel", nPort: 1, build: "heritage", wallType: "channel", nozType: "bell", bellPct: 80, coolMode: "regen", matWall: "cucrzr", matJacket: "in718", epsRegen: 300, extCool: "rad", nCh: 1, chW: 1.2e-3, chH: 3e-3, chT: 0.8e-3, chLand: 1.0e-3,
    filmEta: 0, coolFrac: 1, injType: "impinging", injDp: 0.2, injPitch: 0.016, tpLayout: "single", tpAuto: "auto", tpN: 20000, tpNf: 30000, Nss: 30000, Ptank: 3.5e5, pinOx: 0, pinF: 0, tpUmax: 450, SF: 1.5, thrMin: 1,
    bypMax: 0.25, bypOpen: 1.9, bypFull: 3.0
  };
  const api = { solve, DEFAULTS, G, isa, LHV, FAR_STOICH, staticAt, nozzle, compress, turbine, solveOD, solveMax, solveODPower, speedLines, odGuess, MAPS, isShaft, solveRam, odRam, inletShocks, oblique, normalShock, optimizeRamps, GH2, GJ, YAIR, DEL, scramCore, ramjetCore, pulseCore, freestream };
  if (typeof module !== "undefined") module.exports = api; else root.Cycle = api;
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);

</script>
<script>
/* Propeller blade-element momentum theory (BEMT) with Prandtl tip and hub loss, swirl,
   a generic cambered propeller section (lift slope, stall, profile and compressibility drag).
   Blade planform from diameter, blade count and activity factor; twist set for a design advance ratio. SI units. */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  const PI = Math.PI, NE = 20, XH = 0.2;
  function geom(D, B, AF, Jd) {
    const shape = x => 0.35 + 0.9 * x - 0.95 * x * x;
    let I = 0; const n = 200; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const x = 0.15 + (i + 0.5) * 0.85 / n; I += shape(x) * x * x * x * 0.85 / n; }
    const k = AF * 16 / 100000 / I, el = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < NE; i++) { const t = (i + 0.5) / NE, x = XH + (1 - XH) * (1 - Math.cos(t * PI)) / 2, dx = (1 - XH) * PI / 2 * Math.sin(t * PI) / NE;
      el.push({ x, dx, cD: k * shape(x), tc: 0.2 - 0.14 * x, tw: Math.atan(Jd / (PI * x)) - Math.atan(Jd / (PI * 0.75)) }); }
    return { D, B, AF, Jd, el, cMax: k * 0.563 };
  }
  function section(alpha, M, tc, cr) {
    const a0 = -2.5 * PI / 180, Mc = Math.min(M, 0.9), slope = 2 * PI * 0.95 / Math.sqrt(1 - Mc * Mc);
    const CLmax = 1.35, CLmin = -0.9, as = a0 + CLmax / slope, an = a0 + CLmin / slope, CLlin = slope * (alpha - a0);
    let CL = CLlin, stalled = false;
    if (alpha > as) { CL = Math.max(CLmax - 2.0 * (alpha - as), 1.1 * Math.sin(2 * alpha)); stalled = true; }
    else if (alpha < an) { CL = Math.min(CLmin - 2.0 * (alpha - an), 1.1 * Math.sin(2 * alpha)); stalled = true; }
    if (stalled && cr) CL += Math.min(1, 3 * cr * cr) * (CLlin - CL) * 0.5; // rotational stall delay (Snel), inboard sections
    let CD = 0.0065 + 0.02 * tc + 0.012 * (CL - 0.3) * (CL - 0.3);
    if (stalled) CD = Math.max(CD, 1.9 * Math.pow(Math.sin(alpha), 2) + 0.02);
    const Mcrit = 0.87 - tc - Math.abs(CL) / 10 - 0.1; if (M > Mcrit) CD += 20 * Math.pow(M - Mcrit, 4);
    return { CL, CD };
  }
  function evalProp(g, V, rho, a, rpm, theta75) {
    const R = g.D / 2, Om = rpm * 2 * PI / 60, B = g.B; let T = 0, Q = 0; const dist = [];
    for (const e of g.el) {
      const r = e.x * R, c = e.cD * g.D, beta = theta75 + e.tw, Ur = Om * r;
      let vt = 0, Fl = 1;
      const blade = vi => { let W2, phi, s, Cn, Ct, M;
        for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) { const ua = V + vi, ut = Math.max(1e-6, Ur - vt); W2 = ua * ua + ut * ut; phi = Math.atan2(ua, ut);
          const sp = Math.max(1e-4, Math.abs(Math.sin(phi)));
          const ft = (B / 2) * (1 - e.x) / (e.x * sp), fh = (B / 2) * (e.x - XH) / (XH * sp);
          Fl = Math.max(0.02, (4 / (PI * PI)) * Math.acos(Math.min(1, Math.exp(-ft))) * Math.acos(Math.min(1, Math.exp(-fh))));
          M = Math.sqrt(W2) / a; s = section(beta - phi, M, e.tc, c / r); Cn = s.CL * Math.cos(phi) - s.CD * Math.sin(phi); Ct = s.CL * Math.sin(phi) + s.CD * Math.cos(phi);
          const dQb = 0.5 * rho * W2 * B * c * Ct * r, den = 4 * PI * r * r * rho * Math.max(1e-3, ua) * Fl; vt = Math.min(0.9 * Ur, Math.max(-0.5 * Ur, dQb / den)); }
        return { dT: 0.5 * rho * W2 * B * c * Cn, dQ: 0.5 * rho * W2 * B * c * Ct * r, phi, M, alpha: beta - phi }; };
      const resid = vi => blade(vi).dT - 4 * PI * r * rho * (V + vi) * vi * Fl;
      let lo = -0.45 * V, hi = Math.max(5, 1.5 * Ur), rl = resid(lo), rh = resid(hi), vi = 0;
      if (rl * rh > 0) { vi = Math.abs(rl) < Math.abs(rh) ? lo : hi; }
      else { for (let i = 0; i < 44; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2, rm = resid(m); if ((rm > 0) === (rl > 0)) { lo = m; rl = rm; } else hi = m; } vi = (lo + hi) / 2; }
      const b = blade(vi); T += b.dT * e.dx * R; Q += b.dQ * e.dx * R; dist.push({ x: e.x, vi, alpha: b.alpha, M: b.M });
    }
    const n = rpm / 60, P = Q * Om, J = V / (n * g.D), CT = T / (rho * n * n * Math.pow(g.D, 4)), CP = P / (rho * n * n * n * Math.pow(g.D, 5));
    const Mtip = Math.sqrt(V * V + Math.pow(Om * R, 2)) / a;
    return { T, Q, P, eta: P > 0 && V > 0.5 ? T * V / P : NaN, J, CT, CP, Mtip, theta75, dist, FM: P > 0 ? Math.pow(Math.max(T, 0), 1.5) / (P * Math.sqrt(2 * rho * PI * R * R)) : NaN };
  }
  function solvePower(g, V, rho, a, rpm, Ptarget) { // constant-speed propeller: find blade pitch that absorbs the power
    let lo = -5 * PI / 180, hi = 75 * PI / 180, plo = evalProp(g, V, rho, a, rpm, lo).P, phi = evalProp(g, V, rho, a, rpm, hi).P;
    if (Ptarget > phi) { const o = evalProp(g, V, rho, a, rpm, hi); o.short = true; return o; }
    if (Ptarget < plo) { const o = evalProp(g, V, rho, a, rpm, lo); o.short = true; return o; }
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2, pm = evalProp(g, V, rho, a, rpm, m).P; if (pm < Ptarget) lo = m; else hi = m; if (hi - lo < 2e-5) break; }
    return evalProp(g, V, rho, a, rpm, (lo + hi) / 2);
  }
  function fromParams(p, V) { const n = p.propRPM / 60; const Jd = p.propJd ? p.propJd : Math.max(0.3, Math.min(3.5, (V > 1 ? V : 0.4 * p.propD * n) / (n * p.propD))); return geom(p.propD, p.propB, p.propAF, Jd); }
  const api = { geom, evalProp, solvePower, fromParams, section };
  if (typeof module !== "undefined") module.exports = api; else root.Prop = api;
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);

</script>
<script>
/* Piston-propeller and electric-propeller powertrains.
   Piston: four-stroke spark ignition, air flow from volumetric efficiency and manifold density, fuel-air cycle approximated
   from the Otto efficiency (gamma 1.30) with a 0.85 factor, rich-mixture combustion loss, and a friction MEP that rises with rpm.
   Turbocharging holds a rated manifold pressure up to a critical altitude, with intercooling and exhaust back-pressure losses.
   Electric: battery, inverter and motor efficiencies, C-rate and endurance. Propellers use the BEMT model in prop.js. SI units. */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  const C = () => (typeof module !== "undefined" && typeof require !== "undefined") ? require("./cycle.js") : root.Cycle;
  const PL = () => (typeof module !== "undefined" && typeof require !== "undefined") ? require("./prop.js") : root.Prop;
  const LHV_AVGAS = 43.6e6, FAR_ST = 1 / 14.9, RAIR = 287.05;
  function atm(alt, dISA, mach) { const a = C().isa(alt, dISA || 0), rho = a.P / (RAIR * a.T), sp = Math.sqrt(1.4 * RAIR * a.T); return { T: a.T, P: a.P, rho, a: sp, V: (mach || 0) * sp }; }
  const fmep = rpm => (70 + 32 * rpm / 1000) * 1000; // Pa
  function manifold(p, A, thr) { // manifold pressure and temperature for the induction system
    const ram = 1 + 0.2 * Math.pow(A.V / A.a, 2) * 0.7; // partial ram recovery in the intake
    if (p.induction !== "turbo") return { MAP: Math.min(A.P * ram * 0.97, A.P * ram) * thr, Tm: A.T + 10, PRc: 1, backP: 0 };
    const Pc = C().isa(p.critAlt, 0).P, PRmax = p.ratedMAP / (Pc * 0.97), want = p.ratedMAP * thr, PRc = Math.max(1, Math.min(PRmax, want / (A.P * ram * 0.97)));
    const MAP = A.P * ram * 0.97 * PRc, Tc = A.T * (1 + (Math.pow(PRc, 0.2857) - 1) / 0.72), Tm = A.T + 10 + (1 - p.icEff) * (Tc - A.T);
    return { MAP, Tm, PRc, backP: 12000 * (PRc - 1) };
  }
  function pistonCycle(p, A, rpm, thr, lambda, disp) {
    const m = manifold(p, A, thr), rhoM = m.MAP / (RAIR * m.Tm), N = rpm / 60;
    const mAir = p.volEff * rhoM * disp * N / 2, FAR = FAR_ST / lambda, mFuel = mAir * FAR;
    const etaOtto = 1 - Math.pow(p.CR, -0.30), comb = Math.min(1, lambda), leanGain = lambda > 1 ? 1 + 0.08 * (lambda - 1) : 1;
    const etaI = etaOtto * 0.85 * comb * leanGain, Pind = mFuel * LHV_AVGAS * etaI, Pfr = (fmep(rpm) + m.backP) * disp * N / 2;
    const Pb = Pind - Pfr;
    return { Pb, Pind, Pfr, mAir, mFuel, FAR, MAP: m.MAP, Tm: m.Tm, PRc: m.PRc, etaI, etaOtto, BMEP: Pb / (disp * N / 2), IMEP: Pind / (disp * N / 2) };
  }
  const lam = p => (p.mixture === "economy" ? 1.08 : 0.86);
  function pistonGeom(disp, ncyl) { const v = disp / ncyl, bore = Math.pow(v / (Math.PI / 4 * 0.85), 1 / 3); return { bore, stroke: 0.85 * bore, vcyl: v }; }
  function propFor(p, P, A, geom) { const L = PL(), g = geom || L.fromParams(p, A.V); const o = L.solvePower(g, A.V, A.rho, A.a, p.propRPM, P); o.geom = g; return o; }
  function solvePiston(p) {
    const A = atm(p.alt, p.dISA, p.mach), warn = [], ref1 = pistonCycle(p, A, p.rpm, 1, lam(p), 1e-3);
    if (!(ref1.Pb > 0)) return { p, kind: "piston", ok: false, warn: [{ lvl: "bad", t: "Friction exceeds the indicated power at this rpm and altitude." }], S: {} };
    const disp = p.sizeBy === "W" ? p.disp : p.Pshaft / ref1.Pb * 1e-3, c = pistonCycle(p, A, p.rpm, 1, lam(p), disp);
    const pg = pistonGeom(disp, p.ncyl), Pdel = c.Pb * p.effGearP, prop = propFor(p, Pdel, A), Fn = prop.T, fuel = c.mFuel;
    if (prop.short) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "The propeller can't absorb all the power at any pitch. Use a larger diameter, more blades or a higher activity factor." });
    if (prop.Mtip > 0.88) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Propeller tip Mach " + prop.Mtip.toFixed(2) + " is high. Lower the prop rpm (add a reduction gear) or the diameter." });
    const Up = 2 * pg.stroke * p.rpm / 60; if (Up > 16) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Mean piston speed " + Up.toFixed(1) + " m/s is high for an aircraft engine (most run 10–14 m/s)." });
    if (p.CR > 9.5 && p.induction === "turbo") warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "A compression ratio above ~9.5 with turbocharging risks detonation on 100LL avgas." });
    if (p.CR > 11) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Compression ratio above ~11 is prone to detonation on avgas." });
    const Pf = fuel * LHV_AVGAS, use = Fn * A.V;
    return { p, kind: "piston", ok: true, warn, disp, geom: pg, Pshaft: c.Pb, Pdel, PSFC: fuel / c.Pb, fuel, Fn, TSFC: Fn > 1 ? fuel / Fn : NaN, V0: A.V, W: c.mAir, cyc: c, prop, Up,
      etaTh: c.Pb / Pf, etaP: A.V > 1 ? prop.eta : NaN, etaO: A.V > 1 ? use / Pf : NaN, energy: [["Useful thrust power", use, "#1F7A4D"], ["Propeller and wake losses", Math.max(0, Pdel - use), "#5DCAA5"], ["Friction, gearbox and pumping", c.Pind - c.Pb + (c.Pb - Pdel), "#888780"], ["Heat to exhaust and cooling", Math.max(0, Pf - c.Pind), "#EF9F27"]], Ptot: Pf,
      ref: { kind: "piston", p: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p)), disp, propGeom: prop.geom, Pshaft: c.Pb, PSFC: fuel / c.Pb, Fn } };
  }
  function odPiston(ref, cond) {
    const p = Object.assign({}, ref.p), A = atm(cond.alt, cond.dISA, cond.mach), rpm = (cond.rpmFrac || 1) * p.rpm, lambda = cond.mixture === "economy" ? 1.08 : (cond.mixture === "power" ? 0.86 : lam(p));
    const pp = Object.assign({}, p, { propRPM: p.propRPM * (cond.rpmFrac || 1) });
    const c = pistonCycle(p, A, rpm, cond.thr === undefined ? 1 : cond.thr, lambda, ref.disp);
    if (!(c.Pb > 0)) return { ok: false };
    const Pdel = c.Pb * p.effGearP, prop = propFor(pp, Pdel, A, ref.propGeom);
    return { ok: true, kind: "piston", Pshaft: c.Pb, PSFC: c.mFuel / c.Pb, fuel: c.mFuel, Fn: prop.T, TSFC: prop.T > 1 ? c.mFuel / prop.T : NaN, W: c.mAir, prop, cyc: c, V0: A.V, MAP: c.MAP, rpm,
      etaP: A.V > 1 ? prop.eta : NaN, etaTh: c.Pb / (c.mFuel * LHV_AVGAS), etaO: A.V > 1 ? prop.T * A.V / (c.mFuel * LHV_AVGAS) : NaN };
  }
  function solveElectric(p) {
    const A = atm(p.alt, p.dISA, p.mach), warn = [], Psh = p.Pshaft, Pel = Psh / (p.effMotor * p.effInv), E = p.battE * 3.6e6, Euse = E * p.battUse;
    const prop = propFor(p, Psh * p.effGearP, A), Fn = prop.T, C = Pel / (p.battE * 1000), endur = Euse / Pel;
    if (C > p.battCmax) warn.push({ lvl: "bad", t: "Full power needs a " + C.toFixed(1) + "C discharge, above the pack's " + p.battCmax + "C limit. Add energy or use higher-power cells." });
    if (prop.short) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "The propeller can't absorb all the power at any pitch. Use a larger diameter, more blades or a higher activity factor." });
    if (prop.Mtip > 0.88) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Propeller tip Mach " + prop.Mtip.toFixed(2) + " is high. Lower the motor or prop rpm." });
    const mBatt = p.battE * 1000 / p.battSE, mMotor = Psh / (p.motorSP * 1000), use = Fn * A.V, Pd = Psh * p.effGearP;
    return { p, kind: "electric", ok: true, warn, Pshaft: Psh, Pel, Fn, V0: A.V, prop, Crate: C, endur, range: A.V * endur, mBatt, mMotor, energyPerThrust: Fn > 1 ? Pel / Fn : NaN,
      etaTh: p.effMotor * p.effInv, etaP: A.V > 1 ? prop.eta : NaN, etaO: A.V > 1 ? use / Pel : NaN, W: 0, fuel: 0, TSFC: NaN, PSFC: NaN,
      energy: [["Useful thrust power", use, "#1F7A4D"], ["Propeller and wake losses", Math.max(0, Pd - use), "#5DCAA5"], ["Motor, inverter and gearbox heat", Pel - Pd, "#888780"]], Ptot: Pel,
      ref: { kind: "electric", p: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p)), propGeom: prop.geom, Pshaft: Psh, Fn } };
  }
  function odElectric(ref, cond) {
    const p = ref.p, A = atm(cond.alt, cond.dISA, cond.mach), Psh = p.Pshaft * (cond.thr === undefined ? 1 : cond.thr), Pel = Psh / (p.effMotor * p.effInv);
    const prop = propFor(p, Psh * p.effGearP, A, ref.propGeom), endur = p.battE * 3.6e6 * p.battUse / Pel;
    return { ok: true, kind: "electric", Pshaft: Psh, Pel, Fn: prop.T, prop, V0: A.V, endur, range: A.V * endur, Crate: Pel / (p.battE * 1000), etaP: A.V > 1 ? prop.eta : NaN, etaO: A.V > 1 ? prop.T * A.V / Pel : NaN, W: 0, fuel: 0, TSFC: NaN, PSFC: NaN };
  }
  const api = { solvePiston, odPiston, solveElectric, odElectric, pistonCycle, atm, LHV_AVGAS };
  if (typeof module !== "undefined") module.exports = api; else root.Powertrain = api;
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);

</script>

<script>
/* Rocket engines: liquid (five feed cycles), solid (BATES grain) and hybrid (single-port).
   Thermochemistry: shifting-equilibrium tables precomputed with Cantera + NASA Glenn data. */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  const g0 = 9.80665;
  const TAB = typeof module !== "undefined" && module.exports ? require("./rocket_tables.json") : root.ROCKET_TABLES;
  const isaP = alt => { const h = Math.min(alt, 84000); if (h < 11000) return 101325 * Math.pow(1 - 2.25577e-5 * h, 5.25588);
    if (h < 20000) return 22632 * Math.exp(-(h - 11000) / 6341.6); if (h < 32000) return 5474.9 * Math.pow(1 + (h - 20000) / 216650, -34.163);
    if (h < 47000) return 868.02 * Math.pow(1 + 2.8 * (h - 32000) / 228650, -12.201); return 110.91 * Math.exp(-(h - 47000) / 7922); };
  // propellant data: densities kg/m3; gas-generator / preburner gas properties for drive turbines
  const PROP = {
    lox_rp1: { name: "LOX / RP-1", kind: "liquid", rhoOx: 1141, rhoF: 810, gg: { cp: 2350, g: 1.15 }, pb: "ox", coolF: 2100, OF: 2.6 },
    lox_lh2: { name: "LOX / LH2", kind: "liquid", rhoOx: 1141, rhoF: 70.8, gg: { cp: 7800, g: 1.36 }, pb: "fuel", coolF: 14300, OF: 6.0 },
    lox_ch4: { name: "LOX / methane", kind: "liquid", rhoOx: 1141, rhoF: 422, gg: { cp: 3100, g: 1.2 }, pb: "full", coolF: 3500, OF: 3.6 },
    nto_mmh: { name: "NTO / MMH", kind: "liquid", rhoOx: 1440, rhoF: 875, gg: { cp: 2300, g: 1.25 }, pb: "fuel", coolF: 2900, OF: 2.0 },
    n2o_htpb: { name: "N2O / HTPB (hybrid)", kind: "hybrid", rhoOx: 785, rhoF: 930, a: 1.98e-4, n: 0.325, OF: 7.0 },
    lox_htpb: { name: "LOX / HTPB (hybrid)", kind: "hybrid", rhoOx: 1141, rhoF: 930, a: 3.04e-5, n: 0.681, OF: 2.2 },
    apcp: { name: "AP / Al / HTPB (solid)", kind: "solid", rho: 1770, a: 4.6e-3, n: 0.35 }
  };
  const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + (b - a) * t;
  function bracket(xs, x) { let i = 0; while (i < xs.length - 2 && x > xs[i + 1]) i++; return [i, (x - xs[i]) / (xs[i + 1] - xs[i])]; }
  function rowAt(r, eps, E) { const le = Math.log(eps), L = E.map(Math.log); const [k, t] = bracket(L, le);
    const a = r.exits[k], b = r.exits[k + 1];
    return { CFv: lerp(a.CFv, b.CFv, t), pe: Math.exp(lerp(Math.log(a.pe), Math.log(b.pe), t)), Te: lerp(a.Te, b.Te, t), ue: lerp(a.ue, b.ue, t) }; }
  function chem(key, x, Pc, eps) { // interpolated shifting-equilibrium performance
    const T = TAB[key], nP = T.Pc.length; let xx = Math.min(T.x[T.x.length - 1] * 1.15, Math.max(T.x[0] * 0.85 - (T.xkind === "AL" ? 0.01 : 0), x));
    const [i, tx] = bracket(T.x, xx), [j, tp] = bracket(T.Pc.map(Math.log), Math.log(Math.max(2e5, Pc)));
    const C = (a, b) => T.rows[a * nP + b].r, out = {};
    const corners = [[i, j, (1 - tx) * (1 - tp)], [i + 1, j, tx * (1 - tp)], [i, j + 1, (1 - tx) * tp], [i + 1, j + 1, tx * tp]];
    let cs = 0, Tc = 0, CFv = 0, lpe = 0, Te = 0, fc = 0, M = 0, ptr = 0;
    corners.forEach(([a, b, w]) => { const r = C(a, b), e = rowAt(r, eps, T.eps); cs += w * r.cstar; Tc += w * r.Tc; CFv += w * e.CFv; lpe += w * Math.log(e.pe); Te += w * e.Te; fc += w * (r.fc || 0);
      M += w * r.rho_c * 8314.46 * r.Tc / T.Pc[b]; ptr += w * r.Pt; });
    let gl = 1.05, gh = 1.45; for (let k = 0; k < 40; k++) { const g = (gl + gh) / 2; if (Math.pow(2 / (g + 1), g / (g - 1)) > ptr) gl = g; else gh = g; } // gamma that reproduces the equilibrium throat pressure
    return { cstar: cs, Tc, CFv, pe: Math.exp(lpe), Te, fc, M, gam: (gl + gh) / 2 };
  }
  function optEps(key, x, Pc, pa, epsMax) { if (pa <= 0) return epsMax; let lo = Math.log(1.6), hi = Math.log(epsMax);
    if (chem(key, x, Pc, epsMax).pe * Pc > pa) return epsMax;
    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2; if (chem(key, x, Pc, Math.exp(m)).pe * Pc > pa) lo = m; else hi = m; } return Math.exp((lo + hi) / 2); }
  const twoPhase = (p, eps) => (PROP[p.rkt].kind === "solid" && p.AL > 0 ? 1 - K2P * (p.AL / 0.16) * Math.max(0, Math.log(eps / 7.7)) : 1); /* condensed alumina lags the gas; the loss grows with expansion ratio. Fitted to the Star 48BV, zero at the shuttle booster's area ratio */
  const perf = (c, p0, eps, Pc, pa) => { const p = { etaCF: p0.etaCF * twoPhase(p0, eps), etaCs: p0.etaCs }; const CF = p.etaCF * c.CFv - eps * pa / Pc; return { CF, Isp: p.etaCs * c.cstar * CF / g0, IspV: p.etaCs * c.cstar * p.etaCF * c.CFv / g0 }; };
  const K2P = 0.02;
  const sepRatio = (Pc, pa) => (pa > 0 ? 0.667 * Math.pow(Pc / pa, -0.2) : 0); // Kalt–Bendall free-shock separation
  const turbW = (cp, g, T, PR, eta) => eta * cp * T * (1 - Math.pow(PR, -(g - 1) / g));

  /* ---------- liquid feed systems ---------- */
  function feed(p, pr, mox, mf, Pc, At) {
    // Overrides used by the preliminary-design loop: injDp, dPcool, etaPumpOx/F, etaTurb, Ptank, Tjacket
    const out = { warn: [] }, cyc = p.rCycle, dInj = (p.injDp == null ? 0.2 : p.injDp) * Pc, dCool = p.dPcool != null ? p.dPcool : 0.15 * Pc, dLine = (p.lineDp == null ? 0.15 : p.lineDp) * Pc; // injector, cooling jacket, and feed lines plus valves
    const eO = p.etaPumpOx || p.etaPump, eF = p.etaPumpF || p.etaPump, eT = p.etaTurb || (cyc === "gg" ? 0.6 : 0.75);
    const Pt = cyc === "pf" ? Pc + dInj + dCool + dLine + 1e5 : (p.Ptank || 3.5e5);
    Object.assign(out, { Ptank: Pt, dInj, dCool, dLine, etaT: eT, etaO: eO, etaF: eF });
    // Only propellant bound for the preburner (or turbine) is pumped to the high pressure Pd; the rest goes to chamber pressure plus injector (and jacket) losses.
    const side = cyc === "sc" ? pr.pb : cyc === "exp" ? "fuel" : "full", Pmo = Pc + dInj + dLine, Pmf = Pc + dInj + dCool + dLine, oxPB = Math.min(mox, 0.9 * mf), fPB = mox / 55;
    const pw = Pd => { let o, f;
      if (side === "ox") { o = mox * (Pd - Pt); f = mf * (Pmf - Pt) + fPB * Math.max(0, Pd - Pmf); }
      else if (side === "fuel") { f = mf * (Pd + dCool - Pt); o = mox * (Pmo - Pt) + (cyc === "sc" ? oxPB * Math.max(0, Pd - Pmo) : 0); }
      else { o = mox * (Pd - Pt); f = mf * (Pd + dCool - Pt); }
      o /= pr.rhoOx * eO; f /= pr.rhoF * eF; return { o, f, t: o + f }; };
    const set = Pd => { const q = pw(Pd); Object.assign(out, { Pd, PdOx: side === "fuel" ? Pmo : Pd, PdF: side === "ox" ? Pmf : Pd + dCool, Ppump: q.t, PpOx: q.o, PpF: q.f, side }); };
    if (cyc === "pf") { Object.assign(out, { Ppump: 0, PpOx: 0, PpF: 0, Pd: Pt, PdOx: Pt, PdF: Pt, mgg: 0 });
      if (Pc > 3e6) out.warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "A pressure-fed engine at " + (Pc / 1e6).toFixed(1) + " MPa needs tanks at about " + (out.Ptank / 1e6).toFixed(1) + " MPa. The tank walls get thick enough to cancel the performance gain; pressure-fed engines usually stay under about 2.5 MPa." });
      return out; }
    if (cyc === "gg" || cyc === "ep") { set(Pc + dInj + dLine);
      if (cyc === "gg") { const PR = 20, w = turbW(pr.gg.cp, pr.gg.g, p.Tgg, PR, eT); out.mgg = out.Ppump / w; out.PRt = PR; out.turb = { mt: out.mgg, cp: pr.gg.cp, g: pr.gg.g, T: p.Tgg, PR };
        const Tex = p.Tgg * (1 - eT * (1 - Math.pow(PR, -(pr.gg.g - 1) / pr.gg.g))); out.vgg = Math.sqrt(2 * pr.gg.cp * Tex * 0.55); }
      else { out.mgg = 0; out.Pbatt = out.Ppump / (0.92 * 0.95); out.mBatt = out.Pbatt / 5000 + out.Pbatt * p.tb / 3600 / 180; }
      return out; }
    // closed cycles: find the turbine pressure ratio that balances the pumps; pump discharge rises with it
    const gas = () => { let mt, cp, g, T;
      if (cyc === "exp") { cp = pr.coolF; g = pr.pb === "fuel" && cp > 10000 ? 1.4 : 1.3; mt = mf;
        if (p.Tjacket) T = p.Tjacket; else { const Q = p.qExp * Math.pow(Pc / 1e6, 0.8) * Math.pow(At, 0.9) * 1e6; T = 30 + Q / (mf * pr.coolF) + (cp > 10000 ? 20 : 110); } out.Tturb = T; }
      else { T = p.Tpb; mt = pr.pb === "ox" ? mox + fPB : pr.pb === "fuel" ? mf + oxPB : mox + mf;
        if (pr.pb === "ox") { cp = 1100; g = 1.31; }                                   // oxygen-rich gas is mostly hot oxygen
        else if (pr.pb === "full") { cp = (mox * 1100 + mf * pr.gg.cp) / (mox + mf); g = 1.27; } // one oxygen-rich and one fuel-rich turbine, lumped
        else { cp = pr.gg.cp; g = pr.gg.g; } }
      return { mt, cp, g, T }; }, G = gas();
    const avail = PR => { const Pd = (cyc === "sc" ? 1.08 * PR * (Pc + 0.4 * dInj) : (Pc + dInj) * PR) + dLine; return { Pd, Pa: turbW(G.cp, G.g, G.T, PR, eT) * G.mt, Pr: pw(Pd).t }; };
    let lo = 1.02, hi = null, ok = false;
    for (let PR = 1.05; PR <= 6.001; PR += 0.05) { const q = avail(PR); if (q.Pa >= q.Pr) { hi = PR; ok = true; break; } lo = PR; }
    if (!ok) hi = 6; else for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2, q = avail(m); if (q.Pa >= q.Pr) hi = m; else lo = m; }
    const a = avail(hi); set(a.Pd); out.PRt = hi; out.mgg = 0; out.balanced = ok; out.turb = Object.assign({ PR: hi }, G);
    if (!ok) out.warn.push({ lvl: "bad", t: cyc === "exp" ? "The expander cycle can't close: the heat the fuel picks up in the cooling jacket can't drive the pumps at this chamber pressure and size. Expander engines are limited to roughly 4 to 7 MPa; lower Pc." : "The preburner can't drive the pumps: even at a turbine pressure ratio of 6 there's not enough power. Raise the preburner temperature or lower Pc." });
    else if (hi > 2.6) out.warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "The pumps must deliver " + (a.Pd / 1e6).toFixed(0) + " MPa, " + (a.Pd / Pc).toFixed(1) + "× chamber pressure, to drive the turbine. That's high; real staged-combustion engines run at about 1.5 to 2.2×." });
    return out;
  }

  /* ---------- solid: BATES grain ---------- */
  function batesAb(Dg, Di, Ls, N, w) { const d = Di + 2 * w, L = Ls - 2 * w; if (d >= Dg || L <= 0) return 0; return N * (Math.PI * d * L + 2 * Math.PI / 4 * (Dg * Dg - d * d)); }
  function solid(p, pr, c0, pa) {
    const rho = pr.rho, a = p.sa, n = p.sn, cs = p.etaCs * c0.cstar, Pc = p.Pc, r0 = a * Math.pow(Pc / 1e6, n); // a: burn rate in m/s at 1 MPa
    const ratio = p.coreRatio, web = r0 * p.tb, Dg = 2 * web / (1 - ratio), Di = ratio * Dg;
    const thrustFor = Ls => { const Ab = batesAb(Dg, Di, Ls, p.nSeg, 0); const At = Ab * rho * r0 * cs / Pc; return { At, Ab }; };
    // segment length that gives the required thrust at ignition
    const c = chem(p.rkt, p.AL, Pc, p.eps), pf = perf(c, p, p.eps, Pc, pa);
    const AtReq = p.Fn / (pf.CF * Pc); let lo = 0.05 * Dg, hi = 40 * Dg;
    for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2; if (thrustFor(m).At < AtReq) lo = m; else hi = m; }
    const Ls = (lo + hi) / 2, At = AtReq, Kn = batesAb(Dg, Di, Ls, p.nSeg, 0) / At;
    // regression trace
    const tr = []; let w = 0, t = 0, It = 0, mp = 0; const dt = p.tb / 200;
    for (let k = 0; k < 600; k++) { const Ab = batesAb(Dg, Di, Ls, p.nSeg, w); if (Ab <= 0) break;
      const Pct = Math.pow(Ab / At * rho * a * cs / Math.pow(1e6, n), 1 / (1 - n)), cc = chem(p.rkt, p.AL, Pct, p.eps), q = perf(cc, p, p.eps, Pct, pa), F = q.CF * Pct * At, r = a * Math.pow(Pct / 1e6, n);
      tr.push({ t, Pc: Pct, F, Isp: q.Isp }); It += F * dt; mp += rho * Ab * r * dt; w += r * dt; t += dt; }
    const mprop = rho * (p.nSeg * (Math.PI / 4 * (Dg * Dg - Di * Di) * Ls));
    return { Dg, Di, Ls, At, Kn, r0, web, trace: tr, It, mprop, tburn: t, Ltot: p.nSeg * Ls };
  }
  /* ---------- hybrid: single circular port ---------- */
  function hybrid(p, pr, pa) {
    const a = pr.a, n = pr.n; let c = chem(p.rkt, p.OF, p.Pc, p.eps), pf = perf(c, p, p.eps, p.Pc, pa);
    let mdot = p.Fn / (pf.Isp * g0), mox = mdot * p.OF / (1 + p.OF), mf = mdot - mox;
    const Ap0 = mox / p.Gox0, Dp0 = Math.sqrt(4 * Ap0 / Math.PI), r0 = a * Math.pow(p.Gox0, n), L = mf / (pr.rhoF * r0 * Math.PI * Dp0);
    const At = p.Fn / (pf.CF * p.Pc), tr = []; let Dp = Dp0, t = 0, It = 0, mfuel = 0; const dt = p.tb / 200;
    for (let k = 0; k <= 200; k++) { const G = mox / (Math.PI / 4 * Dp * Dp), r = a * Math.pow(G, n), mft = pr.rhoF * r * Math.PI * Dp * L, OF = mox / mft;
      let Pct = p.Pc; for (let it = 0; it < 4; it++) { const cc = chem(p.rkt, OF, Pct, p.eps); Pct = (mox + mft) * p.etaCs * cc.cstar / At; }
      const cc = chem(p.rkt, OF, Pct, p.eps), q = perf(cc, p, p.eps, Pct, pa), F = q.CF * Pct * At;
      tr.push({ t, Pc: Pct, F, Isp: q.Isp, OF }); if (k < 200) { It += F * dt; mfuel += mft * dt; Dp += 2 * r * dt; t += dt; } }
    return { Dp0, Dpf: Dp, L, At, mox, mf, r0, trace: tr, It, mfuel, mox_tot: mox * p.tb, tburn: p.tb };
  }

  function solveRocket(p) {
    const pr = PROP[p.rkt], warn = [], pa = isaP(p.alt), x = pr.kind === "solid" ? p.AL : p.OF;
    if (!TAB || !TAB[p.rkt]) return { ok: false, kind: "rocket", p, warn: [{ lvl: "bad", t: "Propellant table missing." }] };
    let eps = p.epsMode === "opt" ? optEps(p.rkt, x, p.Pc, pa, p.epsMax) : p.eps; p = Object.assign({}, p, { eps });
    let c = chem(p.rkt, x, p.Pc, eps), pf = perf(c, p, eps, p.Pc, pa);
    const r = { ok: true, kind: "rocket", p, pr, warn, pa, eps, Tc: c.Tc, cstar: p.etaCs * c.cstar, cstarIdeal: c.cstar, CF: pf.CF, CFv: p.etaCF * c.CFv, pe: c.pe * p.Pc, Te: c.Te, fc: c.fc,
      IspV: pf.IspV, IspSL: perf(c, p, eps, p.Pc, 101325).Isp, Pc: p.Pc, OF: x };
    let F = p.Fn, Fmain = F, fd = null;
    if (pr.kind === "liquid") {
      for (let it = 0; it < 4; it++) { const At = Fmain / (pf.CF * p.Pc), md = p.Pc * At / r.cstar, mox = md * x / (1 + x), mf = md - mox; fd = feed(p, pr, mox, mf, p.Pc, At);
        const Fgg = fd.mgg ? fd.mgg * fd.vgg : 0; Fmain = F - Fgg; Object.assign(r, { At, mdot: md, mox, mf }); }
      r.feed = fd; r.mgg = fd.mgg || 0; r.mtot = r.mdot + r.mgg; r.Isp = F / (r.mtot * g0); r.IspMain = pf.Isp; warn.push(...fd.warn);
      r.Fgg = fd.mgg ? fd.mgg * fd.vgg : 0;
      if (p.rCycle === "exp" && !(pr.coolF > 3000)) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Expander cycles need a fuel that absorbs a lot of heat without decomposing (hydrogen or methane). Kerosene and MMH would coke or decompose in the cooling jacket." });
      if (p.rCycle === "sc" && p.rkt === "nto_mmh") warn.push({ lvl: "info", t: "Staged-combustion storable engines exist (the Soviet RD-253 burns NTO/UDMH), but most NTO/MMH engines are small pressure-fed thrusters." });
    } else if (pr.kind === "solid") {
      const s = solid(p, pr, c, pa); r.solid = s; r.At = s.At; r.mdot = F / (pf.Isp * g0); r.mtot = r.mdot; r.Isp = pf.Isp; r.trace = s.trace; r.It = s.It; r.mprop = s.mprop;
      if (p.sn > 0.75) warn.push({ lvl: "bad", t: "A burn-rate pressure exponent of " + p.sn.toFixed(2) + " makes the motor unstable: a small pressure rise burns faster, raising pressure further. Keep n below about 0.7." });
      const pk = Math.max(...s.trace.map(q => q.Pc)); if (pk > 1.4 * p.Pc) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "The BATES grain's burning area grows during the burn, so chamber pressure peaks at " + (pk / 1e6).toFixed(1) + " MPa, " + Math.round((pk / p.Pc - 1) * 100) + "% above ignition. A smaller core or more, shorter segments flattens it." });
    } else {
      const h = hybrid(p, pr, pa); r.hyb = h; r.At = h.At; r.mox = h.mox; r.mf = h.mf; r.mdot = h.mox + h.mf; r.mtot = r.mdot; r.Isp = pf.Isp; r.trace = h.trace; r.It = h.It;
      const ofs = h.trace.map(q => q.OF); r.OFend = ofs[ofs.length - 1];
      warn.push({ lvl: "info", t: "As the port opens up, the oxidizer flux drops and less fuel is released, so the mixture ratio drifts from " + ofs[0].toFixed(1) + " to " + r.OFend.toFixed(1) + " over the burn. That O/F shift is hybrids' signature loss." });
    }
    r.F = F; r.Fn = F; r.fuel = r.mtot; r.TSFC = 1 / (r.Isp * g0);
    r.nCh = pr.kind === "liquid" ? Math.max(1, Math.round(p.nCh || 1)) : 1; r.AtTot = r.At; r.At = r.At / r.nCh; // several thrust chambers can share one turbopump set
    r.Dt = Math.sqrt(4 * r.At / Math.PI); r.Ae = r.At * eps; r.De = Math.sqrt(4 * r.Ae / Math.PI);
    r.Lstar = { lox_rp1: 1.1, lox_lh2: 0.85, lox_ch4: 1.0, nto_mmh: 0.85 }[p.rkt] || 1.0;
    r.Dc = r.Dt * Math.sqrt(p.contr); r.Lc = r.Lstar * r.At / (Math.PI / 4 * r.Dc * r.Dc);
    r.Ln = 0.8 * (r.De - r.Dt) / 2 / Math.tan(15 * Math.PI / 180);
    const kb = sepRatio(p.Pc, pa), sep = pa > 0 && r.pe < kb * pa; r.sep = sep; r.sepRatio = kb;
    if (sep) warn.push({ lvl: "warn", t: "Exit pressure is " + (r.pe / pa * 100).toFixed(0) + "% of ambient, below the Kalt–Bendall estimate (" + (kb * 100).toFixed(0) + "%) for flow separation inside the nozzle. Expect side loads and lost thrust; thrust-optimized bells like the RS-25's tolerate somewhat lower ratios once running, but separation is still a start-up hazard. A smaller area ratio avoids it at this altitude." });
    if (p.Pc > 22e6 && pr.kind === "liquid") warn.push({ lvl: "info", t: "Chamber pressure above about 22 MPa is the territory of the RD-180 (26.7 MPa) and Raptor (about 30 MPa): it takes oxidizer-rich or full-flow staged combustion." });
    // mass estimate (thrust-to-weight correlations by type)
    const TW = { pf: 35, gg: 110, sc: 80, exp: 55, ep: 50 }[p.rCycle] || 0;
    r.Fvac = pr.kind === "liquid" ? F * r.IspV / Math.max(1, r.IspMain || r.Isp) : F;
    if (pr.kind === "liquid") r.mEng = r.Fvac / (g0 * TW) + (r.feed.mBatt || 0);
    else if (pr.kind === "solid") r.mEng = r.mprop / p.massFrac - r.mprop; else r.mEng = (r.hyb.mox_tot + r.hyb.mfuel) * 0.35;
    r.etaO = NaN; r.etaP = NaN; r.etaTh = NaN;
    r.ref = { kind: "rocket", p: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p)), At: r.AtTot, eps, kF: r.Fgg ? F / (F - r.Fgg) : 1, kM: r.mgg ? r.mtot / r.mdot : 1 }; // gas-generator dump thrust and flow ride along off-design
    r.geo = { kind: "rocket", D: r.De, Dt: r.Dt, De: r.De, Dc: r.Dc, Lc: r.Lc, Ln: r.Ln, len: r.Lc + r.Ln + r.Dc, rkind: pr.kind, cyc: p.rCycle, solid: r.solid, hyb: r.hyb };
    return r;
  }
  function odRocket(ref, cond) { // fixed throat and area ratio: throttle (Pc fraction) and altitude
    const p = Object.assign({}, ref.p, { Pc: ref.p.Pc * (cond.thr || 1) }), pr = PROP[p.rkt], pa = isaP(cond.alt), x = pr.kind === "solid" ? p.AL : p.OF, kF = ref.kF || 1, kM = ref.kM || 1;
    const c = chem(p.rkt, x, p.Pc, ref.eps), q = perf(c, p, ref.eps, p.Pc, pa), md = kM * p.Pc * ref.At / (p.etaCs * c.cstar), F = kF * q.CF * p.Pc * ref.At, pe = c.pe * p.Pc;
    return { ok: F > 0, kind: "rocket", Fn: F, F, Isp: F / (md * g0), mdot: md, pe, pa, sep: pa > 0 && pe < sepRatio(p.Pc, pa) * pa, CF: q.CF, Pc: p.Pc, TSFC: md / F };
  }
  // published engines used in the validation table (vacuum and sea-level specific impulse)
  const VALID = [
    { k: "F-1", src: "NASA SP-4206", p: { rkt: "lox_rp1", OF: 2.27, Pc: 7.0e6, eps: 16, rCycle: "gg" }, IspSL: 263, IspV: 304 },
    { k: "Merlin 1D", src: "SpaceX", p: { rkt: "lox_rp1", OF: 2.36, Pc: 9.7e6, eps: 16, rCycle: "gg" }, IspSL: 282, IspV: 311 },
    { k: "RD-180", src: "NPO Energomash", p: { rkt: "lox_rp1", OF: 2.72, Pc: 26.7e6, eps: 36.9, rCycle: "sc" }, IspSL: 311, IspV: 338 },
    { k: "RS-25", src: "Aerojet Rocketdyne", p: { rkt: "lox_lh2", OF: 6.03, Pc: 20.6e6, eps: 69, rCycle: "sc" }, IspSL: 366, IspV: 452 },
    { k: "RL10B-2", src: "Aerojet Rocketdyne", p: { rkt: "lox_lh2", OF: 5.88, Pc: 4.4e6, eps: 280, rCycle: "exp" }, IspSL: null, IspV: 465.5 },
    { k: "Raptor 2", src: "SpaceX (reported)", p: { rkt: "lox_ch4", OF: 3.6, Pc: 30e6, eps: 34, rCycle: "sc" }, IspSL: 327, IspV: 347 },
    { k: "R-4D-11", src: "Aerojet", p: { rkt: "nto_mmh", OF: 1.65, Pc: 0.69e6, eps: 164, rCycle: "pf", etaCs: 0.95, etaCF: 0.97 }, IspSL: null, IspV: 312 },
    { k: "Shuttle SRB", src: "NASA", p: { rkt: "apcp", AL: 0.16, Pc: 6.3e6, eps: 7.72 }, IspSL: 242, IspV: 268 }
  ];
  function validate(base) { return VALID.map(v => { const p = Object.assign({}, base, v.p, { epsMode: "fixed", Fn: 1e6 }), pr = PROP[p.rkt], x = pr.kind === "solid" ? p.AL : p.OF;
    if (!TAB[p.rkt]) return null;
    const c = chem(p.rkt, x, p.Pc, p.eps), Vi = c.cstar * c.CFv / g0;
    const rv = solveRocket(Object.assign({}, p, { alt: 200000 })), rs = solveRocket(Object.assign({}, p, { alt: 0 }));
    return { k: v.k, src: v.src, cyc: p.rCycle, IspV: rv.Isp, IspSL: v.IspSL ? rs.Isp : null, IspVideal: Vi, refV: v.IspV, refSL: v.IspSL }; }).filter(Boolean); }
  const api = { solveRocket, odRocket, chem, optEps, PROP, isaP, validate, VALID, g0, sepRatio, perf };
  if (typeof module !== "undefined" && module.exports) module.exports = api; else root.Rocket = api;
})(this);

</script>
<script>
/* Validation case inputs shared by the studio's validation tables and tests/regress.js. */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  const LB = 0.45359237, R2K = x => x / 1.8, base = { effBasis: "isentropic", coolAuto: false, cool: 0, bleed: 0, effMech: 1, effBurner: 1, dPbypass: 0, dPcore: 0, dPabDry: 0, inletRec: 1, sizeBy: "W", hFuel: 0 };
  const pyc = [ // design-point cases run in NASA pyCycle (tabular thermo); results in ref.json, same order
    Object.assign({}, base, { config: "turbojet", alt: 0, mach: 1e-6, W: 147 * LB, T4: R2K(2370), OPR: 13.5, effHPC: .83, effHPT: .86, dPburner: .03, CvCore: .99, nozzleCore: "cd" }),
    Object.assign({}, base, { config: "turbojet", alt: 10668, mach: .8, W: 70 * LB, T4: R2K(2500), OPR: 10, effHPC: .85, effHPT: .88, dPburner: .04, CvCore: .985, nozzleCore: "conv" }),
    Object.assign({}, base, { config: "separate", alt: 10668, mach: .8, W: 300 * LB, T4: R2K(2857), FPR: 1.685, LPCPR: 1.935, OPR: 1.685 * 1.935 * 9.369, BPR: 5.105, effFan: .8948, effLPC: .9243, effHPC: .8707, effHPT: .8888, effLPT: .8996, dPburner: .054, CvCore: .9933, CvByp: .9939, nozzleCore: "conv", nozzleByp: "conv" }),
    Object.assign({}, base, { config: "separate", alt: 0, mach: .001, W: 1200 * LB, T4: R2K(3000), FPR: 1.45, LPCPR: 1.6, OPR: 1.45 * 1.6 * 16, BPR: 10, effFan: .92, effLPC: .91, effHPC: .87, effHPT: .9, effLPT: .92, dPburner: .04, CvCore: .99, CvByp: .99, nozzleCore: "conv", nozzleByp: "conv" })];
  const api = { pyc };
  if (typeof module !== "undefined" && module.exports) module.exports = api; else root.ValCases = api;
})(this);

</script>
<script>
/* Materials library: approximate handbook values from public datasheets, for preliminary sizing only.
   rho kg/m3, k W/m/K, E Pa, alpha 1/K, nu, yield [[T K, Pa],...] (0.2% proof), Tmax K (practical service limit). */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  const M = {
    narloyz: { name: "NARloy-Z (Cu-Ag-Zr)", rho: 9130, k: 316, E: 127e9, alpha: 17.2e-6, nu: 0.34, yield: [[300, 190e6], [600, 150e6], [810, 95e6], [1000, 40e6]], Tmax: 920, /* 922 K sustained hot-wall temperature used in the SSME chamber life analysis (NASA CR D5-17266-3); normal throat wall is about 813 K */ use: "liner" },
    grcop42: { name: "GRCop-42 (Cu-Cr-Nb)", rho: 8890, k: 330, E: 130e9, alpha: 17.5e-6, nu: 0.34, yield: [[300, 180e6], [600, 150e6], [900, 95e6], [1100, 50e6]], Tmax: 1000, use: "liner" },
    cucrzr: { name: "CuCrZr (C18150)", rho: 8900, k: 320, E: 128e9, alpha: 16.5e-6, nu: 0.34, yield: [[300, 350e6], [600, 250e6], [800, 100e6], [950, 40e6]], Tmax: 750, use: "liner" },
    ss347: { name: "Stainless steel 347", rho: 7960, k: 19, E: 193e9, alpha: 17.3e-6, nu: 0.3, yield: [[300, 240e6], [600, 180e6], [800, 160e6], [1100, 110e6]], Tmax: 1000, use: "liner" },
    inx750: { name: "Inconel X-750", rho: 8280, k: 17, E: 214e9, alpha: 13.5e-6, nu: 0.3, yield: [[300, 815e6], [600, 760e6], [900, 700e6], [1100, 380e6]], Tmax: 1090, use: "liner" },
    in718: { name: "Inconel 718", rho: 8190, k: 15, E: 200e9, alpha: 13.5e-6, nu: 0.29, yield: [[300, 1100e6], [600, 1020e6], [900, 950e6], [1050, 620e6]], Tmax: 980, use: "both" },
    nbc103: { name: "Niobium C-103 (coated)", rho: 8850, k: 42, E: 90e9, alpha: 8.1e-6, nu: 0.38, yield: [[300, 270e6], [1000, 190e6], [1500, 130e6], [1700, 90e6]], Tmax: 1640, use: "liner" },
    ti64: { name: "Ti-6Al-4V", rho: 4430, k: 7, E: 114e9, alpha: 9e-6, nu: 0.34, yield: [[300, 880e6], [500, 660e6], [650, 560e6]], Tmax: 620, use: "jacket" },
    al2219: { name: "Aluminum 2219-T87", rho: 2840, k: 120, E: 73e9, alpha: 22e-6, nu: 0.33, yield: [[90, 440e6], [300, 390e6], [420, 300e6]], Tmax: 420, use: "tank" }
  };
  function yieldAt(key, T) { const y = M[key].yield; if (T <= y[0][0]) return y[0][1]; for (let i = 1; i < y.length; i++) if (T <= y[i][0]) return y[i - 1][1] + (y[i][1] - y[i - 1][1]) * (T - y[i - 1][0]) / (y[i][0] - y[i - 1][0]);
    const a = y[y.length - 2], b = y[y.length - 1]; return Math.max(1e6, b[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * (T - b[0]) / (b[0] - a[0])); }
  const api = { M, yieldAt };
  if (typeof module !== "undefined" && module.exports) module.exports = api; else root.Materials = api;
})(this);

</script>
<script>
/* Liquid rocket preliminary design: chamber and nozzle contour, wall cooling, injector, turbopumps,
   structure, mass buildup and design margins. Sits on top of rocket.js (cycle and chemistry).
   Confidence codes on results: "v" validated against published data in tests, "c" calibrated or
   correlation-based, "u" unvalidated engineering estimate. */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  const NODE = typeof module !== "undefined" && module.exports;
  const Rk = NODE ? require("./rocket.js") : root.Rocket, Mt = NODE ? require("./materials.js") : root.Materials;
  const g0 = 9.80665, RU = 8314.46, SB = 5.670374e-8, PI = Math.PI, D2R = PI / 180, PSI = 0.6;
  /* Coolant and propellant properties: simple fits for supercritical channel flow (preliminary accuracy). */
  const FL = {
    lox: { name: "liquid oxygen", rho: 1141, Pv: 1.0e5 }, nto: { name: "NTO", rho: 1440, Pv: 0.96e5 },
    rp1: { name: "RP-1", Tin: 300, Pv: 2e3, TwLim: 600, lim: "coking", Tfilm: 600, Rsoot: 3.4e-4, /* carbon-deposit resistance near 6.9 MPa (Huzel and Huang), scaled with pressure^-0.5 */
      rho: T => Math.max(480, 810 - 0.72 * (T - 289)), cp: T => 2000 + 3.9 * (Math.min(T, 650) - 300), mu: T => 1.6e-3 * Math.exp(-0.0115 * (Math.min(T, 650) - 290)), k: T => Math.max(0.08, 0.14 - 1.5e-4 * (T - 290)) },
    lh2: { name: "hydrogen", Tin: 35, Pv: 1.0e5, Tfilm: 300, gas: true,
      rho: (T, P) => { const ri = P / (4124 * T); return Math.min(77, ri / (1 + 0.0062 * ri)); }, cp: () => 14300, mu: T => Math.max(4.5e-6, 8.9e-6 * Math.pow(T / 300, 0.68)), k: T => Math.max(0.085, 0.183 * Math.pow(T / 300, 0.75)) },
    ch4: { name: "methane", Tin: 120, Pv: 1.0e5, TwLim: 900, lim: "coking", Tfilm: 400, gas: true,
      rho: (T, P) => Math.max(P / (0.85 * 518.3 * T), 422 - 1.6 * (T - 112)), cp: () => 3400, mu: T => Math.max(1.3e-5, 1.1e-4 * Math.exp(-0.012 * (T - 112))), k: T => Math.max(0.05, 0.19 - 8e-4 * (T - 112)) },
    mmh: { name: "MMH", Tin: 300, Pv: 5e3, TwLim: 560, lim: "decomposition", Tfilm: 550,
      rho: () => 875, cp: () => 2930, mu: T => 8e-4 * Math.exp(-0.012 * (Math.min(T, 500) - 300)), k: () => 0.25 }
  };
  const SIDE = { lox_rp1: ["lox", "rp1"], lox_lh2: ["lox", "lh2"], lox_ch4: ["lox", "ch4"], nto_mmh: ["nto", "mmh"] };
  const aM = (M, g) => Math.pow((2 / (g + 1)) * (1 + (g - 1) / 2 * M * M), (g + 1) / (2 * (g - 1))) / M;
  function machAt(ar, g, sup) { if (ar <= 1.000001) return 1; let lo = sup ? 1 : 1e-4, hi = sup ? 80 : 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2, big = aM(m, g) > ar; if (sup ? big : !big) hi = m; else lo = m; } return (lo + hi) / 2; }

  /* ---------- chamber and nozzle contour (x = 0 at the throat) ---------- */
  function contour(p, r, g) {
    const Rt = r.Dt / 2, Re = r.De / 2, Rc = r.Dc / 2, eps = r.eps, cone = p.nozType === "cone", pct = cone ? 100 : p.bellPct;
    let thN = cone ? 15 : Math.min(45, 20.5 + 3.0 * Math.log(eps) + (80 - pct) * 0.25), thE = cone ? 15 : Math.max(2, 16 - 2.1 * Math.log(eps) + (80 - pct) * 0.3); // fits to Rao's charts
    thN *= D2R; thE *= D2R; const Lcone = (Math.sqrt(eps) - 1) * Rt / Math.tan(15 * D2R), pts = [], thC = 30 * D2R, Ru = 1.5 * Rt, Rd = 0.382 * Rt;
    let aC = thC; if (Rt + Ru * (1 - Math.cos(aC)) > Rc) aC = Math.acos(1 - (Rc - Rt) / Ru) * 0.98;
    const xa = -Ru * Math.sin(aC), ra = Rt + Ru * (1 - Math.cos(aC)), xc = xa - (Rc - ra) / Math.tan(thC);
    let Vc = 0; { let px = xc, pr2 = Rc; const add = (x, rr) => { Vc += PI * (rr * rr + pr2 * pr2) / 2 * (x - px); px = x; pr2 = rr; };
      for (let i = 1; i <= 6; i++) add(xc + (xa - xc) * i / 6, Rc + (ra - Rc) * i / 6); for (let i = 1; i <= 8; i++) { const a = -aC * (1 - i / 8); add(Ru * Math.sin(a), Rt + Ru * (1 - Math.cos(a))); } }
    const Lcyl = Math.max(0.5 * Rc, (r.Lstar * r.At - Vc) / (PI * Rc * Rc)), x0 = xc - Lcyl;
    for (let i = 0; i <= 8; i++) pts.push({ x: x0 + Lcyl * i / 8, r: Rc, z: "cyl" });
    for (let i = 1; i <= 6; i++) pts.push({ x: xc + (xa - xc) * i / 6, r: Rc + (ra - Rc) * i / 6, z: "conv" });
    for (let i = 1; i <= 8; i++) { const a = -aC * (1 - i / 8); pts.push({ x: Ru * Math.sin(a), r: Rt + Ru * (1 - Math.cos(a)), z: i === 8 ? "throat" : "conv" }); }
    for (let i = 1; i <= 6; i++) { const a = thN * i / 6; pts.push({ x: Rd * Math.sin(a), r: Rt + Rd * (1 - Math.cos(a)), z: "div" }); }
    const xN = Rd * Math.sin(thN), rN = Rt + Rd * (1 - Math.cos(thN)); let Ln;
    if (cone) { Ln = xN + (Re - rN) / Math.tan(thN); for (let i = 1; i <= 30; i++) pts.push({ x: xN + (Ln - xN) * i / 30, r: rN + (Re - rN) * i / 30, z: "div" }); }
    else { Ln = Math.max(pct / 100 * Lcone, xN + (Re - rN) / Math.tan(thN) * 1.02); const m1 = Math.tan(thN), m2 = Math.tan(thE);
      let xQ = (Re - rN + m1 * xN - m2 * Ln) / (m1 - m2), rQ = rN + m1 * (xQ - xN); if (!(xQ > xN && xQ < Ln)) { xQ = (xN + Ln) / 2; rQ = (rN + Re) / 2; }
      for (let i = 1; i <= 36; i++) { const t = i / 36, u = 1 - t; pts.push({ x: u * u * xN + 2 * u * t * xQ + t * t * Ln, r: u * u * rN + 2 * u * t * rQ + t * t * Re, z: "div" }); } }
    let s = 0; pts.forEach((q, i) => { const a = pts[Math.max(0, i - 1)], b = pts[Math.min(pts.length - 1, i + 1)];
      q.ds = 0.5 * Math.hypot(b.x - a.x, b.r - a.r) * (i === 0 || i === pts.length - 1 ? 2 : 1) * (i === 0 || i === pts.length - 1 ? 0.5 : 1); q.cos = (b.x - a.x) / Math.max(1e-9, Math.hypot(b.x - a.x, b.r - a.r));
      q.ar = Math.max(1, q.r * q.r / (Rt * Rt)); q.M = machAt(q.ar, g, q.x > 0); if (i) s += Math.hypot(q.x - pts[i - 1].x, q.r - pts[i - 1].r); q.s = s; });
    const lambda = cone ? (1 + Math.cos(15 * D2R)) / 2 : 0.5 * (1 + Math.cos(0.5 * (thE + 15 * D2R * 80 / pct)));
    return { pts, Ln, Lcyl, x0, thN, thE, lambda, Rt, Rc, Re, Ltot: Ln - x0, iT: pts.findIndex(q => q.z === "throat") };
  }
  /* ---------- gas-side heat transfer (Bartz) and wall cooling ---------- */
  function gasProps(c, p) { const g = c.gam, M = c.M, Tc = c.Tc * p.etaCs * p.etaCs, Rg = RU / M, cp = g * Rg / (g - 1), mu = 1.184e-7 * Math.sqrt(M) * Math.pow(Tc, 0.6), Pr = 4 * g / (9 * g - 5); return { g, M, Tc, Rg, cp, mu, Pr }; }
  function cooling(p, r, ct, G, fl) {
    const Rt = ct.Rt, Dt = 2 * Rt, rcv = 0.5 * (1.5 + 0.382) * Rt, g = G.g, wall = Mt.M[p.matWall], f = r.feed, regen = p.coolMode === "regen";
    const hg0 = 0.026 / Math.pow(Dt, 0.2) * Math.pow(G.mu, 0.2) * G.cp / Math.pow(G.Pr, 0.6) * Math.pow(r.Pc / r.cstar, 0.8) * Math.pow(Dt / rcv, 0.1), rec = Math.pow(G.Pr, 1 / 3);
    const hgF = (q, Tw) => { const b = 1 + (g - 1) / 2 * q.M * q.M; return hg0 * Math.pow(1 / q.ar, 0.9) / (Math.pow(0.5 * Tw / G.Tc * b + 0.5, 0.68) * Math.pow(b, 0.12)); };
    const TawF = q => { const b = (g - 1) / 2 * q.M * q.M, T = G.Tc * (1 + rec * b) / (1 + b); return T - p.filmEta * (T - (fl.Tfilm || 500)); };
    const Rs = fl.Rsoot ? fl.Rsoot * Math.sqrt(6.9e6 / r.Pc) : 0, out = { regen, st: [], hg0 };
    const iR = regen ? Math.max(ct.iT + 1, ct.pts.reduce((k, q, i) => (q.x <= 0 || q.ar <= p.epsRegen + 1e-9 ? i : k), 0)) : -1; out.iR = iR;
    if (!regen) { // radiation-cooled wall with fuel film: convection in = radiation out
      ct.pts.forEach(q => { const Taw = TawF(q); let lo = 300, hi = Taw; for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) { const T = (lo + hi) / 2; if ((Taw - T) / (1 / hgF(q, T) + Rs) > 0.85 * SB * Math.pow(T, 4)) lo = T; else hi = T; }
        const T = (lo + hi) / 2; out.st.push({ x: q.x, r: q.r, q: 0.85 * SB * Math.pow(T, 4), Twg: T, Twc: T, Tb: NaN, P: NaN, v: 0 }); });
    } else {
      const tube = p.wallType === "tube", tw = p.chT, land = tube ? 0 : p.chLand, N = Math.max(8, Math.floor(2 * PI * (Rt + tw) / (p.chW + land))), mc = r.mf * p.coolFrac; out.tube = tube;
      let Tb = fl.Tin, P = f.PdF, Q = 0; const rows = [];
      for (let i = iR; i >= 0; i--) { const q = ct.pts[i], rw = q.r + tw, pitch = 2 * PI * rw / N, w = tube ? Math.min(pitch, 2.5 * p.chW) : Math.max(0.4 * p.chW, Math.min(pitch - land, 1.5 * p.chW)), h = tube ? w : p.chH * Math.min(1.5, Math.max(1, q.r / Rt));
        const fin = tube ? 0 : Math.min(pitch - w, 2 * land), pe = tube ? pitch : w + fin, Nl = 2 * PI * rw / pe; // away from the throat, milled channels widen, deepen, then split; tubes follow the wall radius
        const A = tube ? PI / 4 * w * w : w * h, Dh = tube ? w : 2 * w * h / (w + h);
        const rho = fl.rho(Tb, P), mu = fl.mu(Tb), k = fl.k(Tb), cp = fl.cp(Tb), v = mc / (rho * Nl * A), Re = rho * v * Dh / mu, Prc = mu * cp / k;
        const hc0 = 0.023 * Math.pow(Re, 0.8) * Math.pow(Prc, 0.4) * k / Dh, m = Math.sqrt(2 * hc0 / (wall.k * Math.max(fin, 1e-4))), ef = Math.tanh(m * h) / (m * h), hc = tube ? 1.2 * hc0 : hc0 * (w + 2 * ef * h) / pe;
        const Taw = TawF(q); let lo = Tb, hi = Taw; for (let j = 0; j < 50; j++) { const T = (lo + hi) / 2; if ((Taw - T) / (1 / hgF(q, T) + Rs) > (T - Tb) / (tw / wall.k + 1 / hc)) lo = T; else hi = T; }
        const Twg = (lo + hi) / 2, qf = (Twg - Tb) / (tw / wall.k + 1 / hc), Twc = Twg - qf * tw / wall.k, dQ = qf * 2 * PI * q.r * q.ds;
        const fr = Math.pow(-1.8 * Math.log10(Math.pow(3e-6 / Dh / 3.7, 1.11) + 6.9 / Re), -2), dP = fr * q.ds / Dh * rho * v * v / 2;
        rows.push({ x: q.x, r: q.r, q: qf, Twg, Twc, Tb, P, v, w, h, pitch: pe, hg: hgF(q, Twg), hc, i }); Q += dQ; Tb += dQ / (mc * cp); P -= dP; }
      rows.reverse(); out.st = rows; Object.assign(out, { N, Tout: Tb, Pout: P, dP: f.PdF - P, Q, mc });
      for (let i = iR + 1; i < ct.pts.length; i++) { const q = ct.pts[i], Taw = TawF(q); let lo = 300, hi = Taw; for (let j = 0; j < 50; j++) { const T = (lo + hi) / 2; if ((Taw - T) / (1 / hgF(q, T) + Rs) > 0.85 * SB * Math.pow(T, 4)) lo = T; else hi = T; }
        out.st.push({ x: q.x, r: q.r, q: 0.85 * SB * Math.pow((lo + hi) / 2, 4), Twg: (lo + hi) / 2, Twc: (lo + hi) / 2, Tb: NaN, P: NaN, v: 0, ext: true, i }); }
    }
    const main = out.st.filter(s => !s.ext); out.qmax = Math.max(...main.map(s => s.q)); out.TwgMax = Math.max(...main.map(s => s.Twg)); out.TwcMax = Math.max(...main.map(s => s.Twc)); out.vmax = Math.max(...main.map(s => s.v));
    out.TextMax = out.st.some(s => s.ext) ? Math.max(...out.st.filter(s => s.ext).map(s => s.Twg)) : NaN; out.qThroat = (main.find(s => Math.abs(s.x) < 1e-9) || main[0]).q;
    return out;
  }
  /* ---------- nozzle efficiency from geometry: divergence and wall friction ---------- */
  function nozzleEta(p, r, ct, G, cool) {
    let dF = 0; const g = G.g; ct.pts.forEach((q, i) => { if (q.x <= 0) return; const b = 1 + (g - 1) / 2 * q.M * q.M, T = G.Tc / b, P = r.Pc * Math.pow(b, -g / (g - 1)), u = q.M * Math.sqrt(g * G.Rg * T);
      const Tw = cool.st[i] ? cool.st[i].Twg : 800, Ts = 0.5 * (Tw + T) + 0.22 * Math.pow(G.Pr, 1 / 3) * (b - 1) * T, rhoS = P / (G.Rg * Ts), muS = G.mu * Math.pow(Ts / G.Tc, 0.6), sx = q.s - ct.pts[ct.iT].s + ct.Rt;
      const Cf = 0.0592 * Math.pow(rhoS * u * sx / muS, -0.2); dF += Cf * 0.5 * rhoS * u * u * 2 * PI * q.r * q.ds * q.cos; });
    const Fv = r.CFv / p.etaCF * r.Pc * r.At, bl = dF / Fv; return { lambda: ct.lambda, bl, eta: ct.lambda * (1 - bl) };
  }
  /* ---------- injector ---------- */
  function injector(p, r, pr, fo, fl, cool) {
    const type = p.injType, Cd = { impinging: 0.75, coax: 0.8, pintle: 0.7 }[type] || 0.75, dP = r.feed.dInj, Rc = r.Dc / 2;
    const gasF = fl.gas && cool.regen && cool.Tout > 150, rhoF = gasF ? fl.rho(cool.Tout, r.Pc + dP) : pr.rhoF, rhoO = pr.rhoOx;
    const Ao = r.mox / (Cd * Math.sqrt(2 * rhoO * dP)), Af = r.mf / (Cd * Math.sqrt(2 * rhoF * dP)), vo = r.mox / (rhoO * Ao), vf = r.mf / (rhoF * Af);
    const n = type === "pintle" ? 1 : Math.max(1, Math.round(0.8 * PI * Rc * Rc / (p.injPitch * p.injPitch))), k = type === "impinging" ? 2 : 1;
    const dO = Math.sqrt(4 * Ao / (PI * n * k)), dF = Math.sqrt(4 * Af / (PI * n * k)), gap = type === "coax" ? Af / (n * PI * (dO * 1.3 + 6e-4)) : NaN;
    return { type, Cd, n, holes: n * k, dP, stiff: dP / r.Pc, stiffMin: dP / r.Pc * p.thrMin, Ao, Af, dO, dF, gap, vo, vf, J: rhoF * vf * vf / (rhoO * vo * vo), vr: vf / vo, rhoF, gasF, Fel: r.Fn / n };
  }
  /* ---------- turbopumps ---------- */
  const anderson = (Qm3, w, Os) => { const Q = Qm3 * 15850.3, N = w * 30 / PI, Ns = Math.max(200, 2733 * Os); return Math.min(0.85, Math.max(0.3, 0.9 * (0.94 - 0.08955 * Math.pow(Q / N, -0.21333) - 0.29 * Math.pow(Math.log10(2286 / Ns), 2)))); };
  function turbopumps(p, r, pr, fo, fl) {
    const f = r.feed, cyc = p.rCycle; if (cyc === "pf") return null;
    const mk = (name, md, rho, Pin, Pout, Pv, Ulim, key) => { const Q = md / rho, H = (Pout - Pin) / (rho * g0), NPSH = Math.max(1, (Pin - Pv) / (rho * g0)); let n = 1; while (n < 5 && Math.sqrt(g0 * H / (n * PSI)) > Ulim) n++;
      return { name, key, md, rho, Q, H, NPSH, n, Ulim, U: Math.sqrt(g0 * H / (n * PSI)), Pin, Pout, wS: p.Nss / 2733 * Math.pow(g0 * NPSH, 0.75) / Math.sqrt(Q), wNs: 1.0 * Math.pow(g0 * H / n, 0.75) / Math.sqrt(Q) }; };
    const ox = mk("Oxidizer pump", r.mox, pr.rhoOx, p.pinOx || f.Ptank, f.PdOx, fo.Pv, 450, "o"), fu = mk("Fuel pump", r.mf, pr.rhoF, p.pinF || f.Ptank, f.PdF, fl.Pv, fl === FL.lh2 ? 620 : 450, "f");
    const DNlim = fl === FL.lh2 ? 2.0e6 : 1.6e6, wDN = P => Math.pow(DNlim * PI / 30 / 1000 / Math.cbrt(16 * P / (PI * 350e6)), 1.5), dual = p.tpLayout === "dual";
    const auto = p.tpAuto !== "manual", wCap = 120000 * PI / 30; let wo, wf;
    if (dual) { wo = auto ? Math.min(ox.wS, ox.wNs, wDN(f.PpOx), wCap) : p.tpN * PI / 30; wf = auto ? Math.min(fu.wS, fu.wNs, wDN(f.PpF), wCap) : p.tpNf * PI / 30; }
    else wo = wf = auto ? Math.min(ox.wS, ox.wNs, fu.wS, fu.wNs, wDN(f.Ppump), wCap) : p.tpN * PI / 30;
