<script>
/* ---------- hover descriptions: every control gets one. Controls that already carry inline help reuse it. ---------- */
const TIPS={
icEff:"How much of the heat added by the turbocharger the intercooler removes. Cooler intake air is denser and resists knock, so power rises.",
battCmax:"The fastest the pack may be discharged, as multiples of its capacity per hour. Limits peak power for a given pack size.",
effMotor:"Share of electrical power the motor turns into shaft power. The rest becomes heat the cooling system must remove.",
effInv:"Share of battery power the inverter passes to the motor. Losses here add to cooling load and shorten endurance.",
motorSP:"Shaft power per kilogram of motor. A higher value makes the motor lighter for the same power.",
propB:"Number of propeller blades. More blades absorb more power at a given diameter but each works in the wake of the last.",
effGearP:"Share of engine or motor power that reaches the propeller through the reduction drive.",
effFan:"How close the fan comes to ideal compression. Higher efficiency means less shaft power for the same pressure rise, so lower fuel burn.",
effLPC:"Efficiency of the booster stages behind the fan. Raises or lowers the work the low-pressure turbine must supply.",
effHPC:"Efficiency of the main compressor. It handles most of the pressure rise, so it has a strong effect on fuel consumption.",
effHPT:"Share of the ideal work the turbine extracts from the hot gas. A less efficient turbine needs a larger pressure drop to drive the compressor.",
effLPT:"Efficiency of the turbine that drives the fan, booster or output shaft. Sets how much pressure is left for the nozzle or how much shaft power is delivered.",
effBurner:"Share of the fuel's heating value released in the combustor. Unburned fuel is wasted directly.",
effMech:"Share of turbine power that reaches the compressor after bearing and windage losses.",
effGear:"Share of power passed through the reduction gearbox. Losses become heat in the oil system.",
effAB:"Share of afterburner fuel that burns before the nozzle. Afterburners are short, so this is lower than the main combustor.",
dPburner:"Total pressure lost across the combustor, as a share of compressor delivery pressure. Lost pressure is lost thrust.",
dPbypass:"Total pressure lost along the bypass duct. Matters most at high bypass ratio, where most of the thrust comes from this stream.",
dPcore:"Total pressure lost between the last turbine and the nozzle.",
dPab:"Total pressure lost through the afterburner flameholders and duct, paid even when the afterburner is off.",
CvCore:"How close the nozzle jet velocity comes to ideal. Accounts for friction and non-axial flow at the exit.",
CvByp:"How close the bypass jet velocity comes to ideal.",
"geo.M2":"Mach number of the air entering the fan. Higher values shrink the engine diameter but raise losses and noise.",
"geo.htFan":"Fan hub radius divided by tip radius. A smaller hub passes more air through the same diameter.",

"geo.psiLPT":"Work per stage divided by blade speed squared. Higher loading means fewer stages but lower efficiency.",
"geo.psiHPT":"Work per stage divided by blade speed squared for the high-pressure turbine. Higher loading means fewer stages but lower efficiency.",
effBasis:"Whether the efficiencies above are per small pressure step (polytropic) or for the whole component (isentropic). Polytropic values stay comparable as pressure ratio changes.",
nozzleCore:"A converging nozzle chokes at Mach 1 and wastes pressure above that. A converging-diverging nozzle expands further and gains thrust at high pressure ratio.",
induction:"How air reaches the cylinders. A turbocharger holds sea-level manifold pressure up to its critical altitude.",
mixture:"Fuel-to-air setting. Best power runs rich for cooling and knock margin; best economy runs lean and saves fuel at reduced power.",
ncyl:"Number of cylinders. With displacement fixed, more cylinders means smaller bores and smoother running.",
chLand:"Width of the solid rib between cooling channels. Wider lands carry more structural load but leave less wall directly cooled.",
injPitch:"Distance between injector elements. Closer spacing mixes propellants in a shorter chamber but needs more, smaller orifices.",
pinOx:"Pressure at the main oxidizer pump inlet when a low-pressure boost pump feeds it. Higher inlet pressure lets the main pump spin faster without cavitating. Zero means no boost pump.",
pinF:"Pressure at the main fuel pump inlet when a boost pump feeds it. Zero means no boost pump.",
tpN:"Turbopump rotational speed. Faster shafts make smaller, lighter pumps but push suction, bearing and tip-speed limits.",
tpNf:"Fuel turbopump rotational speed when the two pumps run on separate shafts.",

SF:"Factor applied to loads before comparing them with material yield strength. Larger values give thicker, heavier walls.",

nozType:"Shape of the diverging nozzle. A bell is shorter than a cone for the same area ratio and loses less thrust to flow divergence.",
coolMode:"How the chamber wall survives. Regenerative cooling runs propellant through the wall; radiation cooling lets a refractory wall glow; film cooling adds a cool layer along the wall.",
wallType:"Whether coolant flows in channels milled into a liner or through brazed tubes. Channels suit high heat flux; tubes are lighter at low pressure.",
matWall:"Alloy facing the hot gas. Copper alloys conduct heat away fast; steels and nickel alloys tolerate higher temperature but run hotter.",
extCool:"How the nozzle beyond the regeneratively cooled section is kept intact: radiating its heat away or with its own coolant.",
injType:"How propellants meet. Impinging jets, coaxial elements and a single pintle differ in mixing quality, pressure drop and throttling range.",
tpLayout:"One shaft driving both pumps, or a separate turbine and shaft for each propellant so each runs at its own best speed.",

starN:"Number of points or slots cut into the grain port. More of them raise early burning area and flatten or reshape the thrust curve.",
scBurn:"How long the scramjet fires. An uncooled heat-sink wall keeps warming for the whole burn.",
sinkT:"Thickness of the uncooled wall. A thicker wall soaks up more heat before it overheats, at the cost of mass.",
grain:"Cross-section of the propellant port. It sets how burning area changes with time, and so the shape of the thrust curve.",
caseMat:"Material of the pressure case. Higher strength for its density means a lighter motor.",

scCool:"Whether the combustor wall is cooled by fuel for steady flight or left uncooled for a short burn.",
wallMat:"Material of the hot duct. Sets the temperature the wall may reach before it needs cooling.",

etaPump:"Share of pump shaft power that goes into raising propellant pressure. Lower efficiency demands more turbine power.",

PGROUP.forEach(([lab,ks])=>{const og=document.createElement("optgroup");og.label=lab;ks.filter(k=>PRESETS[k]).forEach(k=>{const o=document.createElement("option");o.value=k;o.textContent=PRESETS[k].name;og.appendChild(o);});ps.appendChild(og);});
const oc=document.createElement("option");oc.value="custom";oc.textContent="Custom design";ps.appendChild(oc);
ps.onchange=()=>{if(ps.value==="custom")return;P=withDefaults(PRESETS[ps.value].p);presetName=PRESETS[ps.value].name;update("refit");};

/* ---------- metrics ---------- */
const MET=[
 {k:"Fn",label:p=>hasProp(p)?(p.mach<0.02?"Static thrust":"Thrust"):"Net thrust",u:"force",get:r=>r.Fn,better:0,show:p=>p.config!=="turboshaft"},
 {k:"Pshaft",label:p=>p.config==="piston"?"Brake power":"Shaft power",u:"kw",get:r=>r.Pshaft,better:0,show:p=>!(fam(p)==="jet")},
 {k:"TSFC",label:"TSFC",u:"tsfc",get:r=>r.TSFC,better:-1,show:p=>fam(p)==="jet"||(p.config==="turboprop"&&p.mach>0.05)},
 {k:"PSFC",label:p=>p.config==="piston"?"BSFC":"Power-specific fuel use",u:"psfc",get:r=>r.PSFC,better:-1,show:p=>fam(p)==="gtshaft"||p.config==="piston"},
 {k:"Fs",label:"Specific thrust",u:"fs",get:r=>r.Fs,better:1,show:p=>fam(p)==="jet"},
 {k:"Psp",label:"Specific power",u:"sp",get:r=>r.Psp,better:1,show:p=>fam(p)==="gtshaft"},
 {k:"W",label:"Inlet airflow",u:"flow",get:r=>r.W,better:-1,show:isGT},
 {k:"D",label:p=>isFan(p)?"Fan diameter":"Inlet diameter",u:"len",get:r=>r.geo?r.geo.D:NaN,better:-1,show:isGT},
 {k:"disp",label:"Displacement",u:"vol",get:r=>r.disp,better:-1,show:p=>p.config==="piston"},
 {k:"fuel",label:"Fuel flow",u:"fuel",get:r=>r.fuel,better:-1,show:p=>p.config!=="electric"},
 {k:"endur",label:"Endurance at this power",u:"time",get:r=>r.endur,better:1,show:p=>p.config==="electric"},
 {k:"mBatt",label:"Battery mass",u:"mass",get:r=>r.mBatt,better:-1,show:p=>p.config==="electric"},
 {k:"etaTh",label:p=>p.config==="electric"?"Motor and inverter efficiency":"Thermal efficiency",u:"pct",get:r=>r.etaTh,better:1},
 {k:"etaP",label:p=>hasProp(p)?"Propeller efficiency":"Propulsive efficiency",u:"pct",get:r=>r.etaP,better:1,show:p=>p.config!=="turboshaft"},
 {k:"etaO",label:"Overall efficiency",u:"pct",get:r=>r.etaO,better:1,show:p=>p.config!=="turboshaft"}
];
extPatchMET();
const mEl=$("metrics");MET.forEach(m=>{const d=document.createElement("div");d.className="m";d.innerHTML='<div class="k"></div><div class="v"></div><div class="d"></div>';mEl.appendChild(d);m.el=d;});
function renderMetrics(r){
  MET.forEach(m=>{m.el.hidden=m.show?!m.show(P):false;if(m.el.hidden)return;const v=m.get(r);m.el.querySelector(".k").textContent=typeof m.label==="function"?m.label(P):m.label;
    m.el.querySelector(".v").innerHTML=(isFinite(v)?fmt(v,m.u):(m.k.startsWith("eta")?"n/a":"–"))+'<small>'+(isFinite(v)?unitTxt(m.u):"")+'</small>';
    const dd=m.el.querySelector(".d");dd.className="d";dd.textContent=(m.k==="etaP"||m.k==="etaO")&&!isFinite(v)?"zero at standstill":"";
    if(BASE&&BASE.r.ok&&isFinite(v)){const b=m.get(BASE.r);if(isFinite(b)&&b!==0){const pc=(v-b)/Math.abs(b)*100;if(Math.abs(pc)<0.05)dd.textContent="= baseline";else{dd.textContent=(pc>0?"+":"")+pc.toFixed(1)+"% vs baseline";if(m.better)dd.classList.add(pc*m.better>0?"good":"bad");}}}
  });
}
const ch=(c,t,s)=>'<div class="chip '+c+'"><b>'+t+'</b><span>'+s+'</span></div>', rpmT=v=>num(v,0)+" rpm";
function propChip(r){const pr=r.prop;if(!pr)return "";return ch("c-g","Propeller · "+P.propB+" blades","Ø "+fmt(P.propD,"len")+" "+unitTxt("len")+" · "+rpmT(P.propRPM)+" · pitch "+(pr.theta75*180/PI).toFixed(1)+"° · tip M "+pr.Mtip.toFixed(2));}
function renderSpools(r){
  const g=r.geo,el=$("spools");let h="";
  if(r.kind==="piston"){h+=ch("c-hp","Engine · "+P.ncyl+" cylinders",fmt(r.disp,"vol")+" "+unitTxt("vol")+" · bore "+fmt(r.geom.bore,"len",US?2:3)+" "+unitTxt("len")+" · "+rpmT(P.rpm));h+=ch("c-b","Cycle","BMEP "+fmt(r.cyc.BMEP,"pres")+" "+unitTxt("pres")+" · MAP "+fmt(r.cyc.MAP,"map")+" "+unitTxt("map")+" · piston speed "+r.Up.toFixed(1)+" m/s");h+=propChip(r);el.innerHTML=h;return;}
  if(r.kind==="electric"){h+=ch("c-ab","Battery · "+num(P.battE,1)+" kWh",fmt(r.mBatt,"mass")+" "+unitTxt("mass")+" · "+r.Crate.toFixed(2)+"C at full power");h+=ch("c-g","Motor · "+fmt(r.Pshaft,"kw")+" "+unitTxt("kw"),fmt(r.mMotor,"mass")+" "+unitTxt("mass")+" · draws "+fmt(r.Pel,"kw")+" "+unitTxt("kw"));h+=propChip(r);el.innerHTML=h;return;}
  if(!g){el.innerHTML="";return;}
  if(g.fan)h+=ch("c-fan","Fan · "+g.fan.stages+(g.fan.stages>1?" stages":" stage"),"PR "+r.FPR.toFixed(2)+" · tip "+fmt(g.fan.Utip,"vel")+" "+unitTxt("vel")+" · "+rpmT(g.fan.rpm));
  if(P.geared&&P.config==="separate")h+=ch("c-g","Gearbox "+P.gearRatio.toFixed(2)+":1","LP turbine at "+rpmT(g.lpt?g.lpt.rpm:0));
  if(g.lpc)h+=ch("c-fan","Booster · "+g.lpc.stages+" st","PR "+r.LPCPR.toFixed(2));
  h+=ch("c-hp",(g.fan?"HPC":"Compressor")+" · "+g.hpc.stages+" st","PR "+r.HPCPR.toFixed(2)+" · "+rpmT(g.hpc.rpm));
  h+=ch("c-b","Combustor","T4 "+fmt(P.T4,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp")+" · cooling "+(r.eps*100).toFixed(0)+"%");
  h+=ch("c-t",(g.fan?"HPT":g.shaft?"GG turbine":"Turbine")+" · "+g.hpt.stages+" st","PR "+r.PRt.hpt.toFixed(2));
  if(g.shaft)h+=ch("c-g","Power turbine · "+g.pt.stages+" st","PR "+r.PRt.pt.toFixed(2)+" · "+rpmT(g.pt.rpm));
  else if(g.lpt)h+=ch("c-t","LPT · "+g.lpt.stages+" st","PR "+r.PRt.lpt.toFixed(2)+" · "+rpmT(g.lpt.rpm));
  if(g.gearRatio)h+=ch("c-g","Reduction gearbox "+g.gearRatio.toFixed(1)+":1","to "+rpmT(P.propRPM));
  if(r.ab)h+=ch("c-ab","Afterburner","T7 "+fmt(r.S["7"].Tt,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp"));
  h+=propChip(r);
  el.innerHTML=h;
}
function energyParts(r){
  if(r.energy)return{tot:r.Ptot,parts:r.energy,label:r.kind==="electric"?"Battery power":"Fuel power"};
  const Pf=r.fuel*r.LHV,use=r.Fn>0?r.Fn*r.V0:0;
  if(r.kind==="shaft"){const keJ=Math.max(0,0.5*r.nozCore.W*Math.pow(r.nozCore.Fg/r.nozCore.W,2)-0.5*r.W*r.V0*r.V0);
    if(P.config==="turboshaft")return{tot:Pf,label:"Fuel power",parts:[["Shaft power delivered",r.Pshaft,"#1F7A4D"],["Residual jet energy",keJ,"#5DCAA5"],["Heat out the exhaust and mechanical losses",Math.max(0,Pf-r.Pshaft-keJ),"#EF9F27"]]};
    return{tot:Pf,label:"Fuel power",parts:[["Useful thrust power",use,"#1F7A4D"],["Propeller and wake losses",Math.max(0,r.Pshaft+keJ-use),"#5DCAA5"],["Heat out the exhaust and mechanical losses",Math.max(0,Pf-r.Pshaft-keJ),"#EF9F27"]]};}
  const ke=r.etaTh*Pf;return{tot:Pf,label:"Fuel power",parts:[["Useful thrust power",use,"#1F7A4D"],["Kinetic energy left in the wake",Math.max(0,ke-use),"#5DCAA5"],["Heat out the exhaust",Math.max(0,Pf-ke),"#EF9F27"]]};
}
function renderEnergy(r){
  const e=energyParts(r),Pf=e.tot;
  $("ebar").innerHTML=e.parts.map(q=>'<i style="width:'+(Math.max(0,q[1])/Pf*100)+'%;background:'+q[2]+'" title="'+q[0]+'"></i>').join("");
  $("elegend").innerHTML='<span>'+e.label+" "+fmt(Pf,"kw")+" "+unitTxt("kw")+'</span>'+e.parts.map(q=>'<span><i style="background:'+q[2]+'"></i>'+q[0]+" "+(Math.max(0,q[1])/Pf*100).toFixed(0)+"%</span>").join("");
  $("energyK").textContent=r.kind==="electric"?"Where the battery's energy goes":"Where the fuel's energy goes";
}
function renderNotes(r){
  const el=$("notes"),out=[],ins=t=>'<div class="note ins">'+t+"</div>";
  if(!r.ok){out.push('<div class="note bad"><b>No valid design.</b> '+((r.warn||[]).map(w=>w.t).join(" ")||"Check the inputs.")+'</div>');el.innerHTML=out.join("");return;}
  const V0=r.V0;
  if(r.kind==="piston"){out.push(ins("Each cylinder averages a brake mean effective pressure of <b>"+fmt(r.cyc.BMEP,"pres")+" "+unitTxt("pres")+"</b>. Indicated efficiency is "+(r.cyc.etaI*100).toFixed(0)+"% (the ideal Otto cycle at this compression ratio would give "+(r.cyc.etaOtto*100).toFixed(0)+"%), and friction takes "+(r.cyc.Pfr/r.cyc.Pind*100).toFixed(0)+"% of the indicated power."));
    out.push(ins(P.mixture==="power"?"Running rich for best power: about 14% of the fuel leaves unburned, buying power and detonation margin at the cost of BSFC.":"Running lean of peak: every bit of fuel burns, so BSFC is lower, but power drops and the cylinders need careful monitoring."));}
  else if(r.kind==="electric"){out.push(ins("At full power the pack discharges at <b>"+r.Crate.toFixed(2)+"C</b>, giving "+fmt(r.endur,"time")+" min to the usable limit. The battery weighs "+fmt(r.mBatt,"mass")+" "+unitTxt("mass")+": the same energy in Jet-A would weigh about "+fmt(P.battE*1000/12000,"mass")+" "+unitTxt("mass")+", though a combustion engine would waste about two-thirds of it."));}
  else if(r.kind==="shaft"){out.push(ins("Each kilogram per second of air delivers <b>"+fmt(r.Psp,"sp")+" "+unitTxt("sp")+"</b> of shaft power. The power turbine expands the gas to an exhaust pressure ratio of "+P.NPR.toFixed(2)+", leaving "+fmt(Math.max(0,r.Fjet),"force")+" "+unitTxt("force")+" of residual jet thrust."));}
  else{const nc=r.nozCore,nb=r.nozByp;
    if(nb){const share=r.Fbyp/(r.Fbyp+r.Fcore)*100;out.push(ins("The bypass stream makes <b>"+share.toFixed(0)+"%</b> of the gross thrust. Its jet leaves at "+fmt(nb.V,"vel")+" "+unitTxt("vel")+" and the core jet at "+fmt(nc.V,"vel")+" "+unitTxt("vel")+(V0>1?", against a flight speed of "+fmt(V0,"vel")+" "+unitTxt("vel")+".":".")));}
    else out.push(ins("The jet leaves at "+fmt(nc.V,"vel")+" "+unitTxt("vel")+(V0>1?", "+(nc.V/V0).toFixed(1)+"× flight speed. ":". ")+(r.ab?"The afterburner adds "+((r.fuelAB/r.fuel)*100).toFixed(0)+"% of the fuel flow.":"")));}
  if(r.prop){const pr=r.prop;out.push(ins(V0>1?"The propeller converts <b>"+(pr.eta*100).toFixed(0)+"%</b> of its shaft power into thrust power at a blade pitch of "+(pr.theta75*180/PI).toFixed(1)+"° (advance ratio J = "+pr.J.toFixed(2)+"). A larger, slower propeller would move more air more gently.":"At standstill the propeller makes "+fmt(pr.T,"force")+" "+unitTxt("force")+" (figure of merit "+pr.FM.toFixed(2)+"; 1.0 would be an ideal actuator disk). Static thrust is the hardest condition for a propeller."));}
  else if(fam(P)==="jet"&&V0>1)out.push(ins("Propulsive efficiency is <b>"+(r.etaP*100).toFixed(0)+"%</b>: "+(r.etaP>0.75?"the jets are only modestly faster than the aircraft, so little energy is left behind in the wake.":"the jets are much faster than the aircraft, so a large share of the energy is left spinning in the wake.")+" Thermal efficiency is "+(r.etaTh*100).toFixed(0)+"%, set mostly by OPR and T4."));
  if(BASE&&BASE.r.ok&&BASE.p.config===P.config){const b=BASE.r,k=fam(P)==="jet"?"TSFC":(P.config==="electric"?"endur":"PSFC"),lab={TSFC:"TSFC",PSFC:"fuel use per unit power",endur:"endurance"}[k];const dt=(r[k]-b[k])/b[k]*100;if(isFinite(dt)&&Math.abs(dt)>=0.05)out.push(ins("Compared with the baseline, "+lab+" is <b>"+(dt>0?"+":"")+dt.toFixed(1)+"%</b>."));}
  (r.warn||[]).forEach(w=>out.push('<div class="note '+w.lvl+'">'+w.t+"</div>"));
  el.innerHTML=out.join("");
}

/* ---------- tables ---------- */
const STN={"0":"Freestream","2":"Fan / compressor face","13":"Fan exit, bypass","21":"Fan exit, core","25":"HPC inlet","3":"Compressor exit","4":"Combustor exit","41":"HPT rotor inlet (after vane cooling)","44":"HPT exit","45":"LPT inlet (after rotor cooling)","5":"Turbine exit","16":"Bypass duct exit","6":"Mixer / tailpipe","7":"Afterburner exit","8":"Nozzle inlet"};
const STN_SH={"0":"Freestream","2":"Compressor face","3":"Compressor exit","4":"Combustor exit","41":"Gas-generator turbine inlet (after vane cooling)","44":"Gas-generator turbine exit","45":"Power turbine inlet","5":"Power turbine exit","8":"Exhaust nozzle inlet"};
const ORDER=["0","2","13","16","21","25","3","4","41","44","45","5","6","7","8"];
function rows2(t,rows){$(t).innerHTML="<tbody>"+rows.map(r=>"<tr><td class='l'>"+r[0]+"</td><td>"+r[1]+"</td></tr>").join("")+"</tbody>";}
function renderTables(r){
  $("hSt").textContent=isGT(P)?"Station data":(P.config==="piston"?"Engine data":"Powertrain data");
  $("stSub").textContent=isGT(P)?"Total conditions at each SAE ARP755 station, plus component work and nozzle performance. Mass flows are for the sized engine.":(P.config==="piston"?"Cylinder geometry, breathing and the cycle numbers behind the brake power.":"Energy storage and the power chain from battery to propeller.");
  const pr=r.prop,propRows=pr?[["Propeller thrust",fmt(pr.T,"force")+" "+unitTxt("force")],["Blade pitch at 75% radius",(pr.theta75*180/PI).toFixed(1)+"°"],["Advance ratio J",pr.J.toFixed(3)],["Power coefficient CP",pr.CP.toFixed(4)],["Thrust coefficient CT",pr.CT.toFixed(4)],["Propeller efficiency",isFinite(pr.eta)?(pr.eta*100).toFixed(1)+"%":"n/a (static)"],["Helical tip Mach",pr.Mtip.toFixed(3)]]:[];
  if(r.kind==="piston"){const c=r.cyc;
    rows2("stTable",[["Displacement",fmt(r.disp,"vol")+" "+unitTxt("vol")],["Bore × stroke",fmt(r.geom.bore,"len",US?2:3)+" × "+fmt(r.geom.stroke,"len",US?2:3)+" "+unitTxt("len")],["Compression ratio",P.CR.toFixed(1)+":1"],["Manifold pressure",fmt(c.MAP,"map")+" "+unitTxt("map")],["Charge temperature",fmt(c.Tm,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp")],["Air flow",fmt(c.mAir,"flow",3)+" "+unitTxt("flow")],["Fuel-air ratio",c.FAR.toFixed(4)],["Mean piston speed",r.Up.toFixed(1)+" m/s"]]);
    rows2("compTable",[["Indicated mean effective pressure",fmt(c.IMEP,"pres")+" "+unitTxt("pres")],["Friction mean effective pressure",fmt(c.Pfr/(r.disp*P.rpm/120),"pres")+" "+unitTxt("pres")],["Brake mean effective pressure",fmt(c.BMEP,"pres")+" "+unitTxt("pres")],["Ideal Otto efficiency",(c.etaOtto*100).toFixed(1)+"%"],["Indicated efficiency",(c.etaI*100).toFixed(1)+"%"],["Brake thermal efficiency",(r.etaTh*100).toFixed(1)+"%"],["BSFC",fmt(r.PSFC,"psfc")+" "+unitTxt("psfc")]]);
    rows2("nozTable",propRows);return;}
  if(r.kind==="electric"){
    rows2("stTable",[["Pack energy",num(P.battE,1)+" kWh"],["Usable energy",num(P.battE*P.battUse,1)+" kWh"],["Pack mass",fmt(r.mBatt,"mass")+" "+unitTxt("mass")],["Discharge at full power",r.Crate.toFixed(2)+"C (limit "+P.battCmax.toFixed(1)+"C)"],["Endurance at full power",fmt(r.endur,"time")+" min"],["Range at design speed",r.V0>1?fmt(r.range,"dist")+" "+unitTxt("dist"):"n/a"]]);
    rows2("compTable",[["Battery output",fmt(r.Pel,"kw")+" "+unitTxt("kw")],["Inverter loss",fmt(r.Pel*(1-P.effInv),"kw")+" "+unitTxt("kw")],["Motor loss",fmt(r.Pel*P.effInv*(1-P.effMotor),"kw")+" "+unitTxt("kw")],["Shaft power",fmt(r.Pshaft,"kw")+" "+unitTxt("kw")],["Motor mass",fmt(r.mMotor,"mass")+" "+unitTxt("mass")]]);
    rows2("nozTable",propRows);return;}
  const S=r.S,shaftK=r.kind==="shaft",names=shaftK?STN_SH:STN;let h="<thead><tr><th>Station</th><th>Flow "+unitTxt("flow")+"</th><th>Tt "+unitTxt("temp")+"</th><th>Pt "+unitTxt("pres")+"</th><th>FAR</th></tr></thead><tbody>";
  ORDER.forEach(k=>{if(!S[k]||!names[k])return;if(k==="8"&&P.config!=="separate"&&!shaftK)return;if(k==="25"&&shaftK)return;if((k==="44"||k==="41")&&r.eps===0)return;const s=S[k];h+="<tr><td class='l'><b>"+k+"</b> "+(k==="3"&&!isFan(P)?"Compressor exit":names[k])+"</td><td>"+fmt(s.W,"flow")+"</td><td>"+fmt(s.Tt,"temp")+"</td><td>"+fmt(s.Pt,"pres")+"</td><td>"+s.far.toFixed(4)+"</td></tr>";});
  $("stTable").innerHTML=h+"</tbody>";
  const pw=r.pw;let c="<thead><tr><th>Component</th><th>Pressure ratio</th><th>Power "+unitTxt("power")+"</th></tr></thead><tbody>";
  if(isFan(P))c+="<tr><td>Fan</td><td>"+r.FPR.toFixed(3)+"</td><td>"+fmt(pw.fan,"power")+"</td></tr>";
  if(isFan(P)&&r.LPCPR>1.0001)c+="<tr><td>Booster (LPC)</td><td>"+r.LPCPR.toFixed(3)+"</td><td>"+fmt(pw.lpc,"power")+"</td></tr>";
  c+="<tr><td>"+(isFan(P)?"HPC":"Compressor")+"</td><td>"+r.HPCPR.toFixed(3)+"</td><td>"+fmt(pw.hpc,"power")+"</td></tr>";
  c+="<tr><td>"+(isFan(P)?"HPT":shaftK?"Gas-generator turbine":"Turbine")+"</td><td>"+r.PRt.hpt.toFixed(3)+"</td><td>"+fmt(pw.hpt,"power")+"</td></tr>";
  if(shaftK)c+="<tr><td>Power turbine</td><td>"+r.PRt.pt.toFixed(3)+"</td><td>"+fmt(pw.pt,"power")+"</td></tr>";
  else if(pw.lpt>0)c+="<tr><td>LPT</td><td>"+r.PRt.lpt.toFixed(3)+"</td><td>"+fmt(pw.lpt,"power")+"</td></tr>";
  c+="<tr><td>Combustor fuel-air ratio</td><td colspan=2>"+r.far4.toFixed(4)+" ("+(r.far4/r.FAR_STOICH*100).toFixed(0)+"% of stoichiometric)</td></tr>";
  c+="<tr><td>Inlet recovery</td><td colspan=2>"+(r.pin*100).toFixed(2)+"%</td></tr>";
  $("compTable").innerHTML=c+"</tbody>";
  if(pr){rows2("nozTable",[["Exhaust jet speed",fmt(r.nozCore.V,"vel")+" "+unitTxt("vel")],["Residual jet thrust",fmt(r.Fjet,"force")+" "+unitTxt("force")]].concat(propRows));return;}
  let n="<thead><tr><th>Nozzle</th><th>Jet speed "+unitTxt("vel")+"</th><th>Throat area "+unitTxt("area")+"</th><th>NPR</th><th>Gross thrust "+unitTxt("force")+"</th></tr></thead><tbody>";
  const row=(nm,z)=>"<tr><td>"+nm+(z.choked?" (choked)":"")+"</td><td>"+fmt(z.V,"vel")+"</td><td>"+fmt(z.A8,"area")+"</td><td>"+z.NPR.toFixed(2)+"</td><td>"+fmt(z.Fg,"force")+"</td></tr>";
  n+=row(r.nozByp?"Core":"Exhaust",r.nozCore);if(r.nozByp)n+=row("Bypass",r.nozByp);
  n+="<tr><td>Ram drag</td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td>−"+fmt(r.Fram,"force")+"</td></tr>";
  $("nozTable").innerHTML=n+"</tbody>";
}

/* ---------- charts ---------- */
const css=n=>getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(n).trim();
Chart.defaults.font.family='"IBM Plex Sans",system-ui,sans-serif';Chart.defaults.color=css("--muted");
function chartBase(){return{responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,animation:false,plugins:{legend:{labels:{color:css("--muted"),boxWidth:12,font:{family:"IBM Plex Sans",size:12}}},tooltip:{}},scales:{}};}
function axis(title,pos){return{type:"linear",position:pos||"left",title:{display:true,text:title,color:css("--muted"),font:{family:"IBM Plex Mono",size:11}},ticks:{color:css("--muted"),font:{family:"IBM Plex Mono",size:11}},grid:{color:css("--line")}};}
let tsC=null,trC=null,cpC=null;
const labelPlugin={id:"lbl",afterDatasetsDraw(c){const ctx=c.ctx;ctx.save();ctx.font="500 11px IBM Plex Mono";ctx.fillStyle=css("--ink");c.data.datasets.forEach((ds,i)=>{if(!ds.lbls)return;const meta=c.getDatasetMeta(i);meta.data.forEach((pt,j)=>{if(ds.lbls[j])ctx.fillText(ds.lbls[j],pt.x+5,pt.y-5);});});ctx.restore();}};
function renderTS(r){
  const card=$("tsCard");card.hidden=r.kind==="electric";if(card.hidden)return;
  let ds,o=chartBase();
  if(r.kind==="piston"){ // idealized Otto cycle scaled to this engine
    $("hTs").textContent="Pressure–volume diagram";$("tsSub").textContent="An idealized Otto cycle for one cylinder at this compression ratio and manifold pressure: compression, constant-volume heat release, expansion, and exhaust. The enclosed area is the indicated work per cycle; the real engine's rounded corners and friction take some of it away.";
    const c=r.cyc,CR=P.CR,Vd=r.geom.vcyl,Vc=Vd/(CR-1),V1=Vc+Vd,n=1.3,P1=c.MAP,Wi=c.IMEP*Vd;
    const k=1-Math.pow(CR,1-n),P2=P1*Math.pow(CR,n),P3=P2+Wi*(n-1)/(Vc*k);
    const comp=[],exp=[];for(let i=0;i<=24;i++){const V=V1-(V1-Vc)*i/24;comp.push({x:cv(V,"volc"),y:cv(P1*Math.pow(V1/V,n),"pres")});}
    for(let i=0;i<=24;i++){const V=Vc+(V1-Vc)*i/24;exp.push({x:cv(V,"volc"),y:cv(P3*Math.pow(Vc/V,n),"pres")});}
    ds=[{label:"Compression",data:comp,borderColor:"#378ADD",backgroundColor:"#378ADD",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:2},
      {label:"Combustion and expansion",data:[{x:cv(Vc,"volc"),y:cv(P2,"pres")}].concat(exp),borderColor:"#D85A30",backgroundColor:"#D85A30",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:2},
      {label:"Exhaust",data:[exp[exp.length-1],comp[0]],borderColor:"#888780",backgroundColor:"#888780",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:1.5,borderDash:[5,4]}];
    o.scales={x:axis("Cylinder volume, "+unitTxt("volc")),y:axis("Pressure, "+unitTxt("pres"))};
  }else{
    $("hTs").textContent="Temperature–entropy diagram";$("tsSub").textContent="The cycle traced through each station. Compression climbs steeply, the combustor adds heat at nearly constant pressure, the turbines step back down, and the nozzle converts what is left into jet speed. The area enclosed is the useful work.";
    const S=r.S,s0=S["0"].s,T=k=>cv(S[k].Tt,"temp"),X=k=>cv(S[k].s-s0,"ent");
    const core=["0","2"].concat(isFan(P)?["21"]:[]).concat(r.kind==="shaft"?[]:["25"]).concat(["3","4"]).concat(S["41"]&&r.eps>0?["41","44","45"]:["44"]).concat(["5"]).concat(S["6"]&&P.config==="mixed"?["6"]:[]).concat(S["7"]&&r.ab?["7"]:[]);
    const ks=core.filter(k=>S[k]);
    ds=[{label:"Core stream",data:ks.map(k=>({x:X(k),y:T(k)})),lbls:ks,borderColor:"#D85A30",backgroundColor:"#D85A30",showLine:true,pointRadius:3,borderWidth:2}];
    if(S["13"])ds.push({label:"Bypass stream",data:["0","2","13","16"].filter(k=>S[k]).map(k=>({x:X(k),y:T(k)})),lbls:["","","13","16"],borderColor:"#378ADD",backgroundColor:"#378ADD",showLine:true,pointRadius:3,borderWidth:2});
    o.scales={x:axis("Entropy rise from freestream, "+unitTxt("ent")),y:axis("Total temperature, "+unitTxt("temp"))};
  }
  if(!tsC)tsC=new Chart($("tsChart"),{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o,plugins:[labelPlugin]});else{tsC.data.datasets=ds;tsC.options=o;tsC.update();}
}
const TRX=[
 {id:"BPR",show:isFan},{id:"FPR",show:sep},{id:"LPCPR",show:isFan},{id:"OPR",show:isGT},{id:"T4",show:isGT},{id:"NPR",show:p=>fam(p)==="gtshaft"},{id:"T7",show:hasAB},
 {id:"CR",show:p=>p.config==="piston"},{id:"rpm",show:p=>p.config==="piston"},{id:"ratedMAP",show:p=>p.config==="piston"&&p.induction==="turbo"},{id:"critAlt",show:p=>p.config==="piston"&&p.induction==="turbo"},
 {id:"battE",show:p=>p.config==="electric"},{id:"battSE",show:p=>p.config==="electric"},{id:"effMotor",show:p=>p.config==="electric"},
 {id:"propD",show:hasProp},{id:"propRPM",show:hasProp},{id:"propB",show:hasProp},{id:"propAF",show:hasProp},{id:"propJd",show:hasProp},
 {id:"mach"},{id:"alt"},{id:"Fn",show:p=>fam(p)==="jet"},{id:"Pshaft",show:p=>fam(p)!=="jet"},{id:"gearRatio",show:p=>p.geared&&sep(p)},
 {id:"effFan",show:isFan},{id:"effHPC",show:isGT},{id:"effHPT",show:isGT},{id:"effLPT",show:hasLPT},{id:"dPburner",show:isGT},{id:"bleed",show:isGT}
];
const TRY=[
 {k:"TSFC",label:"TSFC",u:"tsfc",get:r=>r.TSFC,show:p=>p.config!=="turboshaft"&&p.config!=="electric"},{k:"PSFC",label:"Power-specific fuel use",u:"psfc",get:r=>r.PSFC,show:p=>fam(p)==="gtshaft"||p.config==="piston"},
 {k:"Fn",label:"Thrust",u:"force",get:r=>r.Fn,show:p=>p.config!=="turboshaft"},{k:"Pshaft",label:"Shaft power",u:"kw",get:r=>r.Pshaft,show:p=>fam(p)!=="jet"},
 {k:"Fs",label:"Specific thrust",u:"fs",get:r=>r.Fs,show:p=>fam(p)==="jet"},{k:"Psp",label:"Specific power",u:"sp",get:r=>r.Psp,show:p=>fam(p)==="gtshaft"},
 {k:"D",label:"Fan / inlet diameter",u:"len",get:r=>r.geo?r.geo.D:NaN,show:isGT},{k:"W",label:"Airflow",u:"flow",get:r=>r.W,show:p=>p.config!=="electric"},{k:"fuel",label:"Fuel flow",u:"fuel",get:r=>r.fuel,show:p=>p.config!=="electric"},
 {k:"disp",label:"Displacement",u:"vol",get:r=>r.disp,show:p=>p.config==="piston"},{k:"endur",label:"Endurance",u:"time",get:r=>r.endur,show:p=>p.config==="electric"},{k:"mBatt",label:"Battery mass",u:"mass",get:r=>r.mBatt,show:p=>p.config==="electric"},
 {k:"etaTh",label:"Thermal efficiency",u:"pct",get:r=>r.etaTh},{k:"etaP",label:p=>hasProp(p)?"Propeller efficiency":"Propulsive efficiency",u:"pct",get:r=>r.etaP,show:p=>p.config!=="turboshaft"},{k:"etaO",label:"Overall efficiency",u:"pct",get:r=>r.etaO,show:p=>p.config!=="turboshaft"},
 {k:"Mtip",label:"Propeller tip Mach",u:"none",get:r=>r.prop?r.prop.Mtip:NaN,show:hasProp},{k:"T3",label:"Compressor exit T3",u:"temp",get:r=>r.S&&r.S["3"]?r.S["3"].Tt:NaN,show:isGT},
 {k:"vr",label:"Bypass / core jet speed ratio",u:"none",get:r=>r.nozByp?r.nozByp.V/r.nozCore.V:NaN,show:sep},{k:"len",label:"Engine length",u:"len",get:r=>r.geo?r.geo.len:NaN,show:isGT},
 {k:"lpt",label:p=>fam(p)==="gtshaft"?"Power turbine stages":"LPT stages",u:"n0",get:r=>r.geo&&r.geo.lpt?r.geo.lpt.stages:NaN,show:hasLPT},{k:"hpc",label:"Compressor stages",u:"n0",get:r=>r.geo&&r.geo.hpc?r.geo.hpc.stages:NaN,show:isGT},{k:"utip",label:"Fan tip speed",u:"vel",get:r=>r.geo&&r.geo.fan?r.geo.fan.Utip:NaN,show:isFan}
];
const TRDEF={jet:["BPR","TSFC","D"],gtshaft:["OPR","PSFC","Psp"],piston:["CR","PSFC","disp"],electric:["battE","endur","mBatt"]};
extPatchTR();
const defOf=id=>PDEF.find(d=>d.id===id);
const labOf=x=>typeof x.label==="function"?x.label(P):x.label;
let trSel={x:"BPR",y1:"TSFC",y2:"D",min:null,max:null,fam:"jet"};
function fillTradeSelects(){
  const f=P.config==="turboprop"?"turboprop":fam(P);if(trSel.fam!==f){const d=f==="turboprop"?["propD","Fn","etaP"]:TRDEF[f];trSel={x:d[0],y1:d[1],y2:d[2],min:null,max:null,fam:f};}
  const xs=$("trX");xs.innerHTML="";TRX.filter(t=>!t.show||t.show(P)).forEach(t=>{const d=defOf(t.id);const o=document.createElement("option");o.value=t.id;o.textContent=labOf(d)+(unitTxt(d.u)?" ("+unitTxt(d.u)+")":"");xs.appendChild(o);});
  if(![...xs.options].some(o=>o.value===trSel.x)){trSel.x=xs.options[0].value;trSel.min=trSel.max=null;}xs.value=trSel.x;
  const ys=TRY.filter(t=>!t.show||t.show(P));
  ["trY1","trY2"].forEach((id,i)=>{const s=$(id);s.innerHTML="";ys.forEach(t=>{const o=document.createElement("option");o.value=t.k;o.textContent=labOf(t);s.appendChild(o);});const want=i?trSel.y2:trSel.y1;s.value=ys.some(t=>t.k===want)?want:ys[i].k;if(i)trSel.y2=s.value;else trSel.y1=s.value;});
  const d=defOf(trSel.x),[lo,hi]=rangeOf(d);if(trSel.min===null){const v=getP(P,trSel.x);trSel.min=Math.max(lo,v-(hi-lo)*0.3);trSel.max=Math.min(hi,v+(hi-lo)*0.3);if(d.step>=1){trSel.min=Math.round(trSel.min);trSel.max=Math.round(trSel.max);}}
  $("trMin").value=+cv(trSel.min,d.u).toPrecision(4);$("trMax").value=+cv(trSel.max,d.u).toPrecision(4);
}
$("trX").onchange=e=>{trSel.x=e.target.value;trSel.min=trSel.max=null;fillTradeSelects();renderTrade();};
$("trY1").onchange=e=>{trSel.y1=e.target.value;renderTrade();};$("trY2").onchange=e=>{trSel.y2=e.target.value;renderTrade();};
$("trMin").onchange=e=>{trSel.min=back(+e.target.value,defOf(trSel.x).u);renderTrade();};$("trMax").onchange=e=>{trSel.max=back(+e.target.value,defOf(trSel.x).u);renderTrade();};
function renderTrade(){
  const d=defOf(trSel.x),integer=d.step>=1,N=integer?Math.min(31,Math.round(trSel.max-trSel.min)+1):(hasProp(P)?21:31),y1=TRY.find(t=>t.k===trSel.y1),y2=TRY.find(t=>t.k===trSel.y2),A=[],B=[];
  for(let i=0;i<N;i++){let v=trSel.min+(trSel.max-trSel.min)*i/Math.max(1,N-1);if(integer)v=Math.round(v);const q=deep(P);setP(q,trSel.x,v);const r=solveAny(q);const x=cv(v,d.u);if(r.ok){A.push({x,y:cv(y1.get(r),y1.u)});B.push({x,y:cv(y2.get(r),y2.u)});}}
  const cx=cv(getP(P,trSel.x),d.u);
  const ds=[{label:labOf(y1),data:A.filter(p=>isFinite(p.y)),borderColor:"#D85A30",backgroundColor:"#D85A30",showLine:true,pointRadius:integer?3:0,borderWidth:2.5,yAxisID:"y"},{label:labOf(y2),data:B.filter(p=>isFinite(p.y)),borderColor:"#378ADD",backgroundColor:"#378ADD",showLine:true,pointRadius:integer?3:0,borderWidth:2.5,borderDash:[6,4],yAxisID:"y2"}];
  if(R&&R.ok){const a=cv(y1.get(R),y1.u),b=cv(y2.get(R),y2.u);if(isFinite(a))ds.push({label:"Current design",data:[{x:cx,y:a}],backgroundColor:css("--ink"),borderColor:css("--ink"),pointRadius:5,yAxisID:"y"});if(isFinite(b))ds.push({label:"",data:[{x:cx,y:b}],backgroundColor:css("--ink"),borderColor:css("--ink"),pointRadius:5,yAxisID:"y2"});}
  const o=chartBase();o.scales={x:axis(labOf(d)+(unitTxt(d.u)?", "+unitTxt(d.u):"")),y:axis(labOf(y1)+(unitTxt(y1.u)?", "+unitTxt(y1.u):""),"left"),y2:axis(labOf(y2)+(unitTxt(y2.u)?", "+unitTxt(y2.u):""),"right")};o.scales.y2.grid={drawOnChartArea:false};
  o.plugins.legend.labels.filter=it=>it.text!=="";
  if(!trC)trC=new Chart($("trChart"),{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o});else{trC.data.datasets=ds;trC.options=o;trC.update();}
  $("optFPR").hidden=P.config!=="separate";
}
$("optFPR").onclick=()=>{let a=1.15,b=Math.min(2.6,P.OPR/(P.LPCPR*1.2));const f=x=>{const q=deep(P);q.FPR=x;const r=Cycle.solve(q);return r.ok?r.TSFC:1;};const gr=(Math.sqrt(5)-1)/2;let c=b-gr*(b-a),d=a+gr*(b-a),fc=f(c),fd=f(d);for(let i=0;i<40;i++){if(fc<fd){b=d;d=c;fd=fc;c=b-gr*(b-a);fc=f(c);}else{a=c;c=d;fc=fd;d=a+gr*(b-a);fd=f(d);}}const x=(a+b)/2,old=P.FPR,t0=R.TSFC;P.FPR=Math.round(x*1000)/1000;update("slider");$("optMsg").textContent="FPR "+old.toFixed(3)+" → "+P.FPR.toFixed(3)+", TSFC "+((R.TSFC-t0)/t0*100).toFixed(2)+"%. Check the fan tip speed and stage count before you accept it.";};
function carpetDef(){
  const f=fam(P),sc=(v,ks)=>ks.map(k=>v*k),sh=(v,ds)=>ds.map(d=>v+d);
  if(P.config==="separate")return{aId:"BPR",bId:"FPR",A:sc(P.BPR,[0.7,0.85,1,1.15,1.3]),B:sh(P.FPR,[-0.15,-0.075,0,0.075,0.15]).filter(v=>v>1.1),x:["Fs","fs","Specific thrust"],y:["TSFC","tsfc","TSFC"],note:"Lower is better on fuel burn; further right means a smaller engine for the same thrust."};
  if(P.config==="mixed")return{aId:"BPR",bId:"T4",A:sc(P.BPR,[0.6,0.8,1,1.2,1.4]).map(v=>Math.max(0.1,v)),B:sh(P.T4,[-200,-100,0,100,200]),x:["Fs","fs","Specific thrust"],y:["TSFC","tsfc","TSFC"],note:"Lower is better on fuel burn; further right means a smaller engine for the same thrust."};
  if(P.config==="turbojet")return{aId:"OPR",bId:"T4",A:sc(P.OPR,[0.6,0.8,1,1.25,1.5]),B:sh(P.T4,[-200,-100,0,100,200]),x:["Fs","fs","Specific thrust"],y:["TSFC","tsfc","TSFC"],note:"Lower is better on fuel burn; further right means a smaller engine for the same thrust."};
  if(f==="gtshaft")return{aId:"OPR",bId:"T4",A:sc(P.OPR,[0.6,0.8,1,1.25,1.5]),B:sh(P.T4,[-150,-75,0,75,150]),x:["Psp","sp","Specific power"],y:["PSFC","psfc","Power-specific fuel use"],note:"Lower is better on fuel; further right means more power from each kilogram of air, so a smaller gas generator."};
  if(f==="piston")return{aId:"CR",bId:"rpm",A:sh(P.CR,[-1.5,-0.75,0,0.75,1.5]).filter(v=>v>=6),B:sc(P.rpm,[0.8,0.9,1,1.1,1.2]),x:["spv","kwl","Power per litre"],y:["PSFC","psfc","BSFC"],note:"Lower is better on fuel; further right means more power from each litre of displacement, so a lighter engine."};
  return{aId:"battSE",bId:"battE",A:sc(P.battSE,[0.7,1,1.5,2,2.5]),B:sc(P.battE,[0.5,0.75,1,1.5,2]),x:["mBatt","mass","Battery mass"],y:["endur","time","Endurance at full power"],note:"Up is more endurance; left is lighter. Better cells (higher Wh/kg) shift every line up and to the left."};
}
function renderCarpet(){
  const cd=carpetDef(),da=defOf(cd.aId),db=defOf(cd.bId),xg=r=>cd.x[0]==="spv"?r.Pshaft/(r.disp*1000):r[cd.x[0]],yg=r=>r[cd.y[0]];
  const grid=cd.A.map(a=>cd.B.map(b=>{const q=deep(P);setP(q,cd.aId,a);setP(q,cd.bId,b);const r=solveAny(q);return r.ok&&isFinite(xg(r))&&isFinite(yg(r))?{x:cv(xg(r),cd.x[1]),y:cv(yg(r),cd.y[1])}:null;}));
  const lab=(d,v)=>labOf(d).replace(/ T4$/,"")+" "+num(cv(v,d.u),d.u==="temp"||d.u==="rpm"||d.u==="n0"||d.u==="whkg"?0:2)+(unitTxt(d.u)?" "+unitTxt(d.u):"");
  const ds=[];cd.A.forEach((a,i)=>ds.push({label:i===0?"Constant "+labOf(da).toLowerCase():"",data:grid[i].filter(Boolean),lbls:grid[i].filter(Boolean).map((_,j,arr)=>j===arr.length-1?lab(da,a):""),borderColor:"#D85A30",backgroundColor:"#D85A30",showLine:true,pointRadius:2,borderWidth:2}));
  cd.B.forEach((b,j)=>{const pts=cd.A.map((a,i)=>grid[i][j]).filter(Boolean);ds.push({label:j===0?"Constant "+labOf(db).toLowerCase():"",data:pts,lbls:pts.map((_,i)=>i===0?lab(db,b):""),borderColor:"#378ADD",backgroundColor:"#378ADD",showLine:true,pointRadius:2,borderWidth:1.5,borderDash:[5,4]});});
  if(R&&R.ok&&isFinite(xg(R)))ds.push({label:"Current design",data:[{x:cv(xg(R),cd.x[1]),y:cv(yg(R),cd.y[1])}],backgroundColor:css("--ink"),pointRadius:6});
  if(BASE&&BASE.r.ok&&BASE.p.config===P.config&&isFinite(xg(BASE.r)))ds.push({label:"Baseline",data:[{x:cv(xg(BASE.r),cd.x[1]),y:cv(yg(BASE.r),cd.y[1])}],backgroundColor:"transparent",borderColor:css("--ink"),borderWidth:2,pointRadius:7,pointStyle:"rectRot"});
  const o=chartBase();o.scales={x:axis(cd.x[2]+", "+unitTxt(cd.x[1])),y:axis(cd.y[2]+", "+unitTxt(cd.y[1]))};o.plugins.legend.labels.filter=it=>it.text!=="";o.layout={padding:{right:90}};
  if(!cpC)cpC=new Chart($("cpChart"),{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o,plugins:[labelPlugin]});else{cpC.data.datasets=ds;cpC.options=o;cpC.update();}
  $("cpSub").textContent="Orange lines hold "+labOf(da).toLowerCase()+" constant; blue dashed lines hold "+labOf(db).toLowerCase()+" constant, each spanning around the current design. "+cd.note;
}

/* ---------- saved designs ---------- */
const SKEY="jet-engine-design-studio-v1";let SAVED=[];
try{SAVED=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SKEY)||"[]");}catch(e){SAVED=[];}
const persist=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem(SKEY,JSON.stringify(SAVED));}catch(e){}};
$("save").onclick=()=>{const n=(SAVED.length+1)+". "+presetName.replace(/ \(.*\)$/,"")+(ps.value==="custom"?" (modified)":"");SAVED.push({name:n,p:deep(P)});persist();renderSaved();$("svMsg").textContent="Saved “"+n+"”.";};
const ARCH={turbojet:"Turbojet",separate:"Separate-flow turbofan",mixed:"Mixed-flow turbofan",turboprop:"Turboprop",turboshaft:"Turboshaft",piston:"Piston and propeller",electric:"Electric and propeller"};
function cycleTxt(s,r){const p=s.p;if(p.config==="piston")return fmt(r.disp,"vol")+" "+unitTxt("vol")+" · CR "+p.CR.toFixed(1)+" · "+num(p.rpm,0)+" rpm"+(p.induction==="turbo"?" · turbo":"");if(p.config==="electric")return num(p.battE,1)+" kWh at "+num(p.battSE,0)+" Wh/kg";if(fam(p)==="gtshaft")return "OPR "+p.OPR.toFixed(1)+" · T4 "+fmt(p.T4,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp");return "BPR "+r.BPR.toFixed(2)+" · FPR "+r.FPR.toFixed(2)+" · OPR "+p.OPR.toFixed(1)+" · T4 "+fmt(p.T4,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp");}
function renderSaved(){
  const t=$("svTable");if(!SAVED.length){t.innerHTML="<tbody><tr><td class='l' style='color:var(--muted)'>No saved designs yet. Select Save design to add the current one.</td></tr></tbody>";return;}
  const rs=SAVED.map(s=>solveAny(withDefaults(s.p)));
  const rows=[["Architecture",(s,r)=>ARCH[s.p.config]+(s.p.geared?", geared":"")+(s.p.ab?", afterburning":"")],["Altitude / Mach",(s,r)=>fmt(s.p.alt,"alt")+" "+unitTxt("alt")+" / M"+s.p.mach.toFixed(2)],["Cycle",(s,r)=>r.ok?cycleTxt(s,r):"–"],
   ["Thrust",(s,r)=>r.ok&&s.p.config!=="turboshaft"?fmt(r.Fn,"force")+" "+unitTxt("force"):"–"],["Shaft power",(s,r)=>r.ok&&isFinite(r.Pshaft)?fmt(r.Pshaft,"kw")+" "+unitTxt("kw"):"–"],
   ["TSFC",(s,r)=>r.ok&&isFinite(r.TSFC)?fmt(r.TSFC,"tsfc")+" "+unitTxt("tsfc"):"–"],["Power-specific fuel use",(s,r)=>r.ok&&isFinite(r.PSFC)?fmt(r.PSFC,"psfc")+" "+unitTxt("psfc"):"–"],
   ["Size",(s,r)=>!r.ok?"–":r.geo?fmt(r.geo.D,"len")+" "+unitTxt("len")+" inlet/fan":r.disp?fmt(r.disp,"vol")+" "+unitTxt("vol"):r.mBatt?fmt(r.mBatt,"mass")+" "+unitTxt("mass")+" battery":"–"]];
  let h="<thead><tr><th>Design</th>"+SAVED.map((s,i)=>"<th style='text-align:right'>"+s.name.replace(/</g,"")+"<br><button class='btn' data-load='"+i+"' type='button' style='margin-top:4px;font-size:12px'>Load</button> <button class='btn' data-del='"+i+"' type='button' style='margin-top:4px;font-size:12px'>Delete</button></th>").join("")+"</tr></thead><tbody>";
  rows.forEach(([n,f])=>{h+="<tr><td>"+n+"</td>"+SAVED.map((s,i)=>"<td>"+f(s,rs[i])+"</td>").join("")+"</tr>";});
  t.innerHTML=h+"</tbody>";
  t.querySelectorAll("[data-load]").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const s=SAVED[+b.dataset.load];P=withDefaults(s.p);presetName=s.name;ps.value="custom";update("refit");window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:RM?"auto":"smooth"});});
  t.querySelectorAll("[data-del]").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{SAVED.splice(+b.dataset.del,1);persist();renderSaved();});
}
$("svCopy").onclick=async()=>{const txt=JSON.stringify(SAVED,null,1);$("svText").value=txt;try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt);$("svMsg").textContent="Copied "+SAVED.length+" designs.";}catch(e){$("svMsg").textContent="Clipboard blocked here; the JSON is in the box below to copy by hand.";}};
$("svImport").onclick=()=>{try{const a=JSON.parse($("svText").value);const arr=Array.isArray(a)?a:[a];let n=0;arr.forEach(s=>{if(s&&s.p&&s.p.config){SAVED.push({name:String(s.name||"Imported design"),p:s.p});n++;}});persist();renderSaved();$("svMsg").textContent=n?"Imported "+n+" designs.":"No designs found in that JSON.";}catch(e){$("svMsg").textContent="That isn't valid JSON. Paste the text from Copy all as JSON.";}};

/* ---------- validation ---------- */
function renderValidation(){
  const cases=ValCases.pyc;
  let h="<thead><tr><th>Reference case</th><th>Quantity</th><th>pyCycle</th><th>This studio</th><th>Difference</th></tr></thead><tbody>";
  cases.forEach((c,i)=>{const r=Cycle.solve(withDefaults(c)),f=REF[i];const q=[["Net thrust",f.Fn_N,r.Fn,"force"],["TSFC",f.TSFC/35303.9,r.TSFC,"tsfc"],["Compressor exit T3",f.T3,r.S["3"].Tt,"temp"],["Compressor exit P3",f.P3,r.S["3"].Pt,"pres"],["Turbine exit T5",f.T5,r.S["5"].Tt,"temp"],["Turbine exit P5",f.P5,r.S["5"].Pt,"pres"]];
    q.forEach((z,j)=>{const d=(z[2]-z[1])/z[1]*100;h+="<tr><td class='l'>"+(j===0?"<b>"+f.name+"</b>":"")+"</td><td class='l'>"+z[0]+"</td><td>"+fmt(z[1],z[3])+" "+unitTxt(z[3])+"</td><td>"+fmt(z[2],z[3])+" "+unitTxt(z[3])+"</td><td>"+(d>0?"+":"")+d.toFixed(2)+"%</td></tr>";});});
  $("vaTable").innerHTML=h+"</tbody>";
}

/* ---------- 3D ---------- */
const COL={prop:0x1D9E75,pow:0x1D9E75,pt:0x1D9E75,cu:0xBA7517,ox:0x7F77DD,fuel:0xCECBF6,fan:0x378ADD,lpc:0x378ADD,hpc:0x378ADD,hpt:0xD85A30,lpt:0xD85A30,comb:0xEF9F27,cas:0xB4B2A9,sh:0x5F5E5A,noz:0x888780,ab:0xE24B4A,gb:0x0F6E56,sp:0xD3D1C7,vane:0x888780};
const stage=$("stage"),tip=$("tip");
const renderer=new THREE.WebGLRenderer({antialias:true,alpha:true,powerPreference:"high-performance"});renderer.shadowMap.enabled=true;renderer.shadowMap.type=THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
/* render quality: the pixel ratio floats between a floor and a supersampled ceiling, steered by measured frame time */
const QL={mode:"auto",dpr:window.devicePixelRatio||1,pr:Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio||1,2),cap:null,ema:16,n:0,shadow:true,seg:96,lod:2,slow:0};
QL.max=()=>{const w=stage.clientWidth||800,h=stage.clientHeight||500;return Math.max(1,Math.min(3,Math.max(2,QL.dpr*1.5),Math.sqrt(9e6/(w*h))));};
function setPR(v){QL.pr=Math.max(0.75,Math.min(QL.max(),v));renderer.setPixelRatio(QL.pr);size3();const e=$("vQualTxt");if(e)e.textContent=QL.pr.toFixed(2).replace(/0$/,"")+"× pixels"+(QL.shadow?"":" · shadows off")+(QL.lod<2?" · simpler blades":"");}
function setShadow(on){QL.shadow=on;L1.castShadow=on;if(ground)ground.visible=on;renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate=true;}
function setLod(l){if(l===QL.lod)return;QL.lod=l;QL.seg=l===2?96:l===1?64:40;if(typeof schedule3D==="function")schedule3D();}
function govern(ms){if(QL.mode!=="auto"||ms>2000)return;
  if(ms>110){if(++QL.slow>=3){QL.slow=0;QL.ema=30;QL.n=0;if(QL.shadow)setShadow(false);if(QL.lod>0)setLod(QL.lod-1);setPR(Math.min(QL.pr,1));QL.cap=QL.pr;}return;}QL.slow=0; /* far too slow: shed shadows, geometry and pixels at once */
  QL.ema+=(ms-QL.ema)*0.08;if(++QL.n<50)return;QL.n=0;
  if(QL.ema>27){if(QL.pr>1.01||(!QL.shadow&&QL.pr>0.76)){QL.cap=QL.pr*0.9;setPR(QL.pr*0.84);}else if(QL.shadow){setShadow(false);setPR(QL.pr);}else if(QL.lod>0)setLod(QL.lod-1);}
  else if(QL.ema<19){if(!QL.shadow&&QL.pr>=1){setShadow(true);setPR(QL.pr);}else if(QL.pr<QL.max()-0.01&&(!QL.cap||QL.pr*1.1<QL.cap))setPR(QL.pr*1.1);}}
function setQuality(m){QL.mode=m;QL.cap=null;QL.slow=0;if(m==="high"){setLod(2);setShadow(true);setPR(QL.max());}else if(m==="std"){setLod(1);setShadow(false);setPR(Math.min(QL.dpr,1.5));}else{setLod(2);setShadow(true);setPR(Math.min(QL.dpr,2));}}
renderer.setPixelRatio(QL.pr);renderer.domElement.setAttribute("aria-label","3D model of the current engine design");stage.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
const scene=new THREE.Scene(),cam=new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(36,1,0.01,500),DEF=new THREE.Vector3(0.42,0.36,0.83).normalize();
scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xffffff,0x555555,0.6));const L1=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,0.75);L1.position.set(4,8,6);scene.add(L1);scene.add(L1.target);const L2=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,0.25);L2.position.set(-6,-2,-4);scene.add(L2);
L1.castShadow=true;L1.shadow.mapSize.set(2048,2048);L1.shadow.bias=-0.0004;L1.shadow.radius=3;let ground=null;
/* studio environment for reflections: a soft gradient dome with three light panels, prefiltered once */
(function(){try{const es=new THREE.Scene(),sg=new THREE.SphereGeometry(20,24,16),n=sg.attributes.position.count,ca=new Float32Array(n*3);for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const y=sg.attributes.position.getY(i)/20,t=0.5+0.5*y,v=0.38+0.6*t;ca[i*3]=v*0.98;ca[i*3+1]=v;ca[i*3+2]=v*1.04;}
  sg.setAttribute("color",new THREE.BufferAttribute(ca,3));es.add(new THREE.Mesh(sg,new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({vertexColors:true,side:THREE.BackSide})));
  [[6,12,6,10,10,1.7],[-12,5,2,8,5,1.2],[2,3,-13,12,4,1.0]].forEach(a=>{const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(a[3],a[4]),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:new THREE.Color(a[5],a[5],a[5]),side:THREE.DoubleSide}));m.position.set(a[0],a[1],a[2]);m.lookAt(0,0,0);es.add(m);});
  const pm=new THREE.PMREMGenerator(renderer);scene.environment=pm.fromScene(es,0.03).texture;pm.dispose();}catch(e){}})();
const ctrl=new THREE.OrbitControls(cam,renderer.domElement);ctrl.enableDamping=true;ctrl.enablePan=false;ctrl.enableZoom=true;ctrl.zoomSpeed=0.6;ctrl.autoRotateSpeed=0.7;
renderer.domElement.addEventListener("wheel",e=>{if(!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey)e.stopImmediatePropagation();},{capture:true});
function size3(){const w=stage.clientWidth,h=stage.clientHeight;if(QL.pr>QL.max()){QL.pr=QL.max();renderer.setPixelRatio(QL.pr);}renderer.setSize(w,h);cam.aspect=w/h;cam.updateProjectionMatrix();}
new ResizeObserver(size3).observe(stage);size3();setPR(QL.pr);
let CUT=true,eng=null,grid=null,needFit=2,SPOOLS=null;
function setSpoolSpeeds(){if(!SPOOLS)return;const v=typeof flowVel==="function"&&R&&R.ok?flowVel():{nL:1,nH:1};SPOOLS.LP.userData.w=SPOOLS.base.LP*v.nL;SPOOLS.FAN.userData.w=SPOOLS.base.FAN*v.nL;SPOOLS.HP.userData.w=SPOOLS.base.HP*v.nH;}const MATS={};
const MFIN={};[COL.fan,COL.hpt,COL.cu].forEach(c=>MFIN[c]=[0.72,0.3]);[COL.prop,COL.gb].forEach(c=>MFIN[c]=[0.45,0.38]);[COL.sh,COL.noz,COL.vane].forEach(c=>MFIN[c]=[0.62,0.48]);[COL.cas,COL.sp].forEach(c=>MFIN[c]=[0.65,0.42]);[COL.ox,COL.fuel,COL.comb,COL.ab].forEach(c=>MFIN[c]=[0.25,0.55]);
function mat(c,o){const k=c+"_"+o;if(!MATS[k]){const f=MFIN[c]||[0.5,0.45];MATS[k]=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:c,metalness:o<1?Math.min(f[0],0.4):f[0],roughness:f[1],envMapIntensity:o<1?0.6:1,transparent:o<1,opacity:o,side:THREE.DoubleSide,depthWrite:o>=1});}return MATS[k];}
const arc=()=>CUT?PI*1.5:TAU;
/* add points along long straight runs so the lathe shades evenly; corners stay sharp */
function smoothPts(v){if(v.length<2)return v;const o=[v[0]];let L=0;for(let i=1;i<v.length;i++)L+=v[i].distanceTo(v[i-1]);for(let i=1;i<v.length;i++){const a=v[i-1],b=v[i],n=Math.min(6,Math.floor(a.distanceTo(b)/(L/40)));for(let k=1;k<=n;k++)o.push(a.clone().lerp(b,k/(n+1)));o.push(b);}return o;}
/* lofted airfoil blade: sections of {y, c (chord), s (stagger about the span axis), t (thickness ÷ chord), m (camber ÷ chord)} */
function loftBlade(secs,N){N=N||14;const ns=secs.length,pos=[],idx=[];
  secs.forEach(S=>{const cs=Math.cos(S.s),sn=Math.sin(S.s);for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const th=TAU*i/N,x=0.5*(1+Math.cos(th)),up=th<=PI,yt=5*S.t*(0.2969*Math.sqrt(x)-0.126*x-0.3516*x*x+0.2843*x*x*x-0.1036*x*x*x*x),a=(x-0.5)*S.c,b=(4*S.m*x*(1-x)+(up?yt:-yt))*S.c;pos.push(a*cs+b*sn+(S.dx||0),S.y,-a*sn+b*cs);}});
  for(let j=0;j<ns-1;j++)for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const a=j*N+i,b=j*N+(i+1)%N,c=a+N,d=b+N;idx.push(a,c,b,b,c,d);}
  const t0=(ns-1)*N,tc=pos.length/3,T=secs[ns-1];pos.push(T.dx||0,T.y,0);for(let i=0;i<N;i++)idx.push(t0+i,tc,t0+(i+1)%N);
  const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute("position",new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos,3));g.setIndex(idx);g.computeVertexNormals();return g;}
function tag(m,name,info){m.userData.name=name;m.userData.info=info||"";return m;}
function lathe(pts,c,o,name,info){const v=pts.map(p=>new THREE.Vector2(Math.max(0.001,p[1]),p[0]));const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.LatheGeometry(smoothPts(v),QL.seg,0,arc()),mat(c,CUT?o:1));m.rotation.z=-PI/2;return tag(m,name,info);}
function cyl(r,x0,x1,c,name,o=1,open=false){const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r,r,x1-x0,64,1,open,0,open?arc():TAU),mat(c,o));m.rotation.z=-PI/2;m.position.x=(x0+x1)/2;return tag(m,name);}
function coneX(r,x0,x1,c,name){const len=Math.abs(x1-x0),m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(r,len,64),mat(c,1));m.rotation.z=x1<x0?PI/2:-PI/2;m.position.x=(x0+x1)/2;return tag(m,name);}
function ring(x,r,t,c,name){const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(r,t,14,96),mat(c,1));m.rotation.y=PI/2;m.position.x=x;return tag(m,name);}
const tmpM=new THREE.Matrix4(),tmpA=new THREE.Matrix4(),tmpB=new THREE.Matrix4();
function bladeRow(q,c,tw,info){const rh=(q.rh0+q.rh1)/2,rt=(q.rt0+q.rt1)/2,h=rt-rh,n=q.n,ch=Math.max(0.01,q.chord);
  const turb=tw<0,fanB=q.comp==="fan",vane=c===COL.vane,N=QL.lod===2?(n>60?10:14):QL.lod===1?8:6,ns=QL.lod===2?4:QL.lod===1?3:1,secs=[];for(let j=0;j<=ns;j++){const v=j/ns;secs.push({y:-h/2+v*h,c:ch*(fanB?1+0.12*Math.sin(PI*v):turb?1-0.12*v:1-0.18*v),s:tw*(turb?1.12-0.28*v:fanB?0.55+0.65*v:0.72+0.5*v),t:turb?0.2-0.08*v:vane?0.1:fanB?0.085-0.05*v:0.09-0.045*v,m:(turb?-0.2:vane?0.1:0.07-0.03*v)*(tw<0?1:1),dx:fanB?-0.04*ch*Math.sin(PI*v):0});}
  const geo=loftBlade(secs,N),im=new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo,mat(c,1),n);
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){tmpM.makeRotationX(i/n*TAU);tmpA.makeTranslation(0,rh+h/2,0);tmpB.makeRotationY(tw);tmpM.multiply(tmpA).multiply(tmpB);im.setMatrixAt(i,tmpM);}
  const g=new THREE.Group();g.add(tag(im,q.name,info));const hub=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(rh,rh,ch*1.25,64),mat(c,1));hub.rotation.z=-PI/2;g.add(tag(hub,q.name,info));g.position.x=(q.x0+q.x1)/2;return g;}
function geoPiston(r){
  const b=r.geom.bore,s=r.geom.stroke,n=P.ncyl,pitch=b*1.25,L=(n/2)*pitch+b*0.9,H=b*2.3,parts=[],D=P.propD,xp=-0.35*b-0.12;
  parts.push({k:"box",x0:0,x1:L,w:H*0.9,h:H,y:0,z:0,c:"sh",name:"Crankcase ("+fmt(r.disp,"vol")+" "+unitTxt("vol")+")"});
  parts.push({k:"box",x0:L*0.15,x1:L*0.85,w:H*0.6,h:H*0.45,y:-H*0.62,z:0,c:"noz",name:"Oil sump"});
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const side=i%2?-1:1,x=b*0.6+Math.floor(i/2)*pitch+(side<0?pitch*0.45:0);parts.push({k:"cyl",x,side,b,s,z0:H*0.42,name:"Cylinder "+(i+1)+" (bore "+fmt(b,"len",US?2:3)+" × stroke "+fmt(s,"len",US?2:3)+" "+unitTxt("len")+")",phase:(i%4)*PI/2+(i>=4?PI/4:0)});}
  parts.push({k:"shaftP",x0:xp,x1:L,r:b*0.18,name:"Crankshaft / propeller flange"});
  parts.push({k:"prop",x:xp,D,B:P.propB,chord:r.prop.geom.cMax*D,pitch:r.prop.theta75,geom:r.prop.geom,name:"Propeller ("+P.propB+" blades, Ø "+fmt(D,"len")+" "+unitTxt("len")+")"});
  parts.push({k:"cone",x0:xp-0.09*D,x1:xp+0.02,r:Math.max(0.06,0.085*D),name:"Spinner"});
  const zMax=H*0.42+s*2.2+b*0.3,cw=Math.max(zMax*1.08,H*0.75);
  return{kind:"piston",parts,D:2*cw,xMin:xp-0.1*D,xMax:L+0.15,len:L+0.25+0.1*D,prop:{x:xp,D},cowl:[[xp+0.03,0.11*D],[0.05,cw*0.85],[L*0.4,cw],[L+0.12,cw*0.8]],vel:{inlet:40}};
}
function geoElectric(r){
  const k=Math.cbrt(P.Pshaft/57600),pm=r.pd&&r.pd.motor,rm=pm?pm.D/2:0.15*k,Lm=pm?pm.L:0.14*k,D=P.propD,xp=-0.12,parts=[]; /* Preliminary: stator diameter and length from the sized rotor */
  const vol=P.battE/250,bl=Math.cbrt(vol*4)*1.0,bw=bl*0.5,bh=bl*0.3;
  parts.push({k:"motor",x0:0,x1:Lm,r:rm,name:"Electric motor ("+fmt(P.Pshaft,"kw")+" "+unitTxt("kw")+", "+fmt(r.mMotor,"mass")+" "+unitTxt("mass")+")"});
  parts.push({k:"box",x0:Lm+0.05,x1:Lm+0.05+rm*1.3,w:rm*1.4,h:rm*1.2,y:0,z:0,c:"sh",name:"Inverter (DC to AC)"});
  const xb=Lm+0.15+rm*1.3;parts.push({k:"box",x0:xb,x1:xb+bl,w:bw,h:bh,y:0,z:0,c:"ox",o:0.35,name:"Battery pack ("+num(P.battE,1)+" kWh, "+fmt(r.mBatt,"mass")+" "+unitTxt("mass")+")"});
  const nm=Math.max(2,Math.min(8,Math.round(bl/0.25)));for(let i=0;i<nm;i++)parts.push({k:"box",x0:xb+bl*(i+0.08)/nm,x1:xb+bl*(i+0.92)/nm,w:bw*0.85,h:bh*0.8,y:0,z:0,c:"fuel",name:"Battery modules"});
  parts.push({k:"cable",x0:Lm,x1:xb,y:rm*0.5,name:"High-voltage cables"});
  parts.push({k:"shaftP",x0:xp,x1:Lm,r:rm*0.15,name:"Motor shaft"});
  parts.push({k:"prop",x:xp,D,B:P.propB,chord:r.prop.geom.cMax*D,pitch:r.prop.theta75,geom:r.prop.geom,name:"Propeller ("+P.propB+" blades, Ø "+fmt(D,"len")+" "+unitTxt("len")+")"});
  parts.push({k:"cone",x0:xp-0.09*D,x1:xp+0.02,r:Math.max(0.05,0.08*D),name:"Spinner"});
  return{kind:"electric",parts,D:2*rm,xMin:xp-0.1*D,xMax:xb+bl,len:xb+bl-xp+0.1*D,prop:{x:xp,D},vel:{inlet:40}};
}
function addProp(q,group){
  const R=q.D/2,g=q.geom,segs=7;
  const secs=[];for(let i=0;i<=12;i++){const xm=0.16+(1-0.16)*i/12,c=g?Math.max(0.02,(0.35+0.9*xm-0.95*xm*xm)*g.cMax/0.563*q.D):q.chord,tw=g?Math.atan(g.Jd/(PI*xm))-Math.atan(g.Jd/(PI*0.75)):0,beta=q.pitch+tw,tipF=i===12?0.35:i===11?0.8:1,root=i===0?0.45:i===1?0.75:1;secs.push({y:xm*R,c:c*tipF*root,s:-beta,t:(0.2-0.13*xm)/root,m:0.04});}
  const bg=loftBlade(secs,QL.lod===2?18:10);
  for(let b=0;b<q.B;b++){const bl=new THREE.Group();const m=new THREE.Mesh(bg,mat(COL.prop,1));bl.add(tag(m,q.name,"pitch at 75% radius "+(q.pitch*180/PI).toFixed(1)+"°"));
    bl.rotation.x=b/q.B*TAU;group.add(bl);}
  group.position.x=q.x;
}
function addCylinder(q,group){
  const r0=q.b/2,Lb=q.s*1.9,side=q.side,g=new THREE.Group();
  const bar=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r0*1.08,r0*1.08,Lb,28,1,true),mat(COL.cas,CUT?0.3:1));bar.position.y=Lb/2;g.add(tag(bar,q.name));
  const fg=new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r0*1.45,r0*1.45,Lb*0.025,28);for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const f=new THREE.Mesh(fg,mat(COL.cas,CUT?0.3:1));f.position.y=Lb*(0.15+0.8*i/7);g.add(tag(f,"Cooling fins"));}
  const hd=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r0*1.5,r0*1.5,q.b*0.55,28),mat(COL.noz,CUT?0.6:1));hd.position.y=Lb+q.b*0.27;g.add(tag(hd,"Cylinder head (valves and spark plugs)"));
  const pis=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r0,r0,r0*0.9,24),mat(COL.fan,1));g.add(tag(pis,"Piston"));
  const glow=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r0*0.95,r0*0.95,r0*0.12,24),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xEF9F27,transparent:true,opacity:0,depthWrite:false}));glow.position.y=Lb-r0*0.1;g.add(tag(glow,"Combustion (power stroke)"));
  g.position.set(q.x,0,side*q.z0);g.rotation.x=side>0?PI/2:-PI/2;
  group.add(g);PISTONS.push({p:pis,glow,top:Lb-r0*0.7,S:q.s*0.9,phase:q.phase,fire:q.phase});
}
let PISTONS=[];
function build3D(r){
  if(eng){scene.remove(eng);eng.traverse(o=>{if(o.geometry)o.geometry.dispose();});}
  eng=new THREE.Group();scene.add(eng);PISTONS=[];
  if(r.kind==="piston")r.geo=geoPiston(r);else if(r.kind==="electric")r.geo=geoElectric(r);
  const g=r.geo;if(!g)return;
  const spools={LP:new THREE.Group(),HP:new THREE.Group(),FAN:new THREE.Group(),PROP:new THREE.Group()};Object.values(spools).forEach(s=>eng.add(s));
  const rpmLP=g.lpt?g.lpt.rpm:(g.fan?g.fan.rpm:0),rpmHP=g.hpc?g.hpc.rpm:0,rpmFan=g.fan?g.fan.rpm:0,rpmProp=hasProp(P)?P.propRPM:0;
  SPOOLS=spools;SPOOLS.base={LP:rpmLP*0.00045,HP:rpmHP*0.00045,FAN:rpmFan*0.00045,PROP:Math.min(14,rpmProp*0.003)};setSpoolSpeeds();
  const rpmTxt=v=>num(v,0)+" rpm";
  g.parts.forEach(q=>{
    if(q.k==="row"){const c=COL[q.comp],tw=q.comp==="fan"?0.95:(q.comp==="hpt"||q.comp==="lpt"||q.comp==="pt"?-0.7:0.55);const spin=q.comp==="fan"?(P.geared?"FAN":"LP"):q.spool;const rpm=spin==="FAN"?rpmFan:(spin==="LP"?rpmLP:rpmHP);
      spools[spin].add(bladeRow(q,c,tw,rpmTxt(rpm)+(q.comp==="fan"?" · tip "+fmt(g.fan.Utip,"vel")+" "+unitTxt("vel"):"")));}
    else if(q.k==="shaft")spools[q.spool].add(cyl(q.r,q.x0,q.x1,COL.sh,q.name));
    else if(q.k==="cone")(isFan(P)?spools[P.geared?"FAN":"LP"]:hasProp(P)?spools.PROP:eng).add(coneX(q.r,q.x1,q.x0,COL.sp,q.name));
    else if(q.k==="burner"){eng.add(lathe([[q.x0,q.ro],[q.x1,q.ro]],COL.comb,1,"Combustor","T4 "+fmt(P.T4,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp")));eng.add(lathe([[q.x0,q.ri],[q.x1,q.ri]],COL.comb,1,"Combustor"));eng.add(ring(q.x0,(q.ri+q.ro)/2,(q.ro-q.ri)/2,COL.comb,"Combustor dome and fuel injectors"));}
    else if(q.k==="gear"){eng.add(cyl(q.r,q.x0,q.x1,COL.gb,q.name));eng.add(ring((q.x0+q.x1)/2,q.r,q.r*0.12,COL.gb,q.name));}
    else if(q.k==="gearbox"){eng.add(cyl(q.r,q.x0,q.x1,COL.gb,q.name,CUT?0.55:1));eng.add(ring((q.x0+q.x1)/2,q.r*0.7,q.r*0.12,COL.gb,q.name));}
    else if(q.k==="outshaft")spools.LP.add(cyl(q.r,q.x0,q.x1,COL.pow,q.name));
    else if(q.k==="flange")spools.LP.add(cyl(q.r,q.x0,q.x1,COL.pow,q.name));
    else if(q.k==="shaftP")spools.PROP.add(cyl(q.r,q.x0,q.x1,COL.sh,q.name));
    else if(q.k==="prop")addProp(q,spools.PROP);
    else if(q.k==="box"){const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(q.x1-q.x0,q.h,q.w),mat(COL[q.c],q.o?(CUT?q.o:1):1));m.position.set((q.x0+q.x1)/2,q.y,q.z);eng.add(tag(m,q.name));}
    else if(q.k==="motor"){eng.add(cyl(q.r,q.x0,q.x1,COL.cas,q.name,CUT?0.35:1,false));eng.add(cyl(q.r*0.82,q.x0+0.01,q.x1-0.01,COL.cu,"Stator windings (copper)"));spools.PROP.add(cyl(q.r*0.55,q.x0+0.01,q.x1-0.01,COL.pow,"Permanent-magnet rotor"));}
    else if(q.k==="cable"){[0.05,-0.05].forEach(z=>{const m=cyl(0.015,q.x0,q.x1,COL.cu,q.name);m.position.y=q.y;m.position.z=z;eng.add(m);});}
    else if(q.k==="cyl")addCylinder(q,eng);
    else if(q.k==="mesh")eng.add(q.obj);
    else if(q.k==="vanes"){const vr=bladeRow({x0:q.x0,x1:q.x1,rh0:q.ri,rh1:q.ri,rt0:q.ro,rt1:q.ro,n:q.n,chord:(q.x1-q.x0),name:q.name},COL.vane,0.25,"");eng.add(vr);}
    else if(q.k==="plug")eng.add(coneX(q.r,q.x0,q.x1,COL.noz,q.name));
    else if(q.k==="plugTJ")eng.add(coneX(q.r,q.x0,q.x1,COL.noz,q.name));
    else if(q.k==="mixer")eng.add(lathe([[q.x0,g.tEnd.rt*1.02],[q.x1,q.r]],COL.noz,0.85,q.name));
    else if(q.k==="ab"){eng.add(lathe([[q.x0,q.r*0.97],[q.x1,q.r*0.97]],COL.ab,0.3,q.name,"T7 "+fmt(r.S["7"].Tt,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp")));eng.add(ring(q.x0+0.12*q.r,q.r*0.45,q.r*0.025,COL.ab,"Flame holders and spray rings"));eng.add(ring(q.x0+0.12*q.r,q.r*0.78,q.r*0.025,COL.ab,"Flame holders and spray rings"));}
  });
  if(g.casing)eng.add(lathe(g.casing,COL.cas,0.22,isFan(P)?"Core casing":"Compressor and turbine casing"));
  if(g.cfg==="separate"){
    eng.add(lathe(g.cowl,COL.cas,0.4,"Core cowl"));
    const nz=g.coreNoz;eng.add(lathe([[nz.x0,nz.rIn],[nz.xT,nz.r8]],COL.noz,0.85,"Core nozzle","exit "+fmt(r.nozCore.V,"vel")+" "+unitTxt("vel")));
    const N=g.nacelle,loop=N.inner.concat(N.outer.slice().reverse()).concat([N.inner[0]]);eng.add(lathe(loop,COL.cas,0.22,"Nacelle","bypass jet "+fmt(r.nozByp.V,"vel")+" "+unitTxt("vel")));
  }else if(g.shellPts){
    eng.add(lathe(g.shellPts,COL.cas,0.2,P.config==="mixed"?"Outer casing and bypass duct":g.shaft?"Engine casing and exhaust":"Engine casing and nozzle","exit "+fmt(r.nozCore.V,"vel")+" "+unitTxt("vel")));
    if(P.config==="mixed")eng.add(lathe([[g.x.split,g.rSplit],[g.x.turbEnd,g.tEnd.rt*1.04]],COL.cas,0.35,"Splitter / core casing"));
  }else if(g.cowl)eng.add(lathe(g.cowl,COL.cas,0.16,"Cowling"));
  if(grid){scene.remove(grid);grid.geometry.dispose();}
  const bb=new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(eng),sz=bb.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()),ctr=bb.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  const step=Math.max(sz.x,sz.y)>7?1:Math.max(sz.x,sz.y)>1.6?0.5:0.1,span=Math.ceil(Math.max(sz.x,sz.z)*1.4/step)*step;grid=new THREE.GridHelper(span,Math.round(span/step),0x888780,0xB4B2A9);grid.material.transparent=true;grid.material.opacity=0.45;grid.position.set(ctr.x,bb.min.y-0.08*sz.y,0);scene.add(grid);
  if(ground){scene.remove(ground);ground.geometry.dispose();}ground=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(span*1.2,span*1.2),new THREE.ShadowMaterial({opacity:0.2}));ground.rotation.x=-PI/2;ground.position.copy(grid.position);ground.position.y-=0.001;ground.receiveShadow=true;ground.visible=QL.shadow;ground.raycast=()=>{};scene.add(ground);
  {const rr=sz.length()/2,sc=L1.shadow.camera;L1.target.position.copy(ctr);L1.position.set(ctr.x+rr*0.9,ctr.y+rr*2.4,ctr.z+rr*1.3);sc.left=-rr*1.25;sc.right=rr*1.25;sc.top=rr*1.25;sc.bottom=-rr*1.25;sc.near=rr*0.2;sc.far=rr*6;sc.updateProjectionMatrix();eng.traverse(o=>{if(o.isMesh)o.castShadow=!(o.material&&o.material.transparent);});}
  $("stScale").innerHTML="Grid squares: "+(US?fmt(step,"len")+" in":step+" m")+"<br>Drag to rotate · Ctrl/⌘ + scroll to zoom · hover parts for data";
  buildFlow(r,flowVel());
  if(needFit){fit(needFit===2);needFit=0;}
}
function fit(reset){if(!eng)return;const s=new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(eng).getBoundingSphere(new THREE.Sphere());const dir=reset?DEF.clone():cam.position.clone().sub(ctrl.target).normalize();const vf=cam.fov*PI/180,hf=2*Math.atan(Math.tan(vf/2)*cam.aspect),d=s.radius/Math.sin(Math.min(vf,hf)/2)*0.78;ctrl.target.copy(s.center);cam.position.copy(s.center).add(dir.multiplyScalar(d));cam.near=d/100;cam.far=d*20;cam.updateProjectionMatrix();ctrl.update();}
// flow particles
const NP=1600,ppos=new Float32Array(NP*3),pcol=new Float32Array(NP*3),pg=new THREE.BufferGeometry();pg.setAttribute("position",new THREE.BufferAttribute(ppos,3));pg.setAttribute("color",new THREE.BufferAttribute(pcol,3));
const ptex=(()=>{const c=document.createElement("canvas");c.width=c.height=64;const x=c.getContext("2d"),gr=x.createRadialGradient(32,32,0,32,32,32);gr.addColorStop(0,"rgba(255,255,255,1)");gr.addColorStop(0.45,"rgba(255,255,255,0.85)");gr.addColorStop(1,"rgba(255,255,255,0)");x.fillStyle=gr;x.fillRect(0,0,64,64);return new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);})();
const pmat=new THREE.PointsMaterial({size:0.02,map:ptex,alphaTest:0.02,vertexColors:true,transparent:true,opacity:0.9,depthWrite:false});const pts=new THREE.Points(pg,pmat);pts.frustumCulled=false;scene.add(pts);
const FC={cold:new THREE.Color(0x85B7EB),hot:new THREE.Color(0xEF9F27),ab:new THREE.Color(0xE24B4A),warm:new THREE.Color(0xFAC775),prop:new THREE.Color(0x5DCAA5)};
let STR=[],PS=[],KV=0.02;for(let i=0;i<NP;i++)PS.push({x:0,u:0,t:0,s:0});
function ip(p,x){if(x<=p[0][0])return p[0];for(let i=1;i<p.length;i++){if(x<=p[i][0]){const a=p[i-1],b=p[i],t=(x-a[0])/(b[0]-a[0]||1);return [x,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t,a[2]+(b[2]-a[2])*t];}}return p[p.length-1];}
function buildFlow(r,v){
  const g=r.geo;STR=[];if(!g)return;const D=g.prop?Math.max(g.D,g.prop.D):g.D,V0v=Math.max(v.V0,0.55*g.vel.inlet),Vin=g.vel.inlet;KV=0.0045*Math.max(g.len,1.5);pmat.size=1.5*Math.max(0.012,0.006*Math.max(g.len,1.5));
  const xs=g.xMin-0.7*D,xe=g.xMax+1.8*D;
  if(g.prop){const R=g.prop.D/2,x0=g.prop.x,Vd=v.Vdisk||V0v,Vs=v.Vs||V0v,Vf=Math.max(v.V0,Vd*0.6);
    STR.push({f:g.kind?1:0.7,path:[[x0-1.2*D,0.62*R*1.25,0.42*R*1.25],[x0,0.62*R,0.4*R],[x0+0.6*D,0.62*R*0.86,0.4*R*0.86],[xe,0.62*R*0.8,0.4*R*0.8]],seg:[[x0-0.3*D,Vf,"prop"],[x0+0.3*D,Vd,"prop"],[1e9,Vs,"prop"]]});
    if(g.kind)return finishFlow();}
  const corePts=[];const rows=g.parts.filter(q=>q.k==="row"&&q.comp!=="fan");
  const c0=isFan(P)?(g.rh2+g.rSplit)/2:(g.rh2+g.rt2)/2,hw0=isFan(P)?(g.rSplit-g.rh2)/2*0.8:(g.rt2-g.rh2)/2*0.8;
  const xin=g.prop?g.prop.x+0.2*g.prop.D:xs;
  corePts.push([Math.min(xin,-0.3),c0*2,hw0*1.2],[g.prop?-0.05:g.xMin,c0,hw0],[0,c0,hw0]);
  rows.forEach(q=>{const rm=(q.rh0+q.rt0+q.rh1+q.rt1)/4,hh=(q.rt0-q.rh0+q.rt1-q.rh1)/4*0.8;corePts.push([q.x0,rm,hh]);});
  const bu=g.burner;corePts.push([bu.x0,(bu.ri+bu.ro)/2,(bu.ro-bu.ri)/2*0.6]);corePts.push([bu.x1,(bu.ri+bu.ro)/2,(bu.ro-bu.ri)/2*0.6]);
  corePts.sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]);
  if(g.cfg==="separate"){
    const nz=g.coreNoz;corePts.push([g.x.turbEnd,(g.tEnd.rh+g.tEnd.rt)/2,(g.tEnd.rt-g.tEnd.rh)/2*0.7],[nz.xT,(nz.rPlugT+nz.r8)/2,(nz.r8-nz.rPlugT)/2*0.7],[xe,(nz.rPlugT+nz.r8)/2*1.1,(nz.r8-nz.rPlugT)/2*1.1]);
    const bc0=(g.rSplit+g.rt2)/2,bh0=(g.rt2-g.rSplit)/2*0.85,B=g.bypNoz;
    STR.push({f:Math.min(0.78,v.BPR/(1+v.BPR)),path:[[xs,bc0*1.05,bh0*1.3],[g.xMin,bc0,bh0],[g.x.fanEnd,bc0,bh0],[B.x18,(B.rcc+B.r18)/2,(B.r18-B.rcc)/2*0.85],[xe,(B.rcc+B.r18)/2*1.05,(B.r18-B.rcc)/2*1.2]],seg:[[g.xMin,V0v,"cold"],[B.x18,Vin*0.8,"cold"],[1e9,v.Vb,"cold"]]});
    STR.push({f:1,path:corePts,seg:[[0,V0v,"cold"],[bu.x0,150,"cold"],[bu.x1,45,"hot"],[nz.xT,260,"hot"],[1e9,v.Vc,"hot"]]});
  }else{
    const nz=g.nozzle,te=g.tEnd;corePts.push([g.x.turbEnd,(te.rh+te.rt)/2,(te.rt-te.rh)/2*0.7],[g.x6,nz.rtp*0.5,nz.rtp*0.42],[nz.x7,nz.rtp*0.5,nz.rtp*0.42],[nz.xT,nz.r8*0.5,nz.r8*0.42],[nz.xE,nz.r9*0.5,nz.r9*0.42],[xe,nz.r9*0.6,nz.r9*0.55]);
    corePts.sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]);
    const abX=v.ab?nz.x7-(nz.x7-g.x6)*0.9:1e9,mixC=P.config==="mixed"?"warm":"hot";
    const tail=v.ab?[[abX,260,mixC],[nz.x7,320,"ab"],[1e9,v.Vc,"ab"]]:[[nz.xT,Math.max(v.Vc,120),mixC],[1e9,v.Vc,mixC]];
    if(P.config==="mixed"){const oR=g.outerR,bc0=(g.rSplit+g.rt2)/2,bh0=(g.rt2-g.rSplit)/2*0.85;
      STR.push({f:Math.min(0.6,v.BPR/(1+v.BPR)),path:[[xs,bc0,bh0*1.2],[g.xMin,bc0,bh0],[g.x.fanEnd||0,bc0,bh0],[g.x.turbEnd,(te.rt*1.05+oR)/2,(oR-te.rt*1.05)/2*0.8],[g.x6,nz.rtp*0.5,nz.rtp*0.42],[nz.x7,nz.rtp*0.5,nz.rtp*0.42],[nz.xT,nz.r8*0.5,nz.r8*0.42],[nz.xE,nz.r9*0.5,nz.r9*0.42],[xe,nz.r9*0.6,nz.r9*0.55]],seg:[[g.xMin,V0v,"cold"],[g.x6,Vin*0.8,"cold"]].concat(tail)});}
    STR.push({f:1,path:corePts,seg:[[0,V0v,"cold"],[bu.x0,150,"cold"],[bu.x1,45,"hot"],[g.x.turbEnd,260,"hot"]].concat(tail)});
  }
  finishFlow();
  function finishFlow(){STR.forEach(s=>{s.xs=s.path[0][0];s.xe=s.path[s.path.length-1][0];});PS.forEach(q=>resetP(q,true));}
}
function resetP(q,rnd){let i=0;const rr=Math.random();let acc=0;for(i=0;i<STR.length-1;i++){acc+=STR[i].f;if(rr<acc)break;}q.s=i;const s=STR[i];q.x=rnd?s.xs+Math.random()*(s.xe-s.xs):s.xs-Math.random()*0.2;q.u=Math.random()*2-1;q.t=Math.random()*TAU;}
let pending3D=false;function schedule3D(){pending3D=true;}

function pick(e){if(!eng)return;const rct=renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();mv.x=((e.clientX-rct.left)/rct.width)*2-1;mv.y=-((e.clientY-rct.top)/rct.height)*2+1;rc.setFromCamera(mv,cam);
  const hs=rc.intersectObjects(eng.children,true).filter(h=>h.object.userData.name);if(!hs.length){tip.style.display="none";return;}
  const h=hs.find(h=>h.object.material.opacity>=0.5)||hs[0];tip.innerHTML=h.object.userData.name+(h.object.userData.info?"<small>"+h.object.userData.info+"</small>":"");tip.style.display="block";
  let lx=e.clientX-rct.left+14,ly=e.clientY-rct.top+12;if(lx+tip.offsetWidth>rct.width-8)lx=e.clientX-rct.left-tip.offsetWidth-10;if(ly+tip.offsetHeight>rct.height-8)ly=e.clientY-rct.top-tip.offsetHeight-10;tip.style.left=Math.max(4,lx)+"px";tip.style.top=Math.max(4,ly)+"px";}
renderer.domElement.addEventListener("pointermove",pick);renderer.domElement.addEventListener("pointerdown",pick);renderer.domElement.addEventListener("pointerleave",()=>tip.style.display="none");
$("vCut").onchange=e=>{CUT=e.target.checked;schedule3D();};$("vFlow").onchange=e=>{pts.visible=e.target.checked;};$("vRot").onchange=e=>{ctrl.autoRotate=e.target.checked;};$("vReset").onclick=()=>fit(true);$("vQual").onchange=e=>setQuality(e.target.value);
const clk=new THREE.Clock();let crankA=0;
let tPrev=0;function loop(tNow){requestAnimationFrame(loop);if(tPrev)govern(tNow-tPrev);tPrev=tNow;const dt=Math.min(clk.getDelta(),0.05);
  if(pending3D&&R){pending3D=false;build3D(R);}
  if(eng)eng.children.forEach(o=>{if(o.userData.w)o.rotation.x+=o.userData.w*dt;});
  if(PISTONS.length){crankA+=dt*3.4*(OD.show3D&&ODR&&ODR.ok&&ODR.rpm?ODR.rpm/P.rpm:1);for(const c of PISTONS){const t=crankA-c.phase;c.p.position.y=c.top-c.S*(1-Math.cos(t))/2;const ph=((t-c.fire)%(4*PI)+4*PI)%(4*PI);c.glow.material.opacity=ph<0.9*PI?0.85*(1-ph/(0.9*PI)):0;}}
  if(pts.visible&&STR.length){for(let i=0;i<NP;i++){const q=PS[i];let s=STR[q.s];let sg=s.seg[s.seg.length-1];for(const z of s.seg){if(q.x<z[0]){sg=z;break;}}q.x+=sg[1]*KV*dt;if(q.x>s.xe){resetP(q,false);s=STR[q.s];sg=s.seg[0];}
    const p=ip(s.path,q.x),rr=Math.max(0.005,p[1]+q.u*p[2]),c=FC[sg[2]];ppos[i*3]=q.x;ppos[i*3+1]=Math.sin(q.t)*rr;ppos[i*3+2]=Math.cos(q.t)*rr;pcol[i*3]=c.r;pcol[i*3+1]=c.g;pcol[i*3+2]=c.b;}
    pg.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;pg.attributes.color.needsUpdate=true;}
  ctrl.update();renderer.render(scene,cam);}

function odSync(){

Fly it somewhere else, or throttle it back, and the solver re-matches the spools, airflow"+(isFan(P)?" and bypass ratio.":(fam(P)==="gtshaft"?" and power turbine, with the output shaft held at constant speed by the governor.":"."))
    :P.config==="piston"?"The displacement and propeller are now fixed. Change altitude, airspeed, throttle and rpm to see how manifold pressure, power, fuel burn and thrust respond. A naturally aspirated engine loses power with altitude; a turbocharged one holds it to its critical altitude."
    :"The motor, battery and propeller are now fixed. Electric power doesn't fade with altitude the way a combustion engine's does, but the propeller's thrust still changes with air density and airspeed.";
}

  if(q==="design"){Object.assign(OD,{alt:P.alt,mach:P.mach,dISA:P.dISA||0,mode:"T4",T4:P.T4,thr:1,rpmFrac:1,mix:P.mixture||"power"});}else{OD.mode="max";OD.thr=1;OD.rpmFrac=1;}
  odSync();odRun(true);});
function defaultLimits(){const cruise=P.alt>3000;if(!isGT(P))return{};const L={T4max:Math.round(P.T4+(cruise?(fam(P)==="jet"?120:100):0)),nHmax:1.05,nLmax:1.05,T3max:Math.round(P.trj?Math.max(1050,R.S["3"].Tt+20):R.S["3"].Tt+(cruise?80:20))};if(fam(P)==="gtshaft")L.PWmax=Math.round(R.Pshaft*(cruise?1.6:1.0)/1000)*1000;return L;}
function odOnDesignChange(firstTime,famChanged){
  if(!R||!R.ok||!R.ref){ODR=null;renderOD();return;}
  OD.x=null;
  if(!OD.lim||!OD.limTouched||famChanged)OD.lim=defaultLimits();
  if(firstTime||famChanged){const cruise=P.alt>3000;if(hasProp(P))Object.assign(OD,P.mach<0.1?{alt:P.config==="turboprop"?6000:2500,mach:P.config==="turboprop"?0.45:0.2}:{alt:0,mach:0.15});else if(fam(P)==="gtshaft")Object.assign(OD,{alt:3000,mach:0.15});else Object.assign(OD,cruise?{alt:0,mach:0.25}:{alt:9000,mach:Math.min(0.9,Math.max(0.6,P.mach||0.8))});OD.T4=P.T4;OD.mode="max";OD.thr=1;OD.rpmFrac=1;OD.mix=P.mixture||"power";OD.show3D=false;}
  if(isGT(P)&&OD.T4>OD.lim.T4max)OD.T4=OD.lim.T4max;
  odSync();odRun(false);
}
function odCond(T4){return{alt:OD.alt,mach:OD.mach,dISA:OD.dISA,T4:T4,ab:OD.abLit&&P.ab,T7:P.T7};}
function odNonGT(thr,rpmFrac,mix){const c=Object.assign(odCond(0),{thr,rpmFrac,mixture:mix});if(fam(P)==="ram")return Cycle.odRam(R.ref,Object.assign({},c,{phi:P.phi*thr}));if(P.config==="rocket")return Object.assign(Rocket.odRocket(R.ref,Object.assign({},c,{thr:isLiq(P)?thr:1})),{alt:OD.alt});return P.config==="piston"?Powertrain.odPiston(R.ref,c):Powertrain.odElectric(R.ref,c);}
let odTimer=null;
function odRun(userChange){
  if(!R||!R.ref)return;
  if(!odGT()){ODMAX=odNonGT(1,1,"power");ODR=odNonGT(OD.thr,OD.rpmFrac,OD.mix);}
  else{ODMAX=Cycle.solveMax(R.ref,odCond(OD.lim.T4max),OD.lim,OD.x);
    if(OD.mode==="max")ODR=ODMAX;else ODR=Cycle.solveOD(R.ref,odCond(OD.T4),ODMAX&&ODMAX.ok?ODMAX.x:OD.x);
    if(ODR&&ODR.ok)OD.x=ODR.x;}
  renderOD();if(OD.show3D)refreshFlow();
  clearTimeout(odTimer);odTimer=setTimeout(()=>{computeHook();renderMaps();renderHook();},userChange?120:0);
  if(!userChange)scheduleEnvelope();
}
const outOf=o=>isPow(P)?o.Pshaft:o.Fn;
const ODMET=[
 {label:p=>p.config==="piston"||p.config==="electric"||p.config==="turboprop"?"Thrust":"Net thrust",u:"force",get:o=>o.Fn,des:()=>R.Fn,show:()=>P.config!=="turboshaft"},
 {label:p=>p.config==="piston"?"Brake power":"Shaft power",u:"kw",get:o=>o.Pshaft,des:()=>R.Pshaft,show:()=>fam(P)!=="jet"},

 {label:p=>p.config==="piston"?"BSFC":"Power-specific fuel use",u:"psfc",get:o=>o.PSFC,des:()=>R.PSFC,show:()=>fam(P)==="gtshaft"||P.config==="piston"},

 {label:"Overall pressure ratio",u:"none",dp:1,get:o=>o.OPR,des:()=>R.OPR,show:odGT},

 {label:"Endurance at this power",u:"time",get:o=>o.endur,show:()=>P.config==="electric"},
