extPatchODMET();
const odm=$("odMetrics");ODMET.forEach(m=>{const d=document.createElement("div");d.className="m";d.innerHTML='<div class="k"></div><div class="v"></div><div class="d"></div>';odm.appendChild(d);m.el=d;});
const LIMTXT={T4:"the turbine inlet temperature limit",NH:"the core speed limit",NL:"the fan speed limit",T3:"the compressor exit temperature limit",PW:"the shaft power (flat rating) limit, which protects the gearbox and shafts",map:"the edge of the component maps (the solver can't find a matched point beyond it)"};
function renderOD(){
  const notes=[];
  if(!ODR||!ODR.ok){ODMET.forEach(m=>{m.el.querySelector(".v").textContent="–";m.el.querySelector(".d").textContent="";});$("odNotes").innerHTML='<div class="note bad">The engine could not be matched at this condition. Try a lower altitude, a different Mach number, or a less extreme power setting.</div>';return;}
  const o=ODR;
  ODMET.forEach(m=>{m.el.hidden=m.show?!m.show():false;if(m.el.hidden)return;const v=m.get(o);m.el.querySelector(".k").textContent=typeof m.label==="function"?m.label(P):m.label;
    const unit=m.unit||unitTxt(m.u);m.el.querySelector(".v").innerHTML=(isFinite(v)?(m.dp!==undefined?num(cv(v,m.u),m.dp):fmt(v,m.u)):"–")+"<small>"+(isFinite(v)?unit:"")+"</small>";
    const dd=m.el.querySelector(".d");dd.className="d";dd.textContent=isFinite(v)?(m.sub&&m.sub.startsWith("of")?m.sub:""):(m.sub&&!m.sub.startsWith("of")?m.sub:"");if(m.des&&isFinite(v)){const b=m.des();if(isFinite(b)&&b){const pc=(v-b)/Math.abs(b)*100;dd.textContent=(pc>0?"+":"")+pc.toFixed(1)+"% vs design point";}}
    if(m.sm&&isFinite(v)&&v<10){dd.classList.add("bad");dd.textContent="low: risk of surge";}});
  if(odGT()){
    if(OD.mode==="max"&&o.limiting)notes.push('<div class="note ins">Max rating here is set by <b>'+LIMTXT[o.limiting]+"</b>"+(o.limiting!=="T4"?", reached at T4 = "+fmt(o.T4,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp"):"")+".</div>");
    if(OD.mode==="T4"){const over=[];if(o.T4>OD.lim.T4max+0.5)over.push("T4");if(o.nH>OD.lim.nHmax+1e-4)over.push("core speed");if(o.nL&&o.nL>OD.lim.nLmax+1e-4)over.push("fan speed");if(o.T3>OD.lim.T3max+0.5)over.push("T3");if(OD.lim.PWmax&&o.Pshaft>OD.lim.PWmax*1.0001)over.push("shaft power");if(over.length)notes.push('<div class="note warn">This setting exceeds the '+over.join(", ")+" limit.</div>");}
    if(fam(P)==="jet"){const dF=o.Fn/R.Fn;notes.push('<div class="note ins">Thrust is <b>'+(dF*100).toFixed(0)+"%</b> of the design-point thrust"+(o.V0>1?", with ram drag taking "+fmt(o.Fram,"force")+" "+unitTxt("force"):"")+". "+(isFan(P)?"Bypass ratio moves from "+R.BPR.toFixed(2)+" to "+o.BPR.toFixed(2)+(o.BPR>R.BPR?": the core is running relatively slower than the fan, so more air goes around it.":": the core is swallowing a larger share of the air."):"")+"</div>");}
    else notes.push('<div class="note ins">Shaft power is <b>'+(o.Pshaft/R.Pshaft*100).toFixed(0)+"%</b> of the design-point value"+(o.prop?(isFinite(o.prop.eta)?", and the propeller turns "+(o.prop.eta*100).toFixed(0)+"% of it into thrust power at "+(o.prop.theta75*180/PI).toFixed(1)+"° pitch.":", producing "+fmt(o.prop.T,"force")+" "+unitTxt("force")+" of static thrust."):".")+"</div>");
    const off=[];[["fan","Fan"],["lpc","Booster"],["hpc",isFan(P)?"HPC":"Compressor"]].forEach(([k,n])=>{const c=o.comp[k];if(!c||!R.ref[k])return;const m=Cycle.MAPS[R.ref[k].map];if(c.NcMap<m.Nc[0]-1e-6||c.NcMap>m.Nc[m.Nc.length-1]+1e-6)off.push(n);});
    if(off.length)notes.push('<div class="note warn">'+off.join(" and ")+" corrected speed is outside the map's data, so values are extrapolated and less reliable.</div>");
    if(o.ab)notes.push('<div class="note ins">Afterburner lit: the variable nozzle opens to keep the turbines matched as if dry, so the core is unaffected while thrust and fuel flow jump.</div>');
  }else if(P.config==="piston"){const sl=ODMAX.Pshaft/R.Pshaft;notes.push('<div class="note ins">Full throttle here gives <b>'+(sl*100).toFixed(0)+"%</b> of rated power"+(P.induction==="turbo"?(OD.alt<=P.critAlt?": the turbocharger is holding manifold pressure (below its critical altitude).":": above the critical altitude, so the turbo can no longer hold full manifold pressure."):": a naturally aspirated engine loses power roughly with air density.")+" Manifold pressure is "+fmt(o.MAP,"map")+" "+unitTxt("map")+".</div>");
    if(o.prop)notes.push('<div class="note ins">'+(isFinite(o.prop.eta)?"The propeller converts "+(o.prop.eta*100).toFixed(0)+"% of the shaft power into thrust power.":"At standstill the propeller makes "+fmt(o.prop.T,"force")+" "+unitTxt("force")+".")+"</div>");}
  else{notes.push('<div class="note ins">At this power the battery lasts <b>'+fmt(o.endur,"time")+" min</b> to the usable limit"+(o.V0>1?", covering "+fmt(o.range,"dist")+" "+unitTxt("dist"):"")+". Drawing less power stretches endurance almost in proportion.</div>");}
  $("odNotes").innerHTML=notes.join("");
}
function computeHook(){
  HOOK=[];if(!ODMAX||!ODMAX.ok)return;
  if(!odGT()){for(let i=0;i<=14;i++){const t=1-0.8*i/14,o=odNonGT(t,OD.rpmFrac,OD.mix);if(o.ok&&o.Pshaft>0)HOOK.push(o);}return;}
  let x=ODMAX.x;const T4hi=ODMAX.T4,T4lo=Math.max(700,P.T4*0.55);
  for(let i=0;i<=16;i++){const T=T4hi-(T4hi-T4lo)*i/16;const o=Cycle.solveOD(R.ref,odCond(T),x);if(!o.ok)break;x=o.x;HOOK.push(o);}
}
function hookAxes(){const f=fam(P);if(f==="jet")return{x:["Fn","force","Net thrust"],y:["TSFC","tsfc","TSFC"]};if(P.config==="electric")return{x:["Pshaft","kw","Shaft power"],y:["Fn","force","Thrust"]};return{x:["Pshaft","kw",P.config==="piston"?"Brake power":"Shaft power"],y:["PSFC","psfc",P.config==="piston"?"BSFC":"Power-specific fuel use"]};}
function renderHook(){
  const ax=hookAxes(),pts=HOOK.map(o=>({x:cv(o[ax.x[0]],ax.x[1]),y:cv(o[ax.y[0]],ax.y[1])})).filter(p=>isFinite(p.x)&&isFinite(p.y));
  const ds=[{label:"Throttle line",data:pts,borderColor:"#D85A30",backgroundColor:"#D85A30",showLine:true,pointRadius:2,borderWidth:2.5}];
  if(ODR&&ODR.ok)ds.push({label:"Current point",data:[{x:cv(ODR[ax.x[0]],ax.x[1]),y:cv(ODR[ax.y[0]],ax.y[1])}],backgroundColor:css("--ink"),pointRadius:6});
  const o=chartBase();o.scales={x:axis(ax.x[2]+", "+unitTxt(ax.x[1])),y:axis(ax.y[2]+", "+unitTxt(ax.y[1]))};
  if(!hookC)hookC=new Chart($("hookChart"),{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o});else{hookC.data.datasets=ds;hookC.options=o;hookC.update();}
  $("hookSub").textContent=P.config==="electric"?"Thrust against shaft power at this flight condition. An electric motor's efficiency stays nearly flat across the range, so the curve shape comes from the propeller.":P.config==="piston"?"Fuel use per unit power as the throttle closes at this rpm and mixture. Part throttle adds pumping losses, so BSFC rises at low power.":"Fuel consumption as the engine is throttled at this flight condition. It is lowest somewhat below full power, which is why aircraft cruise there.";
}
function renderMaps(){
  const g=$("mapGrid");if(!R||!R.ref||!odGT()){Object.values(mapCharts).forEach(c=>c.destroy());mapCharts={};g.innerHTML="";g.dataset.key="";return;}
  const comps=[["fan","Fan map"],["lpc","Booster map"],["hpc",isFan(P)?"High-pressure compressor map":"Compressor map"]].filter(([k])=>R.ref[k]);
  const key=comps.map(c=>c[0]).join(",");
  if(g.dataset.key!==key){Object.values(mapCharts).forEach(c=>c.destroy());mapCharts={};g.innerHTML=comps.map(([k,t])=>'<div><h3 class="h3">'+t+'</h3><div class="chartbox" style="height:260px"><canvas id="map_'+k+'" aria-label="'+t+'"></canvas></div></div>').join("");g.dataset.key=key;}
  comps.forEach(([k])=>{
    const sl=Cycle.speedLines(R.ref[k]),ds=[];
    sl.lines.forEach((L,i)=>ds.push({label:i===0?"Corrected speed lines":"",data:L.pts.map(p=>({x:cv(p.Wc,"flow"),y:p.PR})),lbls:L.pts.map((p,j)=>j===L.pts.length-1&&(i%2===0||i===sl.lines.length-1)?Math.round(L.rel*100)+"%":""),borderColor:css("--line"),backgroundColor:css("--line"),showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:1.2}));
    ds.push({label:"Surge line",data:sl.stall.map(p=>({x:cv(p.Wc,"flow"),y:p.PR})),borderColor:"#E24B4A",backgroundColor:"#E24B4A",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:2,borderDash:[6,4]});
    if(HOOK.length)ds.push({label:"Operating line",data:HOOK.map(o=>o.comp&&o.comp[k]).filter(Boolean).map(c=>({x:cv(c.Wc,"flow"),y:c.PR})),borderColor:"#D85A30",backgroundColor:"#D85A30",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:2.5});
    ds.push({label:"Design point",data:[{x:cv(sl.design.Wc,"flow"),y:sl.design.PR}],backgroundColor:"transparent",borderColor:css("--ink"),borderWidth:2,pointRadius:6,pointStyle:"rect"});
    if(ODR&&ODR.ok&&ODR.comp&&ODR.comp[k])ds.push({label:"Current point",data:[{x:cv(ODR.comp[k].Wc,"flow"),y:ODR.comp[k].PR}],backgroundColor:css("--ink"),pointRadius:5});
    const o=chartBase();o.scales={x:axis("Corrected flow, "+unitTxt("flow")),y:axis("Pressure ratio")};o.plugins.legend.labels.filter=it=>it.text!=="";o.plugins.legend.labels.font={size:11};
    if(!mapCharts[k])mapCharts[k]=new Chart($("map_"+k),{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o,plugins:[labelPlugin]});else{mapCharts[k].data.datasets=ds;mapCharts[k].options=o;mapCharts[k].update();}
  });
}
let envTimer=null;
function scheduleEnvelope(){clearTimeout(envTimer);envTimer=setTimeout(computeEnvelope,700);}
function envAxes(){const f=fam(P);let mx,st;if(hasProp(P)||f==="gtshaft"){mx=P.config==="turboprop"?0.65:P.config==="turboshaft"?0.4:P.config==="electric"?0.35:0.45;st=0.05;}else if(P.config==="separate"){mx=0.9;st=0.05;}else{mx=Math.max(1.2,Math.ceil((P.mach*1.15)*10)/10);st=0.1;}
  const machs=[];for(let m=0;m<=mx+1e-9;m+=st)machs.push(Math.round(m*100)/100);
  const alts=f==="jet"?(P.config==="separate"?[0,3000,6000,9000,11000,13000]:[0,3000,6000,9000,11000,15000]):P.config==="piston"||P.config==="electric"?[0,1500,3000,4500,6000,7500]:[0,2000,4000,6000,8000,10000];return{machs,alts};}
const ENVY={jet:[["Fn","Net thrust"],["TSFC","TSFC"],["W","Airflow"],["nH","Core speed (% of design)"],["T4","T4 reached"]],gtshaft:[["Pshaft","Shaft power"],["PSFC","Power-specific fuel use"],["Fn","Thrust"],["nH","Gas-generator speed (% of design)"],["T4","T4 reached"]],piston:[["Pshaft","Brake power"],["Fn","Thrust"],["PSFC","BSFC"],["MAP","Manifold pressure"]],electric:[["Fn","Thrust"],["etaP","Propeller efficiency"],["Pel","Battery power"]]};
const ENVU={Fn:"force",TSFC:"tsfc",W:"flow",nH:"pct",T4:"temp",Pshaft:"kw",PSFC:"psfc",MAP:"map",etaP:"pct",Pel:"kw"};
function fillEnvY(){const s=$("envY"),cur=s.value,opts=ENVY[fam(P)].filter(o=>!(o[0]==="Fn"&&P.config==="turboshaft"));s.innerHTML=opts.map(o=>'<option value="'+o[0]+'">'+o[1]+'</option>').join("");s.value=opts.some(o=>o[0]===cur)?cur:opts[0][0];}
function computeEnvelope(){
  if(!R||!R.ref)return;fillEnvY();const token=++envToken,{machs,alts}=envAxes(),ref=R.ref,lim=Object.assign({},OD.lim),res=alts.map(()=>machs.map(()=>null));let ai=0,mi=0,x=null,done=0,total=alts.length*machs.length;const gt=odGT();
  $("envMsg").textContent="Computing 0%";
  const step=()=>{if(token!==envToken)return;const t0=performance.now();
    while(performance.now()-t0<30&&ai<alts.length){let o;const cond={alt:alts[ai],mach:machs[mi],dISA:OD.dISA,ab:OD.abLit&&P.ab,T7:P.T7};
      if(gt)o=Cycle.solveMax(ref,cond,lim,x);else{const c=Object.assign({},cond,{thr:1,rpmFrac:1,mixture:"power"});o=P.config==="piston"?Powertrain.odPiston(ref,c):P.config==="electric"?Powertrain.odElectric(ref,c):Cycle.odRam(ref,Object.assign({},c,{phi:P.phi}));if(o.ok)o.etaP=o.prop?o.prop.eta:NaN;}
      res[ai][mi]=o&&o.ok?o:null;x=o&&o.ok?o.x:null;done++;mi++;if(mi>=machs.length){mi=0;ai++;x=null;}}
    $("envMsg").textContent=ai<alts.length?"Computing "+Math.round(done/total*100)+"%":"";
    ENV={machs,alts,res};renderEnvelope();if(ai<alts.length)setTimeout(step,0);};
  step();
}
$("envY").onchange=()=>renderEnvelope();
function renderEnvelope(){
  if(!ENV)return;const k=$("envY").value,U=ENVU[k],lab=(ENVY[fam(P)].find(o=>o[0]===k)||[k,k])[1];
  const cols=["#85B7EB","#378ADD","#185FA5","#5DCAA5","#1D9E75","#0F6E56"];
  const ds=ENV.alts.map((a,i)=>({label:fmt(a,"alt")+" "+unitTxt("alt"),data:ENV.machs.map((m,j)=>{const o=ENV.res[i][j];return o&&isFinite(o[k])?{x:m,y:cv(o[k],U)}:null;}).filter(Boolean),borderColor:cols[i%cols.length],backgroundColor:cols[i%cols.length],showLine:true,pointRadius:1.5,borderWidth:2}));
  if(ODR&&ODR.ok&&(odGT()?OD.mode==="max":OD.thr>=0.999&&OD.rpmFrac>=0.999)&&isFinite(ODR[k]))ds.push({label:"Current point",data:[{x:OD.mach,y:cv(ODR[k],U)}],backgroundColor:css("--ink"),pointRadius:5});
  const o=chartBase();o.scales={x:axis("Flight Mach number"),y:axis(lab+(U!=="pct"?", "+unitTxt(U):""))};
  if(!envC)envC=new Chart($("envChart"),{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o});else{envC.data.datasets=ds;envC.options=o;envC.update();}
  $("envSub").textContent=(odGT()?"Max-rating ":"Full-power ")+lab.toLowerCase()+" across Mach number at "+ENV.alts.length+" altitudes"+(odGT()?", with the operating limits applied at every point":"")+(OD.dISA?" on a ΔISA "+(OD.dISA>0?"+":"")+fmt(OD.dISA,"dT")+" "+unitTxt("dT")+" day":"")+".";
}
function slip(o){if(!o||!o.prop||!hasProp(P))return{};const a=Cycle.isa(o===R?P.alt:OD.alt,o===R?(P.dISA||0):OD.dISA),rho=a.P/(287.05*a.T),A=PI*P.propD*P.propD/4,T=Math.max(0,o.prop.T),V=o.V0||0,Vs=Math.sqrt(V*V+2*T/(rho*A));return{Vs,Vdisk:(V+Vs)/2};}
function flowVel(){const use=OD.show3D&&ODR&&ODR.ok?ODR:R;const o=use;const base={V0:o.V0||0,Vb:o.nozByp?o.nozByp.V:0,Vc:o.nozCore?o.nozCore.V:0,BPR:o.BPR||0,ab:!!o.ab,nL:use===R?1:(o.nL||1),nH:use===R?1:(o.nH||1),nP:use===R?1:(o.rpm?o.rpm/P.rpm:1)};return Object.assign(base,slip(o));}
function refreshFlow(){if(!R||!R.ok||!R.geo)return;buildFlow(R,flowVel());setSpoolSpeeds();
  $("stName").textContent=OD.show3D&&ODR&&ODR.ok?presetName+" · at "+fmt(OD.alt,"alt")+" "+unitTxt("alt")+", M "+OD.mach.toFixed(2):presetName;}
function renderODValidation(){
  const LB=0.45359237,FT=0.3048,R2K=x=>x/1.8,HP=745.699872,base={effBasis:"isentropic",coolAuto:false,cool:0,bleed:0,effMech:1,effBurner:1,dPbypass:0,dPcore:0,dPabDry:0,inletRec:1,sizeBy:"W",hFuel:0};
  const tj=withDefaults(Object.assign({},base,{config:"turbojet",alt:0,mach:1e-6,W:147*LB,T4:R2K(2370),OPR:13.5,effHPC:.83,effHPT:.86,dPburner:.03,CvCore:.99,nozzleCore:"cd"}));
  const tf=withDefaults(Object.assign({},base,{config:"separate",alt:35000*FT,mach:.8,W:300*LB,T4:R2K(2857),FPR:1.685,LPCPR:1.935,OPR:1.685*1.935*9.369,BPR:5.105,effFan:.8948,effLPC:.9243,effHPC:.8707,effHPT:.8888,effLPT:.8996,dPburner:.054,CvCore:.9933,CvByp:.9939,nozzleCore:"conv",nozzleByp:"conv"}));
  const sets=[[tj,[{alt:5000*FT,mach:.2,T4:R2K(2300)},{alt:30000*FT,mach:.8,T4:R2K(2370)},{alt:0,mach:1e-6,T4:R2K(2100)}],REF_OD.tj],[tf,[{alt:35000*FT,mach:.8,T4:R2K(2700)},{alt:20000*FT,mach:.5,T4:R2K(2800)},{alt:0,mach:.25,T4:R2K(2750)}],REF_OD.tf]];
  let h="<thead><tr><th>Off-design case</th><th>Quantity</th><th>pyCycle</th><th>This studio</th><th>Difference</th></tr></thead><tbody>";

/* ---------- update pipeline ---------- */
let chartTimer=null,lastCfg=null,lastFam=null;
function update(kind){
  if(kind==="slider"&&ps.value!=="custom"){ps.value="custom";presetName="Custom design";}
  if(kind==="refit"||kind==="init")needFit=2;
  if(kind==="slider"&&((rowsById.Fn&&(document.activeElement===rowsById.Fn.rng||document.activeElement===rowsById.Fn.nb))||(rowsById.Pshaft&&(document.activeElement===rowsById.Pshaft.rng||document.activeElement===rowsById.Pshaft.nb))||(rowsById.propD&&document.activeElement===rowsById.propD.rng)))needFit=Math.max(needFit,1);
  syncAll();
  R=solveAny(P);
  if(R.ok&&P.config==="mixed"){P.FPR=R.FPR;}
  $("stArch").textContent=archLabel(P)+(P.geared?" · geared":"")+(R.ab&&!P.trj?" · afterburning":"")+(P.config==="piston"&&P.induction==="turbo"?" · turbocharged":"");
  $("stName").textContent=presetName;
  renderNotes(R);
  if(R.ok){renderMetrics(R);renderSpools(R);renderEnergy(R);renderTables(R);renderTS(R);schedule3D();}
  const famChanged=lastFam!==null&&lastFam!==fam(P);lastFam=fam(P);
  if(kind==="units"){odSync();renderOD();renderHook();renderMaps();renderEnvelope();}else odOnDesignChange(kind==="init",famChanged||kind==="refit");
  if(kind!=="slider"||lastCfg!==P.config){fillTradeSelects();lastCfg=P.config;}
  clearTimeout(chartTimer);chartTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(R.ok){renderTrade();renderCarpet();}if(kind==="units"||kind==="init"){renderSaved();renderValidation();renderODValidation();}},kind==="slider"?(hasProp(P)?260:160):0);
  afterUpdate(kind); // preliminary design, requirements and margins (ui/06, ui/07)
}
$("pin").onclick=()=>{BASE={p:deep(P),r:R};$("pin").textContent="Baseline pinned ✓";setTimeout(()=>$("pin").textContent="Pin as baseline",1600);update("cfg");};

      ds=[{label:"Thrust",data:r.trace.map(q=>({x:q.t,y:cv(q.F,"force")})),borderColor:"#D85A30",backgroundColor:"#D85A30",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:2.5,yAxisID:"y"},{label:r.hyb?"Mixture ratio O/F":"Chamber pressure",data:r.trace.map(q=>({x:q.t,y:r.hyb?q.OF:cv(q.Pc,"mpa")})),borderColor:"#378ADD",backgroundColor:"#378ADD",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:2,yAxisID:"y2"}];
      o.scales={x:axis("Time, s"),y:axis("Thrust, "+unitTxt("force")),y2:axis(r.hyb?"O/F":"Chamber pressure, "+unitTxt("mpa"),"right")};o.scales.y2.grid={drawOnChartArea:false};}
    else{$("hTs").textContent="Specific impulse and thrust against altitude";$("tsSub").textContent="The fixed nozzle at full throttle, climbing from sea level to vacuum. Below the design altitude the exit is overexpanded and ambient pressure pushes back on it; the dashed line marks where flow separation is likely.";
      const A=[],B=[],S=[];for(let i=0;i<=30;i++){const alt=i/30*80000,q=Rocket.odRocket(R.ref,{alt,thr:1});A.push({x:cv(alt,"alt"),y:q.Isp});B.push({x:cv(alt,"alt"),y:cv(q.Fn,"force")});if(q.sep)S.push({x:cv(alt,"alt"),y:q.Isp});}
      ds=[{label:"Specific impulse",data:A,borderColor:"#D85A30",backgroundColor:"#D85A30",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:2.5,yAxisID:"y"},{label:"Thrust",data:B,borderColor:"#378ADD",backgroundColor:"#378ADD",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:2,yAxisID:"y2"}];
      if(S.length)ds.push({label:"Separation likely",data:S,borderColor:"#E24B4A",backgroundColor:"#E24B4A",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:4,borderDash:[4,4],yAxisID:"y"});
      o.scales={x:axis("Altitude, "+unitTxt("alt")),y:axis("Specific impulse, s"),y2:axis("Thrust, "+unitTxt("force"),"right")};o.scales.y2.grid={drawOnChartArea:false};}
  }else{$("hTs").textContent="Temperature–entropy diagram";$("tsSub").textContent="With no turbomachinery, compression comes from slowing the air (shocks add entropy), heat goes in at the combustor, and the nozzle expands the gas back toward ambient pressure.";
    const S=r.S,s0=S["0"].s,ks=["0","2","3","4","10"].filter(k=>S[k]);
    ds=[{label:p.config==="scramjet"?"Static state":"Total state",data:ks.map(k=>({x:cv(S[k].s-s0,"ent"),y:cv(S[k].Tt,"temp")})),lbls:ks,borderColor:"#D85A30",backgroundColor:"#D85A30",showLine:true,pointRadius:3,borderWidth:2}];
    o.scales={x:axis("Entropy rise from freestream, "+unitTxt("ent")),y:axis("Temperature, "+unitTxt("temp"))};}
  if(!tsC)tsC=new Chart($("tsChart"),{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o,plugins:[labelPlugin]});else{tsC.data.datasets=ds;tsC.options=o;tsC.update();}};}
{const _f=carpetDef;carpetDef=function(){const sc=(v,ks)=>ks.map(k=>v*k),sh=(v,ds)=>ds.map(d=>v+d),p=P,isp=["Isp","isp","Fuel specific impulse"],fs=["Fs","fs","Specific thrust"];
  if(p.config==="ramjet")return{aId:"phi",bId:"M3",A:sc(p.phi,[0.6,0.8,1,1.15,1.3]).filter(v=>v<=1.3),B:sh(p.M3,[-0.1,-0.05,0,0.05,0.1]).filter(v=>v>=0.1&&v<=0.45),x:fs,y:isp,note:"Up is more efficient; right is a smaller engine for the same thrust."};
  if(p.config==="scramjet")return{aId:"psi",bId:"phi",A:sh(p.psi,[-2,-1,0,1,2]).filter(v=>v>=3),B:sc(p.phi,[0.6,0.8,1,1.2]).filter(v=>v<=1.3),x:fs,y:isp,note:"Up is more efficient; right is a smaller engine for the same thrust."};
  if(p.config==="pulsejet")return{aId:"tubeL",bId:"phi",A:sc(p.tubeL,[0.7,0.85,1,1.15,1.3]),B:sc(p.phi,[0.8,0.9,1,1.1,1.2]).filter(v=>v<=1.3),x:["Fn","force","Static thrust"],y:["TSFC","tsfc","TSFC"],note:"Lower is better on fuel; right is more thrust."};
  if(isRkt(p)){const ax={x:["IspSL","isp","Sea-level specific impulse"],y:["IspV","isp","Vacuum specific impulse"]};
    if(rkind(p)==="solid")return Object.assign({aId:"AL",bId:"Pc",A:sh(p.AL,[-0.08,-0.04,0,0.02,0.04]).filter(v=>v>=0&&v<=0.2),B:sc(p.Pc,[0.5,0.75,1,1.5]).filter(v=>v<=1.5e7),note:"Up is better in vacuum, right is better at sea level."},ax);
    if(p.epsMode==="opt")return Object.assign({aId:"Pc",bId:"OF",A:sc(p.Pc,[0.5,0.75,1,1.5]).filter(v=>v<=3.2e7),B:sc(p.OF,[0.85,1,1.15]),note:"With the area ratio optimized for the design altitude, both chamber pressure and mixture ratio shift the balance between sea-level and vacuum performance."},ax);
    return Object.assign({aId:"eps",bId:"Pc",A:sc(p.eps,[0.4,0.7,1,1.5,2.5]).filter(v=>v>=2&&v<=300),B:sc(p.Pc,[0.5,0.75,1,1.5]).filter(v=>v<=3.2e7),note:"Up is better in vacuum, right is better at sea level: a bigger bell trades one for the other, and higher chamber pressure buys some of both."},ax);}
  return _f();};}
{const _f=cycleTxt;cycleTxt=function(s,r){const p=s.p;if(isRkt(p))return Rocket.PROP[p.rkt].name+" · "+fmt(p.Pc,"mpa")+" "+unitTxt("mpa")+" · ε "+num(p.eps,0)+" · Isp "+num(r.Isp,0)+" s";if(isRam(p))return p.config==="pulsejet"?num(p.tubeL,2)+" m tube":"M "+p.mach.toFixed(1)+" · φ "+p.phi.toFixed(2);return _f(s,r);};}
/* ---------- 3D models ---------- */
function shockMat(){return new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xE24B4A,transparent:true,opacity:0.18,side:THREE.DoubleSide,depthWrite:false});}
function latheM(pts,c,o,name,info,full){const v=pts.map(p=>new THREE.Vector2(Math.max(0.0005,p[1]),p[0]));const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.LatheGeometry(smoothPts(v),QL.seg,0,full?TAU:arc()),c instanceof THREE.Material?c:mat(c,CUT?o:1));m.rotation.z=-PI/2;return tag(m,name,info);}
function glowMesh(m,base,amp,hz){m.onBeforeRender=()=>{m.material.opacity=base+amp*(0.5+0.5*Math.sin(performance.now()/1000*TAU*hz));};return m;}
function extGeo3(r){if(r.kind==="rocket"&&r.pd&&r.pd.ct)return pdGeo3(r); // preliminary design draws the real geometry (ui/06)
 const p=P,parts=[],add=(o)=>parts.push({k:"mesh",obj:o}),flow=[];let g={};
  if(r.kind==="rocket"){const Dt=r.Dt,De=r.De,Dc=r.Dc,Lc=r.Lc,Ln=r.Ln,rt=Dt/2,re=De/2,rc=Dc/2,x0=0;
    const conv=[[x0,rc],[x0+Lc,rc],[x0+Lc+(rc-rt)*1.2,rt]],xT=conv[2][0],bell=[];for(let i=0;i<=16;i++){const t=i/16,x=xT+Ln*t,rr=rt+(re-rt)*(1-Math.pow(1-t,1.8));bell.push([x,rr]);}
    const xE=xT+Ln;let xS=x0;
    if(r.solid){const s=r.solid,Rg=s.Dg/2,Ri=s.Di/2,L=s.Ltot,gap=0.01*L,ns=p.nSeg;xS=-L;
      add(latheM([[-L-Rg*0.6,0.02],[-L-Rg*0.3,Rg*0.8],[-L,Rg*1.05],[0,Rg*1.05],[Rg*0.4,rc*1.1]],COL.cas,0.25,"Motor case","propellant "+fmt(r.mprop,"tonne")+" "+unitTxt("tonne")));
      for(let i=0;i<ns;i++){const a=-L+i*L/ns+gap,b=-L+(i+1)*L/ns-gap;add(latheM([[a,Ri],[a,Rg],[b,Rg],[b,Ri],[a,Ri]],COL.ox,0.7,"Grain segment "+(i+1),"core Ø "+fmt(s.Di,"len",US?0:2)+" "+unitTxt("len")+" · Kn "+s.Kn.toFixed(0)));}
      add(glowMesh(latheM([[-L,Ri*0.9],[0,Ri*0.9]],new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xEF9F27,transparent:true,opacity:0.3,side:THREE.DoubleSide,depthWrite:false}),1,"Burning core"),0.25,0.1,7));
      add(latheM([[0,rc*1.05],[conv[1][0]*0+Rg*0.4,rc]].concat([[xT,rt]]).concat(bell),COL.noz,0.85,"Nozzle","ε "+r.eps.toFixed(1)+" · exit "+fmt(r.pe,"pres")+" "+unitTxt("pres")));
      flow.push({f:1,path:[[-L,0,Ri*0.6],[0,0,Ri*0.6],[xT,0,rt*0.6],[xE,0,re*0.6],[xE+2.5*De,0,re*1.0]],seg:[[xT,120,"ab"],[1e9,r.Isp*9.80665,"ab"]]});
    }else{
      add(latheM(conv.concat(bell),COL.noz,0.85,"Thrust chamber and nozzle","Tc "+fmt(r.Tc,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp")+" · ε "+r.eps.toFixed(1)+" · exit Ø "+fmt(De,"len",US?1:2)+" "+unitTxt("len")));
      add(glowMesh(latheM([[x0+0.02*Lc,rc*0.92],[x0+Lc,rc*0.92],[xT,rt*0.95]],new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xEF9F27,transparent:true,opacity:0.3,side:THREE.DoubleSide,depthWrite:false}),1,"Combustion"),0.22,0.1,9));
      add(latheM([[x0-rc*0.55,0.01],[x0-rc*0.45,rc*0.75],[x0,rc*1.02]],COL.cas,0.6,"Injector and dome"));
      if(r.hyb){const h=r.hyb,Rg=h.Dpf/2*1.1,Lg=h.L,Lt=Math.max(0.3,h.mox_tot/Rocket.PROP[p.rkt].rhoOx/(PI*Rg*Rg));xS=-Lg-Lt-rc*0.6;
        add(latheM([[-Lg-rc*0.55,Rg],[-rc*0.2,Rg],[x0,rc]],COL.cas,0.3,"Combustion chamber"));add(latheM([[-Lg-rc*0.5,h.Dp0/2],[-Lg-rc*0.5,Rg*0.95],[-rc*0.25,Rg*0.95],[-rc*0.25,h.Dp0/2],[-Lg-rc*0.5,h.Dp0/2]],COL.fuel,0.8,"Fuel grain","port Ø "+fmt(h.Dp0,"len",US?1:3)+" "+unitTxt("len")));
        add(latheM([[-Lg-Lt-rc*0.6,0.01],[-Lg-Lt-rc*0.55,Rg*0.9],[-Lg-rc*0.7,Rg*0.9],[-Lg-rc*0.6,0.01]],COL.ox,0.3,"Oxidizer tank",fmt(h.mox_tot,"mass")+" "+unitTxt("mass")+" of "+(p.rkt==="lox_htpb"?"LOX":"N2O")));
        flow.push({f:1,path:[[-Lg-rc*0.6,0,h.Dp0*0.3],[-rc*0.2,0,h.Dp0*0.4],[x0+Lc,0,rc*0.5],[xT,0,rt*0.6],[xE,0,re*0.6],[xE+2.5*De,0,re]],seg:[[-Lg*0.6,40,"cold"],[xT,120,"ab"],[1e9,r.Isp*9.80665,"ab"]]});
      }else{const cy=p.rCycle,Rp=Math.max(rc*0.45,0.05),yP=rc*1.7;
        if(cy!=="pf"){const tp=new THREE.Group(),pw=" · pump power "+fmt(r.feed.Ppump,"power")+" "+unitTxt("power");tp.add(tag(cyl(Rp,x0-rc*0.4,x0+Lc*0.5,COL.hpc,"Pumps"),"Pumps (oxidizer and fuel)","discharge "+fmt(r.feed.Pd,"mpa")+" "+unitTxt("mpa")+pw));
          tp.add(tag(cyl(Rp*0.9,x0+Lc*0.5,x0+Lc*0.95,cy==="ep"?COL.cu:COL.hpt,"Turbine"),cy==="ep"?"Electric motors":"Turbine",(cy==="ep"?fmt(r.feed.Pbatt,"power")+" "+unitTxt("power")+" electrical":"pressure ratio "+(r.feed.PRt||0).toFixed(2))+pw));
          tp.position.y=yP;add(tp);
          if(cy==="gg"){const gg=tag(cyl(Rp*0.5,x0-rc*0.4,x0+Lc*0.1,COL.comb,"Gas generator"),"Gas generator","T "+fmt(p.Tgg,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp")+" · "+(r.mgg/r.mtot*100).toFixed(1)+"% of flow");gg.position.y=yP+Rp*1.6;add(gg);
            const ex=cyl(Rp*0.35,x0+Lc*0.95,xE*0.9,COL.noz,"Turbine exhaust (dumped overboard)");ex.position.y=yP;add(ex);}
          if(cy==="sc"){const pb=tag(cyl(Rp*0.7,x0-rc*1.2,x0-rc*0.3,COL.comb,"Preburner"),"Preburner","T "+fmt(p.Tpb,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp")+" · all of its gas continues to the main chamber");pb.position.y=yP*0.55;add(pb);}
          if(cy==="exp"){const j=latheM([[x0+Lc*0.1,rc*1.08],[x0+Lc,rc*1.08],[xT,rt*1.15],[xT+Ln*0.35,(rt+(re-rt)*0.5)*1.04]],COL.fuel,0.5,"Regenerative cooling jacket","heats the fuel to "+fmt(r.feed.Tturb,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp")+", which drives the turbine");add(j);}
          if(cy==="ep"){const b=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(Lc*0.8,Rp*1.4,Rp*2.4),mat(COL.ox,CUT?0.5:1));b.position.set(x0+Lc*0.4,yP+Rp*2.6,0);add(tag(b,"Battery pack",fmt(r.feed.mBatt,"mass")+" "+unitTxt("mass")));}
        }else{[1,-1].forEach(s=>{const sp=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(rc*0.8,24,16),mat(COL.cas,CUT?0.45:1));sp.position.set(x0-rc*2.2,s*rc*1.0,0);add(tag(sp,"Pressurant bottle (helium)","tanks at "+fmt(r.feed.Ptank,"mpa")+" "+unitTxt("mpa")));});}
        const l1=cyl(Rp*0.25,x0-rc*0.4,x0,COL.ox,"Oxidizer feed line");l1.position.y=yP*0.5;add(l1);
        flow.push({f:1,path:[[x0,0,rc*0.5],[x0+Lc,0,rc*0.55],[xT,0,rt*0.6],[xE,0,re*0.6],[xE+2.5*De,0,re]],seg:[[x0+Lc,60,"ab"],[xT,300,"ab"],[1e9,r.Isp*9.80665,"ab"]]});}
    }
    g={kind:"rocketx",D:Math.max(De,Dc,r.solid?r.solid.Dg:0),xMin:xS,xMax:xE,len:xE-xS,vel:{inlet:100}};
  }else if(p.config==="ramjet"){const q=r.geo,body=q.body.map(a=>a.slice()),rc=q.rc,r3=q.r3,r8=q.r8,r9=Math.max(q.r9,q.r8*1.01);
    add(latheM(body.concat([[body[body.length-1][0],0.001]]),COL.sp,1,"Inlet spike",q.shocks.length+" cone and ramp angles optimized for M "+p.mach.toFixed(2)));
    add(latheM([[q.xL,rc],[q.xL+0.6*rc,Math.max(rc,r3)*1.02],[q.xc0,r3],[q.xc1,r3],[q.xT,r8],[q.xE,r9]],COL.cas,0.3,"Cowl, combustor and nozzle","exit "+fmt(r.nozCore.V,"vel")+" "+unitTxt("vel")));
    q.shocks.forEach((s,i)=>add(latheM([s.from,s.to],shockMat(),1,"Oblique shock "+(i+1),"β "+dg(r.inl.steps[i].beta)+" · Pt ratio "+r.inl.steps[i].ptr.toFixed(3),true)));
    const xs=q.xL+0.15*rc,ns=r.inl.steps[r.inl.steps.length-1];if(ns&&ns.kind==="normal")add(latheM([[xs,body[body.length-3]?body[body.length-3][1]*0.9:rc*0.5],[xs,rc*1.01]],shockMat(),1,"Terminal normal shock","M "+ns.M1.toFixed(2)+" → "+ns.M2.toFixed(2)+" · Pt ratio "+ns.ptr.toFixed(3),true));
    add(ring(q.xc0,r3*0.55,r3*0.04,COL.comb,"Flameholders (V-gutters)"));add(ring(q.xc0-0.15*r3,r3*0.8,r3*0.025,COL.fuel,"Fuel spray ring"));
    add(glowMesh(latheM([[q.xc0,r3*0.9],[q.xc1,r3*0.95],[q.xT,r8*0.95]],new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xEF9F27,transparent:true,opacity:0.3,side:THREE.DoubleSide,depthWrite:false}),1,"Combustion zone","T4 "+fmt(r.T4,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp")),0.2,0.08,3));
    const D=2*rc;flow.push({f:1,path:[[-0.8*D,rc*0.75,rc*0.15],[q.xL,rc*0.92,rc*0.06],[q.xc0,r3*0.55,r3*0.35],[q.xT,r8*0.5,r8*0.45],[q.xE,r9*0.5,r9*0.45],[q.xE+1.5*D,r9*0.6,r9*0.55]],seg:[[q.xL,r.V0,"cold"],[q.xc0,120,"cold"],[q.xT,250,"hot"],[1e9,r.nozCore.V,"hot"]]});
    g={xMin:-0.1,xMax:q.xE,len:q.xE+0.1,vel:{inlet:r.V0}};
  }else if(p.config==="scramjet"){const q=r.geo,h0=q.h0,h3=q.h3,h10=Math.min(1.5*h0,Math.max(q.h10,h3*1.2)),Lf=q.Lf,Lc=q.Lc,La=q.La,W=q.W2*h0,L=Lf+Lc+La;
    const wall=(pts,name,info,c,o)=>{const sh=new THREE.Shape();sh.moveTo(pts[0][0],pts[0][1]);pts.slice(1).forEach(a=>sh.lineTo(a[0],a[1]));const ge=new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(sh,{depth:W,bevelEnabled:false});ge.translate(0,0,-W/2);const m=new THREE.Mesh(ge,mat(c,o));return tag(m,name,info);};
    const t=h0*0.08;
    add(wall([[0,h0/2],[Lf,h3/2],[Lf+Lc,h3/2*1.15],[L,h10/2],[L,h10/2+t],[Lf+Lc,h3/2*1.15+t*2],[Lf,h3/2+t*2],[0,h0/2+t]],"Forebody and upper inlet ramp","compression to T3/T0 = "+(r.c3.T3/r.T0).toFixed(2),COL.cas,CUT?0.5:1));
    add(wall([[Lf*0.35,-h0/2*0.95],[Lf,-h3/2],[Lf+Lc,-h3/2*1.15],[Lf+Lc+La*0.35,-h10/2*0.7],[Lf+Lc+La*0.35,-h10/2*0.7-t],[Lf+Lc,-h3/2*1.15-t*2],[Lf,-h3/2-t*2],[Lf*0.35,-h0/2*0.95-t]],"Cowl","captures the air behind the ramp shocks",COL.cas,CUT?0.5:1));
    const th=Math.atan((h0-h3)/(2*Lf)),ob=Cycle.oblique(p.mach,th),bt=ob?ob.beta:0.25;
    [1,-1].forEach(s=>{const xe=Math.min(Lf,(h0/2)/Math.tan(bt)),len=xe/Math.cos(bt),ge=new THREE.PlaneGeometry(len,W);ge.rotateX(PI/2);const pl=new THREE.Mesh(ge,shockMat());pl.rotation.z=-s*bt;pl.position.set(xe/2,s*(h0/2-xe*Math.tan(bt)/2),0);add(tag(pl,"Inlet shock","M "+p.mach.toFixed(1)+" · ramp "+dg(th)+" · wave angle "+dg(bt)));});
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const m=cyl(h3*0.04,Lf+Lc*0.05,Lf+Lc*0.05+h3*0.05,COL.fuel,"Hydrogen injectors");m.rotation.set(0,0,0);m.position.set(Lf+Lc*0.08,0,-W/2+W*(i+0.5)/5);add(m);}
    add(glowMesh(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(Lc*0.9,h3*0.9,W*0.95),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xEF9F27,transparent:true,opacity:0.25,depthWrite:false})),0.18,0.1,5));parts[parts.length-1].obj.position.set(Lf+Lc*0.5,0,0);tag(parts[parts.length-1].obj,"Supersonic combustor","M "+r.M3.toFixed(2)+" in · T4 "+fmt(r.T4,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp"));
    flow.push({f:1,path:[[-0.4*L,0,h0*0.4],[0,0,h0*0.4],[Lf,0,h3*0.4],[Lf+Lc,0,h3*0.45],[L,0,h10*0.4],[L*1.4,0,h10*0.5]],seg:[[Lf,r.V0,"cold"],[Lf+Lc*0.1,r.c3.V3,"cold"],[Lf+Lc,r.V4,"hot"],[1e9,r.V10,"hot"]]});
    g={xMin:0,xMax:L,len:L,D:h0,vel:{inlet:r.V0}};
  }else{const L=p.tubeL,Rc=p.chamberD/2,Rt=p.tailD/2,Lc=0.28*L;
    add(latheM([[-0.08*L,Rc*0.75],[0,Rc],[Lc,Rc],[Lc+0.12*L,Rt],[L,Rt],[L,Rt*1.02]],COL.cas,0.35,"Combustion chamber and tailpipe","resonates at "+r.pj.freq.toFixed(0)+" Hz"));
    const vg=bladeRow({x0:-0.02*L,x1:0.0,rh0:Rc*0.12,rh1:Rc*0.12,rt0:Rc*0.95,rt1:Rc*0.95,n:24,chord:0.02*L,name:"Flap-valve grid"},COL.vane,0.1,"spring-steel flaps open when chamber pressure drops below the intake pressure");add(vg);
    add(glowMesh(latheM([[0.01*L,Rc*0.9],[Lc,Rc*0.9],[Lc+0.1*L,Rt*0.9]],new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xEF9F27,transparent:true,opacity:0.3,side:THREE.DoubleSide,depthWrite:false}),1,"Combustion pulses","peak "+fmt(r.pj.T3,"temp")+" "+unitTxt("temp")),0.05,0.45,Math.min(6,r.pj.freq/8)));
    flow.push({f:1,path:[[-0.6*L,0,Rc*0.7],[0,0,Rc*0.6],[Lc,0,Rc*0.6],[Lc+0.12*L,0,Rt*0.6],[L,0,Rt*0.6],[L*1.5,0,Rt*0.9]],seg:[[0,Math.max(r.V0,40),"cold"],[Lc,40,"hot"],[1e9,r.pj.Vj,"hot"]]});
    g={xMin:-0.1*L,xMax:L,len:L*1.1,D:p.chamberD,vel:{inlet:40}};}
  return Object.assign({},r.geo||{},g,{parts,flow,cowl:null,casing:null,shellPts:null,cfg:"ext"});}
function addAxis(){}

<script>
"use strict";
/* ---------- phase 6: mission, optimizer, cross-type comparison ---------- */
const g0=9.80665;
const msKey=p=>isRkt(p)?"rocket":p.trj?"trj":isRam(p)?p.config:fam(p)==="gtshaft"?p.config:fam(p);
const MSDEF={jet:{R:5000e3,alt:10668,mach:0.78,LD:17,m:50000,n:2},trj:{R:4000e3,alt:24000,mach:3.2,LD:6.5,m:40000,n:2},turboprop:{R:1500e3,alt:7000,mach:0.45,LD:15,m:18000,n:2},
  piston:{R:1000e3,alt:2500,mach:0.18,LD:11,m:900,n:1},electric:{R:150e3,alt:1500,mach:0.15,LD:14,m:450,n:1},ramjet:{R:300e3,alt:18000,mach:3,LD:4,m:1500,n:1},
  scramjet:{R:1000e3,alt:30000,mach:10,LD:3,m:1300,n:1},pulsejet:{R:250e3,alt:1000,mach:0.55,LD:6,m:1500,n:1},rocket:{m:null,n:1}};

const MSF=[["R","Range","dist"],["alt","Cruise altitude","alt"],["mach","Cruise Mach number","none"],["LD","Lift-to-drag ratio","none"],["m","Aircraft mass without engines or fuel","mass"],["n","Number of engines","n0"]];
function msRows(){const c=$("msCtl"),rk=isRkt(P);c.innerHTML="";
  MSF.forEach(([k,lab,u])=>{if(rk&&!(k==="m"||k==="n"))return;const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="selrow";
    row.innerHTML='<span>'+(rk&&k==="m"?"Payload and stage structure mass":rk&&k==="n"?"Number of engines (or motors)":lab)+'</span><span style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center"><input type="number" step="any" style="width:110px" aria-label="'+lab+'"><small>'+unitTxt(u)+'</small></span>';
    const inp=row.querySelector("input");inp.value=+cv(MS[k],u).toPrecision(5);inp.onchange=()=>{let v=back(parseFloat(inp.value),u);if(!isFinite(v)||v<=0){inp.value=+cv(MS[k],u).toPrecision(5);return;}if(k==="n")v=Math.max(1,Math.round(v));MS[k]=v;renderMission();};c.appendChild(row);});}
function limsFor(r,p){return{T4max:p.T4+(p.alt>3000?(fam(p)==="jet"?120:100):0),nHmax:1.05,nLmax:1.05,T3max:p.trj?Math.max(1050,r.S["3"].Tt+20):r.S["3"].Tt+(p.alt>3000?80:20),PWmax:fam(p)==="gtshaft"?(r.Pshaft*(p.alt>3000?1.6:1.0)):1e12};}
function bisect(f,lo,hi,target,n){let a=lo,b=hi,best=null;for(let i=0;i<(n||22);i++){const m=(a+b)/2,o=f(m);if(!o||!o.ok){a=m;continue;}best=o;if(o.Fn>target)b=m;else a=m;}return best;}
function cruisePoint(r,p,alt,mach,Treq){const cond={alt,mach,dISA:0};
  if(fam(p)==="jet"){const lim=limsFor(r,p),mx=Cycle.solveMax(r.ref,Object.assign({},cond,{ab:false,T7:p.T7}),lim,null);if(!mx||!mx.ok)return{ok:false,msg:"The engine can't be matched at the cruise condition."};
    if(mx.Fn>=Treq){let x=mx.x;const o=bisect(T=>{const q=Cycle.solveOD(r.ref,Object.assign({},cond,{T4:T,ab:false}),x);if(q&&q.ok)x=q.x;return q;},Math.max(700,p.T4*0.5),mx.T4,Treq);return o?{ok:true,TSFC:o.TSFC,Fmax:mx.Fn,frac:Treq/mx.Fn,ab:false}:{ok:false,msg:"No throttle setting matched the cruise thrust."};}
    if(!p.ab)return{ok:false,short:true,Fmax:mx.Fn,msg:"Not enough thrust: cruise needs "+fmt(Treq,"force")+" "+unitTxt("force")+" per engine but max dry thrust there is "+fmt(mx.Fn,"force")+" "+unitTxt("force")+"."};
    const ab=T7=>Cycle.solveOD(r.ref,Object.assign({},cond,{T4:mx.T4,ab:true,T7}),mx.x),top=ab(p.T7);if(!top||!top.ok||top.Fn<Treq)return{ok:false,short:true,Fmax:top&&top.ok?top.Fn:mx.Fn,msg:"Not enough thrust even with the afterburner at this cruise condition."};
    const o=bisect(ab,Math.max(mx.T4*0.8,900),p.T7,Treq);return o?{ok:true,TSFC:o.TSFC,Fmax:top.Fn,frac:Treq/top.Fn,ab:true}:{ok:false};}
  if(fam(p)==="gtshaft"){const lim=limsFor(r,p),mx=Cycle.solveMax(r.ref,Object.assign({},cond),lim,null);if(!mx||!mx.ok)return{ok:false,msg:"The engine can't be matched at the cruise condition."};if(mx.Fn<Treq)return{ok:false,short:true,Fmax:mx.Fn,msg:"Not enough thrust at cruise: max is "+fmt(mx.Fn,"force")+" "+unitTxt("force")+" per engine."};
    const o=bisect(Pw=>Cycle.solveODPower(r.ref,cond,Pw),0.15*mx.Pshaft,mx.Pshaft,Treq);return o?{ok:true,TSFC:o.fuel/o.Fn,Fmax:mx.Fn,frac:Treq/mx.Fn}:{ok:false};}
  if(p.config==="piston"||p.config==="electric"){const f=t=>(p.config==="piston"?Powertrain.odPiston:Powertrain.odElectric)(r.ref,Object.assign({},cond,{thr:t,rpmFrac:1,mixture:"economy"})),mx=f(1);
    if(!mx||!mx.ok||mx.Fn<Treq)return{ok:false,short:true,Fmax:mx&&mx.Fn,msg:"Not enough thrust at cruise: full power gives "+fmt(mx?mx.Fn:0,"force")+" "+unitTxt("force")+" per engine."};
    const o=bisect(f,0.1,1,Treq);return o?{ok:true,TSFC:o.fuel/o.Fn,Pel:o.Pel,Fmax:mx.Fn,frac:Treq/mx.Fn}:{ok:false};}
  if(isRam(p)){const f=phi=>Cycle.odRam(r.ref,Object.assign({},cond,{phi})),mx=f(p.config==="pulsejet"?p.phi:Math.min(1.3,p.phi*1.3));if(!mx||!mx.ok)return{ok:false,msg:"The engine doesn't run at this cruise condition."};
    if(mx.Fn<Treq)return{ok:false,short:true,Fmax:mx.Fn,msg:"Not enough thrust at cruise: max is "+fmt(mx.Fn,"force")+" "+unitTxt("force")+" per engine."};
    if(p.config==="pulsejet")return{ok:true,TSFC:mx.TSFC,Fmax:mx.Fn,frac:1,note:"A pulsejet runs at one setting; the excess thrust would accelerate the aircraft."};
    const o=bisect(f,0.15,Math.min(1.3,p.phi*1.3),Treq);return o?{ok:true,TSFC:o.TSFC,Fmax:mx.Fn,frac:Treq/mx.Fn}:{ok:false};}
  return{ok:false,msg:"Mission analysis needs an engine that produces thrust."};}
function missionEval(r,p,ms,quick){if(!r||!r.ok)return{ok:false,msg:"No valid design."};
  if(p.config==="turboshaft")return{ok:false,msg:"A turboshaft drives a rotor, which the studio doesn't model, so there's no cruise thrust to size a mission against."};
  const n=ms.n;
  if(isRkt(p)){const mp=r.solid?r.mprop:r.hyb?(r.hyb.mox_tot+r.hyb.mfuel):r.mtot*p.tb,me=engMass(r,quick),m0=ms.m+n*(me+mp),mf=ms.m+n*me,Isp=r.It?r.It/(mp*g0):(p.alt>60000?r.Isp:0.5*(r.IspSL+r.IspV));
    return{ok:true,rocket:true,dv:Isp*g0*Math.log(m0/mf),mp:n*mp,m0,mf,Isp,TW0:n*r.Fn/(m0*g0),Gbo:n*(r.Fvac||r.Fn)/(mf*g0),me:n*me};}
  const a=Cycle.isa(ms.alt,0),V=ms.mach*Math.sqrt(1.4*287.05*a.T),me=engMass(r,quick)+(p.config==="electric"?r.mBatt:0);let mf=0,cp=null,Treq=0;
  for(let it=0;it<4;it++){const mmid=ms.m+n*me+0.5*mf;Treq=mmid*g0/ms.LD/n;cp=cruisePoint(r,p,ms.alt,ms.mach,Treq);if(!cp.ok)return Object.assign({ok:false,Treq},cp);
    if(p.config==="electric"){mf=0;break;}const x=ms.R*g0*cp.TSFC/(V*ms.LD);mf=(ms.m+n*me)*(Math.exp(x)-1)*1.05;}
  const out={ok:true,mf,me:n*me,TSFC:cp.TSFC,Treq,frac:cp.frac,V,m0:ms.m+n*me+mf,ab:cp.ab,note:cp.note};
  if(p.config==="electric"){const Pel=cp.Pel*n,E=Pel*ms.R/V/3.6e6,Eav=n*p.battE*p.battUse;Object.assign(out,{E,Eav,Rmax:Eav*3.6e6/Pel*V});}
  return out;}
function renderMission(){
  const k=msKey(P);if(k!==MSK||!MS){MSK=k;MS=MSDEF[k]?Object.assign({},MSDEF[k]):null;if(MS&&k==="rocket"&&R&&R.ok){const mp=R.solid?R.mprop:R.hyb?(R.hyb.mox_tot+R.hyb.mfuel):R.mtot*P.tb;MS.m=Math.round(mp*0.12);}if(MS)msRows();else $("msCtl").innerHTML="";}
  const el=$("msMetrics"),nt=$("msNotes");
  if(!MS){el.innerHTML="";nt.innerHTML='<div class="note ins">A turboshaft drives a rotor, which the studio doesn\'t model, so there\'s no cruise thrust to size a mission against.</div>';return;}
  const o=missionEval(R,P,MS);const M=(k,v,u,sub)=>'<div class="m"><div class="k">'+k+'</div><div class="v">'+(isFinite(v)?fmt(v,u):"–")+'<small>'+(isFinite(v)?unitTxt(u):"")+'</small></div><div class="d">'+(sub||"")+'</div></div>';
  $("msSub").textContent=isRkt(P)?"Treat the engine as one stage: the propellant it burns over the burn time, plus the engines, payload and structure you enter, gives the stage's ideal velocity change (Tsiolkovsky).":"A Breguet cruise with your engine fixed as designed: at each step the studio finds the throttle setting that balances drag (weight ÷ L/D) at the cruise condition and uses the fuel consumption there. Engine mass comes from the correlations in the validation section; 5% reserve fuel is added.";
  if(!o.ok){el.innerHTML="";nt.innerHTML='<div class="note bad">'+(o.msg||"The mission can't be flown with this engine.")+"</div>";return;}
  if(o.rocket){el.innerHTML=M("Stage Δv",o.dv,"dv")+M("Propellant",o.mp,"mass")+M("Liftoff / ignition mass",o.m0,"mass")+M("Burnout mass",o.mf,"mass")+M("Initial thrust-to-weight",o.TW0,"tw")+M("Acceleration at burnout",o.Gbo,"tw","g")+M("Average specific impulse",o.Isp,"isp")+M("Engine mass",o.me,"mass");
    nt.innerHTML='<div class="note ins">Ideal Δv ignores gravity and drag losses, which take roughly 1–2 km/s from a first stage climbing out of the atmosphere and very little from an upper stage.</div>'+(o.TW0<1.15&&P.alt<20000?'<div class="note warn">Initial thrust-to-weight is '+o.TW0.toFixed(2)+": too low to leave the pad briskly. First stages usually start at 1.2–1.5.</div>":"");return;}
  let h=M("Thrust needed per engine",o.Treq,"force",isFinite(o.frac)?(o.frac*100).toFixed(0)+"% of available":"")+M("Cruise TSFC",o.TSFC,"tsfc")+M("Engine mass"+(P.config==="electric"?" incl. battery":""),o.me,"mass");
  if(P.config==="electric")h+=M("Battery energy needed",o.E,"kwh")+M("Usable on board",o.Eav,"kwh")+M("Range on this battery",o.Rmax,"dist");
  else h+=M("Fuel burned (incl. 5% reserve)",o.mf,"mass")+M("Takeoff mass",o.m0,"mass")+M("Fuel fraction",o.mf/o.m0,"pct")+M("Fuel per 100 km",o.mf/(MS.R/1e5),"mass");
  el.innerHTML=h;const notes=[];
  if(P.config==="electric"&&o.E>o.Eav)notes.push('<div class="note warn">The battery is too small for this range: it would need '+fmt(o.E,"kwh")+" kWh.</div>");
  if(o.ab)notes.push('<div class="note ins">Cruise needs the afterburner lit, as the SR-71 did, which is why its TSFC is so high.</div>');
  if(o.note)notes.push('<div class="note ins">'+o.note+"</div>");
  if(isFinite(o.frac)&&o.frac<0.4&&!isRam(P))notes.push('<div class="note ins">Cruise uses only '+(o.frac*100).toFixed(0)+"% of the thrust available, so the engine runs deep into part power. A smaller engine would weigh less and usually run more efficiently at cruise.</div>");
  if(BASE&&BASE.r.ok&&msKey(BASE.p)===msKey(P)&&!isRkt(P)&&P.config!=="electric"){const b=missionEval(BASE.r,BASE.p,MS);if(b.ok){const d=(o.mf-b.mf)/b.mf*100;notes.push('<div class="note ins">Against the baseline, mission fuel is <b>'+(d>0?"+":"")+d.toFixed(1)+"%</b> ("+fmt(o.mf-b.mf,"mass")+" "+unitTxt("mass")+").</div>");}}
  nt.innerHTML=notes.join("");}
/* ---------- optimizer ---------- */
const OPDEF={separate:["FPR","BPR","OPR"],mixed:["BPR","OPR"],turbojet:["OPR"],gtshaft:["OPR","NPR"],piston:["CR","propD"],electric:["propD","propRPM"],ramjet:["phi","M3"],scramjet:["psi","phi"],pulsejet:["tubeL","phi"],liquid:["Pc","OF","eps"],solid:["AL","Pc"],hybrid:["OF","Pc"]};
const opKey=p=>isRkt(p)?rkind(p):isRam(p)?p.config:fam(p)==="gtshaft"?"gtshaft":fam(p)==="jet"?p.config:p.config;
function opObjectives(){const f=fam(P),o=[];
  if(f==="jet")o.push(["TSFC","Minimize TSFC at the design point",-1],["mission","Minimize mission fuel",-1],["mass","Minimize engine mass",-1],["Fs","Maximize specific thrust",1]);
  else if(f==="gtshaft"){o.push(["PSFC","Minimize power-specific fuel use",-1],["mass","Minimize engine mass",-1],["Psp","Maximize specific power",1]);if(P.config==="turboprop")o.push(["mission","Minimize mission fuel",-1]);}
  else if(f==="piston")o.push(["PSFC","Minimize BSFC",-1],["mission","Minimize mission fuel",-1],["mass","Minimize engine mass",-1]);
  else if(f==="electric")o.push(["Fn","Maximize thrust",1],["etaP","Maximize propeller efficiency",1]);
  else if(f==="ram")o.push(["Isp","Maximize fuel specific impulse",1],["Fs","Maximize specific thrust",1],["mission","Minimize mission fuel",-1]);
  else o.push(["Isp","Maximize specific impulse at the design altitude",1],["dv","Maximize stage Δv",1],["mass","Minimize engine mass",-1]);return o;}
let OPT={run:false,res:null};
function opFill(){const k=opKey(P);if(OPT.key===k)return;OPT.key=k;OPT.res=null;$("opResult").innerHTML="";$("opApply").hidden=true;
  const ob=$("opObj");ob.innerHTML=opObjectives().map(o=>'<option value="'+o[0]+'">'+o[1]+'</option>').join("");
  const vs=TRX.filter(t=>(!t.show||t.show(P))&&!["Fn","Pshaft","alt","mach","propB","nSeg","nShocks"].includes(t.id)),def=OPDEF[k]||[];
  $("opVars").innerHTML=vs.map(t=>{const d=defOf(t.id);return '<label class="chk"><input type="checkbox" value="'+t.id+'"'+(def.includes(t.id)?" checked":"")+'> '+labOf(d)+'</label>';}).join("");
  const cs=[];if(isGT(P))cs.push(["T3max","Max compressor exit temperature","temp",980]);if(isGT(P)||isRam(P))cs.push(["Dmax","Max fan or inlet diameter","len",R&&R.geo&&R.geo.D?Math.round(R.geo.D*1.1*100)/100:3]);
  if(isRkt(P))cs.push(["Demax","Max nozzle exit diameter","len",R&&R.De?Math.round(R.De*1.2*100)/100:2]);
  $("opCons").innerHTML=cs.map(c=>'<div class="selrow"><span>'+c[1]+'</span><span style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center"><input type="number" step="any" style="width:100px" data-c="'+c[0]+'" data-u="'+c[2]+'" value="'+(+cv(c[3],c[2]).toPrecision(4))+'"><small>'+unitTxt(c[2])+'</small></span></div>').join("")+(isRkt(P)&&P.alt<60000?'<label class="chk"><input type="checkbox" id="opNoSep" checked> No flow separation at the design altitude</label>':"");}
function opEval(q,obj,dir,cons){const r=solveAny(q);if(!r.ok||(r.warn||[]).some(w=>w.lvl==="bad"))return{f:1e9,r};let pen=0;
  if(cons.T3max&&r.S&&r.S["3"])pen+=Math.max(0,r.S["3"].Tt-cons.T3max)/50;if(cons.Dmax&&r.geo&&r.geo.D)pen+=Math.max(0,r.geo.D-cons.Dmax)/cons.Dmax*5;if(cons.Demax&&r.De)pen+=Math.max(0,r.De-cons.Demax)/cons.Demax*5;if(cons.noSep&&r.sep)pen+=1;
  let v;if(obj==="mission"){const o=missionEval(r,q,MS||MSDEF[msKey(q)]);v=o.ok?o.mf:NaN;}else if(obj==="mass"){v=engMass(r);if(!r.pd&&isGT(q)&&slsRating(r).fb)v=NaN;}else if(obj==="dv"){const o=missionEval(r,q,MS||{m:1000,n:1});v=o.ok?o.dv:NaN;}else v=r[obj];
  if(!isFinite(v))return{f:1e9,r};return{f:-dir*v+Math.abs(v)*pen,v,r,pen};}
function opRun(){if(OPT.run){OPT.run=false;return;}const ids=[...document.querySelectorAll("#opVars input:checked")].map(i=>i.value);if(!ids.length){$("opMsg").textContent="Pick at least one variable.";return;}
  const ob=opObjectives().find(o=>o[0]===$("opObj").value),cons={};document.querySelectorAll("#opCons input[data-c]").forEach(i=>cons[i.dataset.c]=back(+i.value,i.dataset.u));if($("opNoSep"))cons.noSep=$("opNoSep").checked;
  const D=ids.map(defOf),B=D.map(d=>rangeOf(d)),x0=ids.map((id,i)=>(getP(P,id)-B[i][0])/(B[i][1]-B[i][0])),base=deep(P);
  const mk=x=>{const q=deep(base);ids.forEach((id,i)=>{let v=B[i][0]+Math.min(1,Math.max(0,x[i]))*(B[i][1]-B[i][0]);if(D[i].step>=1)v=Math.round(v);setP(q,id,v);});return q;};
  const F=x=>opEval(mk(x),ob[0],ob[2],cons).f,start=opEval(deep(base),ob[0],ob[2],cons);
  const n=ids.length;let S=[x0.slice()];for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const y=x0.slice();y[i]=y[i]+(y[i]>0.85?-0.12:0.12);S.push(y);}let fS=S.map(F),it=0,evals=n+1;const maxE=ob[0]==="mission"?120:220;
  OPT.run=true;$("opRun").textContent="Stop";$("opApply").hidden=true;
  const step=()=>{if(!OPT.run){done();return;}const t0=performance.now();
    while(performance.now()-t0<40&&evals<maxE){const o=S.map((s,i)=>i).sort((a,b)=>fS[a]-fS[b]);S=o.map(i=>S[i]);fS=o.map(i=>fS[i]);
      const c=S[0].map((_,j)=>S.slice(0,n).reduce((a,s)=>a+s[j],0)/n),w=S[n],xr=c.map((v,j)=>v+(v-w[j])),fr=F(xr);evals++;
      if(fr<fS[0]){const xe=c.map((v,j)=>v+2*(v-w[j])),fe=F(xe);evals++;if(fe<fr){S[n]=xe;fS[n]=fe;}else{S[n]=xr;fS[n]=fr;}}
      else if(fr<fS[n-1]){S[n]=xr;fS[n]=fr;}
      else{const xk=c.map((v,j)=>v+0.5*(w[j]-v)),fk=F(xk);evals++;if(fk<fS[n]){S[n]=xk;fS[n]=fk;}else{for(let i=1;i<=n;i++){S[i]=S[i].map((v,j)=>S[0][j]+0.5*(v-S[0][j]));fS[i]=F(S[i]);evals++;}}}
      it++;if(Math.max(...fS)-Math.min(...fS)<1e-7*Math.abs(fS[0])&&it>8)break;}
    $("opMsg").textContent="Evaluated "+evals+" designs…";if(evals<maxE&&!(Math.max(...fS)-Math.min(...fS)<1e-7*Math.abs(fS[0])&&it>8))setTimeout(step,0);else done();};
  const done=()=>{OPT.run=false;$("opRun").textContent="Run optimizer";const i0=fS.indexOf(Math.min(...fS)),q=mk(S[i0]),best=opEval(q,ob[0],ob[2],cons);
    if(best.f>=1e9){$("opMsg").textContent="No feasible design found within the bounds and constraints.";return;}
    OPT.res={q,ids};const u=ob[0]==="mission"||ob[0]==="mass"?"mass":ob[0]==="dv"?"dv":(TRY.find(t=>t.k===ob[0])||MET.find(m=>m.k===ob[0])||{u:"none"}).u;
    let h="<thead><tr><th>Variable</th><th>Current</th><th>Optimum</th></tr></thead><tbody>";ids.forEach((id,i)=>{const d=D[i];h+="<tr><td class='l'>"+labOf(d)+"</td><td>"+fmt(getP(P,id),d.u)+" "+unitTxt(d.u)+"</td><td><b>"+fmt(getP(q,id),d.u)+"</b> "+unitTxt(d.u)+"</td></tr>";});
    h+="<tr><td class='l'><b>"+ob[1].replace(/^(Minimize|Maximize) /,"").replace(/^./,c=>c.toUpperCase())+"</b></td><td>"+(isFinite(start.v)?fmt(start.v,u)+" "+unitTxt(u):"–")+"</td><td><b>"+fmt(best.v,u)+"</b> "+unitTxt(u)+(isFinite(start.v)&&start.v?" ("+((best.v-start.v)/Math.abs(start.v)*100>0?"+":"")+((best.v-start.v)/Math.abs(start.v)*100).toFixed(1)+"%)":"")+"</td></tr>";
    $("opResult").innerHTML=h+"</tbody>";$("opApply").hidden=false;
    const atB=ids.filter((id,i)=>{const v=getP(q,id);return Math.abs(v-B[i][0])<1e-3*(B[i][1]-B[i][0])||Math.abs(v-B[i][1])<1e-3*(B[i][1]-B[i][0]);}).map(id=>labOf(defOf(id)).toLowerCase());
    $("opMsg").textContent="Done after "+evals+" evaluations."+(best.pen>0?" A constraint is still slightly violated.":"")+(atB.length?" Pinned at a slider limit: "+atB.join(", ")+". The true optimum may lie beyond it.":"");};
  step();}
/* ---------- cross-type comparison ---------- */
let CMP=null,cmpC=null,cmpTok=0;
function altForQ(M,q){if(M<0.05)return 0;const Pt=2*q/(1.4*M*M);let lo=0,hi=80000;for(let i=0;i<50;i++){const m=(lo+hi)/2;if(Cycle.isa(m,0).P>Pt)lo=m;else hi=m;}return (lo+hi)/2;}
function cmpSeries(key,lab,col,p0,machs){const p=withDefaults(p0),r=solveAny(p);if(!r.ok)return null;const pts=[];
  machs.forEach(M=>{const alt=altForQ(M,47880);let o=null;
    try{if(isRkt(p))o=Rocket.odRocket(r.ref,{alt,thr:1});else if(isRam(p))o=Cycle.odRam(r.ref,{alt,mach:M,dISA:0,phi:p.phi});else if(fam(p)==="jet")o=Cycle.solveMax(r.ref,{alt,mach:M,dISA:0,ab:!!p.ab,T7:p.T7},limsFor(r,p),null);}catch(e){o=null;}
    if(o&&o.ok&&o.Fn>0){const isp=isRkt(p)?o.Isp:1/(g0*o.TSFC);if(isFinite(isp)&&isp>0)pts.push({x:M,y:isp});}});
  return{label:lab,data:pts,borderColor:col,backgroundColor:col,showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:2.5};}
function computeCompare(){const tok=++cmpTok,M=[];for(let m=0;m<=16+1e-9;m+=m<1?0.1:m<4?0.25:1)M.push(Math.round(m*100)/100);
  const list=[["cfm56","High-bypass turbofan","#378ADD",M.filter(m=>m<=0.9)],["j79","Turbojet with afterburner","#7F77DD",M.filter(m=>m<=2.4)],["j58","Turbo-ramjet","#1D9E75",M.filter(m=>m<=3.4)],["argus","Pulsejet","#888780",M.filter(m=>m<=0.85)],["talos","Ramjet (kerosene)","#EF9F27",M.filter(m=>m>=1.2&&m<=5.5)],["x43","Scramjet (hydrogen)","#D85A30",M.filter(m=>m>=5&&m<=16)],["merlin","Rocket (LOX/RP-1)","#E24B4A",M.filter(m=>m<=16)]];
  const out=[];let i=0;$("cmpMsg").textContent="Computing…";
  const step=()=>{if(tok!==cmpTok)return;if(i<list.length){const L=list[i++];if(PRESETS[L[0]]){const s=cmpSeries(L[0],L[1],L[2],PRESETS[L[0]].p,L[3]);if(s)out.push(s);}CMP=out;renderCompare();setTimeout(step,0);}else{$("cmpMsg").textContent="";}};step();}
function renderCompare(){if(!CMP)return;const ds=CMP.slice();
  if(R&&R.ok&&(thrustFam(P))){const M=[];for(let m=0;m<=16+1e-9;m+=m<1?0.1:m<4?0.25:1)M.push(Math.round(m*100)/100);const rng=isRkt(P)?M:P.config==="scramjet"?M.filter(m=>m>=4):P.config==="ramjet"?M.filter(m=>m>=1.1&&m<=6):P.config==="pulsejet"?M.filter(m=>m<=0.85):M.filter(m=>m<=(P.trj?3.4:P.mach>1?2.6:Math.max(1,P.mach+0.3)));
    if(!CMP.cur||CMP.curKey!==JSON.stringify(P))try{CMP.cur=cmpSeries("cur","Your design ("+presetName.replace(/ \(.*\)$/,"")+")",css("--ink"),P,rng);CMP.curKey=JSON.stringify(P);}catch(e){CMP.cur=null;}
    if(CMP.cur){CMP.cur.borderDash=[6,4];CMP.cur.borderWidth=3;ds.push(CMP.cur);}}
  const o=chartBase();o.scales={x:axis("Flight Mach number"),y:Object.assign(axis("Specific impulse, s"),{type:"logarithmic",min:150,max:15000})};
  if(!cmpC)cmpC=new Chart($("cmpChart"),{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o});else{cmpC.data.datasets=ds;cmpC.options=o;cmpC.update();}}
let cmpTimer=null;
{const _u=update;update=function(k){_u(k);opFill();clearTimeout(msTimer);msTimer=setTimeout(()=>{try{renderMission();}catch(e){console.error(e);}if(k==="units"&&MS)msRows();},k==="slider"?400:50);
  clearTimeout(cmpTimer);cmpTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(!CMP)computeCompare();else{CMP.curKey=null;renderCompare();}},k==="init"?1500:600);};}
$("opRun").onclick=opRun;$("opApply").onclick=()=>{if(!OPT.res)return;OPT.res.ids.forEach(id=>setP(P,id,getP(OPT.res.q,id)));ps.value="custom";presetName="Optimized design";$("opApply").hidden=true;update("cfg");};


</script>
<script>
"use strict";
/* ---------- preliminary design: liquid rocket pilot (card, charts, real-geometry 3D, validation) ---------- */
const CFT={v:"validated",c:"calibrated",u:"unvalidated"};
const cf=c=>c?'<span class="cf '+c+'">'+CFT[c]+'</span>':"";
let pdTab=0,pdC0=null,pdC1=null;
document.querySelectorAll("#detailSeg button").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const on=b.dataset.d==="1";if(!!P.pd===on)return;P.pd=on;update("refit");});
document.querySelectorAll("#pdTabs button").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{pdTab=+b.dataset.t;renderPD();});
const U=(v,u,dp)=>isFinite(v)?fmt(v,u,dp)+(unitTxt(u)?" "+unitTxt(u):""):"–";
function pdTable(id,head,rows){$(id).innerHTML=(head?"<thead><tr>"+head.map((h,i)=>"<th"+(i?"":" style='text-align:left'")+">"+h+"</th>").join("")+"</tr></thead>":"")+"<tbody>"+rows.map(r=>r.length===1?"<tr><td class='l' colspan='"+(head?head.length:2)+"' style='font-weight:600;padding-top:10px'>"+r[0]+"</td></tr>":"<tr><td class='l'>"+r[0]+"</td>"+r.slice(1).map(c=>"<td>"+c+"</td>").join("")+"</tr>").join("")+"</tbody>";}
let pdTabG=0,pdCG=null;
const gVal=(v,u)=>typeof v==="string"?v:!isFinite(v)?"–":u==="pct"?(v*100).toFixed(1)+"%":u==="none"?(+v).toFixed(2):u?U(v,u):num(v,2);
function renderPDg(){const d=R.pd,tabs=d.tabs.concat([{name:"Mass buildup",mass:1}]);if(pdTabG>=tabs.length)pdTabG=0;const bar=$("pdTabsG"),t=tabs[pdTabG];
  bar.innerHTML=tabs.map((q,i)=>'<button type="button" data-g="'+i+'" aria-pressed="'+(i===pdTabG)+'">'+q.name+'</button>').join("");bar.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{pdTabG=+b.dataset.g;renderPDg();});
  $("pdSub").textContent=d.note+(isGT(P)?(P.pdModeGT==="pred"?" The cycle is using the predicted efficiencies.":" The cycle uses the efficiencies set by hand; the predicted ones are shown beside them."):"");
  let rows;if(t.mass){rows=d.mass.items.map(q=>[q[0]+cf(q[2]),U(q[1],"mass",q[1]<20?1:0)]);rows.push(["<b>Total</b>","<b>"+U(d.mass.total,"mass",d.mass.total<20?1:0)+"</b>"]);
    const stat=engMass(solveConcept(Object.assign({},P,{pd:false})));if(isFinite(stat)&&P.config!=="rocket")rows.push(["Statistical estimate used in Concept mode"+(P.config==="electric"?" (motor and inverter only)":hasProp(P)?" (engine only)":""),U(stat,"mass",0)]);if(P.config==="rocket"&&isFinite(R.mEngStat))rows.push(["Estimate used in Concept mode",U(R.mEngStat,"mass",0)]);pdTable("pdGT",null,rows);}
  else if(t.cols)pdTable("pdGT",t.head,t.rows.map(q=>[q[0]+cf(q[3])].concat(t.cols.map(c=>gVal(q[1](c),q[2])))));
  else pdTable("pdGT",null,t.rows.map(q=>q.length===1?q:[q[0]+cf(q[3]),gVal(q[1],q[2])]));
  const box=$("pdGbox");box.hidden=!t.chart;if(t.chart){const ch=t.chart,COLS=["#D85A30","#378ADD","#1D9E75","#7F77DD","#EF9F27"],o=chartBase(),two=ch.series.some(q=>q.ax==="y2"),s1=ch.series.find(q=>q.ax!=="y2"),s2=ch.series.find(q=>q.ax==="y2");
    const ds=ch.series.map((q,i)=>({label:q.n+(q.u&&unitTxt(q.u)?", "+unitTxt(q.u):""),data:q.x.map((x,k)=>({x,y:q.u?cv(q.y[k],q.u):q.y[k]})),borderColor:COLS[i%5],backgroundColor:COLS[i%5],showLine:true,pointRadius:q.x.length<25?3:0,borderWidth:2,yAxisID:q.ax||"y"}));
    o.scales={x:axis(ch.xl),y:axis(ch.yl||s1.n+(s1.u?", "+unitTxt(s1.u):""))};if(two){o.scales.y2=axis(s2.n+(s2.u?", "+unitTxt(s2.u):""),"right");o.scales.y2.grid={drawOnChartArea:false};}
    if(!pdCG)pdCG=new Chart($("pdGChart"),{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o});else{pdCG.data.datasets=ds;pdCG.options=o;pdCG.update();}}}
function renderPD(){
  const sec=$("pdSec"),on=!!P.pd&&R&&R.ok&&R.pd;sec.hidden=!on;if(!on)return;const gen=!!R.pd.tabs;$("pdTabs").style.display=gen?"none":"";$("pdTabsG").hidden=false;$("pdTabsG").style.display=gen?"":"none";$("pdG").hidden=!gen;
  if(gen){document.querySelectorAll(".pdpane[data-t]").forEach(e=>e.hidden=true);renderPDg();return;}
  const d=R.pd,p=P,r=R,c=d.cool,ct=d.ct;
  document.querySelectorAll("#pdTabs button").forEach(b=>b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",+b.dataset.t===pdTab?"true":"false"));
  document.querySelectorAll(".pdpane[data-t]").forEach(e=>e.hidden=+e.dataset.t!==pdTab);
  const spec=Rocket.solveRocket(Object.assign({},p)),e=d.pred;
  $("pdSub").innerHTML=p.pdMode==="pred"?"The cycle now uses efficiencies and pressure losses predicted from this design: nozzle "+(e.etaCF*100).toFixed(1)+"%"+(d.tp?", pumps "+(e.etaO*100).toFixed(0)+"% and "+(e.etaF*100).toFixed(0)+"%, turbine "+(e.etaT*100).toFixed(0)+"%":"")+(c.regen?", jacket pressure drop "+U(c.dP,"mpa"):"")+". Specific impulse is <b>"+r.Isp.toFixed(1)+" s</b>; with the hand-set values in the sliders it would be "+spec.Isp.toFixed(1)+" s. The cycle and the component design are iterated until they agree."
    :"The cycle uses the efficiencies set by hand. The design below is sized from that cycle, and each table shows what the design itself would predict, so you can see how far the assumptions are from the hardware.";
  if(pdTab===0){
    pdTable("pdT0",null,[["Throat diameter"+(r.nCh>1?" (each of "+r.nCh+" chambers)":""),U(r.Dt,"mm",1)],["Chamber diameter × cylinder length",fmt(r.Dc,"mm",0)+" × "+U(ct.Lcyl,"mm",0)],["Characteristic length L*",r.Lstar.toFixed(2)+" m"+cf("c")],["Nozzle length, throat to exit",U(ct.Ln,"len",US?1:2)],["Exit diameter",U(r.De,"len",US?1:2)],
      ["Overall length, injector face to exit",U(ct.Ltot,"len",US?1:2)],["Bell angles at the inflection and exit",p.nozType==="cone"?"15° cone":(ct.thN*180/PI).toFixed(1)+"° and "+(ct.thE*180/PI).toFixed(1)+"°"+cf("c")],
      ["Combustion gas: molar mass and effective γ",d.G.M.toFixed(2)+" kg/kmol, "+d.G.g.toFixed(3)+cf("v")],["Divergence efficiency",(d.noz.lambda*100).toFixed(2)+"%"+cf("c")],["Wall-friction loss",(d.noz.bl*100).toFixed(2)+"% of thrust"+cf("c")],
      ["Nozzle efficiency: predicted / in use",(d.noz.eta*100).toFixed(2)+"% / "+(r.p.etaCF*100).toFixed(2)+"%"+cf("c")]]);
    const pts=ct.pts,X=q=>cv(q.x,"len"),Y=q=>cv(q.r,"len"),ink=css("--ink"),ds=[{label:"Wall",data:pts.map(q=>({x:X(q),y:Y(q)})).concat([{x:NaN,y:NaN}],pts.map(q=>({x:X(q),y:-Y(q)}))),borderColor:ink,backgroundColor:ink,showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:2}];
    if(c.regen)ds.push({label:"Cooling jacket",data:pts.slice(0,c.iR+1).map(q=>({x:X(q),y:Y(q)+cv(p.chT+p.chH,"len")})).concat([{x:NaN,y:NaN}],pts.slice(0,c.iR+1).map(q=>({x:X(q),y:-Y(q)-cv(p.chT+p.chH,"len")}))),borderColor:"#378ADD",backgroundColor:"#378ADD",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:3});
    ds.push({label:"Throat",data:[{x:0,y:cv(ct.Rt,"len")},{x:0,y:-cv(ct.Rt,"len")}],borderColor:"#D85A30",backgroundColor:"#D85A30",showLine:true,pointRadius:3,borderWidth:1.5,borderDash:[4,3]});
    const o=chartBase(),cvs=$("pdContour"),xr=cv(ct.Ln-ct.x0,"len")*1.06,asp=(cvs.clientHeight||300)/(cvs.clientWidth||900),yr=Math.max(xr*asp,2.3*cv(ct.Re,"len"));
    o.scales={x:Object.assign(axis("Distance from the throat, "+unitTxt("len")),{min:cv(ct.x0,"len")-xr*0.03,max:cv(ct.x0,"len")+(yr/asp)*0.97}),y:Object.assign(axis("Radius, "+unitTxt("len")),{min:-yr/2,max:yr/2})};
    if(!pdC0)pdC0=new Chart(cvs,{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o});else{pdC0.data.datasets=ds;pdC0.options=o;pdC0.update();}
  }
  if(pdTab===1){const wall=Materials.M[p.matWall],fl=d.fl;
    const rows=c.regen?[["Jacket",c.N+(c.tube?" brazed tubes":" milled channels")+", "+fl.name+" coolant, "+Math.round(p.coolFrac*100)+"% of the fuel"],["Peak heat flux (at the throat)",U(c.qmax,"heat")+cf("v")],["Hottest gas-side wall",U(c.TwgMax,"temp")+" (limit "+U(wall.Tmax,"temp")+")"+cf("c")],
        ["Hottest coolant-side wall",U(c.TwcMax,"temp")+(fl.TwLim?" ("+fl.lim+" limit "+U(fl.TwLim,"temp")+")":"")+cf("c")],["Coolant temperature, in → out",fmt(fl.Tin,"temp")+" → "+U(c.Tout,"temp")+cf("c")],["Heat picked up by the coolant",U(c.Q*(r.nCh||1),"power")+cf("c")],
        ["Jacket pressure drop: predicted / allowed for in the cycle",fmt(c.dP,"mpa")+" / "+U(r.feed.dCool,"mpa")+cf("u")],["Fastest coolant velocity",U(c.vmax,"vel")+cf("u")]]
      :[["Wall","Radiation-cooled "+wall.name+", film-cooling effectiveness "+Math.round(p.filmEta*100)+"%"],["Hottest wall",U(c.TwgMax,"temp")+" (limit "+U(wall.Tmax,"temp")+")"+cf("u")],["Peak radiated heat flux",U(c.qmax,"heat")+cf("u")]];
    if(isFinite(c.TextMax)&&p.extCool!=="cooled")rows.push(["Nozzle extension, hottest point",U(c.TextMax,"temp")+cf("u")]);
    if(fl.Rsoot&&c.regen)rows.push(["Carbon layer on the wall","included: kerosene soot insulates the wall and roughly halves the heat flux"+cf("c")]);
    pdTable("pdT1",null,rows);
    const st=c.st,x=s=>cv(s.x,"len"),L=(lab,f,col,ax,dash)=>({label:lab,data:st.map(s=>({x:x(s),y:f(s)})),borderColor:col,backgroundColor:col,showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:2,yAxisID:ax||"y",borderDash:dash});
    const hideExt=p.extCool==="cooled",ds=[L("Gas-side wall",s=>s.ext&&hideExt?NaN:cv(s.Twg,"temp"),"#D85A30")];if(c.regen)ds.push(L("Coolant-side wall",s=>s.ext?NaN:cv(s.Twc,"temp"),"#EF9F27"),L("Coolant",s=>cv(s.Tb,"temp"),"#378ADD"));
    ds.push(L("Heat flux",s=>s.ext&&hideExt?NaN:cv(s.q,"heat"),css("--muted"),"y2",[5,4]),{label:"Wall material limit",data:[{x:x(st[0]),y:cv(wall.Tmax,"temp")},{x:x(st[c.regen?c.iR:st.length-1]),y:cv(wall.Tmax,"temp")}],borderColor:"#E24B4A",backgroundColor:"#E24B4A",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:1.5,borderDash:[2,3]});
    const o=chartBase();o.scales={x:axis("Distance from the throat, "+unitTxt("len")),y:axis("Temperature, "+unitTxt("temp")),y2:axis("Heat flux, "+unitTxt("heat"),"right")};o.scales.y2.grid={drawOnChartArea:false};
    if(!pdC1)pdC1=new Chart($("pdCool"),{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o});else{pdC1.data.datasets=ds;pdC1.options=o;pdC1.update();}
  }
  if(pdTab===2){const j=d.inj,nm={impinging:"Impinging jets",coax:"Coaxial",pintle:"Pintle"}[j.type];
    pdTable("pdT2",null,[["Element type",nm],["Elements"+(r.nCh>1?" per chamber":""),j.type==="pintle"?"1 central pintle":num(j.n,0)+" ("+U(j.Fel,"force",2)+" of thrust each)"],["Pressure drop",U(j.dP,"mpa")+" ("+(j.stiff*100).toFixed(0)+"% of chamber pressure)"],
      ["Pressure drop at minimum throttle",(j.stiffMin*100).toFixed(1)+"% of chamber pressure (keep above about 10%)"+cf("c")],["Oxidizer: total orifice area, orifice diameter",(j.Ao*1e4).toFixed(2)+" cm², "+(j.type==="pintle"?"annular slot":U(j.dO,"mm"))+cf("c")],
      ["Fuel: total orifice area, "+(j.type==="coax"?"annulus gap":"orifice diameter"),(j.Af*1e4).toFixed(2)+" cm², "+(j.type==="pintle"?"radial slots":U(j.type==="coax"?j.gap:j.dF,"mm"))+cf("c")],["Injection velocity, oxidizer / fuel",fmt(j.vo,"vel")+" / "+U(j.vf,"vel")+cf("c")],
      ["Fuel state at the injector",j.gasF?"warm gas from the jacket, "+num(j.rhoF,0)+" kg/m³":"liquid, "+num(j.rhoF,0)+" kg/m³"],["Fuel-to-oxidizer momentum flux ratio",j.J.toFixed(2)+cf("u")],["Discharge coefficient assumed",j.Cd.toFixed(2)+cf("c")]]);
  }
  if(pdTab===3){const t=d.tp;
    if(!t)pdTable("pdT3",null,[["Feed system","Pressure-fed: no turbopumps. The tanks hold "+U(r.feed.Ptank,"mpa")+"."],["Propellant and tank volume for the burn",d.tk.V.toFixed(2)+" m³"+cf("u")],["Tanks, pressurant gas and bottle (stage side, not in engine mass)",U(d.tk.total,"mass")+cf("u")]]);
    else{const q=[t.ox,t.fuel],col=f=>q.map(f),rows=[["Flow"].concat(col(x=>U(x.md,"flow"))),["Pressure, inlet → discharge"].concat(col(x=>fmt(x.Pin,"mpa")+" → "+U(x.Pout,"mpa"))),["Head rise"].concat(col(x=>num(x.H,0)+" m")),["Shaft speed"].concat(col(x=>U(x.rpm,"rpm")+cf("v"))),["Speed is set by"].concat(col(x=>t.auto?x.lim:"hand")),
        ["Stages"].concat(col(x=>x.n+cf("v"))),["Impeller diameter"].concat(col(x=>U(x.D2,"mm",0)+cf("c"))),["Impeller tip speed"].concat(col(x=>U(x.U,"vel")+cf("c"))),["Specific speed (US units)"].concat(col(x=>num(2733*x.Os,0))),["Suction specific speed (US units)"].concat(col(x=>num(x.S,0))),
        ["Efficiency: predicted / in use"].concat(col(x=>(x.eta*100).toFixed(0)+"% / "+((x.key==="o"?r.feed.etaO:r.feed.etaF)*100).toFixed(0)+"%"+cf("c"))),["Shaft power"].concat(col(x=>U(x.P,"power")+cf("v")))];
      t.shafts.forEach(s=>{const nm=t.dual?s.pumps[0].name.replace(" pump","")+" shaft":"Turbopump shaft";rows.push([nm]);rows.push(["Torque, shaft diameter, bearing DN",num(s.tau,0)+" N·m, "+U(s.d,"mm",0),num(s.DN/1e6,2)+" million (limit "+(s.DNlim/1e6).toFixed(1)+")"+cf("c")]);
        if(s.turb)rows.push(["Turbine",(s.turb.imp?"impulse, ":"reaction, ")+s.turb.rows+(s.turb.rows>1?" rows":" row")+", mean diameter "+U(s.turb.Dm,"mm",0),"inlet "+U(s.turb.Tin,"temp")+", pressure ratio "+s.turb.PR.toFixed(2)],["Turbine blade speed ÷ gas spouting speed, efficiency",s.turb.nu.toFixed(2),(s.turb.eta*100).toFixed(0)+"% predicted / "+(r.feed.etaT*100).toFixed(0)+"% in use"+cf("u")]);
        rows.push(["Mass (torque correlation)",U(s.mass,"mass")+cf("c"),""]);});
      if(r.mgg)rows.push(["Gas generator"],["Flow dumped overboard",U(r.mgg,"flow"),(r.mgg/r.mtot*100).toFixed(1)+"% of propellant"]);
      pdTable("pdT3",["","Oxidizer pump","Fuel pump"],rows);}
  }
  if(pdTab===4){const m=d.mass,rows=m.items.map(q=>[q[0],U(q[1],"mass",q[1]<20?1:0)+cf(q[2])]);rows.push(["<b>Engine dry mass (buildup)</b>","<b>"+U(m.total,"mass",m.total<20?1:0)+"</b>"],["Statistical estimate used in Concept mode",U(r.mEngStat,"mass",r.mEngStat<20?1:0)],["Thrust-to-weight (vacuum thrust)",((r.Fvac||r.Fn)/(9.80665*m.total)).toFixed(0)]);
    if(c.regen)rows.push(["Structure"],["Hot-wall pressure stress × safety factor / yield at temperature",U(m.sMax*p.SF,"stress")+" / "+U(m.sMax*p.SF*(1+m.msMin),"stress")+cf("u")],["Thickest closeout or hoop band",U(m.tclMax,"mm")+cf("u")],["Thermal strain between hot wall and jacket",(m.epsTh*100).toFixed(2)+"% (drives low-cycle fatigue life; not assessed)"+cf("u")]);
    rows.push(["Injector face plate thickness",U(m.tFace,"mm",1)+cf("u")],["Stage side (not in engine mass)"],["Propellant volume for a "+num(p.tb,0)+" s burn",d.tk.V.toFixed(2)+" m³"],["Tanks, pressurant and bottle",U(d.tk.total,"mass")+cf("u")]);
    pdTable("pdT4",null,rows);}
}

  {k:"pdTout",label:"Coolant outlet temperature",u:"temp",get:r=>r.pd&&r.pd.cool.regen?r.pd.cool.Tout:NaN,show:isRegen},{k:"pdMg",label:"Smallest design margin",u:"pct",get:r=>r.pd?Math.min(...r.pd.margins.map(m=>m.m)):NaN,show:hasPD},{k:"pdEta",label:"Predicted nozzle efficiency",u:"pct",get:r=>r.pd?r.pd.noz.eta:NaN,show:isPD});
TRX.push({id:"Tmetal",show:pdGT},{id:"webFrac",show:p=>pdSolid(p)&&p.grain!=="bates"},{id:"starIn",show:p=>pdSolid(p)&&(p.grain==="star"||p.grain==="finocyl")},{id:"throatEro",show:pdSolid},{id:"shear",show:p=>p.pd&&p.config==="electric"},{id:"leR",show:p=>p.pd&&p.config==="scramjet"},{id:"chW",show:isRegen},{id:"chH",show:p=>isRegen(p)&&!isTube(p)},{id:"chT",show:isRegen},{id:"coolFrac",show:isRegen},{id:"filmEta",show:isPD},{id:"epsRegen",show:isRegen},{id:"injDp",show:isPD},{id:"lineDp",show:isLiq},{id:"bellPct",show:p=>isPD(p)&&p.nozType==="bell"},{id:"Nss",show:p=>isPD(p)&&isPump(p)},{id:"Ptank",show:p=>isPD(p)&&isPump(p)},{id:"thrMin",show:isPD});
/* ---------- 3D model built from the designed geometry ---------- */
function heatCol(t){t=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,t));const a=[44,127,184],b=[254,224,139],c=[215,48,31],m=t<0.5?[a,b,t*2]:[b,c,t*2-1],f=i=>(m[0][i]+(m[1][i]-m[0][i])*m[2])/255;return{r:f(0),g:f(1),b:f(2)};}
function latheVC(pts,cols,name,info){const v=pts.map(q=>new THREE.Vector2(Math.max(5e-4,q[1]),q[0])),g=new THREE.LatheGeometry(v,QL.seg,0,arc()),n=g.attributes.position.count,ca=new Float32Array(n*3);
  for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const c=cols[k%pts.length];ca[3*k]=c.r;ca[3*k+1]=c.g;ca[3*k+2]=c.b;}g.setAttribute("color",new THREE.BufferAttribute(ca,3));
  const m=new THREE.Mesh(g,new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({vertexColors:true,side:THREE.DoubleSide,metalness:0.15,roughness:0.75}));m.rotation.z=-PI/2;return tag(m,name,info);}
function pdGeo3(r){const p=P,d=r.pd,ct=d.ct,c=d.cool,st=c.st,parts=[],flow=[],n=r.nCh||1,Rc=ct.Rc,Re=ct.Re,Rt=ct.Rt,x0=ct.x0,xE=ct.Ln,wall=Materials.M[p.matWall],noPick=o=>{o.raycast=()=>{};return o;};
  const add=(o,dz)=>{if(dz)o.position.z+=dz;parts.push({k:"mesh",obj:o});return o;},iR=c.regen?c.iR:st.length-1,Tlo=300,Thi=wall.Tmax*1.15,pitchZ=1.1*Math.max(2*Re,2.4*Rc);
  for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const dz=(k-(n-1)/2)*pitchZ,main=ct.pts.slice(0,iR+1);
    add(latheVC(main.map(q=>[q.x,q.r]),main.map((q,i)=>heatCol((st[i].Twg-Tlo)/(Thi-Tlo))),"Hot wall · "+wall.name,"color shows wall temperature, blue (cool) to red (at the "+U(wall.Tmax,"temp")+" limit) · peak "+U(c.TwgMax,"temp")+" · "+U(c.qmax,"heat")+" at the throat"),dz);
    if(iR<ct.pts.length-1)add(latheM(ct.pts.slice(iR).map(q=>[q.x,q.r]),COL.noz,0.92,"Nozzle extension",p.extCool==="cooled"?"separately cooled; not analyzed":"radiation-cooled skirt, hottest point "+U(c.TextMax,"temp")),dz);
    if(c.regen){const hh=i=>st[i].h||p.chH,sh=main.map((q,i)=>[q.x,q.r+p.chT+hh(i)+1.5e-3]);
      add(latheM(sh,COL.cas,0.28,"Cooling jacket · "+Materials.M[p.matJacket].name,c.N+(c.tube?" tubes":" channels")+" · "+d.fl.name+" "+fmt(d.fl.Tin,"temp")+" → "+U(c.Tout,"temp")+" · pressure drop "+U(c.dP,"mpa")),dz);
      const ns=Math.min(c.N,60),pos=[];for(let a=0;a<ns;a++){const ph=arc()*(a+0.5)/ns,sn=Math.sin(ph),cs=Math.cos(ph);for(let i=0;i<main.length-1;i++){const ra=main[i].r+p.chT+hh(i)*0.5,rb=main[i+1].r+p.chT+hh(i+1)*0.5;pos.push(main[i].x,-ra*sn,ra*cs+dz,main[i+1].x,-rb*sn,rb*cs+dz);}}
      const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute("position",new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos,3));parts.push({k:"mesh",obj:noPick(new THREE.LineSegments(g,new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x378ADD,transparent:true,opacity:0.6})))});}
    const face=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(Rc,48),mat(COL.cas,1));face.rotation.y=PI/2;face.position.set(x0,0,dz);parts.push({k:"mesh",obj:tag(face,"Injector face",(d.inj.type==="pintle"?"central pintle":num(d.inj.n,0)+" "+d.inj.type+" elements")+" · pressure drop "+U(d.inj.dP,"mpa"))});
    if(d.inj.type!=="pintle"){const ne=Math.min(d.inj.n,700),ep=[];for(let i=0;i<ne;i++){const ro=Rc*0.95*Math.sqrt((i+0.5)/ne),th=i*2.399963;ep.push(x0+Rc*0.01,ro*Math.cos(th),ro*Math.sin(th)+dz);}
      const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute("position",new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(ep,3));parts.push({k:"mesh",obj:noPick(new THREE.Points(g,new THREE.PointsMaterial({color:0x13181B,size:Math.max(0.004,Rc*0.035)})))});}
    else add(tag(cyl(Rc*0.18,x0,x0+Rc*0.5,COL.sp||COL.cas,"Pintle"),"Pintle injector","one central element; throttles well"),dz);
    add(latheM([[x0-Rc*0.55,0.01],[x0-Rc*0.45,Rc*0.75],[x0,Rc*1.03]],COL.cas,0.7,"Propellant dome and manifold"),dz);
    add(noPick(glowMesh(latheM([[x0+0.02*ct.Lcyl,Rc*0.9],[ct.pts[8].x,Rc*0.9],[0,Rt*0.92]],new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xEF9F27,transparent:true,opacity:0.3,side:THREE.DoubleSide,depthWrite:false}),1,"Combustion"),0.2,0.1,9)),dz);
    flow.push({f:1/n,path:[[x0,0,Rc*0.5],[ct.pts[8].x,0,Rc*0.55],[0,0,Rt*0.6],[xE,0,Re*0.6],[xE+2.5*2*Re,0,Re]].map(q=>[q[0],0,q[2]]),seg:[[ct.pts[8].x,60,"ab"],[0,300,"ab"],[1e9,r.Isp*9.80665,"ab"]],dz});}
  const t=d.tp;if(t){t.shafts.forEach((s,j)=>{const Rm=Math.max(...s.pumps.map(q=>q.D2/2),s.turb?s.turb.Dm/2:0),side=j===0?1:-1,yb=side*(Rc*1.25+Rm+(n>1?0:0)),zb=n>1?0:0;let x=x0-Rc*0.2;const grp=new THREE.Group();
      s.pumps.forEach(q=>{const L=Math.max(0.03,0.28*q.D2*q.n),m=cyl(q.D2/2,x,x+L,q.key==="o"?COL.ox:COL.fuel,q.name);m.position.y=yb;grp.add(tag(m,q.name+" · "+num(q.rpm,0)+" rpm",q.n+(q.n>1?" stages":" stage")+" · impeller "+U(q.D2,"mm",0)+" · "+U(q.P,"power")+" · efficiency "+(q.eta*100).toFixed(0)+"%"));x+=L+0.02*q.D2;});
      if(s.turb){const L=Math.max(0.02,0.14*s.turb.Dm*s.turb.rows),m=cyl(s.turb.Dm/2,x,x+L,COL.hpt,"Turbine");m.position.y=yb;grp.add(tag(m,"Turbine · "+(s.turb.imp?"impulse":"reaction"),s.turb.rows+(s.turb.rows>1?" rows":" row")+" · inlet "+U(s.turb.Tin,"temp")+" · pressure ratio "+s.turb.PR.toFixed(2)+" · efficiency "+(s.turb.eta*100).toFixed(0)+"%"));x+=L;}
      else if(p.rCycle==="ep"){const m=cyl(Rm*0.8,x,x+Rm*1.6,COL.cu,"Electric motor");m.position.y=yb;grp.add(tag(m,"Electric motor",U(r.feed.Pbatt,"power")+" electrical"));x+=Rm*1.6;}
      const sh=cyl(Math.max(s.d/2,Rm*0.06),x0-Rc*0.25,x+0.02,COL.cas,"Shaft");sh.position.y=yb;grp.add(tag(sh,"Shaft",U(s.d,"mm",0)+" · bearing DN "+num(s.DN/1e6,2)+" million"));parts.push({k:"mesh",obj:grp});});
    if(p.rCycle==="gg"||p.rCycle==="sc"){const m=cyl(Rc*0.3,x0-Rc*1.3,x0-Rc*0.55,COL.comb,p.rCycle==="gg"?"Gas generator":"Preburner");m.position.y=Rc*0.9;parts.push({k:"mesh",obj:tag(m,p.rCycle==="gg"?"Gas generator":"Preburner",p.rCycle==="gg"?U(p.Tgg,"temp")+" · "+(r.mgg/r.mtot*100).toFixed(1)+"% of the propellant, dumped after the turbine":U(p.Tpb,"temp")+" · all of its gas continues to the main chamber")});}}
  else [1,-1].forEach(s=>{const sp=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(Rc*0.8,24,16),mat(COL.cas,CUT?0.45:1));sp.position.set(x0-Rc*2.2,s*Rc*1.0,0);parts.push({k:"mesh",obj:tag(sp,"Pressurant bottle (helium)","tanks at "+U(r.feed.Ptank,"mpa"))});});
  const W=Math.max(2*Re,2.4*Rc)*(1+(n-1)*1.1);
  return Object.assign({},r.geo||{},{kind:"rocketx",D:W,xMin:x0-Rc*1.4,xMax:xE,len:xE-x0+Rc*1.4,vel:{inlet:100},parts,flow,cowl:null,casing:null,shellPts:null,cfg:"ext"});}
/* ---------- validation tables ---------- */
function renderValidationPD(){
  const pc=(a,b)=>{const x=(a-b)/b*100;return (x>0?"+":"")+x.toFixed(1)+"%";},cache={},get=k=>cache[k]||(cache[k]=RocketPD.solve(Object.assign(withDefaults(PRESETS[k].p),{pd:true})));
  let h="<thead><tr><th>Engine</th><th>Quantity</th><th>Published</th><th>This studio</th><th>Difference</th><th>Source</th></tr></thead><tbody>";
  RocketPD.PUB.forEach(v=>{const r=get(v.k),m=v.get(r.pd,r);h+="<tr><td class='l'><b>"+v.e+"</b></td><td class='l'>"+v.q+"</td><td>"+(v.lo?fmt(v.lo,v.u)+"–"+U(v.hi,v.u):U(v.pub,v.u))+"</td><td>"+U(m,v.u)+"</td><td>"+(v.u==="n0"?(m===v.pub?"match":"differs"):v.lo&&m>=v.lo&&m<=v.hi?"within range":pc(m,v.pub))+"</td><td class='l'><small>"+v.src+"</small></td></tr>";});
  $("vaPD").innerHTML=h+"</tbody>";
  h="<thead><tr><th>Engine</th><th>Published dry mass</th><th>Mass buildup</th><th>Difference</th><th>Statistical estimate</th><th>Difference</th></tr></thead><tbody>";
  RocketPD.PUBMASS.forEach(([k,n,m])=>{const r=get(k);h+="<tr><td class='l'>"+n+"</td><td>"+U(m,"mass")+"</td><td>"+U(r.mEng,"mass")+"</td><td>"+pc(r.mEng,m)+"</td><td>"+U(r.mEngStat,"mass")+"</td><td>"+pc(r.mEngStat,m)+"</td></tr>";});
  RocketPD.PUBTEST.forEach(([n,m,k,o,inFit])=>{const r=RocketPD.solve(Object.assign(withDefaults(PRESETS[k].p),o,{pd:true}));if(!r.ok)return;h+="<tr><td class='l'>"+n+(inFit?"":" <span class='sub'>(outside the fit)</span>")+"</td><td>"+U(m,"mass")+"</td><td>"+U(r.mEng,"mass")+"</td><td>"+pc(r.mEng,m)+"</td><td>"+U(r.mEngStat,"mass")+"</td><td>"+pc(r.mEngStat,m)+"</td></tr>";});
  $("vaPDm").innerHTML=h+"</tbody>";
  h="<thead><tr><th>Engine</th><th>Published dry mass</th><th>Mass buildup</th><th>Difference</th><th>Statistical estimate</th><th>Difference</th><th>Stages: studio</th><th>Stages: real engine</th></tr></thead><tbody>";
  GTPD.PUBMASS.forEach(([k,n,m])=>{if(!PRESETS[k])return;const q=withDefaults(PRESETS[k].p),r=GTPD.solve(Object.assign({},q,{pd:true})),st=engMass(solveConcept(q)),d=r.pd;
    h+="<tr><td class='l'>"+n+"</td><td>"+U(m,"mass")+"</td><td>"+U(d.mass.total,"mass")+"</td><td>"+pc(d.mass.total,m)+"</td><td>"+U(st,"mass")+"</td><td>"+pc(st,m)+"</td><td>"+d.comps.map(c=>c.n).join(" + ")+", "+d.turbs.map(c=>c.n).join(" + ")+"</td><td>"+(GTPD.PUBSTG[k]||"–")+"</td></tr>";});
  $("vaGT").innerHTML=h+"</tbody>";
  h="<thead><tr><th style='text-align:left'>Area</th><th style='text-align:left'>Deficiency</th><th style='text-align:left'>Measured gap</th><th style='text-align:left'>Planned fix</th></tr></thead><tbody>";
  KNOWN_ISSUES.forEach(q=>{h+="<tr><td class='l'>"+q.area+"</td><td class='l'>"+q.item+"</td><td class='l'>"+q.measure+"</td><td class='l'>"+q.fix+"</td></tr>";});$("vaKI").innerHTML=h+"</tbody>";}
{const _f=renderValidation;renderValidation=function(){_f();try{renderValidationPD();}catch(e){console.error(e);}};}
