<script>
"use strict";
/* ---------- requirements, margins, sizing to the critical condition, and sensitivity ---------- */
let RQ=null,RQK=null,rqTimer=null,RQROWS=[],snC=null;
const rqPower=p=>p.config==="turboshaft",rqSizable=p=>p.config!=="electric"&&p.config!=="pulsejet";
function rqAvail(p,r,pt){ // what the fixed design delivers at full rating at a flight condition
  const cond={alt:pt.alt,mach:pt.mach||0,dISA:0};
  try{if(isRkt(p)){const o=Rocket.odRocket(r.ref,{alt:pt.alt,thr:1});return o.ok?o.Fn:NaN;}
    if(isRam(p)){const o=Cycle.odRam(r.ref,Object.assign({},cond,{phi:p.phi}));return o&&o.ok?o.Fn:NaN;}
    if(isGT(p)){const o=Cycle.solveMax(r.ref,Object.assign({},cond,{ab:!!p.ab,T7:p.T7}),limsFor(r,p),null);return o&&o.ok?(rqPower(p)?o.Pshaft:o.Fn):NaN;}
    const o=(p.config==="piston"?Powertrain.odPiston:Powertrain.odElectric)(r.ref,Object.assign({},cond,{thr:1,rpmFrac:1,mixture:p.mixture}));return o&&o.ok?o.Fn:NaN;}catch(e){return NaN;}}
function rqScalars(p){const a=[];
  if(isRkt(p)){a.push(["IspV","Vacuum specific impulse","isp","min",r=>r.IspV]);if(p.alt<60000)a.push(["IspSL","Sea-level specific impulse","isp","min",r=>r.IspSL]);a.push(["De","Nozzle exit diameter","len","max",r=>r.De]);}
  else if(isPow(p)&&p.config!=="electric")a.push(["PSFC","Power-specific fuel use at the design point","psfc","max",r=>r.PSFC]);
  else if(p.config!=="electric")a.push(["TSFC","TSFC at the design point","tsfc","max",r=>r.TSFC]);
  if(isGT(p)||isRam(p))a.push(["D","Fan or inlet diameter","len","max",r=>r.geo?r.geo.D:NaN]);
  if(isPD(p))a.push(["L","Length, injector face to nozzle exit","len","max",r=>r.pd?r.pd.ct.Ltot:NaN]);
  a.push(["mass","Engine mass","mass","max",r=>engMass(r)]);return a;}
function rqInit(){const out=R&&R.ok?(rqPower(P)?R.Pshaft:R.Fn):null,second=isRkt(P)?{alt:P.alt>60000?0:100000,mach:0,F:null}:{alt:P.alt>3000?0:10668,mach:P.alt>3000?0:Math.max(P.mach,0.6),F:null};
  RQ={pts:[{alt:P.alt,mach:isRkt(P)?0:P.mach,F:out?+out.toPrecision(4):null},second,{alt:P.alt,mach:isRkt(P)?0:P.mach,F:null}],sc:{}};}
function rqBuild(){const c=$("rqCtl"),uF=rqPower(P)?"kw":"force",rk=isRkt(P);let h='<div class="rqh">Flight conditions, at full rating</div><div class="rqpt"><span></span><small>Altitude, '+unitTxt("alt")+'</small><small>'+(rk?"":"Mach")+'</small><small>Required '+(rqPower(P)?"power":"thrust")+', '+unitTxt(uF)+'</small></div>';
  RQ.pts.forEach((q,i)=>{h+='<div class="rqpt"><b>'+"ABC"[i]+'</b><input type="number" step="any" data-pt="'+i+'" data-f="alt" aria-label="Condition '+"ABC"[i]+' altitude"><input type="number" step="any" data-pt="'+i+'" data-f="mach" aria-label="Condition '+"ABC"[i]+' Mach number"'+(rk?' style="visibility:hidden"':"")+'><input type="number" step="any" data-pt="'+i+'" data-f="F" placeholder="not required" aria-label="Condition '+"ABC"[i]+' requirement"></div>';});
  h+='<div class="rqh">Limits</div>';rqScalars(P).forEach(s=>{h+='<div class="selrow"><span>'+s[1]+(s[3]==="min"?" at least":" at most")+'</span><span style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center"><input type="number" step="any" data-sc="'+s[0]+'" data-u="'+s[2]+'" placeholder="none" aria-label="'+s[1]+'"><small>'+unitTxt(s[2])+'</small></span></div>';});
  if(rqSizable(P))h+='<div style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn" id="rqSize" type="button">Size the engine to these conditions</button></div><p class="sub" style="margin-top:6px">Scales the engine so the most demanding condition is met with no margin to spare.</p>';
  c.innerHTML=h;
  c.querySelectorAll("[data-pt]").forEach(inp=>{const q=RQ.pts[+inp.dataset.pt],f=inp.dataset.f,u=f==="alt"?"alt":f==="F"?uF:"none";if(q[f]!==null&&q[f]!==undefined)inp.value=+cv(q[f],u).toPrecision(5);
    inp.onchange=()=>{const v=parseFloat(inp.value);q[f]=isFinite(v)&&(f!=="F"||v>0)?back(v,u):(f==="F"?null:0);renderMargins();};});
  c.querySelectorAll("[data-sc]").forEach(inp=>{const k=inp.dataset.sc,u=inp.dataset.u;if(RQ.sc[k])inp.value=+cv(RQ.sc[k],u).toPrecision(5);inp.onchange=()=>{const v=parseFloat(inp.value);if(isFinite(v)&&v>0)RQ.sc[k]=back(v,u);else delete RQ.sc[k];renderMargins();};});
  if($("rqSize"))$("rqSize").onclick=rqSizeIt;}
function rqRows(p,r){const rows=[],uF=rqPower(p)?"kw":"force";
  RQ.pts.forEach((q,i)=>{if(!q.F)return;const a=rqAvail(p,r,q);rows.push({g:"Requirements",name:(rqPower(p)?"Power":"Thrust")+" at condition "+"ABC"[i]+" ("+fmt(q.alt,"alt")+" "+unitTxt("alt")+(isRkt(p)?"":", Mach "+(+q.mach).toFixed(2))+")",value:a,limit:q.F,u:uF,m:isFinite(a)?(a-q.F)/q.F:-1,pt:i,note:isFinite(a)?"":"the engine can't run there"});});
  rqScalars(p).forEach(s=>{const lim=RQ.sc[s[0]];if(!lim)return;const v=s[4](r);rows.push({g:"Requirements",name:s[1],value:v,limit:lim,u:s[2],m:isFinite(v)?(s[3]==="min"?(v-lim)/lim:(lim-v)/lim):-1});});
  if(r.pd)r.pd.margins.forEach(m=>rows.push({g:"Design limits",name:m.name,value:m.value,limit:m.limit,u:m.u,m:m.m,conf:m.conf,note:m.note}));return rows;}
function renderMargins(){if(!RQ||!R||!R.ok){$("mgTable").innerHTML="";$("rqNotes").innerHTML="";return;}
  const rows=RQROWS=rqRows(P,R),bar=m=>{const w=Math.min(50,Math.abs(m)*100);return '<span class="mgbar"><i class="'+(m<0?"neg":"")+'" style="'+(m<0?"left:"+(50-w)+"%;width:"+w+"%":"width:"+w+"%")+'"></i></span>';};
  let h="<thead><tr><th style='text-align:left'>Check</th><th>Value</th><th>Limit</th><th>Margin</th></tr></thead><tbody>",g="";
  rows.forEach(q=>{if(q.g!==g){g=q.g;h+="<tr><td class='l' colspan='4' style='font-weight:600;padding-top:10px'>"+g+"</td></tr>";}
    const cls=q.m<0?"mg-bad":q.m<0.05?"mg-tight":"mg-ok",f=v=>q.u==="pct"?(v*100).toFixed(1)+"%":q.u==="none"?(+v).toFixed(2):U(v,q.u);
    h+="<tr><td class='l'>"+q.name+cf(q.conf)+(q.note?"<br><small style='color:var(--muted)'>"+q.note+"</small>":"")+"</td><td>"+f(q.value)+"</td><td>"+(q.g==="Requirements"?"":"")+f(q.limit)+"</td><td class='"+cls+"'>"+(q.m>0?"+":"")+(Math.abs(q.m)>9.99?(q.m>0?">999":"<−999"):(q.m*100).toFixed(0))+"%"+bar(q.m)+"</td></tr>";});
  if(!rows.length)h+="<tr><td class='l' colspan='4' style='color:var(--muted)'>No requirements entered yet.</td></tr>";
  $("mgTable").innerHTML=h+"</tbody>";
  const bad=rows.filter(q=>q.m<0),n=[];
  if(rows.length){const worst=rows.reduce((a,b)=>a.m<b.m?a:b);n.push(bad.length?'<div class="note bad"><b>'+bad.length+" of "+rows.length+" checks fail.</b> The largest shortfall: "+worst.name.replace(/ \(.*\)/,"")+", "+(worst.m*100).toFixed(0)+"%.</div>":'<div class="note ins"><b>All '+rows.length+" checks pass.</b> The tightest: "+worst.name.replace(/ \(.*\)/,"")+", +"+(worst.m*100).toFixed(0)+"%.</div>");}
  if(!R.pd)n.push('<div class="note ins">Design-limit checks (temperatures, stresses, loading and stability limits) appear when Detail is switched to Preliminary.</div>');
  $("rqNotes").innerHTML=n.join("");}
function rqSizeIt(){const pts=RQ.pts.filter(q=>q.F);if(!pts.length){$("rqNotes").innerHTML='<div class="note warn">Enter a required thrust or power for at least one condition first.</div>';return;}
  const id=isPow(P)&&P.config!=="piston"?"Pshaft":P.config==="piston"?"disp":(P.sizeBy==="W"&&isGT(P)?"W":"Fn"),d=PDEF.find(q=>q.id===id);if(!d)return;
  for(let it=0;it<5;it++){const r=solveAny(P);if(!r.ok)break;const k=Math.max(...pts.map(q=>{const a=rqAvail(P,r,q);return isFinite(a)&&a>0?q.F/a:NaN;}).filter(isFinite));if(!isFinite(k))break;
    const [lo,hi]=rangeOf(d);setP(P,id,Math.max(Math.min(lo,getP(P,id)*k),Math.min(Math.max(hi,getP(P,id)*k),getP(P,id)*k)));if(Math.abs(k-1)<0.003)break;}
  ps.value="custom";presetName="Sized to requirements";update("refit");}
/* ---------- sensitivity (tornado) ---------- */
let snFam=null;
function snFill(){const sel=$("snMetric"),cur=sel.value,opts=TRY.filter(t=>!t.show||t.show(P)&&t.k!=="Fn"),f=fam(P),def=(TRDEF[f]||[])[1];sel.innerHTML=opts.map(t=>'<option value="'+t.k+'">'+labOf(t)+'</option>').join("");
  if(snFam===f&&opts.some(t=>t.k===cur))sel.value=cur;else if(opts.some(t=>t.k===def))sel.value=def;snFam=f;}
function snRun(){const t=TRY.find(q=>q.k===$("snMetric").value),f=+$("snPct").value;if(!t)return;const r0=solveAny(deep(P)),base=t.get(r0),pts=t.u==="pct",out=[];if(!isFinite(base)){$("snMsg").textContent="That result isn't available for this design.";return;}
  const dv=x=>pts?(x-base)*100:(x-base)/Math.abs(base||1)*100;
  PDEF.forEach(d=>{if((d.show&&!d.show(P))||(d.dis&&d.dis(P))||d.step>=1)return;const v=getP(P,d.id);if(!isFinite(v)||v===0)return;const [lo,hi]=rangeOf(d),ev=x=>{const q=deep(P);setP(q,d.id,Math.min(hi,Math.max(lo,x)));let r;try{r=solveAny(q);}catch(e){return NaN;}return r&&r.ok?t.get(r):NaN;};
    const a=dv(ev(v*(1-f))),b=dv(ev(v*(1+f)));if(!isFinite(a)&&!isFinite(b))return;out.push({n:labOf(d).replace(/ \(.*\)$/,""),a:isFinite(a)?a:0,b:isFinite(b)?b:0});});
  out.sort((x,y)=>Math.max(Math.abs(y.a),Math.abs(y.b))-Math.max(Math.abs(x.a),Math.abs(x.b)));const top=out.slice(0,10).filter(x=>Math.max(Math.abs(x.a),Math.abs(x.b))>1e-6);
  const o=chartBase();o.indexAxis="y";o.scales={x:axis("Change in "+labOf(t).toLowerCase()+(pts?", percentage points":", %")),y:{type:"category",ticks:{color:css("--ink"),font:{family:"IBM Plex Sans",size:12}},grid:{display:false}}};
  const data={labels:top.map(x=>x.n),datasets:[{label:"Input −"+Math.round(f*100)+"%",data:top.map(x=>x.a),backgroundColor:"#378ADD"},{label:"Input +"+Math.round(f*100)+"%",data:top.map(x=>x.b),backgroundColor:"#D85A30"}]};
  if(!snC)snC=new Chart($("snChart"),{type:"bar",data,options:o});else{snC.data=data;snC.options=o;snC.update();}
  $("snMsg").textContent=top.length?"Checked "+out.length+" inputs. Baseline: "+(pts?(base*100).toFixed(1)+"%":U(base,t.u))+".":"None of the inputs changed this result.";}
$("snRun").onclick=()=>{$("snMsg").textContent="Running…";setTimeout(snRun,20);};
/* ---------- called at the end of every update ---------- */
function afterUpdate(kind){renderPD();snFill();const k=msKey(P)+(P.pd?"+pd":"")+(isRkt(P)?P.alt>60000:"");
  if(k!==RQK||!RQ){const keep=RQ&&RQK&&RQK.split("+")[0]===k.split("+")[0]&&!isRkt(P)?RQ:null;RQK=k;if(keep)RQ=keep;else rqInit();rqBuild();}else if(kind==="units")rqBuild();
  clearTimeout(rqTimer);rqTimer=setTimeout(()=>{try{renderMargins();}catch(e){console.error(e);}},kind==="slider"?450:60);}

<script>
"use strict";
/* ---------- project files: everything needed to pick the work up again, versioned ---------- */
const PJ_APP="aircraft-engine-design-studio",PJ_SCHEMA=2;
function pjObject(){return{app:PJ_APP,schema:PJ_SCHEMA,exported:new Date().toISOString(),units:US?"us":"si",design:{name:presetName,p:deep(P)},baseline:BASE?{p:deep(BASE.p)}:null,saved:deep(SAVED),offDesign:deep(OD),mission:MS?{key:MSK,values:deep(MS)}:null,requirements:RQ?{key:RQK,values:deep(RQ)}:null};}
function pjExport(){const txt=JSON.stringify(pjObject(),null,1);$("svText").value=txt;
  try{const a=document.createElement("a");a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([txt],{type:"application/json"}));a.download=(presetName.replace(/[^\w]+/g,"-").replace(/^-|-$/g,"").toLowerCase()||"engine")+".engine-project.json";document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),3000);}catch(e){}
  $("svMsg").textContent="Project file downloaded. If your browser blocked the download, the same text is in the box below to copy.";}
function pjLoad(txt){let o;try{o=JSON.parse(txt);}catch(e){return "That isn't valid JSON.";}
  if(Array.isArray(o)||(o&&o.p&&o.p.config)){let n=0;(Array.isArray(o)?o:[o]).forEach(s=>{if(s&&s.p&&s.p.config){SAVED.push({name:String(s.name||"Imported design"),p:s.p});n++;}});persist();renderSaved();return n?"Imported "+n+" saved designs.":"No designs found in that JSON.";} // schema 1: a bare list of saved designs
  if(!o||o.app!==PJ_APP)return "That isn't a project file from this studio.";
  if(o.schema>PJ_SCHEMA)return "That project file comes from a newer version of the studio (schema "+o.schema+").";
  if(!o.design||!o.design.p||!o.design.p.config)return "The project file has no design in it.";
  if(Array.isArray(o.saved)){SAVED=o.saved.filter(s=>s&&s.p&&s.p.config).map(s=>({name:String(s.name||"Design"),p:s.p}));persist();}
  P=withDefaults(o.design.p);presetName=String(o.design.name||"Imported design");ps.value="custom";US=o.units==="us";BASE=null;
  if(o.offDesign)Object.assign(OD,o.offDesign);
  update("refit");
  if(o.baseline&&o.baseline.p){const bp=withDefaults(o.baseline.p);BASE={p:bp,r:solveAny(bp)};}
  if(o.mission&&o.mission.values&&o.mission.key===msKey(P)){MS=o.mission.values;MSK=o.mission.key;msRows();renderMission();}
  if(o.requirements&&o.requirements.values&&o.requirements.values.pts){RQ=o.requirements.values;rqBuild();renderMargins();}
  update("units");renderSaved();return "Project loaded: "+presetName+", with "+SAVED.length+" saved designs.";}
$("pjExport").onclick=pjExport;$("pjImport").onclick=()=>$("pjFile").click();

$("svImport").onclick=()=>{$("svMsg").textContent=pjLoad($("svText").value);};

<script>
/* ---------- phase 13: trade-off front, throttle response, design report, STL export, grain flattening ---------- */
const p13Val=(r,q,obj)=>{if(obj==="mission"){const o=missionEval(r,q,MS||MSDEF[msKey(q)]);return o.ok?o.mf:NaN;}if(obj==="mass")return !r.pd&&isGT(q)&&slsRating(r).fb?NaN:engMass(r);if(obj==="dv"){const o=missionEval(r,q,MS||{m:1000,n:1});return o.ok?o.dv:NaN;}if(obj==="pdMg")return r.pd?Math.min(...r.pd.margins.map(m=>m.m)):NaN;return r[obj];};
const p13Unit=k=>k==="mission"||k==="mass"?"mass":k==="dv"?"dv":k==="pdMg"?"pct":(TRY.find(t=>t.k===k)||MET.find(m=>m.k===k)||{u:"none"}).u;
function paObjectives(){const o=opObjectives().slice();if(!o.some(x=>x[0]==="mass"))o.push(["mass","Minimize engine mass",-1]);if(P.pd)o.push(["pdMg","Maximize the smallest design margin",1]);return o;}
let PA={run:false,pts:[],key:"",chart:null};
function paFill(){const k=opKey(P)+(P.pd?"+pd":"");if(PA.key===k)return;PA.key=k;PA.pts=[];const o=paObjectives(),h=o.map(x=>'<option value="'+x[0]+'">'+x[1]+'</option>').join("");$("paX").innerHTML=h;$("paY").innerHTML=h;$("paY").selectedIndex=Math.min(o.length-1,o.findIndex(x=>x[0]==="mass")>0?o.findIndex(x=>x[0]==="mass"):1);$("paLim").parentElement.style.display=P.pd?"":"none";paDraw();}
function paRun(){if(PA.run){PA.run=false;return;}const ids=[...document.querySelectorAll("#opVars input:checked")].map(i=>i.value);if(ids.length<1){$("paMsg").textContent="Tick at least one free variable in the optimizer card above.";return;}
  const obs=paObjectives(),ox=obs.find(o=>o[0]===$("paX").value),oy=obs.find(o=>o[0]===$("paY").value);if(ox[0]===oy[0]){$("paMsg").textContent="Pick two different objectives.";return;}
  const cons={};document.querySelectorAll("#opCons input[data-c]").forEach(i=>cons[i.dataset.c]=back(+i.value,i.dataset.u));if($("opNoSep"))cons.noSep=$("opNoSep").checked;const lim=P.pd&&$("paLim").checked;
  const D=ids.map(defOf),B=D.map(d=>rangeOf(d)),base=deep(P),N=P.pd?(rkind(P)==="solid"&&P.config==="rocket"?60:160):260,pts=[];let i=0;
  const mk=x=>{const q=deep(base);ids.forEach((id,j)=>{let v=B[j][0]+x[j]*(B[j][1]-B[j][0]);if(D[j].step>=1)v=Math.round(v);setP(q,id,v);});return q;};
  const evalQ=(q,cur)=>{const e=opEval(q,ox[0],ox[2],cons);if(e.f>=1e9||e.pen>0)return;const r=e.r,x=p13Val(r,q,ox[0]),y=p13Val(r,q,oy[0]);if(!isFinite(x)||!isFinite(y))return;if(lim&&r.pd&&r.pd.margins.some(m=>m.m<0)&&!cur)return;pts.push({q,x,y,cur});};
  evalQ(deep(base),true);PA.run=true;PA.fr=null;PA.ids=ids;PA.ox=ox;PA.oy=oy;$("paRun").textContent="Stop";
  const step=()=>{const t0=performance.now();while(PA.run&&i<N&&performance.now()-t0<40){let x;const un=q=>ids.map((id,j)=>(getP(q,id)-B[j][0])/(B[j][1]-B[j][0]));
      if(i<N*0.4||pts.length<6)x=ids.map((id,j)=>{const c=(getP(base,id)-B[j][0])/(B[j][1]-B[j][0]);return i%3===0?Math.min(1,Math.max(0,c+(Math.random()-0.5)*0.35)):Math.random();});
      else{if(i%12===0||!PA.fr)PA.fr=pts.filter(a=>!pts.some(b=>b!==a&&ox[2]*b.x>=ox[2]*a.x&&oy[2]*b.y>=oy[2]*a.y&&(ox[2]*b.x>ox[2]*a.x||oy[2]*b.y>oy[2]*a.y)));
        const fr=PA.fr,pa=un(fr[Math.floor(Math.random()*fr.length)].q),pb=un(fr[Math.floor(Math.random()*fr.length)].q),t=Math.random();x=pa.map((v,j)=>Math.min(1,Math.max(0,v+t*(pb[j]-v)+(Math.random()-0.5)*0.12)));} /* later samples breed from the current front: blend two front members and mutate */
      evalQ(mk(x));i++;}
    $("paMsg").textContent="Evaluated "+i+" of "+N+" designs, "+pts.length+" feasible…";if(PA.run&&i<N)setTimeout(step,0);else{PA.run=false;$("paRun").textContent="Map the trade-off";
      pts.forEach(a=>{a.dom=pts.some(b=>b!==a&&ox[2]*b.x>=ox[2]*a.x&&oy[2]*b.y>=oy[2]*a.y&&(ox[2]*b.x>ox[2]*a.x||oy[2]*b.y>oy[2]*a.y));});PA.pts=pts;
      $("paMsg").textContent=pts.length+" feasible designs, "+pts.filter(a=>!a.dom).length+" on the front. Click a point on the front to load that design.";paDraw();}};step();}
function paDraw(){const box=$("paBox");box.hidden=!PA.pts.length;$("paT").innerHTML="";if(!PA.pts.length)return;const ox=PA.ox,oy=PA.oy,ux=p13Unit(ox[0]),uy=p13Unit(oy[0]),cx=v=>ux==="pct"?v*100:cv(v,ux),cy=v=>uy==="pct"?v*100:cv(v,uy);
  const front=PA.pts.filter(a=>!a.dom).sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x),rest=PA.pts.filter(a=>a.dom&&!a.cur),cur=PA.pts.filter(a=>a.cur),nm=o=>o[1].replace(/^(Minimize|Maximize) /,"").replace(/^./,c=>c.toUpperCase()),ut=u=>u==="pct"?"%":unitTxt(u);
  const ds=[{label:"Dominated designs",data:rest.map(a=>({x:cx(a.x),y:cy(a.y)})),backgroundColor:"rgba(136,135,128,0.45)",pointRadius:2.5},{label:"Trade-off front",data:front.map(a=>({x:cx(a.x),y:cy(a.y)})),backgroundColor:"#D85A30",borderColor:"#D85A30",showLine:true,pointRadius:5,pointHoverRadius:7,borderWidth:1.5},{label:"Current design",data:cur.map(a=>({x:cx(a.x),y:cy(a.y)})),backgroundColor:"#378ADD",pointStyle:"rectRot",pointRadius:8}];
  const o=chartBase();o.scales={x:axis(nm(ox)+(ut(ux)?", "+ut(ux):"")),y:axis(nm(oy)+(ut(uy)?", "+ut(uy):""))};o.onClick=(e,els)=>{const el=els.find(q=>q.datasetIndex===1);if(!el)return;const a=front[el.index];PA.ids.forEach(id=>setP(P,id,getP(a.q,id)));ps.value="custom";presetName="Trade-off design";update("cfg");};
  if(!PA.chart)PA.chart=new Chart($("paChart"),{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o});else{PA.chart.data.datasets=ds;PA.chart.options=o;PA.chart.update();}
  const pick=front.length<=7?front:Array.from({length:7},(_,i)=>front[Math.round(i*(front.length-1)/6)]),D=PA.ids.map(defOf),f2=(v,u)=>u==="pct"?(v*100).toFixed(1)+"%":fmt(v,u)+" "+unitTxt(u);
  let h="<thead><tr><th>"+nm(ox)+"</th><th>"+nm(oy)+"</th>"+D.map(d=>"<th>"+labOf(d)+"</th>").join("")+"</tr></thead><tbody>";pick.forEach(a=>{h+="<tr><td>"+f2(a.x,ux)+"</td><td>"+f2(a.y,uy)+"</td>"+D.map((d,j)=>"<td>"+fmt(getP(a.q,PA.ids[j]),d.u)+" "+unitTxt(d.u)+"</td>").join("")+"</tr>";});$("paT").innerHTML=h+"</tbody>";}

/* ---------- throttle response: lumped spool, pump and chamber lags ---------- */
let TRc=null;
function trModel(){const r=R,p=P;if(!r||!r.ok)return null;const out={lags:[],note:""};
  if(isGT(p)){const g=p.pd&&r.pd&&r.pd.comps?r:GTPD.solve(Object.assign(deep(p),{pd:true}));if(!g.ok||!g.pd)return null;const d=g.pd,J={},w={},Pw={};
    const add=(c,sp)=>{c.st.forEach(s=>{J[sp]=(J[sp]||0)+(c.mB+c.mD)/c.n*s.rm*s.rm*0.55;});w[sp]=c.rpm*Math.PI/30;};
    d.comps.forEach(c=>add(c,c.comp==="hpc"?"hp":"lp"));d.turbs.forEach(t=>add(t,t.comp==="hpt"?"hp":"lp"));Pw.hp=g.pw.hpc||g.pw.hpt;Pw.lp=(g.pw.fan||0)+(g.pw.lpc||0)||g.pw.pt||g.pw.lpt||0;
    ["hp","lp"].forEach(sp=>{if(!J[sp]||!Pw[sp])return;const E=0.5*J[sp]*w[sp]*w[sp],tau=E*(1-0.6*0.6)/(0.12*Pw[sp]);out.lags.push({n:sp==="hp"?"Core spool, 60% to 100% speed":(g.pw.pt?"Power turbine and load":"Fan spool, 60% to 100% speed"),t:tau,d:"inertia "+J[sp].toFixed(sp==="hp"&&J[sp]<1?3:1)+" kg·m², 12% of spool power available to accelerate"});});
    out.note="Slam from flight idle. Surge margin limits how much extra fuel the control can add, taken here as 12% of spool power.";out.x0=0.07;out.what=Cycle.isShaft(p)?"Shaft power":"Thrust";}
  else if(p.config==="rocket"&&rkind(p)==="liquid"){const g=p.pd&&r.pd&&r.pd.ct?r:RocketPD.solve(Object.assign(deep(p),{pd:true}));if(!g.ok||!g.pd)return null;const d=g.pd,Lstar=1.0,cs=g.cstar||1800;
    out.lags.push({n:"Chamber fill",t:Math.max(1e-4,Lstar/cs*2.2),d:"characteristic length ÷ characteristic velocity"});
    if(d.tp)d.tp.shafts.forEach((s,i)=>{const Dm=Math.max(...s.pumps.map(q=>q.D2)),Jr=0.5*0.3*s.mass*Math.pow(Dm/2*0.8,2),E=0.5*Jr*s.w*s.w,tau=E/(0.35*s.P);out.lags.push({n:(d.tp.shafts.length>1?s.pumps[0].name.replace(" pump","")+" turbopump":"Turbopump")+" spin-up",t:tau,d:"rotor inertia "+Jr.toPrecision(2)+" kg·m², mean accelerating power 35% of rated"});});
    else out.lags.push({n:"Valve opening",t:0.05,d:"typical main valve stroke"});out.note="Start from valve opening. Real starts are sequenced over 1 to 5 seconds to protect the turbines; this is the fastest the hardware could physically respond.";out.x0=0;out.what="Chamber pressure";}
  else if(p.config==="electric"||p.config==="piston"){const D=p.propD,mb=p.pd&&r.pd?(r.pd.mass.items.find(q=>/ropeller/.test(q[0]))||[0,0.02*D*D*D*60])[1]:6*Math.pow(D,2.4),Jp=mb*Math.pow(0.3*D/2,2),w=p.propRPM*Math.PI/30,E=0.5*Jp*w*w,Pa=0.6*r.Pshaft;
    out.lags.push({n:"Propeller and rotor, 50% to 100% speed",t:E*(1-0.25)/Pa,d:"propeller inertia "+Jp.toFixed(2)+" kg·m², 60% of rated power available to accelerate"});if(p.config==="piston"&&p.induction==="turbo")out.lags.push({n:"Turbocharger lag",t:1.2,d:"typical for an aircraft turbocharger"});out.note="A constant-speed propeller changes thrust faster than this by changing pitch; this is the fixed-pitch case.";out.x0=0.2;out.what="Thrust";}
  else return null;
  const T=out.lags.map(l=>l.t),tmax=Math.max(...T)*5,N=80,xs=[],ys=[];let s=out.lags.map(()=>out.x0);for(let i=0;i<=N;i++){const t=tmax*i/N;xs.push(t);ys.push(100*Math.pow(s[s.length-1],isGT(P)?1.6:1));const dt=tmax/N/20;for(let k=0;k<20;k++){let u=1;for(let j=0;j<s.length;j++){s[j]+=(u-s[j])*(1-Math.exp(-dt/Math.max(T[j]/2.2,1e-6)));u=s[j];}}}
  out.xs=xs;out.ys=ys;out.t90=xs[ys.findIndex(y=>y>=90)];return out;}
function renderTR(){const sec=$("rsSec");if(!sec)return;const m=trModel();sec.hidden=!m;if(!m)return;
  $("rsT").innerHTML="<thead><tr><th>Lag</th><th>Time (10% to 90%)</th><th style='text-align:left'>Basis</th></tr></thead><tbody>"+m.lags.map(l=>"<tr><td class='l'>"+l.n+"</td><td>"+(l.t<0.1?(l.t*1000).toFixed(l.t<0.01?1:0)+" ms":l.t.toFixed(2)+" s")+"</td><td class='l'>"+l.d+"</td></tr>").join("")+"<tr><td class='l'><b>"+m.what+" reaches 90%</b></td><td><b>"+(isFinite(m.t90)?(m.t90<0.1?(m.t90*1000).toFixed(0)+" ms":m.t90.toFixed(2)+" s"):"–")+"</b></td><td class='l'>all lags in series</td></tr></tbody>";$("rsNote").textContent=m.note+" First-order estimate, not validated against engine test data.";
  const ms=m.xs[m.xs.length-1]<0.5,o=chartBase();o.scales={x:axis(ms?"Time, ms":"Time, s"),y:axis(m.what+", % of rated")};o.plugins.legend={display:false};const ds=[{label:m.what,data:m.xs.map((x,i)=>({x:ms?x*1000:x,y:m.ys[i]})),borderColor:"#D85A30",showLine:true,pointRadius:0,borderWidth:2}];
  if(!TRc)TRc=new Chart($("rsChart"),{type:"scatter",data:{datasets:ds},options:o});else{TRc.data.datasets=ds;TRc.options=o;TRc.update();}}

/* ---------- STL export of the 3D model ---------- */
function stlExport(){const tris=[],v=new THREE.Vector3(),a=new THREE.Vector3(),b=new THREE.Vector3(),c=new THREE.Vector3();eng.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  eng.traverse(m=>{if(!m.isMesh||!m.visible||!m.geometry||!m.geometry.attributes.position)return;if(m.material&&m.material.transparent&&m.material.opacity<0.5&&!$("stlShell").checked)return;const g=m.geometry,pos=g.attributes.position,idx=g.index,n=idx?idx.count:pos.count;
    for(let i=0;i<n;i+=3){const ia=idx?idx.getX(i):i,ib=idx?idx.getX(i+1):i+1,ic=idx?idx.getX(i+2):i+2;a.fromBufferAttribute(pos,ia).applyMatrix4(m.matrixWorld);b.fromBufferAttribute(pos,ib).applyMatrix4(m.matrixWorld);c.fromBufferAttribute(pos,ic).applyMatrix4(m.matrixWorld);tris.push(a.x,a.y,a.z,b.x,b.y,b.z,c.x,c.y,c.z);}});
  const nT=tris.length/9;if(!nT){$("exMsg").textContent="Nothing to export.";return;}const buf=new ArrayBuffer(84+50*nT),dv=new DataView(buf),sc=1000;dv.setUint32(80,nT,true);let o=84;
  for(let t=0;t<nT;t++){const k=t*9;a.set(tris[k],tris[k+1],tris[k+2]);b.set(tris[k+3],tris[k+4],tris[k+5]);c.set(tris[k+6],tris[k+7],tris[k+8]);v.subVectors(b,a).cross(c.clone().sub(a)).normalize();dv.setFloat32(o,v.x,true);dv.setFloat32(o+4,v.y,true);dv.setFloat32(o+8,v.z,true);o+=12;for(let j=0;j<9;j++){dv.setFloat32(o,tris[k+j]*sc,true);o+=4;}o+=2;}
  p13Save(new Blob([buf],{type:"model/stl"}),".stl");$("exMsg").textContent="Exported "+nT.toLocaleString()+" triangles, in millimetres. This is the display model: surfaces, not watertight solids, so it suits viewing and layout but not machining or printing without repair.";}
function p13Save(blob,ext){try{const a=document.createElement("a");a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download=(presetName.replace(/[^\w]+/g,"-").replace(/^-|-$/g,"").toLowerCase()||"engine")+ext;document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),4000);}catch(e){$("exMsg").textContent="The download was blocked by the browser.";}}

/* ---------- design report ---------- */
function reportHTML(){const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/&/g,"&amp;").replace(/</g,"&lt;"),tb=id=>{const t=$(id);return t&&t.rows.length?"<table>"+t.innerHTML+"</table>":"";},r=R,p=P;let img="";try{renderer.render(scene,cam);img=renderer.domElement.toDataURL("image/png");}catch(e){}
  let h="<!DOCTYPE html><html lang='en'><head><meta charset='utf-8'><title>"+esc(presetName)+" design report</title><style>body{font:14px/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif;max-width:900px;margin:32px auto;padding:0 20px;color:#1a1a18}h1{font-size:24px;margin:0 0 4px}h2{font-size:17px;margin:28px 0 8px;border-bottom:1px solid #ccc;padding-bottom:4px}h3{font-size:14px;margin:16px 0 6px}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;margin:6px 0 12px;font-size:13px}td,th{border-bottom:1px solid #e3e1da;padding:4px 8px;text-align:right;vertical-align:top}td:first-child,th:first-child,td.l,th.l{text-align:left}.sub{color:#666;font-size:12.5px}img{max-width:100%;border:1px solid #ddd;border-radius:6px}.cf{font-size:11px;color:#777;margin-left:4px}.warn{background:#fdf3e0;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:6px;margin:4px 0}@media print{body{margin:0}}</style></head><body>";
  h+="<h1>"+esc(presetName)+"</h1><p class='sub'>"+esc(p.config==="rocket"?Rocket.PROP[p.rkt].kind+" rocket":p.config)+" · "+(p.pd?"Preliminary":"Concept")+" level · generated "+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+" by the Aircraft engine design studio</p>";h+="<p class='warn'><b>For training only, not for design.</b> Produced by an educational tool with simplified, partly validated models. Do not use these results to design, build, modify, operate or certify any hardware.</p>";if(img)h+="<img alt='3D model of the design' src='"+img+"'>";
  h+="<h2>Performance</h2><table><tbody>"+[...document.querySelectorAll("#metrics .m")].filter(m=>!m.hidden&&m.style.display!=="none").map(m=>{const l=m.querySelector(".k"),v=m.querySelector(".v"),d=m.querySelector(".d");return l&&v&&v.textContent.trim()?"<tr><td>"+esc(l.textContent)+"</td><td>"+esc(v.textContent)+(d&&d.textContent?" <span class='sub'>"+esc(d.textContent)+"</span>":"")+"</td></tr>":"";}).join("")+"</tbody></table>";
  const W=(r.warn||[]).filter(w=>w.lvl!=="info");if(W.length)h+="<h2>Warnings</h2>"+W.map(w=>"<div class='warn'>"+esc(w.t)+"</div>").join("");
  h+="<h2>Design inputs</h2><table><tbody>";let g0="";PDEF.filter(d=>(!d.show||d.show(p))&&isFinite(getP(p,d.id))).forEach(d=>{if(d.g!==g0){g0=d.g;h+="<tr><th colspan='2' class='l'>"+esc(g0)+"</th></tr>";}h+="<tr><td>"+esc(labOf(d))+"</td><td>"+fmt(getP(p,d.id),d.u)+" "+unitTxt(d.u)+"</td></tr>";});
  SELS.filter(s=>(!s.show||s.show(p))).forEach(s=>{const o=(typeof s.opts==="function"?s.opts(p):s.opts).find(o=>String(o[0])===String(getP(p,s.id)));if(o)h+="<tr><td>"+esc(labOf(s))+"</td><td>"+esc(o[1])+"</td></tr>";});h+="</tbody></table>";
  if(tb("mgTable"))h+="<h2>Requirements and margins</h2>"+tb("mgTable");
  if(r.pd){h+="<h2>Preliminary design</h2><p class='sub'>Tags: v validated against published data, c calibrated to it, u unvalidated.</p>";
    if(r.pd.tabs){r.pd.tabs.forEach(t=>{h+="<h3>"+esc(t.name)+"</h3><table>";if(t.cols){h+="<thead><tr>"+t.head.map(x=>"<th>"+esc(x)+"</th>").join("")+"</tr></thead><tbody>"+t.rows.map(q=>"<tr><td>"+esc(q[0])+cf(q[3])+"</td>"+t.cols.map(c=>"<td>"+gVal(q[1](c),q[2])+"</td>").join("")+"</tr>").join("");}
        else h+="<tbody>"+t.rows.map(q=>q.length===1?"<tr><th colspan='2' class='l'>"+esc(q[0])+"</th></tr>":"<tr><td>"+esc(q[0])+cf(q[3])+"</td><td>"+gVal(q[1],q[2])+"</td></tr>").join("");h+="</tbody></table>";});
      h+="<h3>Mass buildup</h3><table><tbody>"+r.pd.mass.items.map(q=>"<tr><td>"+esc(q[0])+cf(q[2])+"</td><td>"+U(q[1],"mass",q[1]<20?1:0)+"</td></tr>").join("")+"<tr><td><b>Total</b></td><td><b>"+U(r.pd.mass.total,"mass",0)+"</b></td></tr></tbody></table>";}
    else{const keep=pdTab;for(let t=0;t<5;t++){const b=document.querySelector("#pdTabs button[data-t='"+t+"']");if(!b)continue;pdTab=t;renderPD();const pane=document.querySelector(".pdpane[data-t='"+t+"']");h+="<h3>"+esc(b.textContent)+"</h3>"+[...pane.querySelectorAll("table")].map(x=>"<table>"+x.innerHTML+"</table>").join("");}pdTab=keep;renderPD();}}
  h+="<h2>Limits of this report</h2><p class='sub'>Produced by a conceptual and preliminary design tool for learning and trade studies. Thrust and fuel consumption are typically within 5–10% of real engines; mass within about 10–20%; preliminary-design margins are prompts to look closer, not clearances. Nothing here has been checked for structural or thermal life, and it must not be used for hardware decisions.</p></body></html>";return h;}
function reportExport(){p13Save(new Blob([reportHTML()],{type:"text/html"}),".design-report.html");$("exMsg").textContent="Report saved as an HTML file. Open it in a browser and print to PDF if you need one.";}

/* ---------- solid grain: search for the flattest pressure trace ---------- */
let GF={run:false};
function grainFlatten(){if(GF.run){GF.run=false;return;}const base=deep(P),cand=[];const shapes=P.grain==="star"||P.grain==="finocyl"?[P.grain]:["finocyl","star"];shapes.forEach(g=>{for(let n=5;n<=12;n++)for(let s=0.3;s<=0.9001;s+=0.075)cand.push({grain:g,starN:n,starIn:+s.toFixed(3)});});
  let i=0,best=null;GF.run=true;$("gfBtn").textContent="Stop";const score=r=>{const m=Object.fromEntries(r.pd.margins.map(x=>[x.id,x]));return m.neut?m.neut.value+(m.pt&&m.pt.m<0?0.15*Math.min(1,-m.pt.m):0):9;};
  const step=()=>{const t0=performance.now();while(GF.run&&i<cand.length&&performance.now()-t0<60){const q=Object.assign(deep(base),cand[i]),r=SRMPD.solve(q);if(r.ok&&r.pd){const s=score(r);if(!best||s<best.s)best={s,c:cand[i],pk:r.pd.margins.find(x=>x.id==="neut").value};}i++;}
    $("gfMsg").textContent="Tried "+i+" of "+cand.length+" port shapes…";if(GF.run&&i<cand.length)setTimeout(step,0);else{GF.run=false;$("gfBtn").textContent="Find the flattest grain";if(best){Object.assign(P,best.c);ps.value="custom";presetName=presetName.replace(/ \(flattened\)$/,"")+" (flattened)";update("cfg");$("gfMsg").textContent="Loaded "+(best.c.grain==="star"?"a "+best.c.starN+"-point star":"a tube with "+best.c.starN+" fin slots")+": peak pressure is "+best.pk.toFixed(2)+" times the average. Web fraction was held, so the motor diameter is unchanged.";}}};step();}

function p13Update(){const ex=$("p13Sec");if(!ex)return;paFill();renderTR();$("gfRow").hidden=!(P.pd&&P.config==="rocket"&&rkind(P)==="solid"&&P.grain!=="sphere");}
$("paRun").onclick=paRun;$("exReport").onclick=reportExport;$("exStl").onclick=stlExport;$("gfBtn").onclick=grainFlatten;
{const au=afterUpdate;afterUpdate=function(kind){au(kind);try{p13Update();}catch(e){console.error(e);}};}
